            showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la modification");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void goBack() {
        Main.showAdminUtilisateurs();
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String msg) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(msg);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.feedback;
import entities.user;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.shape.SVGPath;
import services.feedbackServices;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class AdminFeedbackController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> targetTypeFilter;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> ratingFilter;
    @FXML private Label statusLabel;
    @FXML private FlowPane cardsContainer;
    @FXML private HBox paginationBox;

    private feedbackServices feedbackService = new feedbackServices();
    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private ObservableList<feedback> allFeedbacks = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private List<user> allUsers;

    private int itemsPerPage = 6;
    private int currentPage = 1;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        targetTypeFilter.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Toutes les cibles", "Médecin", "Pharmacie"));
        targetTypeFilter.setValue("Toutes les cibles");

        ratingFilter.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Toutes les notes", "5 ⭐", "4 ⭐", "3 ⭐", "2 ⭐", "1 ⭐"));
        ratingFilter.setValue("Toutes les notes");

        searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterFeedbacks(); });
        targetTypeFilter.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterFeedbacks(); });
        ratingFilter.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterFeedbacks(); });

        loadData();
    }

    private void loadData() {
        try {
            allUsers = userService.getAll();
            allFeedbacks.setAll(feedbackService.getAll());
            filterFeedbacks();
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            statusLabel.setText("Erreur de chargement");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void filterFeedbacks() {
        String query = searchField.getText().toLowerCase();
        String targetType = targetTypeFilter.getValue();
        String rating = ratingFilter.getValue();

        List<feedback> filtered = allFeedbacks.stream()
            .filter(fb -> {
                String comment = fb.getComment().toLowerCase();
                String author = getAuthorName(fb.getUserId()).toLowerCase();
                return comment.contains(query) || author.contains(query);
            })
            .filter(fb -> {
                if (targetType.equals("Toutes les cibles")) return true;
                if (targetType.equals("Médecin")) return fb.getTargetType() == feedback.TargetType.doctor;
                if (targetType.equals("Pharmacie")) return fb.getTargetType() == feedback.TargetType.pharmacy;
                return true;
            })
            .filter(fb -> {
                if (rating.equals("Toutes les notes")) return true;
                int r = Integer.parseInt(rating.substring(0, 1));
                return fb.getRating() == r;
            })
            .collect(Collectors.toList());

        displayFeedbacks(filtered);
    }

    @FXML
    private void resetFilters() {
        searchField.clear();
        targetTypeFilter.setValue("Toutes les cibles");
        ratingFilter.setValue("Toutes les notes");
        currentPage = 1;
        filterFeedbacks();
    }

    private void displayFeedbacks(List<feedback> list) {
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();
        int total = list.size();
        statusLabel.setText("Total: " + total + " avis");

        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(new Label("Aucun avis trouvé"));
            paginationBox.getChildren().clear();
            return;
        }

        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) total / itemsPerPage);
        if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages;
        if (currentPage < 1) currentPage = 1;

        int start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, total);

        for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(createFeedbackCard(list.get(i)));
        }

        updatePagination(totalPages);
    }

    private StackPane createFeedbackCard(feedback fb) {
        StackPane cardRoot = new StackPane();
        cardRoot.setPrefWidth(280);
        cardRoot.setMaxWidth(280);

        // Fond principal blanc arrondi
        VBox cardBody = new VBox(10);
        cardBody.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.1), 10, 0, 0, 4); -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 20;");
        cardBody.setPadding(new Insets(60, 20, 20, 20)); // Padding haut pour laisser place au ruban
        StackPane.setMargin(cardBody, new Insets(20, 0, 0, 0));

        // Ruban bleu (Header)
        VBox ribbon = new VBox(2);
        ribbon.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #3B5998; -fx-background-radius: 15 0 20 0; -fx-padding: 8 15;");
        ribbon.setMaxWidth(200);
        ribbon.setMaxHeight(45);
        Label nameLabel = new Label(fb.isAnonyme() ? "Utilisateur Anonyme" : getAuthorName(fb.getUserId()));
        nameLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: white; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 12;");
        String targetName = getTargetName(fb.getTargetType(), fb.getTargetId());
        Label designationLabel = new Label("Pour: " + targetName);
        designationLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #CBD5E0; -fx-font-size: 10;");
        ribbon.getChildren().addAll(nameLabel, designationLabel);

        // Avatar (Pop-out)
        ImageView avatar = new ImageView();
        avatar.setFitHeight(80);
        avatar.setFitWidth(80);
        javafx.scene.shape.Circle clip = new javafx.scene.shape.Circle(40, 40, 40);
        avatar.setClip(clip);
        loadAuthorPhoto(avatar, fb);
        
        StackPane avatarContainer = new StackPane(avatar);
        avatarContainer.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 45; -fx-padding: 3; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.1), 5, 0, 0, 2);");
        avatarContainer.setMaxSize(86, 86);

        // Etoiles
        Label starsLabel = new Label(getStars(fb.getRating()));
        starsLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 18; -fx-text-fill: #F6AD55;");

        // Commentaire avec ligne d'accent
        HBox commentBox = new HBox(15);
        Region accentLine = new Region();
        accentLine.setPrefWidth(3);
        accentLine.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #3B5998; -fx-background-radius: 2;");
        
        Label commentLabel = new Label(fb.getComment());
        commentLabel.setWrapText(true);
        commentLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 13; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-font-style: italic;");
        commentLabel.setPrefHeight(80);
        commentLabel.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        
        commentBox.getChildren().addAll(accentLine, commentLabel);

        // Footer avec date et bouton supprimer
        HBox footer = new HBox(10);
        footer.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
        Label dateLabel = new Label(fb.getCreationDate().toLocalDateTime().format(java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy")));
        dateLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0; -fx-font-size: 10;");
        
        Button deleteBtn = new Button();
        SVGPath trashIcon = new SVGPath();
        trashIcon.setContent("M 6 19 c 0 1.1 0.9 2 2 2 h 8 c 1.1 0 2 -0.9 2 -2 V 7 H 6 v 12 z M 19 4 h -3.5 l -1 -1 h -5 l -1 1 H 5 v 2 h 14 V 4 z");
        trashIcon.setFill(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#C53030"));
        deleteBtn.setGraphic(trashIcon);
        deleteBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FED7D7; -fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-padding: 8; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        deleteBtn.setOnAction(e -> deleteFeedback(fb));
        footer.getChildren().addAll(dateLabel, deleteBtn);

        cardBody.getChildren().addAll(starsLabel, commentBox, footer);

        // Positionnement des éléments dans le StackPane
        cardRoot.getChildren().addAll(cardBody, ribbon, avatarContainer);
        StackPane.setAlignment(ribbon, Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        StackPane.setAlignment(avatarContainer, Pos.TOP_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(avatarContainer, new Insets(0, 20, 0, 0));

        return cardRoot;
    }

    private void loadAuthorPhoto(ImageView imageView, feedback fb) {
        // Si le feedback est anonyme et qu'un avatar a été choisi, l'afficher
        if (fb.isAnonyme() && fb.getAvatarUrl() != null && !fb.getAvatarUrl().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                byte[] bytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(fb.getAvatarUrl().trim().replaceAll("\\s+", ""));
                imageView.setImage(new Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                return;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                // fallback ci-dessous
            }
        }

        // Feedback non anonyme : afficher la photo de profil de l'auteur
        if (!fb.isAnonyme()) {
            user author = allUsers.stream().filter(u -> u.getUserId() == fb.getUserId()).findFirst().orElse(null);
            if (author != null && author.getUserPhoto() != null && !author.getUserPhoto().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    byte[] bytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(author.getUserPhoto().trim().replaceAll("\\s+", ""));
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                    return;
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    // fallback ci-dessous
                }
            }
        }

        // Fallback : avatar par défaut
        setDefaultAvatar(imageView);
    }

    private void setDefaultAvatar(ImageView imageView) {
        imageView.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
    }

    private void deleteFeedback(feedback fb) {
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirm.setTitle("Confirmation");
        confirm.setHeaderText(null);
        confirm.setContentText("Supprimer cet avis définitivement ?");
        if (confirm.showAndWait().orElse(ButtonType.CANCEL) == ButtonType.OK) {
            try {
                feedbackService.delete(fb);
                loadData();
            } catch (SQLException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }

    private String getAuthorName(int userId) {
        return allUsers.stream()
            .filter(u -> u.getUserId() == userId)
            .map(u -> u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName())
            .findFirst()
            .orElse("Utilisateur inconnu");
    }

    private String getTargetName(feedback.TargetType type, int targetId) {
        return allUsers.stream()
            .filter(u -> u.getUserId() == targetId)
            .map(u -> (type == feedback.TargetType.doctor ? "Dr. " : "") + u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName())
            .findFirst()
            .orElse("Cible inconnue");
    }

    private String getStars(int r) {
        return "★".repeat(r) + "☆".repeat(5 - r);
    }

    private void updatePagination(int totalPages) {
        paginationBox.getChildren().clear();
        if (totalPages <= 1) return;

        for (int i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
            Button btn = new Button(String.valueOf(i));
            btn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
            if (i == currentPage) btn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button-active");
            int p = i;
            btn.setOnAction(e -> { currentPage = p; filterFeedbacks(); });
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(btn);
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.AnchorPane;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import tests.Main;

public class AdminMainController {

    @FXML
    private AnchorPane contentContainer;

    @FXML
    private ImageView logoView;

    @FXML
    private ImageView profileImageView;

    @FXML
    private Label userNameLabel;

    @FXML
    private Button pendingBadgeBtn;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        try {
            Image logo = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/logo.png"));
            if (logo != null) {
                logoView.setImage(logo);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Logo non trouvé");
        }

        loadUserInfo();
        updatePendingBadge();
    }

    public void updatePendingBadge() {
        try {
            services.userServices userService = new services.userServices();
            long pendingCount = userService.getAll().stream()
                    .filter(u -> u.getAccountStatus() == entities.user.AccountStatus.pending)
                    .count();
            if (pendingBadgeBtn != null) {
                if (pendingCount > 0) {
                    pendingBadgeBtn.setText("⏳ En attente (" + pendingCount + ")");
                    pendingBadgeBtn.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-color: #ED8936; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
                } else {
                    pendingBadgeBtn.setText("⏳ En attente (0)");
                    pendingBadgeBtn.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-weight: normal;");
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    public void loadUserInfo() {
        try {
            services.userServices userService = new services.userServices();
            int userId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            for (entities.user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == userId) {
                    userNameLabel.setText(u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName());
                    // Charger la photo (Priorité Avatar, sinon Photo Réelle)
                    String photoBase64 = (u.getAvatarUrl() != null && !u.getAvatarUrl().isEmpty()) ? u.getAvatarUrl() : u.getUserPhoto();

                    // Protection legacy
                    if (photoBase64 != null && photoBase64.startsWith("generated_")) {
                        photoBase64 = u.getUserPhoto();
                    }

                    if (photoBase64 != null && !photoBase64.isEmpty()) {
                        try {
                            String cleanBase64 = photoBase64.trim().replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]", "");
                            byte[] imageBytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(cleanBase64);
                            Image image = new Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(imageBytes));
                            profileImageView.setImage(image);
                        } catch (Exception ex) {
                            setDefaultImage();
                        }
                    } else {
                        setDefaultImage();
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void setDefaultImage() {
        try {
            Image defaultImage = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/default-avatar.png"));
            if (defaultImage != null) {
                profileImageView.setImage(defaultImage);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // Image par défaut
        }
    }

    public void updateContent(Parent content) {
        if (contentContainer != null) {
            contentContainer.getChildren().clear();
            contentContainer.getChildren().add(content);
            AnchorPane.setTopAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setBottomAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setLeftAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setRightAnchor(content, 0.0);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToUtilisateurs() {
        Main.showAdminUtilisateurs();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToDashboard() {
        Main.showAdminDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToPendingUsers() {
        Main.showAdminPendingUsers();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToProfile() {
        Main.showUserProfile();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToAvis() {
        Main.showAdminAvis();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToEvents() {
        Main.showAdminEvents();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToEventAnalytics() {
        Main.showAdminEventAnalytics();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToEventParticipants() {
        Main.showAdminEventParticipants();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToEventReservations() {
        Main.showAdminEventReservations();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToEventRecommendations() {
        Main.showAdminEventRecommendations();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToReclamations() {
        Main.showAdminReclamations();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToPharmacies() {
        Main.showAdminPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToProducts() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    private void logout() {
        Main.showLoginPage();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Reclamation;
import entities.Reponse;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.TextArea;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.ReponseServices;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.Date;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

/**
 * STEP 5b — Create this NEW file at:
 * src/main/java/controller/AdminModifierReponseController.java
 *
 * This replaces AdminUpdateReponseController for the new modal flow.
 *
 * HOW IT WORKS:
 * 1. AdminReclamationController opens this dialog
 * 2. initData(reponse, reclamation, userName) pre-fills all fields
 * 3. Admin edits the textarea and saves
 * 4. Dialog closes → parent refreshes cards
 *
 * NOTE: The old AdminUpdateReponseController.java can be DELETED
 * (or kept if used elsewhere). This new controller handles everything.
 */
public class AdminModifierReponseController implements Initializable {

    // ── FXML nodes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private Label    lblResponseMeta;
    @FXML private Label    lblAvatar;
    @FXML private Label    lblUserName;
    @FXML private Label    lblRecInfo;
    @FXML private Label    lblRecSubject;
    @FXML private TextArea taContent;
    @FXML private Label    lblMessage;

    // ── Services ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private final ReponseServices reponseSvc = new ReponseServices();

    // ── Data ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private Reponse     currentReponse;
    private Reclamation currentReclamation;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL url, ResourceBundle rb) {
        // Populated by initData()
    }

    /**
     * Called by AdminReclamationController right after loading this dialog.
     *
     * @param rep      the existing response to edit
     * @param r        the reclamation this response belongs to
     * @param userName the display name of the user who filed the reclamation
     */
    public void initData(Reponse rep, Reclamation r, String userName) {
        this.currentReponse     = rep;
        this.currentReclamation = r;

        // Subtitle: Response ID + original response date
        lblResponseMeta.setText(
                "Response #" + rep.getResponseId() +
                        "  ·  Répondu le " + rep.getResponseDate()
        );

        // Avatar initials
        String initials = buildInitials(userName);
        lblAvatar.setText(initials);
        lblAvatar.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:" + avatarBg(r.getUserId()) + ";" +
                        "-fx-text-fill:"        + avatarFg(r.getUserId()) + ";" +
                        "-fx-background-radius:20;" +
                        "-fx-font-size:14px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight:bold;" +
                        "-fx-pref-width:40;" +
                        "-fx-pref-height:40;" +
                        "-fx-min-width:40;" +
                        "-fx-min-height:40;" +
                        "-fx-alignment:CENTER;"
        );

        lblUserName.setText(userName);

        lblRecInfo.setText(
                "REC #" + r.getReclamationId() +
                        "  ·  " + r.getReclamDate() +
                        "  ·  " + r.getPriority() +
                        "  ·  " + r.getStatusreclam()
        );

        lblRecSubject.setText("\"" + r.getSubject() + "\"");

        // Pre-fill the textarea with existing response content
        taContent.setText(rep.getContent());
    }

    // ── SAVE MODIFICATION ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @FXML
    private void enregistrerModification() {
        String content = taContent.getText().trim();
        if (content.isEmpty()) {
            showMessage("⚠ Le contenu de la réponse ne peut pas être vide.", false);
            return;
        }

        // Update the response object
        currentReponse.setContent(content);
        currentReponse.setResponseDate(Timestamp.valueOf(LocalDateTime.now()));
        // Save to DB
        reponseSvc.update(currentReponse);

        showMessage("✅ Réponse modifiée avec succès !", true);

        // Close the dialog
        closeDialog();
    }

    // ── CANCEL ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @FXML
    private void annuler() {
        closeDialog();
    }

    // ── HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void closeDialog() {
        Stage stage = (Stage) taContent.getScene().getWindow();
        stage.close();
    }

    private void showMessage(String msg, boolean success) {
        lblMessage.setText(msg);
        lblMessage.setStyle(
                success
                        ? "-fx-text-fill:#3B6D11; -fx-font-size:13px;"
                        : "-fx-text-fill:#A32D2D; -fx-font-size:13px;"
        );
    }

    private String buildInitials(String name) {
        if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty()) return "?";
        String[] parts = name.trim().split("\\s+");
        if (parts.length == 1) {
            return parts[0].length() >= 2
                    ? parts[0].substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()
                    : parts[0].toUpperCase();
        }
        return (parts[0].charAt(0) + "" + parts[parts.length - 1].charAt(0)).toUpperCase();
    }

    private String avatarBg(int userId) {
        String[] c = {"#E6F1FB","#EAF3DE","#FAEEDA","#FCEBEB","#EEEDFE","#E1F5EE"};
        return c[userId % c.length];
    }

    private String avatarFg(int userId) {
        String[] c = {"#185FA5","#3B6D11","#854F0B","#A32D2D","#534AB7","#0F6E56"};
        return c[userId % c.length];
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Notification;
import entities.Reclamation;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import services.EscalationService;
import services.NotificationService;
import services.ReclamationServices;
import services.UserService;
import tests.MainFx;

import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * AdminNotificationsController — Admin-only notifications
 *
 * FIXED:
 * - Filters out user-facing confirmation messages
 * - Only shows admin-relevant alerts (escalades, status changes, new reclamations)
 * - Properly detects manual escalation notifications
 */
public class AdminNotificationsController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private VBox      notifList;
    @FXML private Label     lblUnreadBadge;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cmbTrier;

    private final NotificationService notifSvc = new NotificationService();
    private final ReclamationServices recSvc   = new ReclamationServices();
    private final EscalationService   escSvc   = new EscalationService();
    private final UserService         userSvc  = new UserService();

    private final List<NotifItem> masterList = new ArrayList<>();

    // ── User-only notification types that should NEVER appear in admin view ──
    private static final Set<String> USER_ONLY_TYPES = new HashSet<>(Arrays.asList(
            "ESCALADE_ENVOYEE",      // "Votre demande d'escalade a bien été envoyée..."
            "ESCALADE_CONFIRMATION", // User confirmation messages
            "REPONSE_RECUE",         // User received a response
            "STATUT_CHANGE_USER"     // Status change notification to user
    ));

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL url, ResourceBundle rb) {
        cmbTrier.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Plus récent (défaut)",
                "Plus urgent",
                "Escalades"
        ));
        cmbTrier.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        cmbTrier.setOnAction(e -> appliquerTri());

        loadNotifications();
        refreshBadge();
    }

    // ── LOAD ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void loadNotifications() {
        masterList.clear();

        // ── 1. Open escalations from escalation table (most reliable source) ──
        List<Map<String, Object>> escalades = escSvc.getAllOpen();
        for (Map<String, Object> esc : escalades) {
            int    recId     = (Integer)   esc.get("reclamationId");
            String subject   = (String)    esc.get("subject");
            String reason    = (String)    esc.get("reason");
            Integer urgency  = (Integer)   esc.get("urgency"); // manual escalation urgency level
            Timestamp ts     = (Timestamp) esc.get("createdAt");
            Instant instant  = ts != null ? ts.toInstant() : Instant.now();

            String title = "Escalade manuelle : " + nvl(subject);
            String desc = "Raison : " + (reason != null && !reason.isBlank() ? reason : "Non spécifiée");
            if (urgency != null) {
                desc += " [Urgence : " + urgency + "/10]";
            }

            masterList.add(new NotifItem(
                    "ESCALADE_MANUELLE", "⚠", "#FAEEDA",
                    title, desc, instant, recId, 3
            ));
        }

        // ── 2. Persistent DB notifications (notification table) ───────────────
        try {
            List<Notification> dbNotifs = notifSvc.getByUser(1); // admin userId = 1
            for (Notification n : dbNotifs) {
                String type = nvl(n.getType());

                // SKIP user-only confirmation messages
                if (USER_ONLY_TYPES.contains(type)) continue;

                // Map various escalation types to unified handling
                String normalizedType = normalizeEscaladeType(type);

                String icon  = iconFor(normalizedType);
                String color = colorFor(normalizedType);
                int    prio  = prioFor(normalizedType);
                Instant instant = n.getCreatedAt() != null
                        ? n.getCreatedAt().toInstant() : Instant.EPOCH;

                // Build description based on type
                String desc = buildNotifDescription(n, normalizedType);

                masterList.add(new NotifItem(
                        normalizedType, icon, color,
                        n.getMessage(), desc, instant,
                        n.getReclamationId(), prio
                ));
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("⚠️ DB notifications load error: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        // ── 3. Auto-escalated reclamations (urgent + pending + >3 days + no response) ──
        List<Reclamation> autoEscalades = recSvc.getEscalatedReclamations(3);
        for (Reclamation r : autoEscalades) {
            long daysOld = ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(
                    r.getReclamDate().toLocalDate(),
                    java.time.LocalDate.now()
            );

            // Skip if already added from escalation table
            boolean alreadyAdded = masterList.stream()
                    .anyMatch(n -> n.reclamationId == r.getReclamationId()
                            && n.type.contains("ESCALADE"));
            if (alreadyAdded) continue;

            masterList.add(new NotifItem(
                    "ESCALADE_AUTO", "⚠", "#FAEEDA",
                    "Escalade auto : " + nvl(r.getSubject()),
                    "Urgente · " + daysOld + " j sans réponse · SLA dépassé",
                    r.getReclamDate() != null
                            ? r.getReclamDate().toLocalDate().atStartOfDay()
                            .toInstant(java.time.ZoneOffset.UTC)
                            : Instant.EPOCH,
                    r.getReclamationId(), 3
            ));
        }

        // ── 4. Recent reclamations (last 7 days) ──────────────────────────────
        List<Reclamation> recent = recSvc.getRecentReclamations(7);
        for (Reclamation r : recent) {
            int prio = r.getPriority() == null ? 1
                    : r.getPriority().equalsIgnoreCase("Urgente") ? 3
                    : r.getPriority().equalsIgnoreCase("Moyenne") ? 2 : 1;

            masterList.add(new NotifItem(
                    "NOUVELLE", "📥", "#E6F1FB",
                    "Nouvelle réclamation : " + nvl(r.getSubject()),
                    "Priorité : " + nvl(r.getPriority()) + "  ·  Statut : " + nvl(r.getStatusreclam()),
                    r.getReclamDate() != null
                            ? r.getReclamDate().toLocalDate().atStartOfDay()
                            .toInstant(java.time.ZoneOffset.UTC)
                            : Instant.EPOCH,
                    r.getReclamationId(), prio
            ));
        }

        sortBy("Plus récent (défaut)");
        afficherNotifications(masterList);
    }

    // ── TYPE NORMALIZATION ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Normalizes various escalation notification types to a standard type.
     * This handles cases where the DB stores different type strings.
     */
    private String normalizeEscaladeType(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "GENERIC";
        String upper = type.toUpperCase();
        if (upper.contains("ESCALADE")) {
            if (upper.contains("MANUEL") || upper.contains("USER")) return "ESCALADE_MANUELLE";
            if (upper.contains("AUTO")) return "ESCALADE_AUTO";
            return "ESCALADE";
        }
        return type;
    }

    /**
     * Builds additional description for notification based on type.
     */
    private String buildNotifDescription(Notification n, String normalizedType) {
        switch (normalizedType) {
            case "ESCALADE_MANUELLE":
                return "Action requise : répondre à la réclamation";
            case "ESCALADE_AUTO":
                return "SLA dépassé — réclamation urgente sans réponse";
            case "REPONSE_AJOUTEE":
                return "Nouvelle réponse sur REC-" + n.getReclamationId();
            case "STATUT_CHANGE":
                return "Statut mis à jour sur REC-" + n.getReclamationId();
            case "RESOLUTION":
                return "Réclamation résolue";
            default:
                return "";
        }
    }

    // ── SORT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void appliquerTri() {
        String choice = cmbTrier.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
        List<NotifItem> view = new ArrayList<>(masterList);

        switch (nvl(choice)) {
            case "Plus urgent":
                view.sort(Comparator
                        .comparingInt((NotifItem n) -> n.priority).reversed()
                        .thenComparing(Comparator.comparing((NotifItem n) -> n.instant).reversed()));
                break;

            case "Escalades":
                view.removeIf(n -> !n.type.contains("ESCALADE"));
                view.sort(Comparator.comparing((NotifItem n) -> n.instant).reversed());
                break;

            default:
                view.sort(Comparator.comparing((NotifItem n) -> n.instant).reversed());
                break;
        }

        afficherNotifications(view);
    }

    private void sortBy(String mode) {
        switch (mode) {
            case "Plus urgent":
                masterList.sort(Comparator
                        .comparingInt((NotifItem n) -> n.priority).reversed()
                        .thenComparing(Comparator.comparing((NotifItem n) -> n.instant).reversed()));
                break;
            default:
                masterList.sort(Comparator.comparing((NotifItem n) -> n.instant).reversed());
                break;
        }
    }

    // ── RENDER ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void afficherNotifications(List<NotifItem> items) {
        notifList.getChildren().clear();

        if (items.isEmpty()) {
            Label empty = new Label("🔔  Aucune notification pour le moment.");
            empty.setStyle("-fx-font-size:14px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5; -fx-padding:40;");
            notifList.getChildren().add(empty);
            return;
        }

        for (NotifItem item : items) {
            HBox row = buildRow(item);
            notifList.getChildren().add(row);
        }
    }

    private HBox buildRow(NotifItem item) {
        HBox row = new HBox(12);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        row.setPadding(new Insets(10, 8, 10, 8));
        row.setStyle("-fx-background-color:white;"
                + "-fx-background-radius:10;"
                + "-fx-border-color:#e8eef5;"
                + "-fx-border-radius:10;");

        // ── Icon circle ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
        Label icon = new Label(item.icon);
        icon.setPrefWidth(40);
        icon.setPrefHeight(40);
        icon.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        icon.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + item.bgColor + ";"
                + "-fx-background-radius:20;"
                + "-fx-font-size:18px;");

        // ── Body ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        VBox body = new VBox(4);
        HBox.setHgrow(body, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label title = new Label(item.title);
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;");
        title.setWrapText(true);

        Label desc = new Label(item.description);
        desc.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91;");
        desc.setWrapText(true);

        Label timeLabel = new Label(relativeTime(item.instant));
        timeLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5;");

        body.getChildren().addAll(title, desc, timeLabel);

        // ── Right: "Répondre" button for escalades ONLY ───────────────────
        boolean isEscalade = item.type.contains("ESCALADE");

        if (isEscalade) {
            Button btnRepondre = new Button("Répondre →");
            btnRepondre.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:white;"
                            + "-fx-border-color:#E24B4A;"
                            + "-fx-text-fill:#E24B4A;"
                            + "-fx-border-radius:6;"
                            + "-fx-background-radius:6;"
                            + "-fx-font-size:11px;"
                            + "-fx-font-weight:bold;"
                            + "-fx-cursor:hand;"
                            + "-fx-padding:5 12 5 12;");

            btnRepondre.setOnAction(e -> naviguerVersReclamation(item.reclamationId));

            HBox right = new HBox(btnRepondre);
            right.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
            row.getChildren().addAll(icon, body, right);

        } else {
            // Non-escalade rows: NO click handler, NO button
            row.getChildren().addAll(icon, body);
        }

        // Hover effect
        if (isEscalade) {
            row.setStyle(row.getStyle() + "-fx-cursor:hand;");
            row.setOnMouseEntered(e -> row.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#f4f8fc;"
                            + "-fx-background-radius:10;"
                            + "-fx-border-color:#e8eef5;"
                            + "-fx-border-radius:10;"
                            + "-fx-cursor:hand;"));
            row.setOnMouseExited(e -> row.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:white;"
                            + "-fx-background-radius:10;"
                            + "-fx-border-color:#e8eef5;"
                            + "-fx-border-radius:10;"
                            + "-fx-cursor:hand;"));
        } else {
            row.setOnMouseEntered(e -> row.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#f4f8fc;"
                            + "-fx-background-radius:10;"
                            + "-fx-border-color:#e8eef5;"
                            + "-fx-border-radius:10;"));
            row.setOnMouseExited(e -> row.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:white;"
                            + "-fx-background-radius:10;"
                            + "-fx-border-color:#e8eef5;"
                            + "-fx-border-radius:10;"));
        }

        return row;
    }

    // ── NAVIGATION ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void naviguerVersReclamation(int reclamationId) {
        MainFx.selectedReclamationId = reclamationId;
        MainFx.changeScene("/adminreclamations.fxml");
    }

    // ── BADGE ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void refreshBadge() {
        try {
            int unread = notifSvc.countUnread(1);
            if (lblUnreadBadge != null) {
                lblUnreadBadge.setText(String.valueOf(unread));
                lblUnreadBadge.setVisible(unread > 0);
                lblUnreadBadge.setManaged(unread > 0);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("Badge error: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ── FXML HANDLERS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @FXML
    private void handleMarkAllRead() {
        try {
            notifSvc.markAllAsRead(1);
            loadNotifications();
            refreshBadge();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur markAllRead: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML private void goToDashboard()    { MainFx.changeScene("/admindashboard.fxml"); }
    @FXML private void goToReclamations() { MainFx.changeScene("/adminreclamations.fxml"); }
    @FXML private void goToNotifications(){ loadNotifications(); refreshBadge(); }

    // ── HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private String relativeTime(Instant instant) {
        if (instant == null || instant.equals(Instant.EPOCH)) return "";
        long minutes = ChronoUnit.MINUTES.between(instant, Instant.now());
        if (minutes < 1)  return "À l'instant";
        if (minutes < 60) return minutes + " min";
        long hours = minutes / 60;
        if (hours < 24)   return hours + "h";
        long days = hours / 24;
        return days + " j";
    }

    private String iconFor(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "🔔";
        switch (type) {
            case "ESCALADE_AUTO":     return "⚠";
            case "ESCALADE_MANUELLE": return "⚠";
            case "ESCALADE":          return "⚠";
            case "REPONSE_AJOUTEE":   return "💬";
            case "STATUT_CHANGE":     return "🔄";
            case "RESOLUTION":        return "✅";
            default:                  return "🔔";
        }
    }

    private String colorFor(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "#E6F1FB";
        switch (type) {
            case "ESCALADE_AUTO":
            case "ESCALADE_MANUELLE":
            case "ESCALADE":          return "#FAEEDA";
            case "RESOLUTION":        return "#E8F5E9";
            case "REPONSE_AJOUTEE":   return "#E3F2FD";
            default:                  return "#E6F1FB";
        }
    }

    private int prioFor(String type) {
        if (type == null) return 1;
        switch (type) {
            case "ESCALADE_AUTO":
            case "ESCALADE_MANUELLE":
            case "ESCALADE":          return 3;
            case "STATUT_CHANGE":     return 2;
            default:                  return 1;
        }
    }

    private String nvl(String s) { return s != null ? s : "—"; }

    // ── Inner data class ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private static class NotifItem {
        final String  type;
        final String  icon;
        final String  bgColor;
        final String  title;
        final String  description;
        final Instant instant;
        final int     reclamationId;
        final int     priority;

        NotifItem(String type, String icon, String bgColor,
                  String title, String description,
                  Instant instant, int reclamationId, int priority) {
            this.type          = type;
            this.icon          = icon;
            this.bgColor       = bgColor;
            this.title         = title;
            this.description   = description;
            this.instant       = instant;
            this.reclamationId = reclamationId;
            this.priority      = priority;
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Pharmacy;
import javafx.animation.FadeTransition;
import javafx.animation.ParallelTransition;
import javafx.animation.TranslateTransition;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.util.Duration;
import services.PharmacyServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.File;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.*;

public class AdminPharmacyController {

    @FXML private FlowPane pharmacyContainer;
    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cityCombo;
    @FXML private Label totalLabel;
    @FXML private Label pageLabel;

    private PharmacyServices pharmacyServices;

    private List<Pharmacy> pharmacies = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<Pharmacy> displayedPharmacies = new ArrayList<>();

    private int currentPage = 0;
    private final int itemsPerPage = 6;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        pharmacyServices = new PharmacyServices();

        setupCityCombo();
        loadPharmacies();

        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterPharmacies());
        }

        if (cityCombo != null) {
            cityCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterPharmacies());
        }
    }

    private void setupCityCombo() {
        if (cityCombo != null) {
            cityCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(getAllTunisianCities()));
            cityCombo.setValue("Toutes les villes");
            cityCombo.setVisibleRowCount(8);
        }
    }

    private void loadPharmacies() {
        try {
            pharmacies = pharmacyServices.getAll();
            displayedPharmacies = new ArrayList<>(pharmacies);

            loadCities();

            currentPage = 0;
            showCurrentPage();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Erreur : impossible de charger les pharmacies.");
            }
        }
    }

    private void loadCities() {
        if (cityCombo == null) {
            return;
        }

        Set<String> finalCities = new TreeSet<>();
        finalCities.addAll(getAllTunisianCitiesWithoutDefault());

        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : pharmacies) {
            String city = extractCity(pharmacy.getAddress());

            if (!city.isEmpty() && !isCountry(city)) {
                finalCities.add(city);
            }
        }

        List<String> cities = new ArrayList<>();
        cities.add("Toutes les villes");
        cities.addAll(finalCities);

        cityCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(cities));
        cityCombo.setValue("Toutes les villes");
        cityCombo.setVisibleRowCount(8);
    }

    private List<String> getAllTunisianCities() {
        List<String> cities = new ArrayList<>();
        cities.add("Toutes les villes");
        cities.addAll(getAllTunisianCitiesWithoutDefault());
        return cities;
    }

    private List<String> getAllTunisianCitiesWithoutDefault() {
        return Arrays.asList(
                "Ariana", "Béja", "Ben Arous", "Bizerte", "Gabès", "Gafsa",
                "Jendouba", "Kairouan", "Kasserine", "Kébili", "Le Kef",
                "Mahdia", "La Manouba", "Médenine", "Monastir", "Nabeul",
                "Sfax", "Sidi Bouzid", "Siliana", "Sousse", "Tataouine",
                "Tozeur", "Tunis", "Zaghouan"
        );
    }

    private void filterPharmacies() {
        currentPage = 0;

        String keyword = searchField == null ? "" : normalizeText(searchField.getText());
        String selectedCity = cityCombo == null ? "Toutes les villes" : cityCombo.getValue();

        List<Pharmacy> filtered = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : pharmacies) {
            String name = normalizeText(pharmacy.getPharmacyName());
            String address = normalizeText(pharmacy.getAddress());
            String phone = normalizeText(pharmacy.getPhone());
            String email = normalizeText(pharmacy.getEmail());
            String hours = normalizeText(pharmacy.getOpeningHours());
            String status = normalizeText(pharmacy.getPharmacyStatus());
            String city = normalizeText(extractCity(pharmacy.getAddress()));

            boolean matchKeyword = keyword.isEmpty()
                    || name.contains(keyword)
                    || address.contains(keyword)
                    || phone.contains(keyword)
                    || email.contains(keyword)
                    || hours.contains(keyword)
                    || status.contains(keyword)
                    || city.contains(keyword);

            boolean matchCity = selectedCity == null
                    || selectedCity.equals("Toutes les villes")
                    || city.equals(normalizeText(selectedCity))
                    || address.contains(normalizeText(selectedCity));

            if (matchKeyword && matchCity) {
                filtered.add(pharmacy);
            }
        }

        displayedPharmacies = filtered;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    private void showCurrentPage() {
        if (pharmacyContainer == null) {
            return;
        }

        pharmacyContainer.getChildren().clear();

        pharmacyContainer.setHgap(24);
        pharmacyContainer.setVgap(24);
        pharmacyContainer.setPadding(new Insets(20, 0, 95, 0));

        if (displayedPharmacies == null || displayedPharmacies.isEmpty()) {
            VBox emptyBox = new VBox();
            emptyBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
            emptyBox.setSpacing(8);
            emptyBox.setPrefWidth(900);
            emptyBox.setPrefHeight(250);

            Label title = new Label("Aucune pharmacie trouvée.");
            title.setStyle(
                    "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 20px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
            );

            Label text = new Label("Essayez un autre filtre.");
            text.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #64748B; -fx-font-size: 14px;");

            emptyBox.getChildren().addAll(title, text);
            pharmacyContainer.getChildren().add(emptyBox);

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Total : 0 pharmacie");
            }

            if (pageLabel != null) {
                pageLabel.setText("Page 0 / 0");
            }

            return;
        }

        int totalItems = displayedPharmacies.size();
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) totalItems / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < 0) {
            currentPage = 0;
        }

        if (currentPage >= totalPages) {
            currentPage = totalPages - 1;
        }

        int start = currentPage * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, totalItems);

        int index = 0;

        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : displayedPharmacies.subList(start, end)) {
            VBox card = createPharmacyCard(pharmacy);
            pharmacyContainer.getChildren().add(card);
            playCardEntrance(card, index);
            index++;
        }

        if (totalLabel != null) {
            totalLabel.setText("Total : " + totalItems + " pharmacies");
        }

        if (pageLabel != null) {
            pageLabel.setText("Page " + (currentPage + 1) + " / " + totalPages);
        }
    }

    private VBox createPharmacyCard(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        VBox card = new VBox();
        card.setSpacing(14);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(20));
        card.setPrefWidth(300);
        card.setMinWidth(300);
        card.setMaxWidth(300);
        card.setMinHeight(390);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);

        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FFFFFF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 20;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: transparent;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 20;"
        );

        DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
        normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
        normalShadow.setRadius(24);
        normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
        card.setEffect(normalShadow);

        StackPane imageFrame = createImageFrame(pharmacy);

        Label name = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyName()));
        name.setWrapText(true);
        name.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        name.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 20px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;"
        );

        Label phoneTitle = new Label("Téléphone");
        phoneTitle.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #0F172A; -fx-font-size: 13px; -fx-font-weight: bold;");

        Label phone = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getPhone()));
        phone.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #0F172A; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Label hoursTitle = new Label("Horaires");
        hoursTitle.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #0F172A; -fx-font-size: 13px; -fx-font-weight: bold;");

        Label hours = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getOpeningHours()));
        hours.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #0F172A; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Label typeTitle = new Label("Type");
        typeTitle.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #0F172A; -fx-font-size: 13px; -fx-font-weight: bold;");

        Label type = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyStatus()));
        type.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #64748B; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Region spacer = new Region();
        VBox.setVgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Button editButton = new Button("Modifier");
        editButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        editButton.setPrefHeight(42);
        editButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );
        editButton.setOnAction(event -> openUpdatePharmacy(pharmacy));

        Button deleteButton = new Button("Supprimer");
        deleteButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        deleteButton.setPrefHeight(42);
        deleteButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FEE2E2;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #B91C1C;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );
        deleteButton.setOnAction(event -> deletePharmacy(pharmacy));

        HBox actionButtons = new HBox();
        actionButtons.setSpacing(8);
        HBox.setHgrow(editButton, Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox.setHgrow(deleteButton, Priority.ALWAYS);
        actionButtons.getChildren().addAll(editButton, deleteButton);

        setupButtonHover(editButton);
        setupButtonHover(deleteButton);
        setupCardHover(card);

        card.getChildren().addAll(
                imageFrame,
                name,
                phoneTitle,
                phone,
                hoursTitle,
                hours,
                typeTitle,
                type,
                spacer,
                actionButtons
        );

        return card;
    }

    private StackPane createImageFrame(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        StackPane imageFrame = new StackPane();
        imageFrame.setPrefWidth(260);
        imageFrame.setPrefHeight(120);
        imageFrame.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 16;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #DBEAFE;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 16;"
        );

        ImageView imageView = new ImageView();
        imageView.setFitWidth(230);
        imageView.setFitHeight(105);
        imageView.setPreserveRatio(true);
        imageView.setSmooth(true);

        if (loadPharmacyImage(pharmacy, imageView)) {
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(imageView);
        } else {
            Label icon = new Label("⚕");
            icon.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 34px; -fx-text-fill: #94A3B8;");
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(icon);
        }

        return imageFrame;
    }

    private boolean loadPharmacyImage(Pharmacy pharmacy, ImageView imageView) {
        try {
            String imagePath = pharmacy.getImage();

            if (imagePath != null && !imagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                File file = new File(imagePath);

                if (file.exists()) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                    return true;
                }
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur chargement image pharmacie : " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return false;
    }

    private void setupCardHover(VBox card) {
        card.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(-4);

            DropShadow hoverShadow = new DropShadow();
            hoverShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.10));
            hoverShadow.setRadius(32);
            hoverShadow.setOffsetY(12);
            card.setEffect(hoverShadow);

            translate.play();
        });

        card.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(0);

            DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
            normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
            normalShadow.setRadius(24);
            normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
            card.setEffect(normalShadow);

            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupButtonHover(Button button) {
        button.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(-2);
            translate.play();
        });

        button.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(0);
            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void playCardEntrance(Node node, int index) {
        node.setOpacity(0);
        node.setTranslateY(20);

        FadeTransition fade = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        fade.setFromValue(0);
        fade.setToValue(1);
        fade.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        translate.setFromY(20);
        translate.setToY(0);
        translate.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        new ParallelTransition(fade, translate).play();
    }

    private void openUpdatePharmacy(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        if (pharmacy == null) {
            return;
        }

        AdminUpdatePharmacyController.setSelectedPharmacy(pharmacy);
        Main.showAdminUpdatePharmacy();
    }

    private void deletePharmacy(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        if (pharmacy == null) {
            return;
        }

        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirm.setTitle("Confirmation");
        confirm.setHeaderText("Supprimer la pharmacie");
        confirm.setContentText("Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer cette pharmacie ?");

        Optional<ButtonType> result = confirm.showAndWait();

        if (result.isPresent() && result.get() == ButtonType.OK) {
            try {
                pharmacyServices.delete(pharmacy);
                loadPharmacies();
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "Succès", "Pharmacie supprimée avec succès.");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de supprimer la pharmacie.");
            }
        }
    }

    private String extractCity(String address) {
        if (address == null || address.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return "";
        }

        String cleanAddress = address.trim();
        String[] parts = cleanAddress.split(",");

        if (parts.length >= 2) {
            String last = parts[parts.length - 1].trim();
            String beforeLast = parts[parts.length - 2].trim();

            if (isCountry(last)) {
                return beforeLast;
            }

            return last;
        }

        return cleanAddress;
    }

    private boolean isCountry(String value) {
        String normalized = normalizeText(value);
        return normalized.equals("tunisie")
                || normalized.equals("tunisia")
                || normalized.equals("tunisien");
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return "";
        }

        String result = value.trim().toLowerCase();
        result = Normalizer.normalize(result, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        result = result.replaceAll("\\p{M}", "");
        result = result.replaceAll("\\s+", " ");

        return result;
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    @FXML
    public void resetFilters() {
        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.clear();
        }

        if (cityCombo != null) {
            cityCombo.setValue("Toutes les villes");
        }

        displayedPharmacies = new ArrayList<>(pharmacies);
        currentPage = 0;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    @FXML
    public void nextPage() {
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) displayedPharmacies.size() / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < totalPages - 1) {
            currentPage++;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void previousPage() {
        if (currentPage > 0) {
            currentPage--;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showAdminDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showAdminPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goAddPharmacy() {
        Main.showAdminAddPharmacy();
    }

    private void showAlert(Alert.AlertType type, String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(type);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(title);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Product;
import javafx.animation.FadeTransition;
import javafx.animation.ParallelTransition;
import javafx.animation.TranslateTransition;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.util.Duration;
import services.ProductServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.File;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.*;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import services.ExportProductService;

public class AdminProductController {

    @FXML private FlowPane productContainer;
    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> categoryCombo;
    @FXML private Label totalLabel;
    @FXML private Label pageLabel;

    private ProductServices productServices;

    private List<Product> products = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<ProductGroup> groupedProducts = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<ProductGroup> displayedGroups = new ArrayList<>();

    private int currentPage = 0;
    private final int itemsPerPage = 6;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        productServices = new ProductServices();

        setupCategoryCombo();
        loadProducts();

        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterProducts());
        }

        if (categoryCombo != null) {
            categoryCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterProducts());
        }
    }

    private void setupCategoryCombo() {
        if (categoryCombo != null) {
            categoryCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Toutes les catégories"));
            categoryCombo.setValue("Toutes les catégories");
            categoryCombo.setVisibleRowCount(6);
        }
    }

    private void loadProducts() {
        try {
            products = productServices.getAll();

            groupedProducts = groupProducts(products);
            displayedGroups = new ArrayList<>(groupedProducts);

            loadCategories();

            currentPage = 0;
            showCurrentPage();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Erreur : impossible de charger les produits.");
            }
        }
    }

    private void loadCategories() {
        if (categoryCombo == null) {
            return;
        }

        Set<String> categories = new TreeSet<>();
        categories.add("Toutes les catégories");

        for (ProductGroup group : groupedProducts) {
            String category = safe(group.mainProduct.getCategory());

            if (!category.isEmpty()) {
                categories.add(category);
            }
        }

        categoryCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(categories));
        categoryCombo.setValue("Toutes les catégories");
    }

    private List<ProductGroup> groupProducts(List<Product> productList) {
        Map<String, ProductGroup> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        for (Product product : productList) {
            String key = normalizeText(product.getProductName()) + "|" + normalizeText(product.getDosage());

            if (!map.containsKey(key)) {
                map.put(key, new ProductGroup(product));
            }

            map.get(key).items.add(product);
        }

        return new ArrayList<>(map.values());
    }

    private void filterProducts() {
        currentPage = 0;

        String keyword = searchField == null ? "" : normalizeText(searchField.getText());
        String selectedCategory = categoryCombo == null ? "Toutes les catégories" : categoryCombo.getValue();

        List<ProductGroup> filtered = new ArrayList<>();

        for (ProductGroup group : groupedProducts) {
            Product product = group.mainProduct;

            String name = normalizeText(product.getProductName());
            String description = normalizeText(product.getDescription());
            String dosage = normalizeText(product.getDosage());
            String category = normalizeText(product.getCategory());
            String laboratory = normalizeText(product.getLaboratory());
            String therapeuticClass = normalizeText(product.getTherapeuticClass());

            boolean matchKeyword = keyword.isEmpty()
                    || name.contains(keyword)
                    || description.contains(keyword)
                    || dosage.contains(keyword)
                    || category.contains(keyword)
                    || laboratory.contains(keyword)
                    || therapeuticClass.contains(keyword);

            boolean matchCategory = selectedCategory == null
                    || selectedCategory.equals("Toutes les catégories")
                    || category.equals(normalizeText(selectedCategory));

            if (matchKeyword && matchCategory) {
                filtered.add(group);
            }
        }

        displayedGroups = filtered;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    private void showCurrentPage() {
        if (productContainer == null) {
            return;
        }

        productContainer.getChildren().clear();

        productContainer.setHgap(24);
        productContainer.setVgap(24);
        productContainer.setPadding(new Insets(20, 0, 95, 0));

        if (displayedGroups == null || displayedGroups.isEmpty()) {
            VBox emptyBox = new VBox();
            emptyBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
            emptyBox.setSpacing(8);
            emptyBox.setPrefWidth(900);
            emptyBox.setPrefHeight(250);

            Label title = new Label("Aucun produit trouvé.");
            title.setStyle(
                    "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 20px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
            );

            Label text = new Label("Essayez un autre filtre.");
            text.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #64748B; -fx-font-size: 14px;");

            emptyBox.getChildren().addAll(title, text);
            productContainer.getChildren().add(emptyBox);

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Total : 0 produit");
            }

            if (pageLabel != null) {
                pageLabel.setText("Page 0 / 0");
            }

            return;
        }

        int totalItems = displayedGroups.size();
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) totalItems / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < 0) {
            currentPage = 0;
        }

        if (currentPage >= totalPages) {
            currentPage = totalPages - 1;
        }

        int start = currentPage * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, totalItems);

        int index = 0;

        for (ProductGroup group : displayedGroups.subList(start, end)) {
            VBox card = createProductCard(group);
            productContainer.getChildren().add(card);
            playCardEntrance(card, index);
            index++;
        }

        if (totalLabel != null) {
            totalLabel.setText("Total : " + totalItems + " produits");
        }

        if (pageLabel != null) {
            pageLabel.setText("Page " + (currentPage + 1) + " / " + totalPages);
        }
    }

    private VBox createProductCard(ProductGroup group) {
        Product product = group.mainProduct;

        VBox card = new VBox();
        card.setSpacing(10);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(18));
        card.setPrefWidth(300);
        card.setMinWidth(300);
        card.setMaxWidth(300);
        card.setMinHeight(520);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);

        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FFFFFF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 20;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: transparent;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 20;"
        );

        DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
        normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
        normalShadow.setRadius(24);
        normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
        card.setEffect(normalShadow);

        StackPane imageFrame = createImageFrame(product);

        Label name = new Label(safe(product.getProductName()));
        name.setWrapText(true);
        name.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        name.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 18px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;"
        );

        Label description = new Label(safe(product.getDescription()));
        description.setWrapText(true);
        description.setMaxHeight(42);
        description.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #64748B;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                        "-fx-line-spacing: 4;"
        );

        GridPane detailsGrid = new GridPane();
        detailsGrid.setHgap(26);
        detailsGrid.setVgap(8);
        detailsGrid.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);

        addInfo(detailsGrid, "Dosage", safe(product.getDosage()), 0, 0);
        addInfo(detailsGrid, "Prix", formatPrice(product.getPrice()) + " DT", 0, 1);
        addInfo(detailsGrid, "Catégorie", safe(product.getCategory()), 1, 0);
        addInfo(detailsGrid, "Quantité", String.valueOf(product.getAvailableQuantity()), 1, 1);
        addInfo(detailsGrid, "Laboratoire", safe(product.getLaboratory()), 2, 0);
        addInfo(detailsGrid, "Classe", safe(product.getTherapeuticClass()), 2, 1);

        Region spacer = new Region();
        VBox.setVgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Button editButton = new Button("Modifier");
        editButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        editButton.setPrefHeight(44);
        editButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );
        editButton.setOnAction(event -> openUpdateProduct(product));

        Button deleteButton = new Button("Supprimer tout");
        deleteButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        deleteButton.setPrefHeight(40);
        deleteButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FEE2E2;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #B91C1C;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );
        deleteButton.setOnAction(event -> deleteProductGroup(group));

        setupButtonHover(editButton);
        setupButtonHover(deleteButton);
        setupCardHover(card);

        card.getChildren().addAll(
                imageFrame,
                name,
                description,
                detailsGrid,
                spacer,
                editButton,
                deleteButton
        );

        return card;
    }

    private void addInfo(GridPane grid, String titleText, String valueText, int row, int col) {
        VBox box = new VBox();
        box.setSpacing(2);
        box.setPrefWidth(110);

        Label title = new Label(titleText);
        title.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 12px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
        );

        Label value = new Label(valueText == null || valueText.trim().isEmpty() ? "-" : valueText);
        value.setWrapText(true);
        value.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 12px;"
        );

        box.getChildren().addAll(title, value);
        grid.add(box, col, row);
    }

    private StackPane createImageFrame(Product product) {
        StackPane imageFrame = new StackPane();
        imageFrame.setPrefWidth(260);
        imageFrame.setPrefHeight(145);
        imageFrame.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 16;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #DBEAFE;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 16;"
        );

        ImageView imageView = new ImageView();
        imageView.setFitWidth(230);
        imageView.setFitHeight(120);
        imageView.setPreserveRatio(true);
        imageView.setSmooth(true);

        if (loadProductImage(product, imageView)) {
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(imageView);
        } else {
            Label icon = new Label("💊");
            icon.setStyle(
                    "-fx-font-size: 38px;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #94A3B8;"
            );
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(icon);
        }

        return imageFrame;
    }

    private boolean loadProductImage(Product product, ImageView imageView) {
        try {
            String imagePath = product.getImage();

            if (imagePath != null && !imagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                File file = new File(imagePath);

                if (file.exists()) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                    return true;
                }
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur chargement image produit : " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return false;
    }

    private void setupCardHover(VBox card) {
        card.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(-4);

            DropShadow hoverShadow = new DropShadow();
            hoverShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.10));
            hoverShadow.setRadius(32);
            hoverShadow.setOffsetY(12);
            card.setEffect(hoverShadow);

            translate.play();
        });

        card.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(0);

            DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
            normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
            normalShadow.setRadius(24);
            normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
            card.setEffect(normalShadow);

            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupButtonHover(Button button) {
        button.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(-2);
            translate.play();
        });

        button.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(0);
            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void playCardEntrance(Node node, int index) {
        node.setOpacity(0);
        node.setTranslateY(20);

        FadeTransition fade = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        fade.setFromValue(0);
        fade.setToValue(1);
        fade.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        translate.setFromY(20);
        translate.setToY(0);
        translate.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        new ParallelTransition(fade, translate).play();
    }

    private void openUpdateProduct(Product product) {
        if (product == null) {
            return;
        }

        AdminUpdateProductController.setSelectedProduct(product);
        Main.showAdminUpdateProduct();
    }

    private void deleteProductGroup(ProductGroup group) {
        if (group == null || group.items == null || group.items.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirm.setTitle("Confirmation");
        confirm.setHeaderText("Supprimer le produit");
        confirm.setContentText("Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer ce produit ?");

        Optional<ButtonType> result = confirm.showAndWait();

        if (result.isPresent() && result.get() == ButtonType.OK) {
            try {
                for (Product product : group.items) {
                    productServices.delete(product);
                }

                loadProducts();
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "Succès", "Produit supprimé avec succès.");

            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de supprimer le produit.");
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void resetFilters() {
        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.clear();
        }

        if (categoryCombo != null) {
            categoryCombo.setValue("Toutes les catégories");
        }

        displayedGroups = new ArrayList<>(groupedProducts);
        currentPage = 0;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    @FXML
    public void nextPage() {
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) displayedGroups.size() / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < totalPages - 1) {
            currentPage++;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void previousPage() {
        if (currentPage > 0) {
            currentPage--;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showAdminDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showAdminPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goAddProduct() {
        Main.showAdminAddProduct();
    }

    @FXML
    public void exportExcel() {
        if (products == null || products.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Attention", "Aucun produit à exporter.");
            return;
        }

        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
