        fileChooser.setTitle("Enregistrer le fichier Excel");
        fileChooser.setInitialFileName("liste_produits.xlsx");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Fichiers Excel", "*.xlsx"));

        File file = fileChooser.showSaveDialog(productContainer.getScene().getWindow());
        if (file != null) {
            try {
                ExportProductService exportService = new ExportProductService();
                exportService.exportToExcel(products, file);
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "Succès", "Liste des produits exportée en Excel avec succès.");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de générer le fichier Excel.");
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void exportPdf() {
        if (products == null || products.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Attention", "Aucun produit à exporter.");
            return;
        }

        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Enregistrer le fichier PDF");
        fileChooser.setInitialFileName("liste_produits.pdf");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Fichiers PDF", "*.pdf"));

        File file = fileChooser.showSaveDialog(productContainer.getScene().getWindow());
        if (file != null) {
            try {
                ExportProductService exportService = new ExportProductService();
                exportService.exportToPdf(products, file);
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "Succès", "Liste des produits exportée en PDF avec succès.");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de générer le fichier PDF.");
            }
        }
    }

    private String formatPrice(double price) {
        return String.format("%.1f", price);
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return "";
        }

        String result = value.trim().toLowerCase();
        result = Normalizer.normalize(result, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        result = result.replaceAll("\\p{M}", "");
        result = result.replaceAll("\\s+", " ");

        return result;
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    private void showAlert(Alert.AlertType type, String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(type);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(title);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private static class ProductGroup {
        Product mainProduct;
        List<Product> items = new ArrayList<>();

        ProductGroup(Product mainProduct) {
            this.mainProduct = mainProduct;
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Reclamation;
import entities.Reponse;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.scene.shape.Circle;
import javafx.stage.Modality;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.ReclamationServices;
import services.ReponseServices;
import services.UserService;
import tests.MainFx;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.URL;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * FIXED AdminReclamationController
 * - "Répondre" button now opens modal dialog with proper error handling
 * - Added "Supprimer la réponse" button when response exists
 * - Added scroll-to-reclamation support from notifications
 */
public class AdminReclamationController implements Initializable {

    // ── FXML nodes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private TextField   searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> comboStatut;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> comboPriorite;
    @FXML private VBox        cardsContainer;
    @FXML private Label       lblTotal;

    // ── Services ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private final ReclamationServices reclamationSvc = new ReclamationServices();
    private final ReponseServices     reponseSvc     = new ReponseServices();
    private final UserService         userSvc        = new UserService();

    private List<Reclamation> allReclamations;

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @Override
    public void initialize(URL url, ResourceBundle rb) {
        comboStatut.getItems().addAll(
                "Tous les statuts", "En attente", "En cours", "Résolu"
        );
        comboStatut.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();

        comboPriorite.getItems().addAll(
                "Toutes les priorités", "Urgente", "Moyenne", "Faible"
        );
        comboPriorite.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();

        loadAll();
    }

    // ── LOAD & BUILD ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void loadAll() {
        allReclamations = reclamationSvc.getAll();
        buildCards(allReclamations);

        // Handle navigation from notifications with target reclamation
        if (MainFx.selectedReclamationId != 0) {
            final int targetId = MainFx.selectedReclamationId;
            MainFx.selectedReclamationId = 0; // clear immediately
            javafx.application.Platform.runLater(() -> scrollToReclamation(targetId));
        }
    }

    private void buildCards(List<Reclamation> list) {
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();
        lblTotal.setText("Total : " + list.size() + " réclamation(s)");

        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            Label empty = new Label("Aucune réclamation trouvée.");
            empty.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#5d6f91; -fx-font-size:14px; -fx-padding:30;");
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(empty);
            return;
        }

        for (Reclamation r : list) {
            VBox card = buildOneCard(r);
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(card);
        }
    }

    private VBox buildOneCard(Reclamation r) {
        List<Reponse> reponses = reponseSvc.getByReclamation(r.getReclamationId());
        Reponse existingReponse = reponses.isEmpty() ? null : reponses.get(0);

        VBox card = new VBox(0);
        card.setStyle(buildCardStyle(r, existingReponse));
        card.setUserData(r.getReclamationId()); // ← store ID for scroll-to feature

        // ── HEADER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        HBox header = new HBox(12);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        header.setPadding(new Insets(14, 18, 10, 18));

        String initials = userSvc.getInitials(r.getUserId());
        Label avatar = buildAvatar(initials, r.getUserId());

        VBox meta = new VBox(2);
        HBox.setHgrow(meta, Priority.ALWAYS);

        String userName = userSvc.getUserName(r.getUserId());
        Label lblUser = new Label(userName);
        lblUser.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;");

        Label lblIdDate = new Label(
                "REC #" + r.getReclamationId() + "  ·  " + r.getReclamDate()
        );
        lblIdDate.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5;");

        meta.getChildren().addAll(lblUser, lblIdDate);

        HBox badges = new HBox(6);
        badges.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
        badges.getChildren().addAll(
                buildStatusBadge(r.getStatusreclam()),
                buildPriorityBadge(r.getPriority()),
                buildCategoryBadge(r.getCategory()),
                buildEscaladeBadge(r)
        );

        header.getChildren().addAll(avatar, meta, badges);

        // ── SUBJECT & DESCRIPTION ───────────────────────────────────────
        Label lblSubject = new Label(r.getSubject());
        lblSubject.setStyle("-fx-font-size:15px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44; -fx-padding:0 18 4 18;");
        lblSubject.setWrapText(true);

        Label lblDesc = new Label(r.getDescription());
        lblDesc.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91; -fx-padding:0 18 12 18;");
        lblDesc.setWrapText(true);
        lblDesc.setMaxWidth(900);

        Separator divider = new Separator();
        divider.setPadding(new Insets(0, 18, 0, 18));

        // ── RESPONSE ZONE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        VBox responseZone = buildResponseZone(r, existingReponse, userName);

        card.getChildren().addAll(header, lblSubject, lblDesc, divider, responseZone);
        return card;
    }

    /**
     * Builds the bottom zone of the card with status dropdown + response actions.
     */
    private VBox buildResponseZone(Reclamation r, Reponse rep, String userName) {
        VBox zone = new VBox(8);
        zone.setPadding(new Insets(12, 18, 14, 18));
        zone.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#f9fbff; -fx-background-radius:0 0 16 16;");

        // ── Status selector row ─────────────────────────────────────────
        HBox statusRow = new HBox(10);
        statusRow.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        Label statusLabel = new Label("Statut:");
        statusLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91;");

        ComboBox<String> statusCombo = new ComboBox<String>();
        statusCombo.getItems().addAll("En attente", "En cours", "Résolu");
        statusCombo.setValue(r.getStatusreclam());
        statusCombo.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:white;" +
                        "-fx-border-color:#dce5f0;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius:6;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius:6;" +
                        "-fx-font-size:12px;" +
                        "-fx-pref-width:130;"
        );

        // Save button (appears when status changes)
        Button btnSaveStatus = new Button("✓");
        btnSaveStatus.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:#378ADD;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill:white;" +
                        "-fx-font-size:11px;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius:6;" +
                        "-fx-padding:4 10 4 10;" +
                        "-fx-cursor:hand;"
        );
        btnSaveStatus.setVisible(false);
        btnSaveStatus.setManaged(false);

        // Show save button when status changes
        statusCombo.setOnAction(e -> {
            String newStatus = statusCombo.getValue();
            if (newStatus != null && !newStatus.equals(r.getStatusreclam())) {
                btnSaveStatus.setVisible(true);
                btnSaveStatus.setManaged(true);
            } else {
                btnSaveStatus.setVisible(false);
                btnSaveStatus.setManaged(false);
            }
        });

        // Save the new status
        btnSaveStatus.setOnAction(e -> {
            String newStatus = statusCombo.getValue();
            reclamationSvc.updateStatus(r.getReclamationId(), newStatus);
            r.setStatusreclam(newStatus); // update local object
            loadAll(); // refresh all cards
        });

        statusRow.getChildren().addAll(statusLabel, statusCombo, btnSaveStatus);

        // ── Response zone label ─────────────────────────────────────────
        HBox zoneLabel = new HBox(6);
        zoneLabel.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        Label iconReply = new Label("↩ Réponse admin");
        iconReply.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill:#9aabb5; -fx-letter-spacing:0.05em;"
        );
        zoneLabel.getChildren().add(iconReply);

        if (rep != null) {
            // ── Response EXISTS ──────────────────────────────────────
            Label lblContent = new Label(rep.getContent());
            lblContent.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44; -fx-wrap-text:true;");
            lblContent.setWrapText(true);

            Label lblMeta = new Label(
                    "Répondu le " + rep.getResponseDate() +
                            "  ·  Admin  ·  Response #" + rep.getResponseId()
            );
            lblMeta.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5;");

            // Buttons row: Modifier + Supprimer
            HBox btnRow = new HBox(10);
            btnRow.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

            Button btnModifier = new Button("✏ Modifier la réponse");
            btnModifier.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:white;" +
                            "-fx-border-color:#dce5f0;" +
                            "-fx-border-radius:8;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius:8;" +
                            "-fx-font-size:12px;" +
                            "-fx-padding:7 14 7 14;" +
                            "-fx-cursor:hand;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;"
            );
            btnModifier.setOnAction(e -> ouvrirModifierReponse(rep, r, userName));

            Button btnSupprimer = new Button("🗑 Supprimer");
            btnSupprimer.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#FCEBEB;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill:#A32D2D;" +
                            "-fx-font-size:12px;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius:8;" +
                            "-fx-padding:7 14 7 14;" +
                            "-fx-cursor:hand;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight:bold;"
            );
            btnSupprimer.setOnAction(e -> supprimerReponse(rep, r));

            btnRow.getChildren().addAll(btnModifier, btnSupprimer);

            zone.getChildren().addAll(statusRow, zoneLabel, lblContent, lblMeta, btnRow);

        } else {
            // ── No response yet ────────────────────────────────────────
            Label lblNoReponse = new Label("Aucune réponse envoyée pour le moment.");
            lblNoReponse.setStyle(
                    "-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5; -fx-font-style:italic;"
            );

            Button btnRepondre = new Button("➤ Répondre");
            btnRepondre.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#378ADD;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill:white;" +
                            "-fx-font-size:13px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight:bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius:8;" +
                            "-fx-padding:8 18 8 18;" +
                            "-fx-cursor:hand;"
            );
            btnRepondre.setOnAction(e -> ouvrirAjouterReponse(r, userName));

            zone.getChildren().addAll(statusRow, zoneLabel, lblNoReponse, btnRepondre);
        }

        return zone;
    }

    // ── SCROLL TO RECLAMATION (from notifications) ───────────────────────────

    /**
     * Scrolls to and highlights the card matching the given reclamation ID.
     * Called when navigating from AdminNotificationsController.
     */
    private void scrollToReclamation(int targetId) {
        for (javafx.scene.Node node : cardsContainer.getChildren()) {
            if (node instanceof VBox card) {
                Integer cardId = (Integer) card.getUserData();
                if (cardId != null && cardId == targetId) {
                    // Highlight with blue border + glow
                    String highlightStyle =
                            "-fx-background-color:white;" +
                                    "-fx-background-radius:16;" +
                                    "-fx-border-radius:16;" +
                                    "-fx-border-color:#378ADD;" +
                                    "-fx-border-width:3;" +
                                    "-fx-effect:dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(55,138,221,0.4),20,0,0,4);";

                    card.setStyle(highlightStyle);

                    // Scroll into view
                    ScrollPane scrollPane = findScrollPane(card);
                    if (scrollPane != null) {
                        double contentHeight = cardsContainer.getBoundsInParent().getHeight();
                        double cardY = card.getBoundsInParent().getMinY();
                        double viewportHeight = scrollPane.getViewportBounds().getHeight();

                        double vValue = 0;
                        if (contentHeight > viewportHeight) {
                            vValue = cardY / (contentHeight - viewportHeight);
                            vValue = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vValue));
                        }
                        scrollPane.setVvalue(vValue);
                    }

                    // Remove highlight after 3 seconds
                    javafx.animation.PauseTransition pause =
                            new javafx.animation.PauseTransition(javafx.util.Duration.seconds(3));
                    pause.setOnFinished(e -> {
                        // Rebuild card normally by reloading
                        loadAll();
                    });
                    pause.play();

                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Walks up the scene graph to find the parent ScrollPane.
     */
    private ScrollPane findScrollPane(javafx.scene.Node node) {
        javafx.scene.Node parent = node.getParent();
        while (parent != null) {
            if (parent instanceof ScrollPane) {
                return (ScrollPane) parent;
            }
            parent = parent.getParent();
        }
        return null;
    }

    // ── DIALOG: AJOUTER RÉPONSE ───────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * FIXED: Opens add response modal with proper URL validation
     */
    private void ouvrirAjouterReponse(Reclamation r, String userName) {
        try {
            URL fxmlUrl = getClass().getResource("/adminajouterreponse.fxml");

            if (fxmlUrl == null) {
                showError("Fichier adminajouterreponse.fxml introuvable.\n" +
                        "Vérifiez que le fichier existe dans src/main/resources/");
                return;
            }

            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(fxmlUrl);
            Parent root = loader.load();

            AdminAjouterReponseController ctrl = loader.getController();
            if (ctrl == null) {
                showError("Erreur: le contrôleur du dialog n'a pas été initialisé.");
                return;
            }

            ctrl.initData(r, userName);

            Stage dialog = new Stage();
            dialog.setTitle("Répondre à la réclamation");
            dialog.initModality(Modality.APPLICATION_MODAL);
            dialog.setScene(new Scene(root));
            dialog.setResizable(false);
            dialog.showAndWait();

            // Refresh after dialog closes
            loadAll();

        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showError("Impossible d'ouvrir le formulaire de réponse:\n" + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ── DIALOG: MODIFIER RÉPONSE ──────────────────────────────────────────

    private void ouvrirModifierReponse(Reponse rep, Reclamation r, String userName) {
        try {
            URL fxmlUrl = getClass().getResource("/adminmodifierreponse.fxml");

            if (fxmlUrl == null) {
                showError("Fichier adminmodifierreponse.fxml introuvable.");
                return;
            }

            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(fxmlUrl);
            Parent root = loader.load();

            AdminModifierReponseController ctrl = loader.getController();
            if (ctrl == null) {
                showError("Erreur: le contrôleur du dialog n'a pas été initialisé.");
                return;
            }

            ctrl.initData(rep, r, userName);

            Stage dialog = new Stage();
            dialog.setTitle("Modifier la réponse");
            dialog.initModality(Modality.APPLICATION_MODAL);
            dialog.setScene(new Scene(root));
            dialog.setResizable(false);
            dialog.showAndWait();

            loadAll();

        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showError("Impossible d'ouvrir le formulaire de modification:\n" + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ── NEW: DELETE RESPONSE ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Deletes a response after confirmation dialog.
     * Updates reclamation status back to "En attente" after deletion.
     */
    private void supprimerReponse(Reponse rep, Reclamation r) {
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirm.setTitle("Confirmer la suppression");
        confirm.setHeaderText("Supprimer la réponse ?");
        confirm.setContentText(
                "Cette action est irréversible.\n" +
                        "La réponse #" + rep.getResponseId() + " sera supprimée " +
                        "et le statut de la réclamation reviendra à \"En attente\"."
        );

        ButtonType btnOui = new ButtonType("Supprimer", ButtonBar.ButtonData.OK_DONE);
        ButtonType btnNon = new ButtonType("Annuler", ButtonBar.ButtonData.CANCEL_CLOSE);
        confirm.getButtonTypes().setAll(btnOui, btnNon);

        confirm.showAndWait().ifPresent(result -> {
            if (result == btnOui) {
                try {
                    // Delete the response
                    reponseSvc.delete(rep.getResponseId());

                    // Update reclamation status back to "En attente"
                    reclamationSvc.updateStatus(r.getReclamationId(), "En attente");

                    // Refresh cards
                    loadAll();

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    e.printStackTrace();
                    showError("Erreur lors de la suppression:\n" + e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // ── FILTERS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @FXML
    private void applyFilters() {
        if (allReclamations == null) return;

        String search   = searchField.getText().toLowerCase().trim();
        String statut   = comboStatut.getValue();
        String priorite = comboPriorite.getValue();

        List<Reclamation> filtered = allReclamations.stream()
                .filter(r -> {
                    boolean matchSearch = search.isEmpty()
                            || r.getSubject().toLowerCase().contains(search)
                            || r.getDescription().toLowerCase().contains(search)
                            || String.valueOf(r.getUserId()).contains(search)
                            || userSvc.getUserName(r.getUserId()).toLowerCase().contains(search);

                    boolean matchStatut = statut == null
                            || statut.equals("Tous les statuts")
                            || r.getStatusreclam().equalsIgnoreCase(statut);

                    boolean matchPriorite = priorite == null
                            || priorite.equals("Toutes les priorités")
                            || r.getPriority().equalsIgnoreCase(priorite);

                    return matchSearch && matchStatut && matchPriorite;
                })
                .collect(Collectors.toList());

        buildCards(filtered);
    }

    @FXML
    private void reinitialiserFiltres() {
        searchField.clear();
        comboStatut.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        comboPriorite.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        buildCards(allReclamations);
    }

    // ── BADGE BUILDERS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private Label buildAvatar(String initials, int userId) {
        Label av = new Label(initials);
        av.setPrefWidth(38);
        av.setPrefHeight(38);
        av.setMinWidth(38);
        av.setMinHeight(38);
        av.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        av.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:" + avatarColor(userId) + ";" +
                        "-fx-background-radius:19;" +
                        "-fx-font-size:13px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight:bold;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill:" + avatarTextColor(userId) + ";"
        );
        return av;
    }

    private String avatarColor(int userId) {
        String[] colors = {"#E6F1FB","#EAF3DE","#FAEEDA","#FCEBEB","#EEEDFE","#E1F5EE"};
        return colors[userId % colors.length];
    }

    private String avatarTextColor(int userId) {
        String[] colors = {"#185FA5","#3B6D11","#854F0B","#A32D2D","#534AB7","#0F6E56"};
        return colors[userId % colors.length];
    }

    private Label buildStatusBadge(String status) {
        Label b = new Label(status == null ? "—" : status);
        if (status == null) { b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#f0f4fa; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91;"); return b; }
        String s = status.toLowerCase();
        if (s.equals("en attente")) {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#FAEEDA; -fx-text-fill:#854F0B;");
        } else if (s.equals("en cours")) {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#E6F1FB; -fx-text-fill:#185FA5;");
        } else if (s.equals("résolu") || s.equals("resolved")) {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#EAF3DE; -fx-text-fill:#3B6D11;");
        } else {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#f0f4fa; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91;");
        }
        return b;
    }

    private Label buildPriorityBadge(String priority) {
        Label b = new Label(priority == null ? "—" : priority);
        if (priority == null) { b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#f0f4fa; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91;"); return b; }
        String p = priority.toLowerCase();
        if (p.equals("urgente")) {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#FCEBEB; -fx-text-fill:#A32D2D;");
        } else if (p.equals("moyenne")) {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#FAEEDA; -fx-text-fill:#854F0B;");
        } else if (p.equals("faible")) {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#EAF3DE; -fx-text-fill:#3B6D11;");
        } else {
            b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#f0f4fa; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91;");
        }
        return b;
    }

    private Label buildCategoryBadge(String category) {
        Label b = new Label(category == null ? "Autre" : category);
        b.setStyle(badgeBase() + "-fx-background-color:#f0f4fa; -fx-text-fill:#4c6290;");
        return b;
    }

    private Label buildEscaladeBadge(Reclamation r) {
        boolean isEscalated = false;
        try {
            long daysOld = ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(
                    r.getReclamDate().toLocalDate(), LocalDate.now()
            );
            List<Reponse> reps = reponseSvc.getByReclamation(r.getReclamationId());
            isEscalated = "urgente".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getPriority())
                    && "en attente".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getStatusreclam())
                    && reps.isEmpty()
                    && daysOld > 3;
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        Label b = new Label(isEscalated ? "⚠ Escaladée" : "");
        if (isEscalated) {
            b.setStyle(
                    badgeBase() +
                            "-fx-background-color:#FCEBEB;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill:#A32D2D;" +
                            "-fx-border-color:#E24B4A;" +
                            "-fx-border-radius:20;" +
                            "-fx-border-width:1;"
            );
        }
        b.setVisible(isEscalated);
        b.setManaged(isEscalated);
        return b;
    }

    private String badgeBase() {
        return "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold;" +
                "-fx-background-radius:20; -fx-padding:3 10 3 10;";
    }

    private String buildCardStyle(Reclamation r, Reponse rep) {
        String base =
                "-fx-background-color:white;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius:16;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius:16;" +
                        "-fx-effect:dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(15,23,42,0.08),10,0,0,3);";

        try {
            long daysOld = ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(
                    r.getReclamDate().toLocalDate(), LocalDate.now()
            );
            if ("urgente".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getPriority())
                    && "en attente".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getStatusreclam())
                    && rep == null
                    && daysOld > 3) {
                return base + "-fx-border-color:#E24B4A; -fx-border-width:0 0 0 4;";
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        if (rep != null) {
            return base + "-fx-border-color:#639922; -fx-border-width:0 0 0 4;";
        }

        return base + "-fx-border-color:#EF9F27; -fx-border-width:0 0 0 4;";
    }

    // ── UTILITY ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void showError(String msg) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, msg, ButtonType.OK);
        alert.setHeaderText("Erreur");
        alert.setTitle("Erreur");
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    // ── NAVIGATION ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @FXML private void ouvrirAdminDashboard()    { MainFx.changeScene("/admindashboard.fxml"); }
    @FXML private void ouvrirAdminReclamations() { MainFx.changeScene("/adminreclamations.fxml"); }
    @FXML
    private void goToNotifications() {
        MainFx.changeScene("/adminnotifications.fxml");
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Pharmacy;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import services.PharmacyServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.File;
import java.util.List;

public class AdminUpdatePharmacyController {

    @FXML private TextField pharmacyIdField;

    @FXML private ImageView imagePreview;
    @FXML private Label imageLabel;

    @FXML private TextField pharmacyNameField;
    @FXML private TextField addressField;
    @FXML private TextField phoneField;
    @FXML private TextField emailField;
    @FXML private TextField openingHoursField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> pharmacyStatusCombo;

    private PharmacyServices pharmacyServices;

    private static Pharmacy selectedPharmacy;
    private String selectedImagePath = "";

    public static void setSelectedPharmacy(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        selectedPharmacy = pharmacy;
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        pharmacyServices = new PharmacyServices();

        if (pharmacyStatusCombo != null) {
            pharmacyStatusCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                    "Jour",
                    "Pharmacie de nuit"
            ));
            pharmacyStatusCombo.setValue("Jour");
        }

        if (selectedPharmacy != null) {
            loadSelectedPharmacy();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void loadPharmacy() {
        if (pharmacyIdField == null) {
            loadSelectedPharmacy();
            return;
        }

        String idText = safe(pharmacyIdField.getText());

        if (idText.isEmpty()) {
            if (selectedPharmacy != null) {
                loadSelectedPharmacy();
            } else {
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "ID obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir l'ID de la pharmacie.");
            }
            return;
        }

        int pharmacyId;

        try {
            pharmacyId = Integer.parseInt(idText);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "ID invalide", "Veuillez saisir un ID numérique.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            List<Pharmacy> pharmacies = pharmacyServices.getAll();

            for (Pharmacy pharmacy : pharmacies) {
                if (pharmacy.getPharmacyId() == pharmacyId) {
                    selectedPharmacy = pharmacy;
                    loadSelectedPharmacy();
                    return;
                }
            }

            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Introuvable", "Aucune pharmacie trouvée avec cet ID.");

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de chercher la pharmacie.");
        }
    }

    private void loadSelectedPharmacy() {
        if (selectedPharmacy == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (pharmacyIdField != null) {
            pharmacyIdField.setText(String.valueOf(selectedPharmacy.getPharmacyId()));
        }

        if (pharmacyNameField != null) {
            pharmacyNameField.setText(safe(selectedPharmacy.getPharmacyName()));
        }

        if (addressField != null) {
            addressField.setText(safe(selectedPharmacy.getAddress()));
        }

        if (phoneField != null) {
            phoneField.setText(safe(selectedPharmacy.getPhone()));
        }

        if (emailField != null) {
            emailField.setText(safe(selectedPharmacy.getEmail()));
        }

        if (openingHoursField != null) {
            openingHoursField.setText(safe(selectedPharmacy.getOpeningHours()));
        }

        if (pharmacyStatusCombo != null) {
            pharmacyStatusCombo.setValue(normalizeStatusForCombo(selectedPharmacy.getPharmacyStatus()));
        }

        selectedImagePath = safe(selectedPharmacy.getImage());

        if (imageLabel != null) {
            if (selectedImagePath.isEmpty()) {
                imageLabel.setText("Aucune image sélectionnée");
            } else {
                imageLabel.setText(new File(selectedImagePath).getName());
            }
        }

        loadImagePreview();
    }

    private String normalizeStatusForCombo(String status) {
        String value = safe(status);

        if (value.equalsIgnoreCase("Nuit")
                || value.equalsIgnoreCase("Pharmacie nuit")
                || value.equalsIgnoreCase("Pharmacie de nuit")) {
            return "Pharmacie de nuit";
        }

        return "Jour";
    }

    private void loadImagePreview() {
        try {
            if (imagePreview == null) {
                return;
            }

            if (selectedImagePath == null || selectedImagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                imagePreview.setImage(null);
                return;
            }

            File file = new File(selectedImagePath);

            if (file.exists()) {
                imagePreview.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
            } else {
                imagePreview.setImage(null);
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur chargement image pharmacie : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void chooseImage() {
        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Choisir une image de la pharmacie");

        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().addAll(
                new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Images", "*.png", "*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.gif", "*.webp"),
                new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Tous les fichiers", "*.*")
        );

        File selectedFile = fileChooser.showOpenDialog(null);

        if (selectedFile != null) {
            selectedImagePath = selectedFile.getAbsolutePath();

            if (imageLabel != null) {
                imageLabel.setText(selectedFile.getName());
            }

            if (imagePreview != null) {
                imagePreview.setImage(new Image(selectedFile.toURI().toString()));
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void removeImage() {
        selectedImagePath = "";

        if (imagePreview != null) {
            imagePreview.setImage(null);
        }

        if (imageLabel != null) {
            imageLabel.setText("Aucune image sélectionnée");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void updatePharmacy() {
        if (selectedPharmacy == null) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Aucune pharmacie sélectionnée.");
            return;
        }

        String pharmacyName = pharmacyNameField == null ? "" : safe(pharmacyNameField.getText());
        String address = addressField == null ? "" : safe(addressField.getText());
        String phone = phoneField == null ? "" : safe(phoneField.getText());
        String email = emailField == null ? "" : safe(emailField.getText());
        String openingHours = openingHoursField == null ? "" : safe(openingHoursField.getText());
        String pharmacyStatus = pharmacyStatusCombo == null ? "Jour" : safe(pharmacyStatusCombo.getValue());

        if (pharmacyName.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir le nom de la pharmacie.");
            return;
        }

        if (phone.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir le téléphone de la pharmacie.");
            return;
        }

        if (openingHours.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir les horaires de la pharmacie.");
            return;
        }

        if (address.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir l'adresse de la pharmacie.");
            return;
        }

        if (pharmacyStatus.isEmpty()) {
            pharmacyStatus = "Jour";
        }

        try {
            selectedPharmacy.setPharmacyName(pharmacyName);
            selectedPharmacy.setAddress(address);
            selectedPharmacy.setPhone(phone);
            selectedPharmacy.setEmail(email);
            selectedPharmacy.setOpeningHours(openingHours);
            selectedPharmacy.setPharmacyStatus(pharmacyStatus);
            selectedPharmacy.setImage(selectedImagePath);

            pharmacyServices.update(selectedPharmacy);

            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "Succès", "Pharmacie modifiée avec succès.");
            Main.showAdminPharmacies();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de modifier la pharmacie : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showAdminDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showAdminPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goBack() {
        Main.showAdminPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void cancelUpdate() {
        Main.showAdminPharmacies();
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    private void showAlert(Alert.AlertType type, String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(type);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(title);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Product;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import services.ProductServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.File;
import java.util.List;

public class AdminUpdateProductController {

    @FXML private TextField productIdField;

    @FXML private ImageView imagePreview;

    @FXML private TextField nameField;
    @FXML private TextField descriptionField;
    @FXML private TextField dosageField;
    @FXML private TextField formField;
    @FXML private TextField laboratoryField;
    @FXML private TextField therapeuticClassField;
    @FXML private TextField priceField;
    @FXML private TextField categoryField;

    private ProductServices productServices;

    private static Product selectedProduct;
    private String selectedImagePath = "";

    public static void setSelectedProduct(Product product) {
        selectedProduct = product;
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        productServices = new ProductServices();

        if (selectedProduct != null) {
            loadSelectedProduct();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void searchProduct() {
        if (productIdField == null) {
            loadSelectedProduct();
            return;
        }

        String searchText = safe(productIdField.getText());

        if (searchText.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Nom obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir le nom du produit.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            List<Product> results = productServices.searchByName(searchText);

            if (results.isEmpty()) {
                showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Introuvable", "Aucun produit trouvé avec ce nom.");
                return;
            }

            // Prendre le premier résultat trouvé
            selectedProduct = results.get(0);
            loadSelectedProduct();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de chercher le produit.");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void loadProduct() {
        loadSelectedProduct();
    }

    private void loadSelectedProduct() {
        if (selectedProduct == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (productIdField != null) {
            productIdField.setText(String.valueOf(selectedProduct.getProductId()));
        }

        if (nameField != null) {
            nameField.setText(safe(selectedProduct.getProductName()));
        }

        if (descriptionField != null) {
            descriptionField.setText(safe(selectedProduct.getDescription()));
        }

        if (dosageField != null) {
            dosageField.setText(safe(selectedProduct.getDosage()));
        }

        if (formField != null) {
            formField.setText(safe(selectedProduct.getForm()));
        }

        if (laboratoryField != null) {
            laboratoryField.setText(safe(selectedProduct.getLaboratory()));
        }

        if (therapeuticClassField != null) {
            therapeuticClassField.setText(safe(selectedProduct.getTherapeuticClass()));
        }

        if (priceField != null) {
            priceField.setText(String.valueOf(selectedProduct.getPrice()));
        }

        if (categoryField != null) {
            categoryField.setText(safe(selectedProduct.getCategory()));
        }

        selectedImagePath = safe(selectedProduct.getImage());
        loadImagePreview();
    }

    private void loadImagePreview() {
        try {
            if (imagePreview == null) {
                return;
            }

            if (selectedImagePath == null || selectedImagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                imagePreview.setImage(null);
                return;
            }

            File file = new File(selectedImagePath);

            if (file.exists()) {
                imagePreview.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
            } else {
                imagePreview.setImage(null);
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur chargement image : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void chooseImage() {
        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Choisir une image du produit");

        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().addAll(
                new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Images", "*.png", "*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.gif", "*.webp"),
                new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Tous les fichiers", "*.*")
        );

        File selectedFile = fileChooser.showOpenDialog(null);

        if (selectedFile != null) {
            selectedImagePath = selectedFile.getAbsolutePath();

            if (imagePreview != null) {
                imagePreview.setImage(new Image(selectedFile.toURI().toString()));
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void removeImage() {
        selectedImagePath = "";

        if (imagePreview != null) {
            imagePreview.setImage(null);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void updateProduct() {
        if (selectedProduct == null) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Aucun produit sélectionné.");
            return;
        }

        String name = nameField == null ? "" : safe(nameField.getText());
        String description = descriptionField == null ? "" : safe(descriptionField.getText());
        String dosage = dosageField == null ? "" : safe(dosageField.getText());
        String form = formField == null ? "" : safe(formField.getText());
        String laboratory = laboratoryField == null ? "" : safe(laboratoryField.getText());
        String therapeuticClass = therapeuticClassField == null ? "" : safe(therapeuticClassField.getText());
        String priceText = priceField == null ? "" : safe(priceField.getText()).replace(",", ".");
        String category = categoryField == null ? "" : safe(categoryField.getText());

        if (name.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir le nom du produit.");
            return;
        }

        if (priceText.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir le prix du produit.");
            return;
        }

        double price;

        try {
            price = Double.parseDouble(priceText);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Prix invalide", "Veuillez saisir un prix valide. Exemple : 4.500");
            return;
        }

        try {
            selectedProduct.setProductName(name);
            selectedProduct.setDescription(description);
            selectedProduct.setDosage(dosage);
            selectedProduct.setForm(form);
            selectedProduct.setLaboratory(laboratory);
            selectedProduct.setTherapeuticClass(therapeuticClass);
            selectedProduct.setPrice(price);
            selectedProduct.setCategory(category);
            selectedProduct.setImage(selectedImagePath);

            /*
             Important :
             On ne modifie plus ici :
             - quantité disponible
             - statut disponibilité
             - pharmacie
             - date mise à jour

             Ces informations seront gérées dans la partie stock du pharmacien.
            */

            productServices.update(selectedProduct);

            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "Succès", "Produit modifié avec succès.");
            Main.showAdminProducts();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de modifier le produit : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showAdminDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showAdminPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goBackProducts() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void cancelUpdate() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goBack() {
        Main.showAdminProducts();
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    private void showAlert(Alert.AlertType type, String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(type);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(title);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.event;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.eventServ;
import utils.RoleGuard;

import java.io.IOException;

public class Ajouterevent {

    @FXML private TextField        tfTitre;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cbType;
    @FXML private TextArea         taDescription;
    @FXML private DatePicker       dpDate;
    @FXML private TextField        tfTime;
    @FXML private TextField        tfLieu;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cbStatus;
    @FXML private TextField        tfCapacite;
    @FXML private Label            lblMessage;

    private final eventServ service = new eventServ();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // [SECURITY] Layer 2 — controller guard: redirect non-admin after scene is ready
        Platform.runLater(() -> {
            if (RoleGuard.denyIfNotAdmin()) {
                tests.Main.showPatientEvents();
            }
        });

        cbType.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Don de sang",
                "Vaccination",
                "Conférence médicale",
                "Consultation",
                "Sensibilisation",
                "Atelier santé",
                "Dépistage",
                "Premiers secours",
                "Conférence",
                "Séminaire",
                "Atelier"));
        cbStatus.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Planifié", "En cours", "Terminé", "Annulé"));
        cbType.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        cbStatus.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();

        // Capacité par défaut = 50
        if (tfCapacite != null) tfCapacite.setText("50");
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleAjouter() {
        // [SECURITY] Layer 2 — action-level guard (defence against UI manipulation)
        if (RoleGuard.denyIfNotAdmin()) return;
        if (!valider()) return;

        int capacite = 0;
        try {
            capacite = Integer.parseInt(tfCapacite.getText().trim());
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            // default to 0 if invalid, but we should ideally validate
        }

        event e = new event(
                tfTitre.getText().trim(),
                cbType.getValue(),
                taDescription.getText().trim(),
                dpDate.getValue(),
                tfTime.getText().trim(),
                tfLieu.getText().trim(),
                cbStatus.getValue(),
                null,
                capacite
        );

        service.ajouter(e);
        afficherMessage("✅ Événement ajouté avec succès !", "#16A34A");
        tests.Main.showAdminEvents();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleVoirListe() {
        tests.Main.showAdminEvents();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleReset() {
        if (tfTitre != null) tfTitre.clear();
        if (cbType != null) cbType.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        if (taDescription != null) taDescription.clear();
        if (dpDate != null) dpDate.setValue(null);
        if (tfTime != null) tfTime.clear();
        if (tfLieu != null) tfLieu.clear();
        if (cbStatus != null) cbStatus.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        if (tfCapacite != null) tfCapacite.clear();
        if (lblMessage != null) lblMessage.setText("");
    }

    private boolean valider() {
        if (tfTitre.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
            afficherMessage("❌ Le titre est obligatoire.", "#DC2626"); return false;
        }
        if (dpDate.getValue() == null) {
            afficherMessage("❌ La date est obligatoire.", "#DC2626"); return false;
        }
        if (tfLieu.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
            afficherMessage("❌ Le lieu est obligatoire.", "#DC2626"); return false;
        }
        try {
            int cap = Integer.parseInt(tfCapacite.getText().trim());
            if (cap <= 0) {
                afficherMessage("❌ La capacité doit être positive.", "#DC2626"); return false;
            }
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            afficherMessage("❌ La capacité doit être un nombre.", "#DC2626"); return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void afficherMessage(String msg, String color) {
        if (lblMessage != null) {
            lblMessage.setText(msg);
            lblMessage.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: " + color + ";");
        }
    }

    private void naviguer(String fxml) {
        try {
            Parent root = FXMLLoader.load(getClass().getResource(fxml));
            Stage stage = (Stage) tfTitre.getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.userDS;
import entities.medicalFile;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import services.userservDS;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import services.VoskService;
import java.io.IOException;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

public class AjoutermedicalFile {

    @FXML private TextField searchPatient;
    @FXML private ListView<userDS> listPatients;
    @FXML private DatePicker creationdate;
    @FXML private DatePicker lastvisit;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> patientstatus;
    @FXML private TextArea generalhistory;
    @FXML private TextArea antecedents;
    @FXML private TextArea allergies;
    @FXML private TextArea followup;
    @FXML private ImageView imgDoctor;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorName;

    private userservDS userService;
    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService;
    private ObservableList<userDS> patientsList;
    private userDS selectedPatient;
    
    private boolean isRecording = false;
    private Button activeMicButton = null;

    // Timer pour la recherche dynamique
    private javafx.animation.Timeline searchTimeline;

    public AjoutermedicalFile() {
        userService = new userservDS();
        medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
        patientsList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        ObservableList<String> statuses = FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "stable", "en amélioration", "critique"
        );
        patientstatus.setItems(statuses);

        creationdate.setValue(LocalDate.now());

        // Personnaliser l'affichage du ListView
        setupListViewCellFactory();

        setupSearchListener();
        setupSelectionListener();
        setupValidationListeners();

        // Charger tous les patients au départ (optionnel)
        loadAllPatients();
        chargerProfilMedecin();
    }

    private void chargerProfilMedecin() {
        if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Dr. Saif Gharbi");
        List<userDS> docs = userService.searchAllByNameOrSurname("Saif");
        for (userDS d : docs) {
            if (d.getFirstName().equalsIgnoreCase("Saif") && d.getLastName().equalsIgnoreCase("Gharbi")) {
                String photoPath = d.getUserphoto();
                if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty() && imgDoctor != null) {
                    try {
                        java.io.File file = new java.io.File(photoPath);
                        if (file.exists()) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                        } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath)));
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {}
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    private void setupListViewCellFactory() {
        // Personnaliser comment chaque patient est affiché dans la liste
        listPatients.setCellFactory(listView -> new ListCell<userDS>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(userDS patient, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(patient, empty);
                if (empty || patient == null) {
                    setText(null);
                    setGraphic(null);
                } else {
                    setText(patient.getFullName());

                    // Vous pouvez ajouter une icône ou un style personnalisé
                    setStyle("-fx-padding: 8px; -fx-border-color: transparent transparent #e0e0e0 transparent;");
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private void setupSearchListener() {
        // Utiliser un délai pour éviter trop de requêtes pendant la frappe
        searchTimeline = new javafx.animation.Timeline();
        searchTimeline.setCycleCount(1);

        searchPatient.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            // Annuler la recherche précédente
            if (searchTimeline.getStatus() == javafx.animation.Animation.Status.RUNNING) {
                searchTimeline.stop();
            }

            // Démarrer une nouvelle recherche après 300ms de pause
            searchTimeline.getKeyFrames().clear();
            searchTimeline.getKeyFrames().add(
                    new javafx.animation.KeyFrame(
                            javafx.util.Duration.millis(300),
                            event -> performSearch(newVal)
                    )
            );
            searchTimeline.play();
        });
    }

    private void performSearch(String keyword) {
        if (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty()) {
            List<userDS> result = userService.searchByNameOrSurname(keyword);
            patientsList.setAll(result);
            listPatients.setItems(patientsList);
            listPatients.setVisible(!result.isEmpty());

            // Afficher un message si aucun résultat
            if (result.isEmpty()) {
                showAlert("Information", "Aucun patient trouvé pour: " + keyword);
                listPatients.setVisible(false);
            } else {
                System.out.println("✅ " + result.size() + " patient(s) trouvé(s)");
            }
        } else {
            // Si le champ est vide, afficher tous les patients ou cacher la liste
            if (searchPatient.getText() == null || searchPatient.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
                loadAllPatients();
            } else {
                listPatients.getItems().clear();
                listPatients.setVisible(false);
            }
        }
    }

    private void loadAllPatients() {
        // Optionnel : charger tous les patients pour un aperçu initial
        try {
            List<userDS> allPatients = userService.getAll();
            if (!allPatients.isEmpty()) {
                patientsList.setAll(allPatients);
                listPatients.setItems(patientsList);
                listPatients.setVisible(true);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("Erreur chargement patients: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void setupSelectionListener() {
        listPatients.setOnMouseClicked(event -> {
            selectedPatient = listPatients.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
            if (selectedPatient != null) {
                searchPatient.setText(selectedPatient.getFullName());
                listPatients.setVisible(false);
                searchPatient.setStyle("-fx-border-color: green; -fx-border-width: 2px;");

                // Optionnel : afficher une confirmation
                System.out.println("✅ Patient sélectionné: " + selectedPatient.getFullName());
            }
        });

        // Quand le champ de recherche reçoit le focus, cacher le highlight vert
        searchPatient.focusedProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            if (newVal) {
                searchPatient.setStyle(null);
            }
        });
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleVoiceInput(ActionEvent event) {
        Button micButton = (Button) event.getSource();
        String targetId = (String) micButton.getUserData();
        
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-background-radius: 10;");
            isRecording = false;
            activeMicButton = null;
        } else {
            if (!VoskService.getInstance().isReady()) {
                showAlert("Modèle Vocal Manquant", "Le dossier 'model' contenant le modèle Vosk (ex: français) est introuvable à la racine du projet.");
                return;
            }
            
            isRecording = true;
            activeMicButton = micButton;
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-text-fill: white;");
            
            VoskService.getInstance().startListening(text -> {
                Platform.runLater(() -> {
                    appendToField(targetId, text);
                });
            });
        }
    }

    private void appendToField(String id, String text) {
        if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty()) return;
        
        String formattedText = text + " ";
        switch (id) {
            case "searchPatient": searchPatient.appendText(formattedText); break;
            case "generalhistory": generalhistory.appendText(formattedText); break;
            case "antecedents": antecedents.appendText(formattedText); break;
            case "allergies": allergies.appendText(formattedText); break;
            case "followup": followup.appendText(formattedText); break;
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void ajoutds() {
        if (!validateInputs()) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            medicalFile mf = createMedicalFile();
            medicalFileService.add(mf);

            showAlert("Succès", "Dossier médical ajouté pour " + selectedPatient.getFullName());
            
            // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
            tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Base de données: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private boolean validateInputs() {
        clearValidationErrors();
        boolean valid = true;
        Node firstInvalidField = null;

        if (selectedPatient == null) {
            markInvalid(searchPatient);
            firstInvalidField = searchPatient;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (creationdate.getValue() == null) {
            markInvalid(creationdate);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = creationdate;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (lastvisit.getValue() == null) {
            markInvalid(lastvisit);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = lastvisit;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (patientstatus.getValue() == null) {
            markInvalid(patientstatus);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = patientstatus;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(generalhistory)) {
            markInvalid(generalhistory);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = generalhistory;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(antecedents)) {
            markInvalid(antecedents);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = antecedents;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(allergies)) {
            markInvalid(allergies);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = allergies;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(followup)) {
            markInvalid(followup);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = followup;
            valid = false;
        }

        if (!valid) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez remplir tous les champs obligatoires.");
            firstInvalidField.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }
        if (selectedPatient == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez sélectionner un patient dans la liste");
            searchPatient.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        if (creationdate.getValue() == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Date de création obligatoire");
            creationdate.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        if (patientstatus.getValue() == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Sélectionnez un statut");
            patientstatus.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        if (generalhistory.getText() == null || generalhistory.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Historique général obligatoire");
            generalhistory.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        if (antecedents.getText() == null || antecedents.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Antécédents médicaux obligatoires");
            antecedents.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        if (allergies.getText() == null || allergies.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Allergies obligatoires");
            allergies.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        if (followup.getText() == null || followup.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Suivi médical obligatoire");
            followup.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        if (lastvisit.getValue() == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Date de dernière visite obligatoire");
            lastvisit.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void setupValidationListeners() {
        searchPatient.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            if (selectedPatient != null && !selectedPatient.getFullName().equals(newVal)) {
                selectedPatient = null;
                searchPatient.setStyle(null);
            }
            clearInvalid(searchPatient);
        });
        creationdate.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(creationdate));
        lastvisit.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(lastvisit));
        patientstatus.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(patientstatus));
        generalhistory.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(generalhistory));
        antecedents.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(antecedents));
        allergies.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(allergies));
        followup.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(followup));
    }

    private boolean isBlank(TextInputControl field) {
        return field.getText() == null || field.getText().trim().isEmpty();
    }

    private void markInvalid(Node field) {
        if (!field.getStyleClass().contains("error-field")) {
            field.getStyleClass().add("error-field");
        }
    }

    private void clearInvalid(Node field) {
        field.getStyleClass().remove("error-field");
    }

    private void clearValidationErrors() {
        clearInvalid(searchPatient);
        clearInvalid(creationdate);
        clearInvalid(lastvisit);
        clearInvalid(patientstatus);
        clearInvalid(generalhistory);
        clearInvalid(antecedents);
        clearInvalid(allergies);
        clearInvalid(followup);
    }

    private medicalFile createMedicalFile() {
        medicalFile mf = new medicalFile();
        mf.setPatientId(selectedPatient.getUserId());
        mf.setDoctorId(tests.Main.currentUserId);
        mf.setCreationdate(creationdate.getValue());
        mf.setLastvisit(lastvisit.getValue());
        mf.setPatientstatus(patientstatus.getValue());
        mf.setGeneralhistory(generalhistory.getText());
        mf.setAntecedents(antecedents.getText());
        mf.setAllergies(allergies.getText());
        mf.setFollowup(followup.getText());
        return mf;
    }

    @FXML
    public void retour(ActionEvent event) {
        allerGererPatients(event);
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerGererPatients(ActionEvent event) {
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
        }
        // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerAjouterDossier(ActionEvent event) {
        // Déjà sur cette interface, on peut rafraîchir ou ne rien faire
        clearFields();
    }

    private void clearFields() {
        searchPatient.clear();
        searchPatient.setStyle(null);
        clearValidationErrors();
        listPatients.getItems().clear();
        creationdate.setValue(LocalDate.now());
        lastvisit.setValue(null);
        patientstatus.setValue(null);
        generalhistory.clear();
        antecedents.clear();
        allergies.clear();
        followup.clear();
        selectedPatient = null;

        // Recharger tous les patients
        loadAllPatients();
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(title.equals("Erreur") ? Alert.AlertType.ERROR : Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Participant;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.Participantserv;

import java.io.IOException;

public class Ajouterparticipant {

    @FXML private TextField tfNom;
    @FXML private TextField tfPrenom;
    @FXML private TextField tfEmail;
    @FXML private TextField tfTelephone;
    @FXML private Label     lblMessage;

    private final Participantserv service = new Participantserv();

    @FXML
    private void handleAjouter() {
        // ── Validation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        if (tfNom.getText().isEmpty() || tfPrenom.getText().isEmpty() ||
            tfEmail.getText().isEmpty() || tfTelephone.getText().isEmpty()) {
            showMessage("⚠️ Tous les champs sont obligatoires.", "red");
            return;
        }
        if (!tfEmail.getText().contains("@")) {
            showMessage("⚠️ Email invalide.", "red");
            return;
        }

        Participant p = new Participant(
            tfNom.getText().trim(),
            tfPrenom.getText().trim(),
            tfEmail.getText().trim(),
            tfTelephone.getText().trim()
        );

        service.ajouter(p);
        showMessage("✅ Participant ajouté avec succès !", "green");
        handleReset();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleReset() {
        if (tfNom != null) tfNom.clear();
        if (tfPrenom != null) tfPrenom.clear();
        if (tfEmail != null) tfEmail.clear();
        if (tfTelephone != null) tfTelephone.clear();
        if (lblMessage != null) lblMessage.setText("");
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleVoirListe() {
        tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants();
    }

    private void showMessage(String msg, String color) {
        lblMessage.setText(msg);
        lblMessage.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: " + color + ";");
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Prescription;
import entities.medicalFile;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.Prescriptionserv;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import services.userservDS;
import services.VoskService;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import java.io.File;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

public class AjouterPrescriptionController {

    @FXML private ComboBox<userDS> comboPatient;
    @FXML private DatePicker datePrescription;
    @FXML private TextField txtDuree;
    @FXML private TextArea txtPrescription;

    private Prescriptionserv prescriptionService;
    private userservDS userService;
    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService;
    private boolean isRecording = false;

    public AjouterPrescriptionController() {
        prescriptionService = new Prescriptionserv();
        userService = new userservDS();
        medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Charger la liste des patients
        List<userDS> patients = userService.getAll();
        comboPatient.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(patients));
        
        // Custom display pour le ComboBox
        comboPatient.setCellFactory(lv -> new ListCell<userDS>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(userDS item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                setText(empty ? null : item.getFullName());
            }
        });
        comboPatient.setButtonCell(new ListCell<userDS>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(userDS item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                setText(empty ? null : item.getFullName());
            }
        });

        datePrescription.setValue(LocalDate.now());
        setupValidationListeners();
    }

    public void preselectPatient(int patientId) {
        for (userDS patient : comboPatient.getItems()) {
            if (patient.getUserId() == patientId) {
                comboPatient.getSelectionModel().select(patient);
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleVoiceInput(ActionEvent event) {
        Button micButton = (Button) event.getSource();
        String targetId = (String) micButton.getUserData();
        
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-background-radius: 10;");
            isRecording = false;
        } else {
            if (!VoskService.getInstance().isReady()) {
                showAlert("Modèle Vocal Manquant", "Le dossier 'model' contenant le modèle Vosk est introuvable à la racine.");
                return;
            }
            
            isRecording = true;
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-text-fill: white;");
            
            VoskService.getInstance().startListening(text -> {
                Platform.runLater(() -> {
                    appendToField(targetId, text);
                });
            });
        }
    }

    private void appendToField(String id, String text) {
        if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty()) return;
        String formattedText = text + " ";
        if ("txtDuree".equals(id)) {
            txtDuree.appendText(formattedText);
        } else if ("txtPrescription".equals(id)) {
            txtPrescription.appendText(formattedText);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void ajouterPrescription(ActionEvent event) {
        userDS selectedPatient = comboPatient.getValue();
        LocalDate date = datePrescription.getValue();
        String duree = txtDuree.getText();
        String detail = txtPrescription.getText();

        if (!validateInputs(selectedPatient, date, duree, detail)) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            // Récupérer l'idfile du patient sélectionné
            List<medicalFile> dossiers = medicalFileService.getByPatientId(selectedPatient.getUserId());
            if (dossiers.isEmpty()) {
                showAlert("Erreur", "Le patient sélectionné n'a pas de dossier médical. Impossible de créer la prescription.");
                return;
            }
            int idFile = dossiers.get(0).getIdfile();

            Prescription p = new Prescription(date, detail, duree, tests.Main.currentUserId, selectedPatient.getUserId(), idFile);

            prescriptionService.add(p);
            showAlert("Succès", "La prescription a été ajoutée avec succès !");
            retourner(event);
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de l'ajout : " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private boolean validateInputs(userDS selectedPatient, LocalDate date, String duree, String detail) {
        clearValidationErrors();
        boolean valid = true;
        Node firstInvalidField = null;

        if (selectedPatient == null) {
            markInvalid(comboPatient);
            firstInvalidField = comboPatient;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (date == null) {
            markInvalid(datePrescription);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = datePrescription;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (duree == null || duree.trim().isEmpty()) {
            markInvalid(txtDuree);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = txtDuree;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (detail == null || detail.trim().isEmpty()) {
            markInvalid(txtPrescription);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = txtPrescription;
            valid = false;
        }

        if (!valid) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez remplir tous les champs obligatoires.");
            firstInvalidField.requestFocus();
        }

        return valid;
    }

    private void setupValidationListeners() {
        comboPatient.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(comboPatient));
        datePrescription.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(datePrescription));
        txtDuree.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(txtDuree));
        txtPrescription.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(txtPrescription));
    }

    private void markInvalid(Node field) {
        if (!field.getStyleClass().contains("error-field")) {
            field.getStyleClass().add("error-field");
        }
    }

    private void clearInvalid(Node field) {
        field.getStyleClass().remove("error-field");
    }

    private void clearValidationErrors() {
        clearInvalid(comboPatient);
        clearInvalid(datePrescription);
        clearInvalid(txtDuree);
        clearInvalid(txtPrescription);
    }

    @FXML
    public void retourner(javafx.event.Event event) {
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
        }
        tests.Main.showDoctorDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerGererPatients(javafx.event.Event event) {
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
        }
        // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
