        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.AnchorPane;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class AssistantMainController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private AnchorPane contentContainer;
    @FXML private ImageView logoView;
    @FXML private ImageView profileImageView;
    @FXML private Label userNameLabel;

    private static AssistantMainController instance;
    private userServices userService = new userServices();

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        instance = this;
        try {
            Image logo = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/logo.png"));
            if (logo != null) logoView.setImage(logo);
        } catch (Exception e) {}

        loadUserInfo();
        goToPatients();
    }

    public void loadUserInfo() {
        try {
            int userId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == userId) {
                    userNameLabel.setText(u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName());
                    String photoBase64 = (u.getAvatarUrl() != null && !u.getAvatarUrl().isEmpty()) ? u.getAvatarUrl() : u.getUserPhoto();
                    
                    if (photoBase64 != null && photoBase64.startsWith("generated_")) {
                        photoBase64 = u.getUserPhoto();
                    }

                    if (photoBase64 != null && !photoBase64.isEmpty()) {
                        try {
                            String cleanBase64 = photoBase64.trim().replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]", "");
                            byte[] imageBytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(cleanBase64);
                            profileImageView.setImage(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(imageBytes)));
                        } catch (Exception e) {
                            setDefaultProfileImage();
                        }
                    } else {
                        setDefaultProfileImage();
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void setDefaultProfileImage() {
        try {
            Image defaultImage = new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png", 80, 80, true, true);
            if (defaultImage != null) profileImageView.setImage(defaultImage);
        } catch (Exception e) {}
    }

    public void updateContent(javafx.scene.Parent content) {
        if (contentContainer != null) {
            contentContainer.getChildren().clear();
            contentContainer.getChildren().add(content);
            AnchorPane.setTopAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setBottomAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setLeftAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setRightAnchor(content, 0.0);
        }
    }

    @FXML private void goToPatients() { Main.showAssistantPatients(); }
    @FXML private void goToAppointments() { Main.showAssistantAppointments(); }
    @FXML private void goToAvailability() { Main.showAssistantAvailability(); }
    @FXML private void goToProfile() { Main.showUserProfile(); }
    @FXML private void logout() { Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import entities.user.Role;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class AssistantPatientListController {

    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private FlowPane cardsContainer;
    @FXML private Label statusLabel;
    @FXML private HBox paginationBox;
    @FXML private DatePicker datePicker;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> sortComboBox;

    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private ObservableList<user> allPatientsList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private ObservableList<user> filteredPatientsList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();

    private int itemsPerPage = 8;
    private int currentPage = 1;
    private int totalPages = 1;
    private int assistantDoctorId = 0;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Initialize ComboBoxes
        sortComboBox.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Ordre alphabétique", "A - Z", "Z - A"));
        sortComboBox.setValue("Ordre alphabétique");

        // Listeners for real-time filtering
        searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterPatients(); });
        sortComboBox.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { filterPatients(); });
        datePicker.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterPatients(); });

        loadAssistantInfo();
        loadPatients();
    }

    private void loadAssistantInfo() {
        try {
            int currentUserId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == currentUserId) {
                    this.assistantDoctorId = u.getDoctorId();
                    break;
                }
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    public void loadPatients() {
        try {
            allPatientsList.clear();
            List<user> users = userService.getAll();
            for (user u : users) {
                // Show patients linked to the same doctor as the assistant
                if (u.getRole() == Role.patient && (u.getDoctorId() == assistantDoctorId || assistantDoctorId == 0)) {
                    allPatientsList.add(u);
                }
            }
            filterPatients();
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            if (statusLabel != null) statusLabel.setText("Erreur de chargement");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void filterPatients() {
        String query = searchField.getText().toLowerCase();
        String sortOrder = sortComboBox.getValue();

        List<user> filtered = allPatientsList.stream()
            .filter(u -> (u.getFirstName().toLowerCase().contains(query) || u.getLastName().toLowerCase().contains(query)))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());

        // Alphabetical sorting
        if ("A - Z".equals(sortOrder)) {
            filtered.sort((u1, u2) -> (u1.getFirstName() + u1.getLastName()).compareToIgnoreCase(u2.getFirstName() + u2.getLastName()));
        } else if ("Z - A".equals(sortOrder)) {
            filtered.sort((u1, u2) -> (u2.getFirstName() + u2.getLastName()).compareToIgnoreCase(u1.getFirstName() + u1.getLastName()));
        }

        filteredPatientsList.setAll(filtered);
        updatePagination();
        displayCurrentPage();
        if (statusLabel != null) statusLabel.setText("Total patients: " + filteredPatientsList.size());
    }

    @FXML
    private void resetFilters() {
        searchField.setText("");
        sortComboBox.setValue("Ordre alphabétique");
        datePicker.setValue(null);
        currentPage = 1;
        filterPatients();
    }

    private void updatePagination() {
        totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) filteredPatientsList.size() / itemsPerPage);
        if (totalPages == 0) totalPages = 1;
        paginationBox.getChildren().clear();

        Button prevBtn = new Button("◀");
        prevBtn.setDisable(currentPage == 1);
        prevBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
        prevBtn.setOnAction(e -> { currentPage--; displayCurrentPage(); });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(prevBtn);

        for (int i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
            Button pageBtn = new Button(String.valueOf(i));
            pageBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
            if (i == currentPage) {
                pageBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button-active");
            }
            int pageNum = i;
            pageBtn.setOnAction(e -> { currentPage = pageNum; displayCurrentPage(); });
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(pageBtn);
        }

        Button nextBtn = new Button("▶");
        nextBtn.setDisable(currentPage == totalPages);
        nextBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
        nextBtn.setOnAction(e -> { currentPage++; displayCurrentPage(); });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(nextBtn);
    }

    private void displayCurrentPage() {
        if (cardsContainer == null) return;
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();

        if (filteredPatientsList.isEmpty()) {
            Label emptyLabel = new Label("Aucun patient trouvé.");
            emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 14;");
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
            return;
        }

        int start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, filteredPatientsList.size());

        for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(createPatientCard(filteredPatientsList.get(i)));
        }
        updatePagination();
    }

    private VBox createPatientCard(user patient) {
        VBox card = new VBox(12);
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 12; -fx-background-radius: 12; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.05), 10, 0, 0, 2);");
        card.setPadding(new Insets(15));
        card.setPrefWidth(280);

        HBox header = new HBox(12);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        ImageView photo = new ImageView();
        photo.setFitHeight(55);
        photo.setFitWidth(55);
        javafx.scene.shape.Circle clip = new javafx.scene.shape.Circle(27.5, 27.5, 27.5);
        photo.setClip(clip);

        String b64 = patient.getUserPhoto();
        if (b64 != null && !b64.trim().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                String clean = b64.trim().replaceAll("\\s+", "");
                byte[] bytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(clean);
                photo.setImage(new javafx.scene.image.Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                photo.setImage(new javafx.scene.image.Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
            }
        } else {
            photo.setImage(new javafx.scene.image.Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
        }

        VBox nameBox = new VBox(2);
        Label name = new Label(patient.getFirstName() + " " + patient.getLastName());
        name.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 15; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        
        Label statusTag = new Label("Patient");
        statusTag.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #9F7AEA; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-padding: 2 8; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-font-size: 10;");
        
        nameBox.getChildren().addAll(name, statusTag);
        header.getChildren().addAll(photo, nameBox);

        VBox infoBox = new VBox(5);
        Label email = new Label("📧 " + patient.getEmail());
        email.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        Label phone = new Label("📞 " + patient.getPhone());
        phone.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        Label lastVisit = new Label("📅 Dernière visite: 2026-05-01");
        lastVisit.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0;");
        
        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(email, phone, lastVisit);

        Button btnDossier = new Button("📂 Voir Dossier");
        btnDossier.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-padding: 8 12; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        btnDossier.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        btnDossier.setOnAction(e -> {
            Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
            alert.setTitle("Dossier Médical");
            alert.setHeaderText("Dossier de " + patient.getFirstName());
            alert.setContentText("Consultation du dossier médical à venir.");
            alert.showAndWait();
        });

        card.getChildren().addAll(header, new Separator(), infoBox, btnDossier);
        return card;
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.HealthLog;
import entities.user;
import javafx.animation.KeyFrame;
import javafx.animation.Timeline;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.AnchorPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.util.Duration;
import services.HealthLogService;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;

public class ChatbotController {

    @FXML private VBox messageContainer;
    @FXML private VBox optionsContainer;
    @FXML private ScrollPane scrollPane;
    @FXML private VBox chatRoot;

    private user currentUser;
    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private HealthLogService healthService = new HealthLogService();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        loadUser();
        startFriendlyConversation();
    }

    private void loadUser() {
        try {
            int id = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == id) { this.currentUser = u; break; }
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    private void startFriendlyConversation() {
        String name = (currentUser != null) ? currentUser.getFirstName() : "Patient";
        addBotMessage("Bonjour " + name + ". Je suis votre assistant Shifaa.");
        addDelayedBotMessage("Comment puis-je vous aider aujourd'hui ?", 1.0, () -> {
            showOptions(
                new Option("Faire mon suivi de routine", () -> handleRoutine()),
                new Option("Signaler un nouveau symptôme", () -> handleNewSymptom())
            );
        });
    }

    // --- SUIVI DE ROUTINE ---

    private void handleRoutine() {
        addUserMessage("Je souhaite faire mon suivi.");
        addDelayedBotMessage("C'est une excellente habitude. Comment s'est passée votre nuit ?", 1.0, () -> {
            showOptions(
                new Option("J'ai très bien dormi", () -> handleRoutineStep2(true)),
                new Option("Mon sommeil était agité", () -> handleRoutineStep2(false))
            );
        });
    }

    private void handleRoutineStep2(boolean goodSleep) {
        addUserMessage(goodSleep ? "Très bien dormi" : "Sommeil agité");
        String sleepResult = goodSleep ? "Sommeil: Excellent" : "Sommeil: Agité";
        
        addDelayedBotMessage("Avez-vous bien pris votre traitement habituel ?", 1.0, () -> {
            showOptions(
                new Option("Oui, c'est fait", () -> {
                    addUserMessage("Traitement pris");
                    saveLog("Routine", sleepResult + " | Traitement: Pris", 1);
                    addBotMessage("Très bien. Tout semble sous contrôle. Données enregistrées dans votre tableau de bord.");
                    showOptions(new Option("Merci, à bientôt", () -> closeChat()));
                }),
                new Option("Pas encore", () -> {
                    addUserMessage("Pas encore");
                    saveLog("Routine", sleepResult + " | Traitement: Non pris", 3);
                    addBotMessage("Pensez à le prendre dès que possible. Données enregistrées.");
                    showOptions(new Option("C'est noté", () -> closeChat()));
                })
            );
        });
    }

    // --- SYMPTÔMES ---

    private void handleNewSymptom() {
        addUserMessage("J'ai un nouveau symptôme");
        addDelayedBotMessage("D'accord. Quelle zone vous préoccupe principalement ?", 0.8, () -> {
            showOptions(
                new Option("Tête ou visage", () -> assessIntensity("Neurologue", "Tête")),
                new Option("Poitrine ou respiration", () -> handleEmergency()),
                new Option("Ventre ou digestion", () -> assessIntensity("Gastro-entérologue", "Ventre")),
                new Option("Dos ou articulations", () -> assessIntensity("Orthopédiste", "Articulations"))
            );
        });
    }

    private void assessIntensity(String specialty, String zone) {
        addUserMessage("Évaluation de la douleur: " + zone);
        addDelayedBotMessage("Sur une échelle de 1 à 10, comment évaluez-vous cette gêne ?", 1.0, () -> {
            showOptions(
                new Option("Faible (1-3)", () -> finalizeTriage(specialty, zone, 2)),
                new Option("Moyenne (4-6)", () -> finalizeTriage(specialty, zone, 5)),
                new Option("Forte (7-10)", () -> finalizeTriage(specialty, zone, 9))
            );
        });
    }

    private void finalizeTriage(String specialty, String zone, int severity) {
        addUserMessage("Intensité: " + severity + "/10");
        saveLog("Symptom", "Douleur " + zone + " (Intensité: " + severity + ")", severity);
        
        if (severity >= 7) {
            addDelayedBotMessage("Cette douleur semble importante. Je vous conseille de prendre rendez-vous rapidement avec un " + specialty + ".", 1.0, () -> {
                showOptions(new Option("Prendre rendez-vous", () -> { Main.showPatientDoctors(); closeChat(); }));
            });
        } else {
            addDelayedBotMessage("Données enregistrées. Un suivi avec un " + specialty + " est conseillé si cela persiste.", 1.0, () -> {
                showOptions(
                    new Option("Prendre rendez-vous", () -> { Main.showPatientDoctors(); closeChat(); }),
                    new Option("Fermer l'assistant", () -> closeChat())
                );
            });
        }
    }

    private void handleEmergency() {
        addUserMessage("Poitrine / Respiration");
        saveLog("Symptom", "URGENCE : Douleur thoracique", 10);
        chatRoot.setStyle(chatRoot.getStyle() + "; -fx-border-color: #EF4444; -fx-border-width: 2;");
        addDelayedBotMessage("ALERTE : Une douleur thoracique nécessite une attention immédiate.", 0.5, () -> {
            addDelayedBotMessage("Veuillez contacter immédiatement le 190 (SAMU Tunisie).", 1.0, () -> {
                addDelayedBotMessage("Ne restez pas seul et attendez les secours.", 0.8, () -> {
                    showOptions(new Option("Bien reçu", () -> closeChat()));
                });
            });
        });
    }

    private void saveLog(String type, String result, int severity) {
        if (currentUser != null) {
            try {
                HealthLog log = new HealthLog(currentUser.getUserId(), type, result, severity);
                healthService.add(log);
            } catch (SQLException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
        }
    }

    // --- UI ENGINE (CLEAN) ---

    private void addDelayedBotMessage(String text, double delay, Runnable onFinished) {
        HBox typing = new HBox(new Label("..."));
        typing.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F8FAFC; -fx-padding: 5 10; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-text-fill: #94A3B8;");
        messageContainer.getChildren().add(typing);
        scrollToBottom();

        new Timeline(new KeyFrame(Duration.seconds(delay), e -> {
            messageContainer.getChildren().remove(typing);
            addBotMessage(text);
            if (onFinished != null) onFinished.run();
        })).play();
    }

    private void addBotMessage(String text) {
        Label label = new Label(text);
        label.setWrapText(true);
        label.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F1F5F9; -fx-padding: 10 12; -fx-background-radius: 15 15 15 5; -fx-text-fill: #334155; -fx-max-width: 240; -fx-font-size: 13;");
        HBox hbox = new HBox(label);
        hbox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        messageContainer.getChildren().add(hbox);
        scrollToBottom();
    }

    private void addUserMessage(String text) {
        Label label = new Label(text);
        label.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #1E293B; -fx-padding: 8 10; -fx-background-radius: 15 15 5 15; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-max-width: 240; -fx-font-size: 12;");
        HBox hbox = new HBox(label);
        hbox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
        messageContainer.getChildren().add(hbox);
        scrollToBottom();
    }

    private void showOptions(Option... options) {
        optionsContainer.getChildren().clear();
        for (Option opt : options) {
            Button btn = new Button(opt.text);
            btn.setPrefWidth(280);
            btn.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
            btn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 8; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-padding: 8; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-text-fill: #475569; -fx-font-size: 12;");
            btn.setOnMouseEntered(e -> btn.setStyle(btn.getStyle() + "; -fx-background-color: #F8FAFC; -fx-border-color: #64748B;"));
            btn.setOnMouseExited(e -> btn.setStyle(btn.getStyle().replace("-fx-background-color: #F8FAFC; -fx-border-color: #64748B;", "")));
            btn.setOnAction(e -> opt.action.run());
            optionsContainer.getChildren().add(btn);
        }
    }

    private void scrollToBottom() { Platform.runLater(() -> scrollPane.setVvalue(1.0)); }

    @FXML private void closeChat() {
        if (chatRoot.getParent() instanceof AnchorPane) {
            ((AnchorPane)chatRoot.getParent()).getChildren().remove(chatRoot);
        }
    }

    private static class Option {
        String text; Runnable action;
        Option(String t, Runnable a) { this.text = t; this.action = a; }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.userDS;
import entities.medicalFile;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import java.io.File;

public class ConsultationMedicalFileController {

    @FXML private Label lblPatientName;
    @FXML private Label lblDossierId;
    @FXML private Label lblCreationDate;
    @FXML private Label lblLastVisit;
    @FXML private Label lblStatus;
    @FXML private Label txtHistory;
    @FXML private Label txtAntecedents;
    @FXML private Label txtAllergies;
    @FXML private Label txtFollowup;
    @FXML private ImageView patientPhoto;
    @FXML private ImageView imgDoctor;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorName;

    // Données courantes pour la modification
    private medicalFile currentMedicalFile;
    private userDS currentPatient;

    private services.userservDS userService = new services.userservDS();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        chargerProfilMedecin();
    }

    private void chargerProfilMedecin() {
        if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Dr. Saif Gharbi");
        java.util.List<userDS> docs = userService.searchAllByNameOrSurname("Saif");
        for (userDS d : docs) {
            if (d.getFirstName().equalsIgnoreCase("Saif") && d.getLastName().equalsIgnoreCase("Gharbi")) {
                String photoPath = d.getUserphoto();
                if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty() && imgDoctor != null) {
                    try {
                        java.io.File file = new java.io.File(photoPath);
                        if (file.exists()) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                        } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath)));
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {}
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    public void setDossierData(medicalFile mf, userDS patient) {
        if (patient != null && lblPatientName != null) {
            String name = patient.getFullName();
            if (name == null || name.trim().isEmpty() || name.equals("null null")) {
                lblPatientName.setText("Patient Inconnu");
            } else {
                lblPatientName.setText(name);
            }
        }
        
        if (mf != null) {
            if (lblDossierId != null) lblDossierId.setText("Dossier #" + mf.getIdfile());
            if (lblCreationDate != null) lblCreationDate.setText(mf.getCreationdate() != null ? mf.getCreationdate().toString() : "N/A");
            if (lblLastVisit != null) lblLastVisit.setText(mf.getLastvisit() != null ? mf.getLastvisit().toString() : "N/A");
            
            if (lblStatus != null) {
                String status = mf.getPatientstatus() != null ? mf.getPatientstatus().toLowerCase() : "stable";
                lblStatus.setText(status.toUpperCase());
                
                // Coloration dynamique du badge de statut
                if (status.contains("stable")) {
                    lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #C6F6D5; -fx-text-fill: #22543D; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
                } else if (status.contains("amelioration") || status.contains("amélioration")) {
                    lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FEFCBF; -fx-text-fill: #744210; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
                } else if (status.contains("critique")) {
                    lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FED7D7; -fx-text-fill: #822727; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
                } else {
                    lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
                }
            }

            if (txtHistory != null) txtHistory.setText(mf.getGeneralhistory() != null ? mf.getGeneralhistory() : "Aucun historique");
            if (txtAntecedents != null) txtAntecedents.setText(mf.getAntecedents() != null ? mf.getAntecedents() : "Aucun antécédent");
            if (txtAllergies != null) txtAllergies.setText(mf.getAllergies() != null ? mf.getAllergies() : "Aucune allergie");
            if (txtFollowup != null) txtFollowup.setText(mf.getFollowup() != null ? mf.getFollowup() : "Aucun suivi");
        }

        // Stocker les données pour la modification ultérieure
        this.currentMedicalFile = mf;
        this.currentPatient = patient;

        // Charger la photo
        String b64 = patient.getUserphoto();
        if (b64 != null && !b64.trim().isEmpty() && patientPhoto != null) {
            try {
                // Essayer d'abord comme Base64
                if (b64.length() > 50) { // Probablement du Base64
                    String clean = b64.trim().replaceAll("\\s+", "");
                    byte[] bytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(clean);
                    patientPhoto.setImage(new Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                } else {
                    // Sinon essayer comme fichier
                    File file = new File(b64);
                    if (file.exists()) {
                        patientPhoto.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                    } else if (getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + b64) != null) {
                        patientPhoto.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + b64)));
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                patientPhoto.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
            }
        } else if (patientPhoto != null) {
            patientPhoto.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void closeWindow(javafx.event.ActionEvent event) {
        // Retour vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }

    @FXML
    public void ouvrirModification(javafx.event.ActionEvent event) {
        if (currentMedicalFile != null && currentPatient != null) {
            tests.Main.showModifierDossier(currentMedicalFile, currentPatient);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void supprimerDossier() {
        if (currentMedicalFile == null) return;

        javafx.scene.control.Alert alert = new javafx.scene.control.Alert(javafx.scene.control.Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        alert.setTitle("Confirmation de suppression");
        alert.setHeaderText("Supprimer ce dossier médical ?");
        alert.setContentText("Cette action est irréversible. Voulez-vous continuer ?");

        java.util.Optional<javafx.scene.control.ButtonType> result = alert.showAndWait();
        if (result.isPresent() && result.get() == javafx.scene.control.ButtonType.OK) {
            try {
                services.medicalFileServ service = new services.medicalFileServ();
                service.delete(currentMedicalFile.getIdfile());
                
                // Retourner à la liste des patients
                tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
            } catch (java.sql.SQLException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Prescription;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import services.SmsService;
import javafx.stage.Stage;

import java.io.IOException;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import java.io.File;
import java.util.List;
import services.userservDS;

public class ConsultationPrescriptionController {

    @FXML private Label lblPatientName;
    @FXML private Label lblDate;
    @FXML private Label lblDuration;
    @FXML private Label lblDetails;
    @FXML private Label lblPrescriptionId;
    
    private Prescription currentPrescription;
    private userDS currentPatient;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Nothing to initialize since sidebar is removed
    }

    public void setPrescriptionData(Prescription p, userDS patient) {
        this.currentPrescription = p;
        this.currentPatient = patient;
        if (patient != null) {
            lblPatientName.setText(patient.getFullName());
        } else {
            lblPatientName.setText("Patient inconnu (ID: " + p.getPatientId() + ")");
        }
        
        lblDate.setText(p.getPrescriptionDate().toString());
        lblDuration.setText(p.getTreatmentDuration());
        lblDetails.setText(p.getPrescription());
        lblPrescriptionId.setText("Prescription #" + p.getPrescriptionId());
    }

    @FXML
    public void envoyerSMS() {
        if (currentPatient == null || currentPrescription == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Données manquantes pour l'envoi du SMS.");
            return;
        }

        String messageBody = String.format(
            "Bonjour %s %s (ID: %d), votre traitement: %s. Durée: %s.",
            currentPatient.getFirstName(),
            currentPatient.getLastName(),
            currentPatient.getUserId(),
            currentPrescription.getPrescription(),
            currentPrescription.getTreatmentDuration()
        );

        try {
            SmsService.sendSms("+21651342604", messageBody);
            showAlert("Succès", "SMS envoyé avec succès au patient !");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur Twilio", "Échec de l'envoi : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    @FXML
    public void retourner(javafx.event.Event event) {
        tests.Main.showDoctorDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerGererPatients(javafx.event.Event event) {
        // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Prescription;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import services.userservDS;
import services.SmsService;

import com.itextpdf.text.BaseColor;
import com.itextpdf.text.Document;
import com.itextpdf.text.Element;
import com.itextpdf.text.Font;
import com.itextpdf.text.FontFactory;
import com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph;
import com.itextpdf.text.Phrase;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPCell;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPTable;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfWriter;

import java.awt.Desktop;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;

public class ConsultationPrescriptionPatientController {

    @FXML private Label lblPatientName;
    @FXML private Label lblDate;
    @FXML private Label lblDuration;
    @FXML private Label lblDetails;
    @FXML private Label lblPrescriptionId;

    private int currentPatientId;
    private Prescription currentPrescription;
    private userDS currentPatient;
    private userDS currentDoctor;
    private userservDS userService = new userservDS();

    public void setPrescriptionData(Prescription p) {
        this.currentPrescription = p;
        this.currentPatientId = p.getPatientId();
        this.currentPatient = userService.getById(currentPatientId);
        this.currentDoctor = userService.getById(p.getDoctorId());
        if (currentPatient != null) {
            lblPatientName.setText(currentPatient.getFullName());
        }
        
        if (lblPrescriptionId != null) lblPrescriptionId.setText("Ordonnance #" + p.getPrescriptionId());
        if (lblDate != null) lblDate.setText(p.getPrescriptionDate().toString());
        if (lblDuration != null) lblDuration.setText(p.getTreatmentDuration());
        if (lblDetails != null) lblDetails.setText(p.getPrescription());
    }

    public void initData(Prescription p) {
        setPrescriptionData(p);
    }

    @FXML
    public void exporterPDF(ActionEvent event) {
        if (currentPrescription == null || currentPatient == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "DonnÃ©es manquantes pour exporter le PDF.");
            return;
        }

        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Exporter l'ordonnance numÃ©rique");
        fileChooser.setInitialFileName("Ordonnance_" + currentPrescription.getPrescriptionId() + ".pdf");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("PDF Files", "*.pdf"));

        File file = fileChooser.showSaveDialog(((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow());
        if (file == null) return;

        try {
            Document document = new Document();
            PdfWriter.getInstance(document, new FileOutputStream(file));
            document.open();

            Font titleFont = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 22, Font.BOLD, new BaseColor(45, 55, 72));
            Font sectionFont = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 14, Font.BOLD, new BaseColor(255, 92, 92));
            Font labelFont = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 11, Font.BOLD, new BaseColor(74, 85, 104));
            Font textFont = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 11, Font.NORMAL, BaseColor.DARK_GRAY);

            Paragraph title = new Paragraph("ORDONNANCE NUMERIQUE", titleFont);
            title.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
            title.setSpacingAfter(20);
            document.add(title);

            document.add(new Paragraph("Informations du medecin", sectionFont));
            PdfPTable doctorTable = new PdfPTable(2);
            doctorTable.setWidthPercentage(100);
            doctorTable.setSpacingBefore(8);
            doctorTable.setSpacingAfter(18);
            doctorTable.setWidths(new float[]{1.2f, 3f});

            addInfoRow(doctorTable, "Nom et prenom", currentDoctor != null ? currentDoctor.getFullName() : "N/A", labelFont, textFont);
            addInfoRow(doctorTable, "Numero", valueOrDefault(currentDoctor != null ? currentDoctor.getPhoneNumber() : null), labelFont, textFont);
            addInfoRow(doctorTable, "Specialite", valueOrDefault(currentDoctor != null ? currentDoctor.getSpeciality() : null), labelFont, textFont);
            addInfoRow(doctorTable, "Email", valueOrDefault(currentDoctor != null ? currentDoctor.getEmail() : null), labelFont, textFont);
            document.add(doctorTable);

            document.add(new Paragraph("Prescription", sectionFont));
            PdfPTable prescriptionTable = new PdfPTable(2);
            prescriptionTable.setWidthPercentage(100);
            prescriptionTable.setSpacingBefore(8);
            prescriptionTable.setSpacingAfter(20);
            prescriptionTable.setWidths(new float[]{1.2f, 3f});

            addInfoRow(prescriptionTable, "Patient", currentPatient.getFullName(), labelFont, textFont);
            addInfoRow(prescriptionTable, "Date", currentPrescription.getPrescriptionDate().toString(), labelFont, textFont);
            addInfoRow(prescriptionTable, "Duree", currentPrescription.getTreatmentDuration(), labelFont, textFont);
            addInfoRow(prescriptionTable, "Ordonnance", currentPrescription.getPrescription(), labelFont, textFont);
            document.add(prescriptionTable);

            Paragraph signatureTitle = new Paragraph("Signature du medecin", sectionFont);
            signatureTitle.setSpacingBefore(20);
            signatureTitle.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_RIGHT);
            document.add(signatureTitle);
            addSignature(document);

            document.close();
            showAlert("Succes", "Ordonnance PDF exportee avec succes.");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de l'export PDF : " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void addInfoRow(PdfPTable table, String label, String value, Font labelFont, Font textFont) {
        PdfPCell labelCell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(label, labelFont));
        labelCell.setBackgroundColor(new BaseColor(247, 250, 252));
        labelCell.setPadding(8);
        labelCell.setBorderColor(new BaseColor(226, 232, 240));
        table.addCell(labelCell);

        PdfPCell valueCell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(valueOrDefault(value), textFont));
        valueCell.setPadding(8);
        valueCell.setBorderColor(new BaseColor(226, 232, 240));
        table.addCell(valueCell);
    }

    private String valueOrDefault(String value) {
        return value == null || value.trim().isEmpty() ? "N/A" : value;
    }

    private void addSignature(Document document) throws Exception {
        if (currentDoctor == null || currentDoctor.getImageSignature() == null || currentDoctor.getImageSignature().trim().isEmpty()) {
            Paragraph missingSignature = new Paragraph("Signature non disponible");
            missingSignature.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_RIGHT);
            document.add(missingSignature);
            return;
        }

        String signaturePath = currentDoctor.getImageSignature();
        com.itextpdf.text.Image signatureImage = null;
        File signatureFile = new File(signaturePath);
        if (signatureFile.exists()) {
            signatureImage = com.itextpdf.text.Image.getInstance(signatureFile.getAbsolutePath());
        } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + signaturePath) != null) {
            signatureImage = com.itextpdf.text.Image.getInstance(getClass().getResource("/" + signaturePath));
        }

        if (signatureImage != null) {
            signatureImage.scaleToFit(160, 80);
            signatureImage.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_RIGHT);
            document.add(signatureImage);
        } else {
            Paragraph missingSignature = new Paragraph("Signature non disponible");
            missingSignature.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_RIGHT);
            document.add(missingSignature);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void ajouterAGoogleCalendar(ActionEvent event) {
        if (currentPrescription == null || currentPatient == null) return;

        try {
            String title = URLEncoder.encode("💊 Médicament: " + currentPatient.getFullName(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            String description = URLEncoder.encode("Rappel Shifaa - Traitement: " + currentPrescription.getPrescription(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            
            String dateStr = currentPrescription.getPrescriptionDate().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd"));
            String dates = dateStr + "T080000Z/" + dateStr + "T083000Z";
            
            // Calcul de la durée pour la récurrence
            String durStr = currentPrescription.getTreatmentDuration().replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
            int count = durStr.isEmpty() ? 7 : Integer.parseInt(durStr);
            String recur = URLEncoder.encode("RRULE:FREQ=DAILY;COUNT=" + count, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

            String url = "https://www.google.com/calendar/render?action=TEMPLATE" +
                         "&text=" + title +
                         "&details=" + description +
                         "&dates=" + dates +
                         "&recur=" + recur;

            Desktop.getDesktop().browse(new URI(url));
            
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("Erreur ouverture Google Calendar: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void envoyerSMS(ActionEvent event) {
        if (currentPatient == null || currentPrescription == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Données manquantes pour l'envoi du SMS.");
            return;
        }

        String messageBody = String.format(
            "Bonjour %s %s (ID: %d), votre traitement: %s. Durée: %s.",
            currentPatient.getFirstName(),
            currentPatient.getLastName(),
            currentPatient.getUserId(),
            currentPrescription.getPrescription(),
            currentPrescription.getTreatmentDuration()
        );

        try {
            SmsService.sendSms("+21651342604", messageBody);
            showAlert("Succès", "SMS envoyé avec succès !");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur Twilio", "Échec de l'envoi : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    @FXML
    public void retourner(ActionEvent event) {
        tests.Main.showPatientPrescriptions();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.chart.*;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.AnalyticsServ;
import utils.SessionManager;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class DashboardAnalyticsController {

    @FXML private Label lblUserName;
    @FXML private Label lblUserRole;
    @FXML private Label lblInitials;

    // Stats Labels
    @FXML private Label lblTotalEvents;
    @FXML private Label lblTotalParticipants;
    @FXML private Label lblSaturation;
    @FXML private Label lblAvgPlaces;
    @FXML private Label lblWaitlist;

    // Charts
    @FXML private PieChart pieEventStatus;
    @FXML private BarChart<String, Number> barPopularEvents;
    @FXML private LineChart<String, Number> lineTemporal;
    @FXML private PieChart pieResStatus;

    private final AnalyticsServ analyticsServ = new AnalyticsServ();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        loadUserProfile();
        refreshData();
    }

    private void refreshData() {
        // Load data in background to keep UI responsive
        new Thread(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> eventStats = analyticsServ.getEventStats();
            Map<String, Object> partStats = analyticsServ.getParticipantStats();
            Map<String, Object> seatStats = analyticsServ.getSeatStats();
            Map<String, Object> tempStats = analyticsServ.getTemporalStats();

            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                updateTopStats(eventStats, partStats, seatStats);
                updateEventStatusChart(eventStats);
                updatePopularEventsChart(eventStats);
                updateTemporalChart(tempStats);
                updateResStatusChart(partStats);
            });
        }).start();
    }

    private void updateTopStats(Map<String, Object> ev, Map<String, Object> part, Map<String, Object> seat) {
        if (lblTotalEvents      != null) lblTotalEvents.setText(String.valueOf(ev.getOrDefault("total_evenements", 0)));
        if (lblTotalParticipants!= null) lblTotalParticipants.setText(String.valueOf(part.getOrDefault("total_participants", 0)));
        if (lblSaturation       != null) lblSaturation.setText(String.valueOf(seat.getOrDefault("saturation_globale", "0%")));
        if (lblAvgPlaces        != null) lblAvgPlaces.setText(String.valueOf(part.getOrDefault("moyenne_places_par_resa", 0)));
        if (lblWaitlist         != null) lblWaitlist.setText(String.valueOf(ev.getOrDefault("total_en_liste_attente", 0)));
    }

    private void updateEventStatusChart(Map<String, Object> ev) {
        if (pieEventStatus == null) return;
        ObservableList<PieChart.Data> data = FXCollections.observableArrayList(
            new PieChart.Data("Actifs (" + ev.get("actifs") + ")", (int) ev.get("actifs")),
            new PieChart.Data("Terminés (" + ev.get("termines") + ")", (int) ev.get("termines")),
            new PieChart.Data("Annulés (" + ev.get("annules") + ")", (int) ev.get("annules"))
        );
        pieEventStatus.setData(data);
    }

    private void updateResStatusChart(Map<String, Object> part) {
        if (pieResStatus == null) return;

        int confirme = (int) part.getOrDefault("count_confirme", 0);
        int refuse   = (int) part.getOrDefault("count_refuse",   0);
        int attente  = (int) part.getOrDefault("count_attente",  0);
        int annule   = (int) part.getOrDefault("count_annule",   0);

        // Show chart only when there is data to display
        if (confirme + refuse + attente + annule == 0) {
            pieResStatus.setData(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                new PieChart.Data("Aucune réservation", 1)
            ));
            return;
        }

        ObservableList<PieChart.Data> data = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
        if (confirme > 0) data.add(new PieChart.Data("Confirmées (" + confirme + ")", confirme));
        if (refuse   > 0) data.add(new PieChart.Data("Refusées ("  + refuse   + ")", refuse));
        if (attente  > 0) data.add(new PieChart.Data("En attente (" + attente + ")", attente));
        if (annule   > 0) data.add(new PieChart.Data("Annulées ("  + annule   + ")", annule));
        pieResStatus.setData(data);
    }

    private void updatePopularEventsChart(Map<String, Object> ev) {
        if (barPopularEvents == null) return;
        XYChart.Series<String, Number> series = new XYChart.Series<>();
        series.setName("Places réservées");

        List<Map<String, Object>> popular = (List<Map<String, Object>>) ev.get("plus_populaires");
        if (popular != null) {
            for (Map<String, Object> item : popular) {
                series.getData().add(new XYChart.Data<>((String) item.get("titre"), (Integer) item.get("places")));
            }
        }
        barPopularEvents.getData().clear();
        barPopularEvents.getData().add(series);
    }

    private void updateTemporalChart(Map<String, Object> temp) {
        if (lineTemporal == null) return;
        XYChart.Series<String, Number> series = new XYChart.Series<>();
        series.setName("Réservations");

        List<Map<String, Object>> daily = (List<Map<String, Object>>) temp.get("reservations_7_derniers_jours");
        if (daily != null) {
            for (Map<String, Object> item : daily) {
                series.getData().add(new XYChart.Data<>((String) item.get("date"), (Integer) item.get("count")));
            }
        }
        lineTemporal.getData().clear();
        lineTemporal.getData().add(series);
    }

    private void loadUserProfile() {
        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser != null && lblUserName != null) {
            String email = currentUser.getEmail();
            String name = (email != null && email.contains("@"))
                    ? email.substring(0, email.indexOf('@')) : "Utilisateur";
            name = name.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + name.substring(1).toLowerCase();
            lblUserName.setText(name);
            if (lblUserRole != null) lblUserRole.setText(currentUser.getRole() != null ? currentUser.getRole().name() : "");
            if (lblInitials != null) lblInitials.setText(name.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase());
        }
    }

    // Navigation
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerEvenements()   { tests.Main.showAdminEvents(); }
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerParticipants() { tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants(); }
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerReservations() { tests.Main.showAdminEventReservations(); }
    @FXML public void handleLogout()               { tests.Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

public class DashboardAppointment {

    @FXML private Button tabAjouter;
    


    // PANES
    @FXML private VBox paneListe;
    @FXML private VBox paneStats;
    @FXML private VBox paneAjouter;
    @FXML private VBox paneModifier;
    @FXML private VBox paneSupprimer;

    // ==== GLOBAL ====
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;
    private final appointmentServices service = new appointmentServices();

    @FXML private Label statTotal;
    @FXML private Label statToday;
    @FXML private FlowPane listContainer;

    // ==== PANE AJOUTER ====
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> addPatientId;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> addDoctorSpec;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> addDoctorName;
    @FXML private DatePicker addDate;
    @FXML private TextField addTime;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> addType;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> addPayment;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> addMode;
    @FXML private TextArea addReason;

    // ==== PANE MODIFIER ====
    @FXML private DatePicker updDatePicker;
    @FXML private VBox updResultsContainer;
    @FXML private VBox updFormContainer;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> updPatientId;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> updDoctorSpec;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> updDoctorName;
    @FXML private DatePicker updDate;
    @FXML private TextField updTime;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> updType;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> updPayment;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> updMode;
    @FXML private TextArea updReason;
    private int currentUpdateId = -1;

    // ==== PANE SUPPRIMER ====
    @FXML private DatePicker delDatePicker;
    @FXML private VBox delResultsContainer;
    @FXML private VBox delInfoContainer;
    @FXML private Label delDetailsLabel;
    private int currentDeleteId = -1;

    // ==== PANE STATS ====
    @FXML private VBox doctorPerformanceVBox;
    @FXML private PieChart typePieChart;
    @FXML private Label insightFillRate;
    @FXML private Label insightOptimalSlots;

    private java.util.Map<String, List<String>> doctorMap;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        String[] types    = {"Contrôle", "Urgence", "Suivi", "Consultation"};
        String[] payments = {"espèce", "carte bancaire"};
        String[] modes    = {"Présentiel", "En ligne"};

        addType.getItems().addAll(types);
        updType.getItems().addAll(types);
        addPayment.getItems().addAll(payments);
        updPayment.getItems().addAll(payments);
        addMode.getItems().addAll(modes);
        updMode.getItems().addAll(modes);

        loadDoctors();
        showListePane();
    }



    private void loadDoctors() {
        doctorMap = service.getDoctorsBySpecialty();
        addDoctorSpec.getItems().clear();
        updDoctorSpec.getItems().clear();
        addDoctorSpec.getItems().addAll(doctorMap.keySet());
        updDoctorSpec.getItems().addAll(doctorMap.keySet());
        
        List<String> patients = service.getPatientsList();
        addPatientId.getItems().clear();
        updPatientId.getItems().clear();
        addPatientId.getItems().addAll(patients);
        updPatientId.getItems().addAll(patients);
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleSpecChangeAdd() {
        String spec = addDoctorSpec.getValue();
        addDoctorName.getItems().clear();
        
        // Update types based on specialty
        addType.getItems().clear();
        String[] baseTypes = {"Contrôle", "Urgence", "Suivi", "Consultation"};
        addType.getItems().addAll(baseTypes);
        
        if (spec != null) {
            addDoctorName.getItems().addAll(doctorMap.get(spec));
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleSpecChangeUpd() {
        String spec = updDoctorSpec.getValue();
        updDoctorName.getItems().clear();
        
        // Update types based on specialty
        updType.getItems().clear();
        String[] baseTypes = {"Contrôle", "Urgence", "Suivi", "Consultation"};
        updType.getItems().addAll(baseTypes);
        
        if (spec != null) {
            updDoctorName.getItems().addAll(doctorMap.get(spec));
        }
    }

    private int extractId(String doctorInfo) {
        if (doctorInfo == null || !doctorInfo.contains("ID: ")) return -1;
        try {
            return Integer.parseInt(doctorInfo.split("ID: ")[1].replace(")", ""));
        } catch (Exception e) { return -1; }
    }

    // TAB SWITCHING
    private void resetTabs() {
        if (tabAjouter != null) {
            tabAjouter.getStyleClass().remove("btn-add-highlight-active");
        }
        paneListe.setVisible(false);
        paneStats.setVisible(false);
        paneAjouter.setVisible(false);
        paneModifier.setVisible(false);
        paneSupprimer.setVisible(false);
        messageLabel.setVisible(false);
    }

    @FXML public void showListePane()     { resetTabs(); activate(null, paneListe); loadList(); }
    @FXML public void showStatsPane()     { resetTabs(); activate(null, paneStats); loadStats(); }
    @FXML public void showAjouterPane()   { 
        resetTabs(); 
        activate(tabAjouter, paneAjouter); 
        loadDoctors();
    }

    @FXML public void showModifierPane()  {
        resetTabs(); activate(null,  paneModifier);
        updFormContainer.setVisible(false);
        if (updDatePicker != null) updDatePicker.setValue(null);
        if (updResultsContainer != null) updResultsContainer.getChildren().clear();
    }
    @FXML public void showSupprimerPane() {
        resetTabs(); activate(null, paneSupprimer);
        delInfoContainer.setVisible(false);
        if (delDatePicker != null) delDatePicker.setValue(null);
        if (delResultsContainer != null) delResultsContainer.getChildren().clear();
    }

    private void activate(Button tab, VBox pane) {
        if (tab != null) {
            if (tab == tabAjouter) {
                tab.getStyleClass().add("btn-add-highlight-active");
            }
        }
        pane.setVisible(true);
    }


    private boolean isUrgent(appointment a) {
        if (a.getType() != null && a.getType().equalsIgnoreCase("Urgence")) {
            return true;
        }
        if (a.getReason() == null) return false;
        String reason = a.getReason().toLowerCase();
        return reason.contains("urgence") || 
               reason.contains("douleur thoracique") || 
               reason.contains("saignement") ||
               reason.contains("malaise") ||
               reason.contains("grave");
    }

    private void loadList() {
        try {
            List<appointment> all = service.getAll();
            
            // === ALGORITHME DE TRIAGE INTELLIGENT ===
            all.sort((a1, a2) -> {
                boolean wait1 = "EN_ATTENTE".equals(a1.getStatus());
                boolean wait2 = "EN_ATTENTE".equals(a2.getStatus());
                
                // Les "En attente" passent avant les autres
                if (wait1 && !wait2) return -1;
                if (!wait1 && wait2) return 1;
                
                if (wait1 && wait2) {
                    boolean urg1 = isUrgent(a1);
                    boolean urg2 = isUrgent(a2);
                    // Les Urgences passent avant les non-urgences
                    if (urg1 && !urg2) return -1;
                    if (!urg1 && urg2) return 1;
                }
                
                // Sinon on garde l'ordre d'arrivée (FIFO par ID)
                return Integer.compare(a1.getAppointmentId(), a2.getAppointmentId());
            });

            listContainer.getChildren().clear();

            if (all.isEmpty()) {
                Label empty = new Label("Aucun rendez-vous enregistré.");
                empty.setStyle("-fx-padding: 20; -fx-text-fill: #6B7280; -fx-font-size: 14px;");
                listContainer.getChildren().add(empty);
                statTotal.setText("0");
                statToday.setText("0");
                return;
            }

            int totalCount = all.size();
            int confirmedCount = 0;

            for (appointment a : all) {
                if ("CONFIRME".equals(a.getStatus())) confirmedCount++;

                // --- CARD MAIN CONTAINER ---
                VBox card = new VBox();
                card.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card");
                
                String status = a.getStatus() != null ? a.getStatus() : "EN_ATTENTE";
                if ("EN_ATTENTE".equals(status) && isUrgent(a)) {
                    card.setStyle("-fx-border-color: #EF4444; -fx-border-width: 2; -fx-background-color: #FFF5F5; -fx-background-radius: 15; -fx-border-radius: 15;");
                }

                // --- HEADER: BADGES ---
                HBox header = new HBox();
                header.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-header");
                
                String type = a.getType() != null ? a.getType() : "Général";
                Label typeBadge = new Label(type);
                switch (type) {
                    case "Urgence" -> typeBadge.getStyleClass().add("badge-red");
                    case "Suivi" -> typeBadge.getStyleClass().add("badge-green");
                    case "Consultation" -> typeBadge.getStyleClass().add("badge-yellow");
                    default -> typeBadge.getStyleClass().add("badge-blue");
                }
                
                Region spacer = new Region();
                HBox.setHgrow(spacer, javafx.scene.layout.Priority.ALWAYS);
                
                Label statusBadge = new Label(statusLabel(status));
                switch (status) {
                    case "CONFIRME" -> statusBadge.getStyleClass().add("badge-green");
                    case "REFUSE" -> statusBadge.getStyleClass().add("badge-red");
                    default -> statusBadge.getStyleClass().add("badge-yellow");
                }
                
                if ("EN_ATTENTE".equals(status) && isUrgent(a)) {
                    Label urgBadge = new Label("🚨 PRIORITÉ URGENCE");
                    urgBadge.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FEF2F2; -fx-text-fill: #DC2626; -fx-padding: 4 10; -fx-background-radius: 12; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-border-color: #FCA5A5; -fx-border-radius: 12;");
                    
                    // Simple animation (clignotement)
                    javafx.animation.FadeTransition ft = new javafx.animation.FadeTransition(javafx.util.Duration.millis(800), urgBadge);
                    ft.setFromValue(1.0);
                    ft.setToValue(0.4);
                    ft.setCycleCount(javafx.animation.Animation.INDEFINITE);
                    ft.setAutoReverse(true);
                    ft.play();
                    
                    header.getChildren().addAll(urgBadge, new Region() {{ HBox.setHgrow(this, Priority.ALWAYS); }}, typeBadge, spacer, statusBadge);
                } else {
                    header.getChildren().addAll(typeBadge, spacer, statusBadge);
                }

                // --- AVATAR SECTION ---
                VBox avatarContainer = new VBox();
                avatarContainer.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-avatar-container");
                
                ImageView avatar = new ImageView(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/avatar_patient.png")));
                avatar.setFitWidth(70);
                avatar.setFitHeight(70);
                avatar.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-avatar");
                
                // Circle clip
                Circle clip = new Circle(35, 35, 35);
                avatar.setClip(clip);
                
                avatarContainer.getChildren().add(avatar);

                // --- INFO SECTION ---
                VBox info = new VBox();
                info.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-info");
                
                Label nameLabel = new Label(service.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId()));
                nameLabel.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-patient-name");
                
                Label docLabel = new Label("Dr. " + service.getUserFullName(a.getDoctorId()));
                docLabel.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-detail");
                
                Label dateLabel = new Label(a.getAppointmentDate() != null ? a.getAppointmentDate().toString() : "Date N/A");
                dateLabel.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-date");
                
                Label timeLabel = new Label("🕒 " + (a.getAppointmentTime() != null ? a.getAppointmentTime().toString() : "Heure N/A"));
                timeLabel.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-detail");
                
                Label reasonLabel = new Label(a.getReason() != null ? a.getReason() : "Pas de motif");
                reasonLabel.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-detail");
                reasonLabel.setWrapText(true);
                reasonLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-style: italic;");

                info.getChildren().addAll(nameLabel, docLabel, dateLabel, timeLabel, reasonLabel);

                // --- ACTIONS SECTION ---
                HBox actions = new HBox();
                actions.getStyleClass().add("appointment-card-actions");

                if ("EN_ATTENTE".equals(status)) {
                    Button btnConf = new Button("Confirmer");
                    btnConf.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #2ECC71; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-border-radius: 4; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-padding: 5 15;");
                    btnConf.setOnAction(e -> {
                        if (a.getReason() != null && a.getReason().contains("[En ligne]")) {
                            String uuid = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "");
                            String meetLink = "https://meet.google.com/" + uuid.substring(0, 3) + "-" + uuid.substring(3, 7) + "-" + uuid.substring(7, 10);
                            
                            a.setMeetingLink(meetLink);
                            a.setStatus("CONFIRME");
                            try { 
                                service.update(a); 
                                loadList(); 
                                showMessage("Confirmé ! Lien généré.", false); 
                                
                                // ENVOI EMAIL AVEC LIEN POUR EN LIGNE
                                String patientName = service.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId());
                                String patientEmail = service.getUserEmail(a.getPatientId());
                                String docName = service.getUserFullName(a.getDoctorId());
                                String dateStr = a.getAppointmentDate().toString();
                                String timeStr = a.getAppointmentTime().toString();
                                
                                EmailService.sendOnlineConfirmation(patientEmail, patientName, dateStr, timeStr, docName, meetLink);

                            }
                            catch (Exception ex) { showMessage("Erreur: " + ex.getMessage(), true); }
                        } else {
                            a.setStatus("CONFIRME");
                            try { 
                                service.update(a); 
                                loadList(); 
                                showMessage("Confirmé !", false); 
                                
                                // ENVOI EMAIL SI PRÉSENTIEL
                                boolean isPresentiel = a.getReason() == null || !a.getReason().contains("[En ligne]");
                                
                                if (isPresentiel) {
                                    String patientName = service.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId());
                                    String patientEmail = service.getUserEmail(a.getPatientId());
                                    String docName = service.getUserFullName(a.getDoctorId());
                                    String dateStr = a.getAppointmentDate().toString();
                                    String timeStr = a.getAppointmentTime().toString();
                                    String docAddress = service.getUserAddress(a.getDoctorId());
                                    
                                    EmailService.sendConfirmationEmail(patientEmail, patientName, dateStr, timeStr, docName, docAddress);
                                }
                            }
                            catch (Exception ex) { showMessage("Erreur: " + ex.getMessage(), true); }
                        }



                    });

                    Button btnRef = new Button("Refuser");
                    btnRef.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EF4444; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-border-radius: 4; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-padding: 5 15;");
                    btnRef.setOnAction(e -> {
                        a.setStatus("REFUSE");
                        try { service.update(a); loadList(); showMessage("Refusé.", false); }
                        catch (Exception ex) { showMessage("Erreur: " + ex.getMessage(), true); }
                    });
                    actions.getChildren().addAll(btnConf, btnRef);
                } else {
                    Button btnMod = new Button("Modifier");
                    btnMod.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F3F4F6; -fx-border-color: #D1D5DB; -fx-border-radius: 4; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-padding: 5 15;");
                    btnMod.setOnAction(e -> {
                        showModifierPane();
                        loadAppointmentToUpdate(a.getAppointmentId());
                    });

                    Button btnDel = new Button("Supprimer");
                    btnDel.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EF4444; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-border-radius: 4; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-padding: 5 15;");
                    btnDel.setOnAction(e -> {
                        showSupprimerPane();
                        if (a.getAppointmentDate() != null) {
                            delDatePicker.setValue(a.getAppointmentDate().toLocalDate());
                            handleSearchByDate();
                        }
                    });
                    actions.getChildren().addAll(btnMod, btnDel);
                }

                // --- ASSEMBLE CARD ---
                card.getChildren().addAll(header, avatarContainer, info, actions);
                listContainer.getChildren().add(card);
            }

            statTotal.setText(String.valueOf(totalCount));
            statToday.setText(String.valueOf(confirmedCount));


        } catch (SQLException e) {
            showMessage("Erreur de chargement DB: " + e.getMessage(), true);
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // ADD — écriture en DB
    // ==========================================
    @FXML
    public void handleAdd() {
        try {
            int docId = extractId(addDoctorName.getValue());
            int patId = extractId(addPatientId.getValue());
            if (patId == -1 || docId == -1) {
                showMessage("Patient et Médecin sont obligatoires.", true); return;
            }
            if (addDate.getValue() == null) { showMessage("Veuillez choisir une date.", true); return; }
            if (addTime.getText().isBlank())  { showMessage("Veuillez entrer une heure.", true); return; }
            if (addType.getValue() == null)   { showMessage("Veuillez choisir un type.", true); return; }
            if (addPayment.getValue() == null) { showMessage("Veuillez choisir un mode de paiement.", true); return; }

            String timeText = addTime.getText().trim();
            if (timeText.length() == 5) timeText += ":00";

            appointment a = new appointment();
            a.setPatientId(patId);
            a.setDoctorId(docId);
            a.setAppointmentDate(Date.valueOf(addDate.getValue()));
            a.setAppointmentTime(Time.valueOf(timeText));
            a.setType(addType.getValue());
            
            String modeStr = addMode.getValue() != null ? "[" + addMode.getValue() + "] " : "";
            a.setReason(modeStr + addReason.getText().trim());
            
            a.setPaymentMethodFromString(addPayment.getValue());
            a.setStatus("CONFIRME");

            service.add(a);
            showMessage("✓ Rendez-vous ajouté avec succès !", false);

            addPatientId.setValue(null); addTime.clear(); addReason.clear();
            addDate.setValue(null); addType.setValue(null); addPayment.setValue(null);
            addDoctorSpec.setValue(null); addDoctorName.getItems().clear();

            showListePane();

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            showMessage("L'ID Patient doit être un nombre.", true);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            showMessage("Format d'heure invalide. Utilisez HH:mm", true);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showMessage("Erreur d'ajout: " + e.getMessage(), true);
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // UPDATE — modification en DB
    // ==========================================
    @FXML
    public void handleSearchByDateUpd() {
        if (updDatePicker.getValue() == null) return;
        try {
            Date d = Date.valueOf(updDatePicker.getValue());
            List<appointment> list = service.getAppointmentsByDate(d);
            
            updResultsContainer.getChildren().clear();
            updFormContainer.setVisible(false);
            
            if (list.isEmpty()) {
                showMessage("Aucun rendez-vous le " + d, true);
                return;
            }
            
            for (appointment a : list) {
                Button b = new Button(String.format("Modifier RDV #%d — %s — %s", 
                                      a.getAppointmentId(), a.getAppointmentTime(), service.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId())));
                b.getStyleClass().add("search-result-btn");
                b.setPrefWidth(600);
                b.setOnAction(e -> {
                    loadAppointmentToUpdate(a.getAppointmentId());
                });
                updResultsContainer.getChildren().add(b);
            }
            showMessage(list.size() + " rendez-vous trouvés pour modification.", false);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showMessage("Erreur: " + e.getMessage(), true);
        }
    }

    private void loadAppointmentToUpdate(int id) {
        try {
            appointment a = service.getAppointmentById(id);
            if (a != null) {
                currentUpdateId = id;
                // Select patient in ComboBox
                String patFullName = service.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId());
                updPatientId.setValue(patFullName + " (ID: " + a.getPatientId() + ")");
                
                // Select doctor in ComboBoxes
                boolean found = false;
                if (doctorMap != null) {
                    for (Map.Entry<String, List<String>> entry : doctorMap.entrySet()) {
                        for (String docInfo : entry.getValue()) {
                            if (extractId(docInfo) == a.getDoctorId()) {
                                updDoctorSpec.setValue(entry.getKey());
                                handleSpecChangeUpd();
                                updDoctorName.setValue(docInfo);
                                found = true; break;
                            }
                        }
                        if (found) break;
                    }
                }

                if (a.getAppointmentDate() != null) updDate.setValue(a.getAppointmentDate().toLocalDate());
                if (a.getAppointmentTime() != null) updTime.setText(a.getAppointmentTime().toString());
                updType.setValue(a.getType());
                updPayment.setValue(a.getPaymentMethodAsString());
                updReason.setText(a.getReason() != null ? a.getReason() : "");
                updFormContainer.setVisible(true);
                showMessage("Rendez-vous #" + id + " chargé.", false);
            } else {
                updFormContainer.setVisible(false);
                showMessage("Aucun rendez-vous avec l'ID " + id + ".", true);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showMessage("Erreur de chargement: " + e.getMessage(), true);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleUpdate() {
        if (currentUpdateId == -1) return;
        try {
            int docId = extractId(updDoctorName.getValue());
            int patId = extractId(updPatientId.getValue());
            if (docId == -1 || patId == -1) { showMessage("Veuillez sélectionner un médecin et un patient.", true); return; }

            String timeText = updTime.getText().trim();
            if (timeText.length() == 5) timeText += ":00";

            appointment original = service.getAppointmentById(currentUpdateId);
            String originalStatus = (original != null && original.getStatus() != null)
                    ? original.getStatus() : "EN_ATTENTE";

            appointment a = new appointment();
            a.setAppointmentId(currentUpdateId);
            a.setPatientId(patId);
            a.setDoctorId(docId);
            a.setAppointmentDate(Date.valueOf(updDate.getValue()));
            a.setAppointmentTime(Time.valueOf(timeText));
            a.setType(updType.getValue());
            
            String modeStr = updMode.getValue() != null ? "[" + updMode.getValue() + "] " : "";
            a.setReason(modeStr + updReason.getText().trim());
            
            a.setPaymentMethodFromString(updPayment.getValue());
            a.setStatus(originalStatus);

            service.update(a);
            showMessage("✓ Rendez-vous #" + currentUpdateId + " mis à jour !", false);
            showListePane();

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            showMessage("Patient ID doit être un nombre.", true);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            showMessage("Heure invalide.", true);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showMessage("Erreur de mise à jour: " + e.getMessage(), true);
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // DELETE — suppression en DB
    // ==========================================
    @FXML
    public void handleSearchByDate() {
        if (delDatePicker.getValue() == null) return;
        try {
            Date d = Date.valueOf(delDatePicker.getValue());
            List<appointment> list = service.getAppointmentsByDate(d);
            
            delResultsContainer.getChildren().clear();
            delInfoContainer.setVisible(false);
            
            if (list.isEmpty()) {
                showMessage("Aucun rendez-vous le " + d, true);
                return;
            }
            
            for (appointment a : list) {
                Button b = new Button(String.format("RDV #%d — %s — %s", 
                                      a.getAppointmentId(), a.getAppointmentTime(), service.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId())));
                b.getStyleClass().add("btn-secondary");
                b.setPrefWidth(500);
                b.setOnAction(e -> {
                    currentDeleteId = a.getAppointmentId();
                    delDetailsLabel.setText(String.format(
                        "Patient: %s | Médecin: %s | Heure: %s | Type: %s",
                        service.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId()), service.getUserFullName(a.getDoctorId()), a.getAppointmentTime(), a.getType()));
                    delInfoContainer.setVisible(true);
                });
                delResultsContainer.getChildren().add(b);
            }
            showMessage(list.size() + " rendez-vous trouvés.", false);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showMessage("Erreur: " + e.getMessage(), true);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleDelete() {
        if (currentDeleteId == -1) return;
        try {
            appointment a = new appointment();
            a.setAppointmentId(currentDeleteId);
            service.delete(a);
            showMessage("✓ Rendez-vous #" + currentDeleteId + " supprimé !", false);
            currentDeleteId = -1;
            handleSearchByDate(); // Refresh the list for the current date
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showMessage("Erreur lors de la suppression: " + e.getMessage(), true);
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // UTILS / NAVIGATION
    // ==========================================
    private String statusLabel(String status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case "CONFIRME" -> "✓ Confirmé";
            case "REFUSE"   -> "✖ Refusé";
            default         -> "⏳ En attente";
        };
    }

    private void showMessage(String text, boolean isError) {
