import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.shape.Circle;
import services.CalendarEventService;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.time.format.TextStyle;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class DoctorDashboardController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private Label lblDate;
    @FXML private Label lblWelcome;
    @FXML private Label lblTodayRDV;
    @FXML private Label lblNewPatients;
    @FXML private Label lblRating;
    @FXML private VBox vboxAgenda;

    // Calendar elements
    @FXML private HBox weekRow;
    @FXML private Label monthYearLabel;
    @FXML private Label selectedDateLabel;
    @FXML private VBox calendarEventsContainer;

    // Add Event Form
    @FXML private VBox addEventForm;
    @FXML private TextField eventTitleField;
    @FXML private TextField eventTimeField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> eventTypeCombo;
    @FXML private TextArea eventDescField;

    private CalendarEventService calendarService = new CalendarEventService();
    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private services.feedbackServices feedbackService = new services.feedbackServices();
    private services.appointmentServices appointmentService = new services.appointmentServices();

    private LocalDate currentWeekStart;
    private LocalDate selectedDate;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        currentWeekStart = LocalDate.now().with(java.time.DayOfWeek.MONDAY);
        selectedDate = LocalDate.now();
        
        setCurrentDate();
        loadDoctorInfo();
        loadRealStats();
        loadAgenda();
        
        // Init ComboBox
        eventTypeCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Réunion", "Consultation", "Rappel", "Autre"));
        eventTypeCombo.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        
        renderWeek();
    }

    private void setCurrentDate() {
        LocalDate now = LocalDate.now();
        DateTimeFormatter formatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("EEEE, d MMMM yyyy", Locale.FRENCH);
        String formattedDate = now.format(formatter);
        lblDate.setText(formattedDate.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + formattedDate.substring(1));
    }

    private void loadDoctorInfo() {
        try {
            int doctorId = Main.currentUserId;
            user doctor = userService.getById(doctorId);
            if (doctor != null) {
                lblWelcome.setText("Bonjour, Dr. " + doctor.getLastName() + " ! 👋");
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void loadRealStats() {
        try {
            int doctorId = Main.currentUserId;
            
            // 1. RDV Aujourd'hui (Appointments + Events)
            List<entities.appointment> allAppts = appointmentService.getAppointmentsByDoctor(doctorId);
            long todayApptCount = allAppts.stream()
                .filter(a -> a.getAppointmentDate() != null && a.getAppointmentDate().toLocalDate().equals(LocalDate.now()))
                .filter(a -> "CONFIRME".equals(a.getStatus()))
                .count();
                
            List<CalendarEvent> todayEvents = calendarService.getByUserAndDate(doctorId, LocalDate.now());
            lblTodayRDV.setText(String.valueOf(todayApptCount + todayEvents.size()));

            // 2. Count patients for this doctor
            long patientCount = userService.getAll().stream()
                    .filter(u -> u.getRole() == user.Role.patient && u.getDoctorId() == doctorId)
                    .count();
            lblNewPatients.setText(String.valueOf(patientCount));

            // 3. Average Rating
            double avg = feedbackService.getAll().stream()
                    .filter(f -> f.getTargetId() == doctorId && f.getTargetType() == entities.feedback.TargetType.doctor)
                    .mapToInt(entities.feedback::getRating)
                    .average().orElse(0.0);
            lblRating.setText(String.format("%.1f/5", avg));

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void loadAgenda() {
        try {
            int doctorId = Main.currentUserId;
            
            // Charger les rendez-vous réels
            List<entities.appointment> allAppts = appointmentService.getAppointmentsByDoctor(doctorId);
            List<entities.appointment> todayAppts = allAppts.stream()
                .filter(a -> a.getAppointmentDate() != null && a.getAppointmentDate().toLocalDate().equals(LocalDate.now()))
                .filter(a -> "CONFIRME".equals(a.getStatus()))
                .sorted((a1, a2) -> a1.getAppointmentTime().compareTo(a2.getAppointmentTime()))
                .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toList());

            // Charger les événements manuels
            List<CalendarEvent> todayEvents = calendarService.getByUserAndDate(doctorId, LocalDate.now());
            
            vboxAgenda.getChildren().clear();
            
            if (todayAppts.isEmpty() && todayEvents.isEmpty()) {
                Label emptyLabel = new Label("Aucune consultation prévue aujourd'hui.");
                emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0; -fx-font-style: italic;");
                vboxAgenda.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
            } else {
                // Ajouter les rendez-vous en premier
                for (entities.appointment a : todayAppts) {
                    vboxAgenda.getChildren().add(createAppointmentCardFromAppt(a));
                }
                // Ajouter les événements
                for (CalendarEvent event : todayEvents) {
                    vboxAgenda.getChildren().add(createAppointmentCard(event));
                }
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private Node createAppointmentCardFromAppt(entities.appointment a) {
        HBox card = new HBox(15);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EBF8FF; -fx-padding: 12; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-border-color: #BEE3F8; -fx-border-radius: 10;");
        
        Label time = new Label(a.getAppointmentTime().toString().substring(0, 5));
        time.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2B6CB0; -fx-min-width: 50;");
        
        VBox info = new VBox(2);
        String patientName = appointmentService.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId());
        Label title = new Label("RDV : " + patientName);
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        Label desc = new Label(a.getType() + " - " + (a.getReason() != null ? a.getReason() : ""));
        desc.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568;");
        info.getChildren().addAll(title, desc);
        
        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
        
        Label badge = new Label("PATIENT");
        badge.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #BEE3F8; -fx-text-fill: #2B6CB0; -fx-font-size: 9; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 2 6; -fx-background-radius: 5;");
        
        card.getChildren().addAll(time, info, spacer, badge);
        return card;
    }

    private Node createAppointmentCard(CalendarEvent event) {
        HBox card = new HBox(15);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F7FAFC; -fx-padding: 12; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 10;");
        
        Label time = new Label(event.getTime());
        time.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #4A6CF7; -fx-min-width: 50;");
        
        VBox info = new VBox(2);
        Label title = new Label(event.getTitle());
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        Label desc = new Label(event.getDescription());
        desc.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        info.getChildren().addAll(title, desc);
        
        card.getChildren().addAll(time, info);
        return card;
    }

    // ========== CALENDAR LOGIC ==========

    private void renderWeek() {
        weekRow.getChildren().clear();
        String monthName = currentWeekStart.getMonth().getDisplayName(TextStyle.FULL, Locale.FRENCH);
        monthName = monthName.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + monthName.substring(1);
        monthYearLabel.setText(monthName + " " + currentWeekStart.getYear());

        for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
            LocalDate date = currentWeekStart.plusDays(i);
            weekRow.getChildren().add(createDayNode(date));
        }
        loadCalendarEvents();
    }

    private Node createDayNode(LocalDate date) {
        VBox vbox = new VBox(5);
        vbox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        vbox.setPrefWidth(50);
        vbox.setStyle("-fx-padding: 10; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        
        if (date.equals(selectedDate)) {
            vbox.setStyle(vbox.getStyle() + "-fx-background-color: #4A6CF7;");
        } else if (date.equals(LocalDate.now())) {
            vbox.setStyle(vbox.getStyle() + "-fx-background-color: #EEF2FF;");
        }

        Label dayName = new Label(date.format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("E", Locale.FRENCH)).toUpperCase());
        dayName.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 10; -fx-text-fill: " + (date.equals(selectedDate) ? "white" : "#718096") + ";");
        
        Label dayNum = new Label(String.valueOf(date.getDayOfMonth()));
        dayNum.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 14; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: " + (date.equals(selectedDate) ? "white" : "#2D3748") + ";");

        vbox.getChildren().addAll(dayName, dayNum);
        vbox.setOnMouseClicked(e -> {
            selectedDate = date;
            renderWeek();
        });
        
        return vbox;
    }

    private void loadCalendarEvents() {
        calendarEventsContainer.getChildren().clear();
        DateTimeFormatter dtf = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("d MMMM yyyy", Locale.FRENCH);
        selectedDateLabel.setText(selectedDate.format(dtf));

        try {
            int doctorId = Main.currentUserId;
            
            // 1. Ajouter les Rendez-vous réels
            List<entities.appointment> allAppts = appointmentService.getAppointmentsByDoctor(doctorId);
            List<entities.appointment> dayAppts = allAppts.stream()
                .filter(a -> a.getAppointmentDate() != null && a.getAppointmentDate().toLocalDate().equals(selectedDate))
                .filter(a -> "CONFIRME".equals(a.getStatus()))
                .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toList());

            for (entities.appointment a : dayAppts) {
                HBox item = new HBox(10);
                item.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
                item.setStyle("-fx-padding: 5 0;");
                
                Circle dot = new Circle(4, javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#4A6CF7")); // Blue for appointments
                String pName = appointmentService.getUserFullName(a.getPatientId());
                Label txt = new Label(a.getAppointmentTime().toString().substring(0, 5) + " - " + pName);
                txt.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
                
                item.getChildren().addAll(dot, txt);
                calendarEventsContainer.getChildren().add(item);
            }

            // 2. Ajouter les Événements manuels
            List<CalendarEvent> events = calendarService.getByUserAndDate(doctorId, selectedDate);
            for (CalendarEvent ev : events) {
                HBox item = new HBox(10);
                item.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
                item.setStyle("-fx-padding: 5 0;");
                
                Circle dot = new Circle(4, javafx.scene.paint.Color.web(ev.getColor()));
                Label txt = new Label(ev.getTime() + " - " + ev.getTitle());
                txt.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568;");
                
                item.getChildren().addAll(dot, txt);
                calendarEventsContainer.getChildren().add(item);
            }

            if (dayAppts.isEmpty() && events.isEmpty()) {
                Label l = new Label("Aucun événement");
                l.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0; -fx-font-size: 11;");
                calendarEventsContainer.getChildren().add(l);
            }
            
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    // ========== ADD EVENT FORM LOGIC ==========

    @FXML private void openAddEventForm() {
        addEventForm.setVisible(true);
        addEventForm.setManaged(true);
        eventTitleField.requestFocus();
    }

    @FXML private void cancelAddEvent() {
        hideForm();
    }

    @FXML private void confirmAddEvent() {
        String title = eventTitleField.getText().trim();
        String time = eventTimeField.getText().trim();

        if (title.isEmpty() || !time.matches("\\d{2}:\\d{2}")) {
            return;
        }

        String typeStr = eventTypeCombo.getValue();
        EventType type;
        String color;
        switch (typeStr) {
            case "Réunion": type = EventType.reunion; color = "#4A6CF7"; break;
            case "Consultation": type = EventType.consultation; color = "#10B981"; break;
            case "Rappel": type = EventType.rappel; color = "#F59E0B"; break;
            default: type = EventType.autre; color = "#E879F9"; break;
        }

        CalendarEvent ev = new CalendarEvent(Main.currentUserId, selectedDate, time, title, eventDescField.getText().trim(), type, color);
        try {
            calendarService.add(ev);
            hideForm();
            loadCalendarEvents();
            renderWeek();
            loadAgenda(); // refresh today's agenda if applicable
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void hideForm() {
        addEventForm.setVisible(false);
        addEventForm.setManaged(false);
        eventTitleField.clear();
        eventTimeField.clear();
        eventDescField.clear();
    }

    @FXML private void prevWeek() { currentWeekStart = currentWeekStart.minusWeeks(1); renderWeek(); }
    @FXML private void nextWeek() { currentWeekStart = currentWeekStart.plusWeeks(1); renderWeek(); }

    @FXML private void goToAddPatient() { Main.showDoctorPatients(); }
    @FXML private void goToAddFile() { Main.showAddMedicalFile(); }
    @FXML private void goToAddPrescription() { Main.showAjouterPrescription(); }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.AnchorPane;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class DoctorMainController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private AnchorPane contentContainer;
    @FXML private ImageView logoView;
    @FXML private ImageView profileImageView;
    @FXML private Label userNameLabel;

    private static DoctorMainController instance;
    private userServices userService = new userServices();

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        instance = this;
        try {
            Image logo = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/logo.png"));
            if (logo != null) logoView.setImage(logo);
        } catch (Exception e) {}

        loadUserInfo();
    }

    public void loadUserInfo() {
        try {
            int userId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == userId) {
                    userNameLabel.setText("Dr. " + u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName());
                    String photoBase64 = (u.getAvatarUrl() != null && !u.getAvatarUrl().isEmpty()) ? u.getAvatarUrl() : u.getUserPhoto();
                    
                    if (photoBase64 != null && photoBase64.startsWith("generated_")) {
                        photoBase64 = u.getUserPhoto();
                    }

                    if (photoBase64 != null && !photoBase64.isEmpty()) {
                        try {
                            String cleanBase64 = photoBase64.trim().replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]", "");
                            byte[] imageBytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(cleanBase64);
                            profileImageView.setImage(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(imageBytes)));
                        } catch (Exception e) {
                            setDefaultProfileImage();
                        }
                    } else {
                        setDefaultProfileImage();
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void setDefaultProfileImage() {
        try {
            Image defaultImage = new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png", 80, 80, true, true);
            if (defaultImage != null) profileImageView.setImage(defaultImage);
        } catch (Exception e) {}
    }

    public void updateContent(javafx.scene.Parent content) {
        if (contentContainer != null) {
            contentContainer.getChildren().clear();
            contentContainer.getChildren().add(content);
            AnchorPane.setTopAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setBottomAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setLeftAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setRightAnchor(content, 0.0);
        }
    }

    @FXML private void goToDashboard() { Main.showDoctorDashboard(); }
    @FXML private void goToStats()     { Main.showStatistiques(); }
    @FXML private void goToPatients()  { Main.showDoctorPatients(); }
    @FXML private void goToProfile()   { Main.showUserProfile(); }
    @FXML private void goToFeedbacks() { Main.showDoctorFeedbacks(); }
    @FXML private void goToEvents()    { Main.showDoctorEvents(); }
    @FXML private void logout()        { Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import entities.user.Role;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import services.userServices;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import entities.medicalFile;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import com.itextpdf.text.Document;
import com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph;
import com.itextpdf.text.Phrase;
import com.itextpdf.text.BaseColor;
import com.itextpdf.text.Font;
import com.itextpdf.text.FontFactory;
import com.itextpdf.text.Element;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfWriter;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPTable;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPCell;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;

public class DoctorPatientListController {

    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private FlowPane cardsContainer;
    @FXML private Label statusLabel;
    @FXML private HBox paginationBox;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> statusFilter;
    @FXML private DatePicker datePicker;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> sortComboBox;

    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private ObservableList<user> allPatientsList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private ObservableList<user> filteredPatientsList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();

    private int itemsPerPage = 8;
    private int currentPage = 1;
    private int totalPages = 1;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Initialize ComboBoxes
        statusFilter.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Tous les statuts", "Actif", "En attente", "Suspendu"));
        statusFilter.setValue("Tous les statuts");

        sortComboBox.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Ordre alphabétique", "A - Z", "Z - A"));
        sortComboBox.setValue("Ordre alphabétique");

        // Listeners for real-time filtering
        searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterPatients(); });
        statusFilter.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterPatients(); });
        sortComboBox.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { filterPatients(); });
        datePicker.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> { currentPage = 1; filterPatients(); });

        loadPatients();
    }

    public void loadPatients() {
        try {
            allPatientsList.clear();
            medicalFileServ medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
            List<medicalFile> doctorFiles = medicalFileService.getByDoctorId(Main.currentUserId);
            java.util.Set<Integer> patientIds = doctorFiles.stream().map(medicalFile::getPatientId).collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toSet());

            List<user> users = userService.getAll();
            for (user u : users) {
                if (u.getRole() == Role.patient && patientIds.contains(u.getUserId())) {
                    allPatientsList.add(u);
                }
            }
            filterPatients();
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            if (statusLabel != null) statusLabel.setText("Erreur de chargement");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void filterPatients() {
        String query = searchField.getText().toLowerCase();
        String status = statusFilter.getValue();
        String sortOrder = sortComboBox.getValue();

        List<user> filtered = allPatientsList.stream()
            .filter(u -> (u.getFirstName().toLowerCase().contains(query) || u.getLastName().toLowerCase().contains(query)))
            .filter(u -> {
                if (status == null || status.equals("Tous les statuts")) return true;
                String userStatus = u.getAccountStatus() != null ? u.getAccountStatus().name() : "";
                if (status.equals("Actif")) return userStatus.equalsIgnoreCase("active");
                if (status.equals("En attente")) return userStatus.equalsIgnoreCase("pending");
                if (status.equals("Suspendu")) return userStatus.equalsIgnoreCase("suspended");
                return true;
            })
            .collect(Collectors.toList());

        // Alphabetical sorting
        if ("A - Z".equals(sortOrder)) {
            filtered.sort((u1, u2) -> (u1.getFirstName() + u1.getLastName()).compareToIgnoreCase(u2.getFirstName() + u2.getLastName()));
        } else if ("Z - A".equals(sortOrder)) {
            filtered.sort((u1, u2) -> (u2.getFirstName() + u2.getLastName()).compareToIgnoreCase(u1.getFirstName() + u1.getLastName()));
        }

        filteredPatientsList.setAll(filtered);
        updatePagination();
        displayCurrentPage();
        if (statusLabel != null) statusLabel.setText("Total dossiers: " + filteredPatientsList.size());
    }

    @FXML
    private void resetFilters() {
        searchField.setText("");
        statusFilter.setValue("Tous les statuts");
        sortComboBox.setValue("Ordre alphabétique");
        datePicker.setValue(null);
        currentPage = 1;
        filterPatients();
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleNewDossier() {
        tests.Main.showAddMedicalFile();
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleExportPDF() {
        if (filteredPatientsList.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Export PDF", "Aucun patient à exporter.");
            return;
        }

        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Enregistrer la liste des patients");
        fileChooser.setInitialFileName("Liste_Patients_Doctor.pdf");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("PDF Files", "*.pdf"));
        
        File file = fileChooser.showSaveDialog(cardsContainer.getScene().getWindow());
        
        if (file != null) {
            Document document = new Document();
            try {
                PdfWriter.getInstance(document, new FileOutputStream(file));
                document.open();
                
                // Design colors
                BaseColor colorPrimary = new BaseColor(74, 108, 247); // Blue #4A6CF7
                BaseColor colorSecondary = new BaseColor(45, 55, 72); // Slate #2D3748
                BaseColor colorBorder = new BaseColor(226, 232, 240); // Light gray
                
                // Fonts
                Font fontTitle = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 20, Font.BOLD, colorPrimary);
                Font fontHeader = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 12, Font.BOLD, BaseColor.WHITE);
                Font fontData = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 11, Font.NORMAL, colorSecondary);
                
                // Title
                Paragraph title = new Paragraph("LISTE DES PATIENTS - SHIFAA", fontTitle);
                title.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                title.setSpacingAfter(20);
                document.add(title);
                
                Paragraph subtitle = new Paragraph("Généré le : " + java.time.LocalDate.now().toString(), 
                                                 FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 10, Font.NORMAL, BaseColor.GRAY));
                subtitle.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                subtitle.setSpacingAfter(30);
                document.add(subtitle);
                
                // Table
                PdfPTable table = new PdfPTable(3); // Patient, Téléphone, Dernière Visite
                table.setWidthPercentage(100);
                table.setWidths(new float[]{4f, 3f, 3f});
                
                // Headers
                String[] headers = {"NOM DU PATIENT", "NUMÉRO DE TÉLÉPHONE", "DERNIÈRE VISITE"};
                for (String h : headers) {
                    PdfPCell cell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(h, fontHeader));
                    cell.setBackgroundColor(colorSecondary);
                    cell.setHorizontalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                    cell.setPadding(10);
                    table.addCell(cell);
                }
                
                // Data
                for (user p : filteredPatientsList) {
                    addStyledCell(table, p.getFirstName() + " " + p.getLastName(), fontData, BaseColor.WHITE, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, (p.getPhone() != null ? p.getPhone() : "N/A"), fontData, BaseColor.WHITE, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, "2026-05-01", fontData, BaseColor.WHITE, colorBorder); // Valeur simulée ici car non dispo dans entité user directe
                }
                
                document.add(table);
                document.close();
                
                showAlert("Succès", "Le rapport PDF a été généré avec succès !");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la génération : " + e.getMessage());
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }

    private void addStyledCell(PdfPTable table, String text, Font font, BaseColor bgColor, BaseColor borderColor) {
        PdfPCell cell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(text, font));
        cell.setBackgroundColor(bgColor);
        cell.setBorderColor(borderColor);
        cell.setPadding(8);
        cell.setHorizontalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
        table.addCell(cell);
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private void updatePagination() {
        totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) filteredPatientsList.size() / itemsPerPage);
        if (totalPages == 0) totalPages = 1;
        paginationBox.getChildren().clear();

        Button prevBtn = new Button("◀");
        prevBtn.setDisable(currentPage == 1);
        prevBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
        prevBtn.setOnAction(e -> { currentPage--; displayCurrentPage(); });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(prevBtn);

        for (int i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
            Button pageBtn = new Button(String.valueOf(i));
            pageBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
            if (i == currentPage) {
                pageBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button-active");
            }
            int pageNum = i;
            pageBtn.setOnAction(e -> { currentPage = pageNum; displayCurrentPage(); });
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(pageBtn);
        }

        Button nextBtn = new Button("▶");
        nextBtn.setDisable(currentPage == totalPages);
        nextBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
        nextBtn.setOnAction(e -> { currentPage++; displayCurrentPage(); });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(nextBtn);
    }

    private void displayCurrentPage() {
        if (cardsContainer == null) return;
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();

        if (filteredPatientsList.isEmpty()) {
            Label emptyLabel = new Label("Aucun patient trouvé.");
            emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 14;");
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
            return;
        }

        int start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, filteredPatientsList.size());

        for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(createPatientCard(filteredPatientsList.get(i)));
        }
        updatePagination();
    }

    private VBox createPatientCard(user patient) {
        VBox card = new VBox(12);
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 12; -fx-background-radius: 12; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.05), 10, 0, 0, 2);");
        card.setPadding(new Insets(15));
        card.setPrefWidth(280);

        HBox header = new HBox(12);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        // Photo circulaire
        ImageView photo = new ImageView();
        photo.setFitHeight(55);
        photo.setFitWidth(55);
        javafx.scene.shape.Circle clip = new javafx.scene.shape.Circle(27.5, 27.5, 27.5);
        photo.setClip(clip);

        String b64 = patient.getUserPhoto();
        if (b64 != null && !b64.trim().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                String clean = b64.trim().replaceAll("\\s+", "");
                byte[] bytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(clean);
                photo.setImage(new javafx.scene.image.Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                photo.setImage(new javafx.scene.image.Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
            }
        } else {
            photo.setImage(new javafx.scene.image.Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
        }

        VBox nameBox = new VBox(2);
        Label name = new Label(patient.getFirstName() + " " + patient.getLastName());
        name.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 15; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        
        Label statusTag = new Label(patient.getAccountStatus() == entities.user.AccountStatus.active ? "Actif" : "En attente");
        statusTag.getStyleClass().add("status-tag");
        statusTag.getStyleClass().add(patient.getAccountStatus() == entities.user.AccountStatus.active ? "status-stable" : "status-amelioration");
        
        nameBox.getChildren().addAll(name, statusTag);
        header.getChildren().addAll(photo, nameBox);

        // Informations détaillées
        VBox infoBox = new VBox(5);
        Label email = new Label("📧 " + patient.getEmail());
        email.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        Label phone = new Label("📞 " + patient.getPhone());
        phone.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        Label lastVisit = new Label("📅 Dernière visite: 2026-05-01");
        lastVisit.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0;");
        
        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(email, phone, lastVisit);

        // Boutons d'action spécifiques au Docteur
        HBox actions = new HBox(10);
        actions.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        
        Button btnDossier = new Button("📂 Dossier");
        btnDossier.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-padding: 8 12; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        HBox.setHgrow(btnDossier, Priority.ALWAYS);
        btnDossier.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        btnDossier.setOnAction(e -> {
            tests.Main.showConsultationDossier(patient);
        });
        
        Button btnOrdonnance = new Button("💊 Ordonnance");
        btnOrdonnance.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-padding: 8 12; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        HBox.setHgrow(btnOrdonnance, Priority.ALWAYS);
        btnOrdonnance.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        btnOrdonnance.setOnAction(e -> {
            tests.Main.showPrescriptionsForPatient(patient);
        });

        actions.getChildren().addAll(btnDossier, btnOrdonnance);

        card.getChildren().addAll(header, new Separator(), infoBox, actions);
        return card;
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.PasswordField;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import services.EmailService;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;

public class ForgotPasswordController {

    @FXML private VBox step1Email;
    @FXML private VBox step2Code;
    @FXML private VBox step3Password;
    
    @FXML private TextField emailField;
    @FXML private TextField codeField;
    @FXML private PasswordField newPasswordField;
    @FXML private PasswordField confirmPasswordField;
    
    @FXML private Label mainTitle;
    @FXML private Label subTitle;
    @FXML private Label errorLabel;

    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private String generatedCode;
    private user targetUser;

    @FXML
    private void handleSendCode() {
        String email = emailField.getText().trim();
        if (email.isEmpty()) {
            errorLabel.setText("Veuillez saisir votre email");
            return;
        }

        try {
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(email)) {
                    targetUser = u;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (targetUser != null) {
                generatedCode = EmailService.generateVerificationCode();

                // Afficher un indicateur d'attente
                errorLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #3182CE;");
                errorLabel.setText("⏳ Envoi du code en cours...");

                final String codeToSend = generatedCode;
                final String emailToSend = email;

                // Envoi dans un thread séparé pour ne pas bloquer l'UI
                Thread emailThread = new Thread(() -> {
                    boolean sent = EmailService.sendResetPasswordEmail(emailToSend, codeToSend);
                    javafx.application.Platform.runLater(() -> {
                        errorLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
                        if (sent) {
                            showStep2();
                        } else {
                            errorLabel.setText("❌ Échec envoi email. Vérifiez votre connexion internet.");
                        }
                    });
                });
                emailThread.setDaemon(true);
                emailThread.start();
            } else {
                errorLabel.setText("Aucun compte trouvé avec cet e-mail");
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            errorLabel.setText("Erreur base de données");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleVerifyCode() {
        String code = codeField.getText().trim();
        if (code.equals(generatedCode)) {
            showStep3();
        } else {
            errorLabel.setText("Code de vérification incorrect");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleChangePassword() {
        String pass = newPasswordField.getText();
        String confirm = confirmPasswordField.getText();

        if (pass.isEmpty() || confirm.isEmpty()) {
            errorLabel.setText("Veuillez remplir tous les champs");
            return;
        }

        if (!pass.equals(confirm)) {
            errorLabel.setText("Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas");
            return;
        }

        try {
            targetUser.setPassword(pass);
            userService.update(targetUser);
            
            mainTitle.setText("Succès !");
            subTitle.setText("Votre mot de passe a été modifié.");
            step3Password.setVisible(false);
            errorLabel.setText("");
            
            // Redirection après 2 secondes ou via bouton
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            errorLabel.setText("Erreur lors de la mise à jour");
        }
    }

    private void showStep2() {
        step1Email.setVisible(false);
        step2Code.setVisible(true);
        mainTitle.setText("Vérification");
        subTitle.setText("Entrez le code envoyé à " + targetUser.getEmail());
        errorLabel.setText("");
    }

    private void showStep3() {
        step2Code.setVisible(false);
        step3Password.setVisible(true);
        mainTitle.setText("Sécurité");
        subTitle.setText("Choisissez un nouveau mot de passe robuste");
        errorLabel.setText("");
    }

    @FXML
    private void backToLogin() {
        Main.showLoginPage();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.userDS;
import entities.medicalFile;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.chart.BarChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.CategoryAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.NumberAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.XYChart;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.userservDS;
import services.medicalFileServ;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;

import com.itextpdf.text.Document;
import com.itextpdf.text.DocumentException;
import com.itextpdf.text.Element;
import com.itextpdf.text.FontFactory;
import com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph;
import com.itextpdf.text.Phrase;
import com.itextpdf.text.BaseColor;
import com.itextpdf.text.Font;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPCell;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPTable;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfWriter;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;

public class GererPatientsController {

    // ================= FXML COMPONENTS =================
    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private Label lblStatistiques;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> filterStatus;
    @FXML private DatePicker filterDate;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> sortOrder;
    @FXML private Pagination pagination;
    
    @FXML private ImageView imgDoctor;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorName;

    
    private final int ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 8;

    // ================= SERVICES =================
    private userservDS userService;
    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService;
    private List<medicalFile> dossiersAffiches = new ArrayList<>();
    private java.util.Map<Integer, userDS> patientCache = new java.util.HashMap<>();

    public GererPatientsController() {
        userService = new userservDS();
        medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Initialisation des filtres
        filterStatus.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Tous", "stable", "critique", "en amelioration"));
        sortOrder.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Nom (A - Z)", "Nom (Z - A)"));
        
        // Listeners pour filtrage automatique
        searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> appliquerFiltres());
        filterStatus.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> appliquerFiltres());
        filterDate.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> appliquerFiltres());
        sortOrder.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> appliquerFiltres());
        
        // Configuration de la pagination
        pagination.setPageFactory(this::createPage);
        
        chargerTousLesDossiers();
        chargerPatients(); // Pré-remplir le cache
        chargerProfilMedecin();
    }

    private void chargerPatients() {
        List<userDS> patients = userService.getAll();
        for (userDS p : patients) {
            patientCache.put(p.getUserId(), p);
        }
    }



    private void chargerProfilMedecin() {
        lblDoctorName.setText("Dr. Saif Gharbi");
        // Simuler la photo du médecin (on peut aussi chercher dans la DB)
        List<userDS> docs = userService.searchAllByNameOrSurname("Saif");
        for (userDS d : docs) {
            if (d.getFirstName().equalsIgnoreCase("Saif") && d.getLastName().equalsIgnoreCase("Gharbi")) {
                String photoPath = d.getUserphoto();
                if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                    try {
                        File file = new File(photoPath);
                        if (file.exists()) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                        } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath)));
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {}
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    private Node createPage(int pageIndex) {
        FlowPane flowPane = new FlowPane(20, 20);
        flowPane.setPadding(new Insets(10));
        flowPane.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        
        int fromIndex = pageIndex * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
        int toIndex = Math.min(fromIndex + ITEMS_PER_PAGE, dossiersAffiches.size());
        
        if (fromIndex < dossiersAffiches.size()) {
            for (int i = fromIndex; i < toIndex; i++) {
                medicalFile mf = dossiersAffiches.get(i);
                userDS patient = patientCache.get(mf.getPatientId());
                if (patient == null) {
                    patient = userService.getById(mf.getPatientId());
                    if (patient != null) patientCache.put(patient.getUserId(), patient);
                }
                if (patient != null) {
                    VBox card = createDossierCard(mf, patient);
                    flowPane.getChildren().add(card);
                }
            }
        }
        
        return flowPane;
    }

    private void updatePagination() {
        int pageCount = (int) Math.ceil((double) dossiersAffiches.size() / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
        pagination.setPageCount(pageCount > 0 ? pageCount : 1);
        pagination.setCurrentPageIndex(0);
        // Forcer la mise à jour de la page actuelle si nécessaire
        pagination.setPageFactory(this::createPage);
    }

    @FXML
    public void appliquerFiltres() {
        String keyword = searchField.getText();
        List<medicalFile> baseList;
        
        if (keyword != null && !keyword.trim().isEmpty()) {
            baseList = medicalFileService.searchByPatientNameOrSurname(keyword, tests.Main.currentUserId);
        } else {
            baseList = medicalFileService.getByDoctorId(tests.Main.currentUserId);
        }
        
        // Filtrer par statut
        String selectedStatus = filterStatus.getValue();
        if (selectedStatus != null && !selectedStatus.equals("Tous")) {
            baseList = baseList.stream()
                .filter(mf -> mf.getPatientstatus() != null && mf.getPatientstatus().equalsIgnoreCase(selectedStatus))
                .toList();
        }
        
        // Filtrer par date de dernière visite
        if (filterDate.getValue() != null) {
            java.time.LocalDate selectedDate = filterDate.getValue();
            baseList = baseList.stream()
                .filter(mf -> mf.getLastvisit() != null && mf.getLastvisit().equals(selectedDate))
                .toList();
        }
        
        // Tri
        String selectedSort = sortOrder.getValue();
        if (selectedSort != null) {
            baseList = new java.util.ArrayList<>(baseList); // Rendre la liste mutable pour le tri
            baseList.sort((a, b) -> {
                userDS pA = patientCache.get(a.getPatientId());
                if (pA == null) {
                    pA = userService.getById(a.getPatientId());
                    if (pA != null) patientCache.put(pA.getUserId(), pA);
                }
                userDS pB = patientCache.get(b.getPatientId());
                if (pB == null) {
                    pB = userService.getById(b.getPatientId());
                    if (pB != null) patientCache.put(pB.getUserId(), pB);
                }
                String nameA = (pA != null) ? pA.getFullName() : "";
                String nameB = (pB != null) ? pB.getFullName() : "";
                if (selectedSort.equals("Nom (A - Z)")) {
                    return nameA.compareToIgnoreCase(nameB);
                } else {
                    return nameB.compareToIgnoreCase(nameA);
                }
            });
        }
        
        this.dossiersAffiches = baseList;
        updatePagination();
        lblStatistiques.setText("Total dossiers: " + baseList.size());
    }

    @FXML
    public void exporterPDF() {
        if (dossiersAffiches.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Export PDF", "Aucun dossier à exporter.");
            return;
        }

        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Enregistrer le rapport PDF");
        fileChooser.setInitialFileName("Liste_Dossiers_Medicaux.pdf");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("PDF Files", "*.pdf"));
        
        File file = fileChooser.showSaveDialog(pagination.getScene().getWindow());
        
        if (file != null) {
            Document document = new Document();
            try {
                PdfWriter.getInstance(document, new FileOutputStream(file));
                document.open();
                
                // Couleurs du design
                BaseColor colorPrimary = new BaseColor(255, 92, 92); // Corail #FF5C5C
                BaseColor colorSecondary = new BaseColor(74, 85, 104); // Bleu Ardoise #4A5568
                BaseColor colorHeaderBg = new BaseColor(45, 55, 72); // Gris foncé #2D3748
                BaseColor colorBorder = new BaseColor(226, 232, 240); // Gris clair #E2E8F0

                // Polices
                Font fontTitle = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 22, Font.BOLD, colorPrimary);
                Font fontHeader = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 12, Font.BOLD, BaseColor.WHITE);
                Font fontData = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 11, Font.NORMAL, colorSecondary);
                
                // Titre principal
                Paragraph title = new Paragraph("RAPPORT MÉDICAL - SHIFAA", fontTitle);
                title.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                title.setSpacingAfter(10);
                document.add(title);
                
                // Sous-titre
                Paragraph subtitle = new Paragraph("Généré le : " + java.time.LocalDate.now().toString(), 
                                                FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 10, Font.NORMAL, BaseColor.GRAY));
                subtitle.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                subtitle.setSpacingAfter(30);
                document.add(subtitle);
                
                // Table
                PdfPTable table = new PdfPTable(5); // ID, Patient, Statut, Création, Visite
                table.setWidthPercentage(100);
                table.setSpacingBefore(10f);
                
                // Headers
                String[] headers = {"ID", "NOM DU PATIENT", "STATUT", "CRÉATION", "DERNIÈRE VISITE"};
                for (String h : headers) {
                    PdfPCell cell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(h, fontHeader));
                    cell.setBackgroundColor(colorHeaderBg);
                    cell.setHorizontalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                    cell.setVerticalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_MIDDLE);
                    cell.setPadding(10);
                    cell.setBorderColor(colorBorder);
                    table.addCell(cell);
                }
                
                // Data
                boolean alternate = false;
                for (medicalFile mf : dossiersAffiches) {
                    userDS p = userService.getById(mf.getPatientId());
                    String patientName = (p != null) ? p.getFullName() : "N/A";
                    
                    BaseColor rowColor = alternate ? new BaseColor(247, 250, 252) : BaseColor.WHITE;
                    
                    addStyledCell(table, String.valueOf(mf.getIdfile()), fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, patientName, fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, mf.getPatientstatus() != null ? mf.getPatientstatus().toUpperCase() : "N/A", fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, mf.getCreationdate() != null ? mf.getCreationdate().toString() : "N/A", fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, mf.getLastvisit() != null ? mf.getLastvisit().toString() : "N/A", fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    
                    alternate = !alternate;
                }
                
                document.add(table);
                document.close();
                
                showAlert("Succès", "Le fichier PDF a été généré avec succès !");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la génération du PDF : " + e.getMessage());
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void reinitialiserFiltres() {
        searchField.clear();
        filterStatus.getSelectionModel().select("Tous");
        filterDate.setValue(null);
        sortOrder.getSelectionModel().clearSelection();
        chargerTousLesDossiers();
    }

    @FXML
    public void rechercherPatient() {
        appliquerFiltres();
    }

    @FXML
    public void afficherTousLesPatients() {
        reinitialiserFiltres();
    }

    private void chargerTousLesDossiers() {
        List<medicalFile> allFiles = medicalFileService.getByDoctorId(tests.Main.currentUserId);
        this.dossiersAffiches = allFiles;
        updatePagination();
        lblStatistiques.setText("Total dossiers: " + allFiles.size());
    }



    private VBox createDossierCard(medicalFile mf, userDS patient) {
        VBox card = new VBox(15);
        card.getStyleClass().add("patient-card");
        card.setPrefWidth(280);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(15));
        card.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_CENTER);

        HBox header = new HBox();
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        String dateCreation = (mf.getCreationdate() != null) ? mf.getCreationdate().toString() : "N/A";
        Label lblRole = new Label("📅 " + dateCreation);
        lblRole.getStyleClass().add("card-badge");
        
        String status = (mf.getPatientstatus() != null && !mf.getPatientstatus().isEmpty()) 
                        ? mf.getPatientstatus() : "stable";
        Label lblStatus = new Label(status);
        lblStatus.getStyleClass().add("card-badge");
        
        Region headerSpacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(headerSpacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
        header.getChildren().addAll(lblRole, headerSpacer, lblStatus);

        ImageView imageView = new ImageView();
        imageView.setFitWidth(150);
        imageView.setFitHeight(150);
        imageView.setPreserveRatio(true);
        imageView.getStyleClass().add("card-image");
        
        String photoPath = patient.getUserphoto();
        boolean hasImage = false;
        if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                // 1. Essayer comme chemin absolu ou relatif au projet
                File file = new File(photoPath);
                if (file.exists()) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                    hasImage = true;
                } 
                // 2. Essayer dans les ressources
                else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath)));
                    hasImage = true;
                }
                // 3. Essayer dans un dossier 'images' dans les ressources
                else if (getClass().getResource("/images/" + photoPath) != null) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/images/" + photoPath)));
                    hasImage = true;
                }
            } catch (Exception e) { 
                System.err.println("Erreur chargement photo: " + photoPath);
            }
        }
        
        VBox imageWrapper = new VBox(imageView);
        imageWrapper.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        imageWrapper.setMinHeight(160);
        if (!hasImage) {
            imageWrapper.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #e0e0e0; -fx-background-radius: 5;");
        }

        VBox infoBox = new VBox(8);
        infoBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        
        HBox dateBox = new HBox(5);
        String patientRole = (patient.getRole() != null) ? patient.getRole().toUpperCase() : "PATIENT";
        Label iconLabel = new Label("📁 dossier   👤 " + patientRole);
        iconLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #888; -fx-font-size: 11px;");
        dateBox.getChildren().addAll(iconLabel);

        Label lblName = new Label(patient.getFullName());
        lblName.getStyleClass().add("card-title");
        
        String dateStr = (mf.getLastvisit() != null) ? mf.getLastvisit().toString() : 
                         (mf.getCreationdate() != null ? mf.getCreationdate().toString() : "Aucune date");
        Label lblVal = new Label(dateStr); 
        lblVal.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 18px;");

        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(dateBox, lblName, lblVal);

        HBox actionBox = new HBox(10);
        actionBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        
        Button btnVoir = new Button("👁 Voir");
        btnVoir.getStyleClass().addAll("btn-card-action");
        btnVoir.setOnAction(e -> consulterDossier(mf, patient));
        
        Button btnModif = new Button("✎ Modi...");
        btnModif.getStyleClass().add("btn-card-action");
        btnModif.setOnAction(e -> modifierDossier(mf, patient));
        
        Button btnDel = new Button("🗑");
        btnDel.getStyleClass().add("btn-card-delete");
        btnDel.setOnAction(e -> supprimerDossier(mf));

        actionBox.getChildren().addAll(btnVoir, btnModif, btnDel);

        card.getChildren().addAll(header, imageWrapper, infoBox, actionBox);
        return card;
    }

    public void consulterDossier(medicalFile mf, userDS patient) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/ConsultationMedicalFile.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            ConsultationMedicalFileController controller = loader.getController();
            controller.setDossierData(mf, patient);
            Stage stage = new Stage();
            stage.setTitle("Consultation Dossier #" + mf.getIdfile());
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.show();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible d'ouvrir la consultation: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    public void modifierDossier(medicalFile mf, userDS patient) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/ModifiermedicalFile.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            ModifiermedicalFile controller = loader.getController();
            controller.prechargerPatient(patient);
            
            Stage stage = (Stage) pagination.getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.setTitle("Modifier Dossier #" + mf.getIdfile());
        } catch (IOException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible d'ouvrir le dossier: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    public void supprimerDossier(medicalFile mf) {
        Alert confirmation = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirmation.setTitle("Confirmation");
        confirmation.setHeaderText("Supprimer le dossier");
        confirmation.setContentText("Êtes-vous sûr ?");
        Optional<ButtonType> result = confirmation.showAndWait();
        if (result.isPresent() && result.get() == ButtonType.OK) {
            try {
                medicalFileService.delete(mf.getIdfile());
                chargerTousLesDossiers();
            } catch (SQLException e) {
                showAlert("Erreur", e.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerAjouterDossier(ActionEvent event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/AjoutermedicalFile.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.setTitle("Ajouter dossier médical");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerStatistiques(javafx.event.Event event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/Statistiques.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.setTitle("Mes Statistiques");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible d'ouvrir les statistiques : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerPrescriptions(javafx.event.Event event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/Prescription.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.setTitle("Gestion des Prescriptions");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible d'ouvrir l'interface des prescriptions : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void addStyledCell(PdfPTable table, String text, Font font, BaseColor bgColor, BaseColor borderColor) {
        PdfPCell cell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(text, font));
        cell.setBackgroundColor(bgColor);
        cell.setBorderColor(borderColor);
        cell.setPadding(8);
        cell.setHorizontalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
        table.addCell(cell);
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.appointment;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import services.appointmentServices;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;

public class GetAllAppointment {

    @FXML private ListView<String> listAppointments;
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;

    private final appointmentServices service = new appointmentServices();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        loadRdv();
    }

    @FXML
    public void loadRdv() {
        try {
            List<appointment> all = service.getAll();
            ObservableList<String> items = FXCollections.observableArrayList();

            for (appointment a : all) {
                // ✅ Protection null sur paymentMethod
                String payment = (a.getPaymentMethod() != null)
                        ? a.getPaymentMethodAsString()
                        : "Non précisé";

                // ✅ Protection null sur date et time
                String date = (a.getAppointmentDate() != null)
                        ? a.getAppointmentDate().toString()
                        : "N/A";

                String time = (a.getAppointmentTime() != null)
                        ? a.getAppointmentTime().toString()
                        : "N/A";

                String line = String.format(
                        "RDV #%d  |  Date : %s  |  Heure : %s  |  Raison : %s  |  Type : %s  |  Paiement : %s  |  Patient ID : %d  |  Médecin ID : %d",
                        a.getAppointmentId(),
                        date,
                        time,
                        a.getReason(),
                        a.getType(),
                        payment,
                        a.getPatientId(),
                        a.getDoctorId()
                );
                items.add(line);
            }

            listAppointments.setItems(items);
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #38A169;");
            messageLabel.setText(all.size() + " rendez-vous chargés.");

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur DB : " + e.getMessage());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur : " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.availability;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.beans.property.SimpleIntegerProperty;
import javafx.beans.property.SimpleStringProperty;
import services.availabilityServices;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;

public class GetAllAvailability {

    @FXML private TableView<availability> tableAvailability;
    @FXML private TableColumn<availability, Integer> colId;
    @FXML private TableColumn<availability, Integer> colDoctor;
    @FXML private TableColumn<availability, String>  colDate;
    @FXML private TableColumn<availability, String>  colTime;
    @FXML private TableColumn<availability, String>  colStatus;
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;

    private final ObservableList<availability> list = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private final availabilityServices service = new availabilityServices();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {

        colId.setCellValueFactory(d ->
                new SimpleIntegerProperty(d.getValue().getAvailabilityId()).asObject());

        colDate.setCellValueFactory(d -> {
            // ✅ Protection null sur date
            java.sql.Date date = d.getValue().getDate();
            return new SimpleStringProperty(date != null ? date.toString() : "");
        });

        colTime.setCellValueFactory(d -> {
            // ✅ Protection null sur time
            java.sql.Time time = d.getValue().getTime();
            return new SimpleStringProperty(time != null ? time.toString() : "");
        });

        colStatus.setCellValueFactory(d -> {
            // ✅ Protection null sur statusRDV
            availability.StatusRDV status = d.getValue().getStatusRDV();
            return new SimpleStringProperty(status != null ? status.name() : "");
        });

        colDoctor.setCellValueFactory(d ->
                new SimpleIntegerProperty(d.getValue().getDoctorId()).asObject());

        tableAvailability.setItems(list);
        loadDisponibilites();
    }

    @FXML
    public void loadDisponibilites() {
        try {
            list.clear();
            List<availability> all = service.getAll();
            list.addAll(all);
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #38A169;");
            messageLabel.setText(all.size() + " disponibilités chargées.");
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur DB : " + e.getMessage());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // ✅ Capture toute autre exception inattendue
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur inattendue : " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.event;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;

import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.eventServ;
import services.Participantserv;
import services.ReservationServ;
import entities.Participant;
import entities.Reservation;
import utils.SessionManager;
import utils.RoleGuard;
import entities.user;
import java.util.HashSet;
