import utils.EventImageGenerator;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class GetAllevent {

    // ── FXML fields ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // NOTE: tableView fields kept as null-safe for backward compat
    // but are no longer in FXML — cards replace them
    @FXML private FlowPane     cardsPane;
    @FXML private TextField    tfRecherche;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cbFiltreStatut;
    @FXML private Label        lblTotal;
    @FXML private Label        statTotal;
    @FXML private Label        statActifs;
    @FXML private Label        statPlanifies;
    @FXML private Label        statTermines;
    
    @FXML private Button       btnAjouter;

    // Profile Sidebar
    @FXML private ImageView    imgAvatar;
    @FXML private Label        lblUserName;
    @FXML private Label        lblUserRole;
    @FXML private Label        lblInitials;

    private eventServ   service;
    private List<event> allEvents;

    // Patient participation state
    private boolean    isPatient      = false;
    private Set<Integer> myEventIds   = new HashSet<>();
    private int          myParticipantId = -1;

    // ── Colors per type ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private static final String[][] TYPE_COLORS = {
        {"CONFERENCE",  "#1e3a5f", "#2563eb", "#dbeafe", "#1e40af"},
        {"SEMINAIRE",   "#14532d", "#16a34a", "#dcfce7", "#14532d"},
        {"WORKSHOP",    "#78350f", "#d97706", "#fef9c3", "#78350f"},
        {"COMPETITION", "#3b0764", "#9333ea", "#f3e8ff", "#6b21a8"},
    };

    // ── Init ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        if (cbFiltreStatut != null) {
            cbFiltreStatut.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                    "Tous", "Planifié", "En cours", "Terminé", "Annulé"));
            cbFiltreStatut.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        }
        service = new eventServ();
        chargerDonnees();
        loadUserProfile();

        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        isPatient = currentUser != null
                && currentUser.getRole() != null
                && "patient".equalsIgnoreCase(currentUser.getRole().name());
        if (isPatient) {
            if (btnAjouter != null) {
                btnAjouter.setVisible(false);
                btnAjouter.setManaged(false);
            }
            loadMyReservations();
            afficherCartes(allEvents); // rebuild cards with patient buttons
        }
    }

    private void loadUserProfile() {
        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser != null) {
            String userName = extractNameFromEmail(currentUser.getEmail());
            if (lblUserName != null) lblUserName.setText(userName);
            if (lblUserRole != null) lblUserRole.setText(currentUser.getRole() != null ? currentUser.getRole().name() : "");

            String avatarPath = currentUser.getAvatarUrl();
            if (avatarPath != null && !avatarPath.isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    if (!avatarPath.startsWith("/")) avatarPath = "/" + avatarPath;
                    Image img = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream(avatarPath));
                    if (imgAvatar != null) imgAvatar.setImage(img);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    if (imgAvatar != null) imgAvatar.setVisible(false);
                    if (lblInitials != null) { lblInitials.setVisible(true); lblInitials.setText(getInitials(userName)); }
                }
            } else {
                if (imgAvatar != null) imgAvatar.setVisible(false);
                if (lblInitials != null) { lblInitials.setVisible(true); lblInitials.setText(getInitials(userName)); }
            }
        }
    }

    private String extractNameFromEmail(String email) {
        if (email == null || email.isEmpty()) return "Utilisateur";
        int atIndex = email.indexOf('@');
        if (atIndex > 0) {
            String namePart = email.substring(0, atIndex);
            // Just capitalize the first letter for simplicity
            return namePart.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + namePart.substring(1).toLowerCase();
        }
        return email;
    }

    private String getInitials(String name) {
        if (name == null || name.isEmpty()) return "U";
        String[] parts = name.split(" ");
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (String p : parts) if (!p.isEmpty()) sb.append(p.charAt(0));
        return sb.toString().toUpperCase();
    }

    // ── Load data + update stats ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void chargerDonnees() {
        allEvents = service.getAll();

        long total     = allEvents.size();
        long actifs    = allEvents.stream().filter(e -> "En cours".equalsIgnoreCase(e.getStatus())).count();
        long planifies = allEvents.stream().filter(e -> "Planifié".equalsIgnoreCase(e.getStatus())).count();
        long termines  = allEvents.stream().filter(e -> "Terminé".equalsIgnoreCase(e.getStatus())).count();

        if (statTotal    != null) statTotal.setText(String.valueOf(total));
        if (statActifs   != null) statActifs.setText(String.valueOf(actifs));
        if (statPlanifies!= null) statPlanifies.setText(String.valueOf(planifies));
        if (statTermines != null) statTermines.setText(String.valueOf(termines));

        // Global filling rate statistic
        int totalCap = allEvents.stream().mapToInt(event::getCapaciteMax).sum();
        int totalParticipants = allEvents.stream().mapToInt(e -> service.getParticipantCount(e.getId())).sum();
        if (totalCap > 0) {
            double fillingRate = (double) totalParticipants / totalCap * 100;
            if (lblTotal != null) {
                lblTotal.setText(String.format("Total : %d événements | Remplissage global : %.1f%%", total, fillingRate));
            }
        } else if (lblTotal != null) {
            lblTotal.setText("Total : " + total + " événement(s)");
        }

        afficherCartes(allEvents);
    }

    // ── Render cards into FlowPane ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void afficherCartes(List<event> list) {
        if (cardsPane == null) return;
        cardsPane.getChildren().clear();

        // Filling rate already handled in chargerDonnees

        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            Label empty = new Label("📭  Aucun événement trouvé.");
            empty.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#a0aab8; -fx-font-size:15px; -fx-padding:40;");
            cardsPane.getChildren().add(empty);
            return;
        }

        for (event ev : list) {
            cardsPane.getChildren().add(buildCard(ev));
        }
    }

    // ── Build one event card ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private VBox buildCard(event ev) {

        // ── Determine type colors ──
        String[] tc = getTypeColors(ev.getType());
        String darkColor  = tc[0]; // banner dark
        String midColor   = tc[1]; // banner mid
        String badgeBg    = tc[2]; // type badge bg
        String badgeFg    = tc[3]; // type badge text

        // ── Status badge ──
        String[] sc = getStatusColors(ev.getStatus());
        String statusBg  = sc[0];
        String statusFg  = sc[1];
        String statusIcon= sc[2];

        // ══ BANNER (colored illustration area) ══
        StackPane banner = new StackPane();
        banner.setPrefHeight(140);
        banner.setStyle(String.format(
            "-fx-background-color: linear-gradient(to bottom right, %s, %s); " +
            "-fx-background-radius: 12 12 0 0;", darkColor, midColor));

        // Banner title text
        VBox bannerText = new VBox(4);
        bannerText.setAlignment(Pos.BOTTOM_LEFT);
        bannerText.setPadding(new Insets(0, 0, 16, 16));
        Label bannerTitle = new Label(ev.getType().toUpperCase());
        bannerTitle.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                             "-fx-text-fill:rgba(255,255,255,0.9);");
        Label bannerSub = new Label(getTypeSubtitle(ev.getType()));
        bannerSub.setStyle("-fx-font-size:9px; -fx-text-fill:rgba(255,255,255,0.55);");
        bannerText.getChildren().addAll(bannerTitle, bannerSub);

        // Decorative circles on banner
        Label circle1 = new Label();
        circle1.setMinSize(80, 80);
        circle1.setMaxSize(80, 80);
        circle1.setStyle("-fx-background-color:rgba(255,255,255,0.06); -fx-background-radius:40;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(circle1, Pos.TOP_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(circle1, new Insets(-20, -20, 0, 0));

        Label circle2 = new Label();
        circle2.setMinSize(50, 50);
        circle2.setMaxSize(50, 50);
        circle2.setStyle("-fx-background-color:rgba(255,255,255,0.04); -fx-background-radius:25;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(circle2, Pos.BOTTOM_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(circle2, new Insets(0, 10, 10, 0));

        // ── Banner content: PNG image or emoji fallback ──
        String genImgPath = EventImageGenerator.getImagePath(ev.getType());
        boolean imageLoaded = false;
        if (genImgPath != null) {
            try {
                Image img = new Image(new File(genImgPath).toURI().toString());
                if (!img.isError()) {
                    ImageView iv = new ImageView(img);
                    iv.setFitWidth(290);
                    iv.setFitHeight(140);
                    iv.setPreserveRatio(false);
                    iv.setSmooth(true);
                    banner.getChildren().add(iv);
                    imageLoaded = true;
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        if (!imageLoaded) {
            String emoji = getCategoryEmoji(ev.getType(), ev.getImagePath());
            Label emojiIcon = new Label(emoji);
            emojiIcon.setStyle("-fx-font-size:46px; -fx-opacity:0.80;");
            StackPane.setAlignment(emojiIcon, Pos.CENTER);
            StackPane.setMargin(emojiIcon, new Insets(0, 0, 20, 0));
            banner.getChildren().addAll(circle1, circle2, emojiIcon, bannerText);
        }

        // Type badge (top-left overlay)
        Label typeBadge = new Label(ev.getType());
        typeBadge.setStyle(String.format(
            "-fx-background-color:%s; -fx-text-fill:%s; " +
            "-fx-background-radius:20; -fx-padding:3 10 3 10; " +
            "-fx-font-size:11.5px; -fx-font-weight:bold;", badgeBg, badgeFg));
        StackPane.setAlignment(typeBadge, Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        StackPane.setMargin(typeBadge, new Insets(12, 0, 0, 12));
        banner.getChildren().add(typeBadge);

        // Status badge (top-right overlay)
        Label statusBadge = new Label(statusIcon + " " + ev.getStatus());
        statusBadge.setStyle(String.format(
            "-fx-background-color:%s; -fx-text-fill:%s; " +
            "-fx-background-radius:20; -fx-padding:3 10 3 10; " +
            "-fx-font-size:11.5px; -fx-font-weight:bold;", statusBg, statusFg));
        StackPane.setAlignment(statusBadge, Pos.TOP_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(statusBadge, new Insets(12, 12, 0, 0));
        banner.getChildren().add(statusBadge);

        // ══ CARD BODY ══
        VBox body = new VBox(10);
        body.setPadding(new Insets(14, 16, 14, 16));
        body.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#ffffff;");

        // Title
        Label title = new Label(ev.getTitre());
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-size:15px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332; " +
                       "-fx-wrap-text:true;");
        title.setMaxWidth(260);
        title.setWrapText(true);

        // Meta info
        VBox meta = new VBox(5);
        if (ev.getDate() != null) {
            String dateStr = ev.getDate().toString();
            String timeStr = (ev.getTime() != null && !ev.getTime().isEmpty())
                           ? " · " + ev.getTime() : "";
            meta.getChildren().add(metaChip("📅", dateStr + timeStr));
        }
        if (ev.getLieu() != null && !ev.getLieu().isEmpty()) {
            meta.getChildren().add(metaChip("📍", ev.getLieu()));
        }

        // Registration status (Coordinateur Feature)
        int count = service.getParticipantCount(ev.getId());
        int max   = ev.getCapaciteMax();
        String capStr = (max > 0) ? count + " / " + max : count + " inscrits";
        String capColor = (max > 0 && count >= max) ? "#e05a5a" : "#4a5568";
        HBox capChip = metaChip("👥", capStr);
        ((Label)capChip.getChildren().get(1)).setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-text-fill:" + capColor + "; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        meta.getChildren().add(capChip);

        // Simple progress bar
        if (max > 0) {
            ProgressBar pb = new ProgressBar((double) count / max);
            pb.setPrefWidth(260);
            pb.setMaxHeight(6);
            pb.getStyleClass().add("event-progress");
            if (count >= max) pb.setStyle("-fx-accent: #e05a5a;");
            else if (count >= max * 0.8) pb.setStyle("-fx-accent: #f6ad55;");
            else pb.setStyle("-fx-accent: #48bb78;");
            meta.getChildren().add(pb);
        }

        // Separator line
        Separator sep = new Separator();
        sep.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#f0f4f0;");

        // Description
        Label desc = new Label(ev.getDescription() != null ? ev.getDescription() : "");
        desc.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-text-fill:#718096; -fx-wrap-text:true;");
        desc.setWrapText(true);
        desc.setMaxWidth(260);
        // Clamp to 2 lines visually
        desc.setMaxHeight(38);

        // ══ ACTION BUTTONS ══
        if (isPatient) {
            // Patient view: single Participer / Inscrit / Complet button
            int currentCount = service.getParticipantCount(ev.getId());
            int maxCap       = ev.getCapaciteMax();
            boolean alreadyIn = myEventIds.contains(ev.getId());
            boolean isFull    = maxCap > 0 && currentCount >= maxCap;

            HBox btnRow = new HBox();
            Button btnAction;
            if (alreadyIn) {
                btnAction = new Button("✅  Inscrit");
                btnAction.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#dcfce7; -fx-text-fill:#14532d; " +
                    "-fx-background-radius:7; -fx-font-size:12.5px; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                    "-fx-padding:7 14 7 14; -fx-cursor:default; -fx-pref-width:10000px;");
                btnAction.setDisable(true);
            } else if (isFull) {
                btnAction = new Button("🔒  Complet");
                btnAction.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#fee2e2; -fx-text-fill:#7f1d1d; " +
                    "-fx-background-radius:7; -fx-font-size:12.5px; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                    "-fx-padding:7 14 7 14; -fx-cursor:default; -fx-pref-width:10000px;");
                btnAction.setDisable(true);
            } else {
                btnAction = new Button("🎟  Participer");
                btnAction.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#2563eb; -fx-text-fill:#ffffff; " +
                    "-fx-background-radius:7; -fx-font-size:12.5px; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                    "-fx-padding:7 14 7 14; -fx-cursor:hand; -fx-pref-width:10000px;");
                btnAction.setOnAction(e -> doParticiper(ev));
            }
            btnAction.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            HBox.setHgrow(btnAction, Priority.ALWAYS);
            btnRow.getChildren().add(btnAction);
            body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, sep, desc, btnRow);
        } else {
            // Admin view: Modifier + Supprimer + Participants
            HBox btnRow1 = new HBox(8);
            Button btnModifier  = styledBtn("✏  Modifier",  "#2d3748", "#ffffff");
            Button btnSupprimer = styledBtn("🗑  Supprimer", "#e05a5a", "#ffffff");
            btnModifier.setOnAction(e  -> doModifier(ev));
            btnSupprimer.setOnAction(e -> doSupprimer(ev));
            btnRow1.getChildren().addAll(btnModifier, btnSupprimer);

            HBox btnRow2 = new HBox();
            Button btnPart = new Button("👥  Participants");
            btnPart.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:transparent; -fx-border-color:#e2e8f0; " +
                "-fx-border-width:1.5px; -fx-border-radius:7px; -fx-background-radius:7px; " +
                "-fx-text-fill:#4a5568; -fx-font-size:12.5px; -fx-padding:6 14 6 14; " +
                "-fx-cursor:hand; -fx-pref-width:10000px;");
            btnPart.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            HBox.setHgrow(btnPart, Priority.ALWAYS);
            btnPart.setOnAction(e -> handleNaviguerParticipants());
            btnRow2.getChildren().add(btnPart);

            body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, sep, desc, btnRow1, btnRow2);
        }

        // ══ CARD CONTAINER ══
        VBox card = new VBox();
        card.setPrefWidth(290);
        card.setMaxWidth(290);
        card.setStyle(
            "-fx-background-color:#ffffff; " +
            "-fx-background-radius:12; " +
            "-fx-border-color:" + getBorderColor(ev.getType()) + "; " +
            "-fx-border-width:0 0 0 4; " +
            "-fx-border-radius:12; " +
            "-fx-effect:dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.09),8,0,0,2);");

        // Hover effect
        card.setOnMouseEntered(e -> card.setStyle(card.getStyle().replace(
            "dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.09),8,0,0,2)",
            "dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.18),16,0,0,4)")));
        card.setOnMouseExited(e -> card.setStyle(card.getStyle().replace(
            "dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.18),16,0,0,4)",
            "dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.09),8,0,0,2)")));

        card.getChildren().addAll(banner, body);
        return card;
    }

    // ── Helper: meta chip row ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private HBox metaChip(String icon, String text) {
        HBox row = new HBox(6);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        Label ico = new Label(icon);
        ico.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px;");
        Label lbl = new Label(text);
        lbl.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568;");
        lbl.setWrapText(false);
        lbl.setMaxWidth(230);
        row.getChildren().addAll(ico, lbl);
        return row;
    }

    // ── Helper: styled button ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private Button styledBtn(String text, String bg, String fg) {
        Button b = new Button(text);
        b.setStyle(String.format(
            "-fx-background-color:%s; -fx-text-fill:%s; " +
            "-fx-background-radius:7; -fx-font-size:12.5px; " +
            "-fx-padding:7 14 7 14; -fx-cursor:hand; " +
            "-fx-font-weight:bold;", bg, fg));
        return b;
    }

    // ── Helper: type colors [dark, mid, badgeBg, badgeFg] ────────────────────
    private String[] getTypeColors(String type) {
        if (type == null) return new String[]{"#1a2332","#2d3748","#e2e8f0","#4a5568"};
        switch (normalizeType(type)) {
            // ── Catégories médicales ──────────────────────────────────────────
            case "DON DE SANG":          return new String[]{"#7f1d1d","#dc2626","#fee2e2","#7f1d1d"};
            case "VACCINATION":          return new String[]{"#14532d","#16a34a","#dcfce7","#14532d"};
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE":  return new String[]{"#1e3a5f","#2563eb","#dbeafe","#1e40af"};
            case "CONSULTATION":         return new String[]{"#312e81","#7c3aed","#ede9fe","#4c1d95"};
            case "SENSIBILISATION":      return new String[]{"#92400e","#d97706","#fef3c7","#92400e"};
            case "ATELIER SANTE":        return new String[]{"#134e4a","#0d9488","#ccfbf1","#134e4a"};
            case "DEPISTAGE":            return new String[]{"#1e3a5f","#0284c7","#e0f2fe","#075985"};
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":     return new String[]{"#7c2d12","#ea580c","#ffedd5","#7c2d12"};
            // ── Catégories génériques ─────────────────────────────────────────
            case "CONFERENCE":           return new String[]{"#1e3a5f","#2563eb","#dbeafe","#1e40af"};
            case "SEMINAIRE":            return new String[]{"#14532d","#16a34a","#dcfce7","#14532d"};
            case "ATELIER":              return new String[]{"#78350f","#d97706","#fef9c3","#78350f"};
            case "COMPETITION":          return new String[]{"#3b0764","#9333ea","#f3e8ff","#6b21a8"};
            default:                     return new String[]{"#1a2332","#2d3748","#e2e8f0","#4a5568"};
        }
    }

    private String normalizeType(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "";
        // Remove accents + uppercase for switch matching
        return type.toUpperCase()
            .replace("É","E").replace("È","E").replace("Ê","E")
            .replace("À","A").replace("Â","A")
            .replace("Î","I").replace("Ï","I")
            .replace("Ô","O").replace("Ù","U").replace("Û","U").replace("Ü","U")
            .replace("Ç","C");
    }

    private String getBorderColor(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "#e2e8f0";
        switch (normalizeType(type)) {
            case "DON DE SANG":         return "#dc2626";
            case "VACCINATION":         return "#16a34a";
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE": return "#2563eb";
            case "CONSULTATION":        return "#7c3aed";
            case "SENSIBILISATION":     return "#d97706";
            case "ATELIER SANTE":       return "#0d9488";
            case "DEPISTAGE":           return "#0284c7";
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":    return "#ea580c";
            case "CONFERENCE":          return "#2563eb";
            case "SEMINAIRE":           return "#16a34a";
            case "ATELIER":             return "#d97706";
            case "COMPETITION":         return "#9333ea";
            default:                    return "#e2e8f0";
        }
    }

    private String getTypeSubtitle(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "";
        switch (normalizeType(type)) {
            case "DON DE SANG":         return "Santé · Solidarité · Urgences";
            case "VACCINATION":         return "Prévention · Immunité · Protection";
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE": return "Médecine · Spécialistes · Innovations";
            case "CONSULTATION":        return "Santé · Diagnostic · Suivi";
            case "SENSIBILISATION":     return "Prévention · Éducation · Communauté";
            case "ATELIER SANTE":       return "Pratique · Bien-être · Formation";
            case "DEPISTAGE":           return "Dépistage · Prévention · Bilan";
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":    return "Urgences · Formation · Sécurité";
            case "CONFERENCE":          return "Médecine · Spécialistes · Innovations";
            case "SEMINAIRE":           return "Échanges · Expertise · Collaboration";
            case "ATELIER":             return "Pratique · Interactif · Compétences";
            case "COMPETITION":         return "Compétition · Excellence · Innovation";
            default:                    return "Événement santé";
        }
    }

    // ── Helper: emoji icon per medical category ───────────────────────────────
    private String getCategoryEmoji(String type, String imagePath) {
        // If imagePath starts with "emoji:" use that directly
        if (imagePath != null && imagePath.startsWith("emoji:")) {
            return imagePath.substring(6);
        }
        if (type == null) return "📋";
        switch (normalizeType(type)) {
            case "DON DE SANG":         return "🩸";
            case "VACCINATION":         return "💉";
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE": return "🏥";
            case "CONSULTATION":        return "🩺";
            case "SENSIBILISATION":     return "❤";
            case "ATELIER SANTE":       return "🥗";
            case "DEPISTAGE":           return "🔬";
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":    return "🚑";
            case "CONFERENCE":          return "🎓";
            case "SEMINAIRE":           return "📚";
            case "ATELIER":             return "🛠";
            case "COMPETITION":         return "🏆";
            default:                    return "🏥";
        }
    }

    // ── Helper: status colors [bg, fg, icon] ──────────────────────────────────
    private String[] getStatusColors(String status) {
        if (status == null) return new String[]{"#e2e8f0","#4a5568","◉"};
        switch (status.toLowerCase()) {
            case "en cours":  return new String[]{"#d4edda","#1a6b2e","✓"};
            case "planifié":  return new String[]{"#cce5ff","#0c4a8c","◷"};
            case "terminé":   return new String[]{"#e2e8f0","#4a5568","◉"};
            case "annulé":    return new String[]{"#fdeaea","#a0291b","✕"};
            // English fallbacks
            case "ongoing":   return new String[]{"#d4edda","#1a6b2e","✓"};
            case "planned":   return new String[]{"#cce5ff","#0c4a8c","◷"};
            case "completed": return new String[]{"#e2e8f0","#4a5568","◉"};
            case "cancelled": return new String[]{"#fdeaea","#a0291b","✕"};
            default:          return new String[]{"#e2e8f0","#4a5568","◉"};
        }
    }

    // ── Patient: load my reservations ────────────────────────────────────────
    private void loadMyReservations() {
        myEventIds.clear();
        myParticipantId = -1;
        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser == null) return;

        Participantserv pServ = new Participantserv();
        List<Participant> all = pServ.getAll();
        Participant me = all.stream()
            .filter(p -> p.getEmail() != null && p.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(currentUser.getEmail()))
            .findFirst().orElse(null);

        if (me == null) return; // no participant profile yet — will be created on first booking

        myParticipantId = me.getId();
        ReservationServ rServ = new ReservationServ();
        rServ.getByParticipant(myParticipantId).forEach(r -> myEventIds.add(r.getEventId()));
    }

    // ── Patient: participate in an event ─────────────────────────────────────
    private void doParticiper(event ev) {
        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser == null) return;

        // Find or create participant profile for this user
        if (myParticipantId < 0) {
            Participantserv pServ = new Participantserv();
            List<Participant> all = pServ.getAll();
            Participant me = all.stream()
                .filter(p -> p.getEmail() != null && p.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(currentUser.getEmail()))
                .findFirst().orElse(null);

            if (me == null) {
                String email = currentUser.getEmail();
                String name  = extractNameFromEmail(email);
                String phone = currentUser.getPhone() != null ? currentUser.getPhone() : "";
                me = new Participant(name, "", email, phone);
                pServ.ajouter(me);
                // Re-fetch to get assigned id
                me = pServ.getAll().stream()
                    .filter(p -> p.getEmail() != null && p.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(email))
                    .findFirst().orElse(null);
                if (me == null) {
                    showAlert("Erreur : impossible de créer le profil participant.");
                    return;
                }
            }
            myParticipantId = me.getId();
        }

        // Check not already registered
        if (myEventIds.contains(ev.getId())) {
            showAlert("Vous êtes déjà inscrit(e) à cet événement.");
            return;
        }

        ReservationServ rServ = new ReservationServ();
        Reservation res = rServ.soumettreDemande(ev.getId(), myParticipantId, 1);
        if (res == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur lors de l'inscription. Veuillez réessayer.");
            return;
        }

        switch (res.getStatut()) {
            case CONFIRME:
                myEventIds.add(ev.getId());
                new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION,
                    "✅ Inscription confirmée pour « " + ev.getTitre() + " » !",
                    ButtonType.OK).showAndWait();
                break;
            case LISTE_D_ATTENTE:
                myEventIds.add(ev.getId());
                new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION,
                    "⏳ Événement complet — vous avez été ajouté(e) à la liste d'attente.",
                    ButtonType.OK).showAndWait();
                break;
            case REFUSE:
                new Alert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING,
                    "❌ Inscription refusée. " + (res.getMotifRefus() != null ? res.getMotifRefus() : ""),
                    ButtonType.OK).showAndWait();
                break;
            default:
                showAlert("Statut inconnu : " + res.getStatut());
        }

        // Refresh cards to reflect new state
        chargerDonnees(); // internally calls afficherCartes which uses isPatient flag
    }

    // ── Action: modifier from card ────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void doModifier(event ev) {
        if (RoleGuard.denyIfNotAdmin()) return;
        tests.Main.showAdminModifierEvent(ev);
    }

    // ── Action: supprimer from card ───────────────────────────────────────────
    private void doSupprimer(event ev) {
        if (RoleGuard.denyIfNotAdmin()) return;
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
            "Supprimer « " + ev.getTitre() + " » ?",
            ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        confirm.setHeaderText("Confirmer la suppression");
        confirm.showAndWait().ifPresent(btn -> {
            if (btn == ButtonType.YES) {
                service.supprimer(ev.getId());
                chargerDonnees();
            }
        });
    }

    // ── FXML handlers (kept exactly as before) ────────────────────────────────
    @FXML public void handleRecherche()    { appliquerFiltres(); }
    @FXML public void handleFiltreStatut() { appliquerFiltres(); }

    private void appliquerFiltres() {
        if (allEvents == null) return;
        String recherche    = tfRecherche    != null ? tfRecherche.getText().toLowerCase().trim() : "";
        String statutFiltre = cbFiltreStatut != null ? cbFiltreStatut.getValue() : "Tous";

        List<event> filtered = allEvents.stream().filter(e -> {
            boolean matchR = e.getTitre().toLowerCase().contains(recherche);
            boolean matchS = "Tous".equals(statutFiltre)
                          || statutFiltre.equalsIgnoreCase(e.getStatus());
            return matchR && matchS;
        }).collect(Collectors.toList());

        afficherCartes(filtered);
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleActualiser() {
        if (tfRecherche    != null) tfRecherche.clear();
        if (cbFiltreStatut != null) cbFiltreStatut.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        chargerDonnees();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleAjouter() {
        if (RoleGuard.denyIfNotAdmin()) return;
        tests.Main.showAdminAjouterEvent();
    }

    // kept for backward compat (not used in card view — doModifier used instead)
    @FXML
    public void handleModifier() {
        showAlert("Cliquez sur le bouton ✏ Modifier de la carte souhaitée.");
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleSupprimer() {
        showAlert("Cliquez sur le bouton 🗑 Supprimer de la carte souhaitée.");
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleNaviguerParticipants() { tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants(); }

    @FXML
    public void handleNaviguerAnalytiques() { tests.Main.showAdminEventAnalytics(); }

    @FXML
    public void handleNaviguerReservations()    { tests.Main.showAdminEventReservations(); }
    @FXML
    public void handleNaviguerRecommandations() { tests.Main.showAdminEventRecommendations(); }

    @FXML
    public void handleNaviguerEvenements() { tests.Main.showAdminEvents(); }

    @FXML
    public void handleLogout() { tests.Main.showLoginPage(); }

    // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void naviguer(String fxml) {
        try {
            Parent root = FXMLLoader.load(getClass().getResource(fxml));
            Stage stage = (Stage) cardsPane.getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.centerOnScreen();
        } catch (IOException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    private void showAlert(String msg) {
        new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, msg, ButtonType.OK).showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Participant;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.LoyaltyService;
import services.Participantserv;
import utils.SessionManager;
import entities.user;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class GetAllparticipant {

    @FXML private FlowPane     cardsPane;
    @FXML private TextField    tfRecherche;
    @FXML private Label        lblTotal;

    // Profile Sidebar
    @FXML private ImageView imgAvatar;
    @FXML private Label lblUserName;
    @FXML private Label lblUserRole;
    @FXML private Label lblInitials;

    private Participantserv service      = new Participantserv();
    private LoyaltyService  loyaltyServ = new LoyaltyService();
    private List<Participant> allParticipants;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        chargerDonnees();
        loadUserProfile();
    }

    private void chargerDonnees() {
        allParticipants = service.getAll();
        // Enrich each participant with live loyalty data
        allParticipants.forEach(p -> loyaltyServ.enrich(p));
        afficherCartes(allParticipants);
    }

    private void afficherCartes(List<Participant> list) {
        if (cardsPane == null) return;
        cardsPane.getChildren().clear();

        if (lblTotal != null)
            lblTotal.setText("Total : " + list.size() + " participant(s) trouvé(s)");

        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            Label empty = new Label("📭 Aucun participant trouvé.");
            empty.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#a0aab8; -fx-font-size:15px; -fx-padding:40;");
            cardsPane.getChildren().add(empty);
            return;
        }

        for (Participant p : list) {
            cardsPane.getChildren().add(buildCard(p));
        }
    }

    private VBox buildCard(Participant p) {
        String borderColor = p.getPriorityColor();

        // ══ CARD CONTAINER ══
        VBox card = new VBox(0);
        card.setPrefWidth(270);
        card.setMaxWidth(270);
        card.setStyle(
            "-fx-background-color:#ffffff; -fx-background-radius:14; " +
            "-fx-border-color:" + borderColor + "; -fx-border-width:0 0 0 4; " +
            "-fx-border-radius:14; " +
            "-fx-effect:dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.08),8,0,0,2);");

        // ── BANNER (badge + priority) ─────────────────────────────────────────
        HBox banner = new HBox(8);
        banner.setPadding(new Insets(10, 14, 10, 14));
        banner.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        banner.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#f8fafc; -fx-background-radius:14 14 0 0;");

        Label badgeLbl = new Label(p.getBadgeEmoji() + " " + p.getBadge().name());
        badgeLbl.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + p.getBadgeColor() + "22; " +
                          "-fx-text-fill:" + p.getBadgeColor() + "; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                          "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-background-radius:14; -fx-padding:3 9 3 9;");

        Label prioLbl = new Label(p.getPriorityEmoji() + " " + p.getPriorityLevel().name());
        prioLbl.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + p.getPriorityColor() + "22; " +
                         "-fx-text-fill:" + p.getPriorityColor() + "; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                         "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-background-radius:14; -fx-padding:3 9 3 9;");

        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label scoreLbl = new Label("🏅 " + p.getLoyaltyPoints() + " pts");
        scoreLbl.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568;");

        banner.getChildren().addAll(badgeLbl, prioLbl, spacer, scoreLbl);

        // ── BODY ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        VBox body = new VBox(10);
        body.setPadding(new Insets(14, 16, 14, 16));

        // Avatar + Name
        HBox header = new HBox(12);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        Label avatar = new Label(p.getNom().substring(0, 1).toUpperCase());
        avatar.setStyle(
            "-fx-background-color:" + p.getPriorityColor() + "; " +
            "-fx-background-radius:22; -fx-text-fill:white; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
            "-fx-alignment:center; -fx-min-width:44; -fx-min-height:44; -fx-font-size:17px;");

        VBox nameBox = new VBox(2);
        Label name = new Label(p.getNom() + " " + p.getPrenom());
        name.setStyle("-fx-font-size:14px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332;");
        Label sub = new Label("ID: #" + p.getId());
        sub.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#718096;");
        nameBox.getChildren().addAll(name, sub);
        header.getChildren().addAll(avatar, nameBox);

        // Info
        VBox info = new VBox(6);
        info.getChildren().addAll(
            infoRow("✉", p.getEmail()),
            infoRow("📞", p.getTelephone() != null ? p.getTelephone() : "—"),
            infoRow("✅", p.getAttendanceCount() + " présences   |   ↩ " + p.getCancellationCount() + " annulations")
        );

        // Actions
        HBox actions = new HBox(6);
        actions.setPadding(new Insets(6, 0, 0, 0));

        Button btnProfil = new Button("👤 Profil");
        btnProfil.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#ede9fe; -fx-text-fill:#7c3aed; " +
                           "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-background-radius:7; -fx-padding:6 10 6 10; -fx-cursor:hand; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        btnProfil.setOnAction(e -> doProfil(p));

        Button btnEdit = new Button("✏ Modifier");
        btnEdit.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#edf2f7; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568; " +
                         "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-background-radius:7; -fx-padding:6 10 6 10; -fx-cursor:hand;");
        btnEdit.setOnAction(e -> doModifier(p));

        Button btnDel = new Button("🗑");
        btnDel.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#fff5f5; -fx-text-fill:#c53030; " +
                        "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-background-radius:7; -fx-padding:6 10 6 10; -fx-cursor:hand;");
        btnDel.setOnAction(e -> doSupprimer(p));

        actions.getChildren().addAll(btnProfil, btnEdit, btnDel);

        body.getChildren().addAll(header, new Separator(), info, actions);
        card.getChildren().addAll(banner, body);

        // Hover
        String baseStyle = card.getStyle();
        card.setOnMouseEntered(ev -> card.setStyle(baseStyle.replace(
            "dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.08),8,0,0,2)",
            "dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.16),14,0,0,4)")));
        card.setOnMouseExited(ev -> card.setStyle(baseStyle));

        return card;
    }

    private void doProfil(Participant p) {
        tests.Main.showAdminParticipantProfile(p);
    }

    private HBox infoRow(String icon, String text) {
        HBox row = new HBox(8);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        Label ico = new Label(icon);
        ico.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#a0aec0; -fx-font-size:13px;");
        Label lbl = new Label(text);
        lbl.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#4a5568; -fx-font-size:12px;");
        row.getChildren().addAll(ico, lbl);
        return row;
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleRecherche() {
        String mot = tfRecherche.getText().toLowerCase().trim();
        List<Participant> filtered = allParticipants.stream()
            .filter(p -> p.getNom().toLowerCase().contains(mot) || p.getPrenom().toLowerCase().contains(mot))
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        afficherCartes(filtered);
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleActualiser() {
        service = new Participantserv();
        chargerDonnees();
        loadUserProfile();
    }

    private void loadUserProfile() {
        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser != null) {
            String userName = extractNameFromEmail(currentUser.getEmail());
            if (lblUserName != null) lblUserName.setText(userName);
            if (lblUserRole != null) lblUserRole.setText(currentUser.getRole() != null ? currentUser.getRole().name() : "");

            String avatarPath = currentUser.getAvatarUrl();
            if (avatarPath != null && !avatarPath.isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    if (!avatarPath.startsWith("/")) avatarPath = "/" + avatarPath;
                    Image img = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream(avatarPath));
                    if (imgAvatar != null) imgAvatar.setImage(img);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    if (imgAvatar != null) imgAvatar.setVisible(false);
                    if (lblInitials != null) { lblInitials.setVisible(true); lblInitials.setText(getInitials(userName)); }
                }
            } else {
                if (imgAvatar != null) imgAvatar.setVisible(false);
                if (lblInitials != null) { lblInitials.setVisible(true); lblInitials.setText(getInitials(userName)); }
            }
        }
    }

    private String extractNameFromEmail(String email) {
        if (email == null || email.isEmpty()) return "Utilisateur";
        int atIndex = email.indexOf('@');
        if (atIndex > 0) {
            String namePart = email.substring(0, atIndex);
            return namePart.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + namePart.substring(1).toLowerCase();
        }
        return email;
    }

    private String getInitials(String name) {
        if (name == null || name.isEmpty()) return "U";
        String[] parts = name.split(" ");
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (String p : parts) if (!p.isEmpty()) sb.append(p.charAt(0));
        return sb.toString().toUpperCase();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleAjouter() {
        tests.Main.showAdminAjouterParticipant();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleNaviguerEvenements() { tests.Main.showAdminEvents(); }

    @FXML
    public void handleNaviguerParticipants() { tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants(); }

    @FXML
    public void handleLogout() { tests.Main.showLoginPage(); }

    private void doModifier(Participant p) {
        tests.Main.showAdminModifierParticipant(p);
    }

    private void doSupprimer(Participant p) {
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
            "Supprimer " + p.getNom() + " " + p.getPrenom() + " ?",
            ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        confirm.showAndWait().ifPresent(btn -> {
            if (btn == ButtonType.YES) {
                service.supprimer(p.getId());
                chargerDonnees();
            }
        });
    }

    private void naviguer(String fxml) {
        try {
            Parent root = FXMLLoader.load(getClass().getResource(fxml));
            Stage stage = (Stage) cardsPane.getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.centerOnScreen();
        } catch (IOException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.HealthLog;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.chart.LineChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.PieChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.XYChart;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import services.HealthLogService;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;

public class HealthStatisticsController {

    @FXML private LineChart<String, Number> severityChart;
    @FXML private PieChart typePieChart;
    @FXML private VBox historyContainer;
    
    @FXML private Label totalLogsLabel;
    @FXML private Label avgSeverityLabel;
    @FXML private Label lastActivityLabel;
    @FXML private Label alertsCountLabel;

    private HealthLogService healthService = new HealthLogService();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        loadData();
    }

    private void loadData() {
        try {
            int userId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            List<HealthLog> logs = healthService.getByUserId(userId);
            
            if (logs.isEmpty()) {
                totalLogsLabel.setText("0");
                avgSeverityLabel.setText("0.0");
                lastActivityLabel.setText("Aucune donnée");
                return;
            }

            // 1. SUMMARY TILES
            totalLogsLabel.setText(String.valueOf(logs.size()));
            double avg = logs.stream().filter(l -> l.getType().equals("Symptom")).mapToInt(HealthLog::getSeverity).average().orElse(0.0);
            avgSeverityLabel.setText(String.format("%.1f", avg));
            lastActivityLabel.setText(logs.get(0).getDate().toString().substring(0, 10));
            
            long emergencyCount = logs.stream().filter(l -> l.getSeverity() >= 10).count();
            alertsCountLabel.setText(String.valueOf(emergencyCount));

            // 2. MODERN CARDS HISTORY
            historyContainer.getChildren().clear();
            for (HealthLog log : logs) {
                historyContainer.getChildren().add(createLogCard(log));
            }

            // 3. LINE CHART
            severityChart.getData().clear();
            XYChart.Series<String, Number> series = new XYChart.Series<>();
            int count = 0;
            for (int i = logs.size() - 1; i >= 0 && count < 8; i--) {
                HealthLog log = logs.get(i);
                if (log.getType().equals("Symptom")) {
                    series.getData().add(new XYChart.Data<>(log.getDate().toString().substring(5, 10), log.getSeverity()));
                    count++;
                }
            }
            severityChart.getData().add(series);

            // 4. PIE CHART
            long routine = logs.stream().filter(l -> l.getType().equals("Routine")).count();
            long symptom = logs.stream().filter(l -> l.getType().equals("Symptom")).count();
            ObservableList<PieChart.Data> pieData = FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                new PieChart.Data("Suivis (" + routine + ")", routine),
                new PieChart.Data("Alertes (" + symptom + ")", symptom)
            );
            typePieChart.setData(pieData);

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private HBox createLogCard(HealthLog log) {
        HBox card = new HBox(15);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        card.setPadding(new javafx.geometry.Insets(15));
        
        // Couleur selon sévérité
        String color = "#10B981"; // Vert (Routine)
        if (log.getType().equals("Symptom")) {
            if (log.getSeverity() >= 7) color = "#EF4444"; // Rouge (Urgent)
            else if (log.getSeverity() >= 4) color = "#F59E0B"; // Orange (Modéré)
            else color = "#3B82F6"; // Bleu (Faible)
        }

        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 12; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.03), 8, 0, 0, 2); -fx-border-color: " + color + "; -fx-border-width: 0 0 0 5;");

        VBox info = new VBox(5);
        Label typeLbl = new Label(log.getType().toUpperCase());
        typeLbl.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: " + color + "; -fx-font-size: 10;");
        
        Label resLbl = new Label(log.getResult());
        resLbl.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 14; -fx-text-fill: #1E293B; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        
        Label dateLbl = new Label(log.getDate().toString().substring(0, 16));
        dateLbl.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #94A3B8; -fx-font-size: 11;");
        
        info.getChildren().addAll(typeLbl, resLbl, dateLbl);
        HBox.setHgrow(info, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label sevLbl = new Label(log.getSeverity() + "/10");
        sevLbl.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 18; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #475569;");

        card.getChildren().addAll(info, sevLbl);
        return card;
    }
}
package Controler;

import javafx.fxml.FXML;

public class LandingController {

package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.PasswordField;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import services.EmailService;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.TextInputDialog;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.sql.SQLException;

public class LoginController {

    @FXML private TextField emailField;
    @FXML private PasswordField passwordField;
    @FXML private TextField passwordVisible;
    @FXML private Button eyeBtn;
    @FXML private Label errorLabel;

    private boolean passwordShown = false;
    private userServices userService = new userServices();

    @FXML
    private void handleLogin() {
        // Lire le mot de passe depuis le champ actif
        String email = emailField.getText().trim();
        String password = passwordShown ? passwordVisible.getText() : passwordField.getText();

        if (email.isEmpty() || password.isEmpty()) {
            errorLabel.setText("Veuillez remplir tous les champs");
            return;
        }

        if (!email.matches("^[\\w-\\.]+@([\\w-]+\\.)+[\\w-]{2,4}$")) {
            errorLabel.setText("Format d'email invalide");
            return;
        }

        try {
            user authenticatedUser = authenticate(email, password);

            if (authenticatedUser != null) {
                // --- VÉRIFICATION DU STATUT DU COMPTE ---
                if (authenticatedUser.getAccountStatus() == user.AccountStatus.pending) {
                    errorLabel.setText("Votre compte est en attente de validation par l'admin.");
                    return;
                } else if (authenticatedUser.getAccountStatus() == user.AccountStatus.blocked) {
                    errorLabel.setText("Votre compte est bloqué. Contactez le support.");
                    return;
                }

                // Initialiser la session
                utils.SessionManager.getInstance().login(authenticatedUser);

                String role = authenticatedUser.getRole().name();
                Main.redirectToSpace(authenticatedUser.getUserId(), role);
            } else {
                errorLabel.setText("Email ou mot de passe incorrect");
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            errorLabel.setText("Erreur de connexion à la base de données");
        }
    }

    private user authenticate(String email, String password) throws SQLException {
        for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
            if (u.getEmail().equals(email) && u.getPassword().equals(password)) {
                return u;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToSignUp() {
        Main.showSignUpPage();
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleGoogleLogin() {
        processOAuthLogin("Google");
    }

    private void processOAuthLogin(String provider) {
        errorLabel.setText("Ouverture de " + provider + "...");
        services.OAuthService.openOAuthWindow(provider, oAuthUser -> {
            try {
                user existingUser = null;
                for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                    if (u.getEmail().equals(oAuthUser.getEmail())) {
                        existingUser = u;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (existingUser != null) {
                    utils.SessionManager.getInstance().login(existingUser);
                    Main.redirectToSpace(existingUser.getUserId(), existingUser.getRole().name());
                } else {
                    oAuthUser.setPassword("oauth_" + System.currentTimeMillis());
                    userService.add(oAuthUser);
                    for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                        if (u.getEmail().equals(oAuthUser.getEmail())) {
                            utils.SessionManager.getInstance().login(u);
                            Main.redirectToSpace(u.getUserId(), u.getRole().name());
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (SQLException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                errorLabel.setText("Erreur base de données (OAuth)");
            }
        }, errorMessage -> {
            errorLabel.setText(errorMessage);
        });
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleFaceIdLogin() {
        String email = emailField.getText().trim();
        if (email.isEmpty()) {
            errorLabel.setText("Saisissez votre email pour le Face ID");
            return;
        }

        errorLabel.setText("Préparation de la webcam...");
        services.FaceIdService faceIdService = new services.FaceIdService();
        faceIdService.scanAndLogin(email, new services.FaceIdService.FaceIdCallback() {
            @Override
            public void onSuccess(user user) {
                utils.SessionManager.getInstance().login(user);
                Main.redirectToSpace(user.getUserId(), user.getRole().name());
            }

            @Override
            public void onFailure(String message) {
                errorLabel.setText(message);
            }
        });
    }

    @FXML
    private void togglePasswordVisibility() {
        passwordShown = !passwordShown;
        if (passwordShown) {
            // Copier le texte masqué → champ visible
            passwordVisible.setText(passwordField.getText());
            passwordField.setVisible(false);
            passwordField.setManaged(false);
            passwordVisible.setVisible(true);
            passwordVisible.setManaged(true);
            eyeBtn.setText("🙈");
            passwordVisible.requestFocus();
            passwordVisible.positionCaret(passwordVisible.getText().length());
        } else {
            // Copier le texte visible → champ masqué
            passwordField.setText(passwordVisible.getText());
            passwordVisible.setVisible(false);
            passwordVisible.setManaged(false);
            passwordField.setVisible(true);
            passwordField.setManaged(true);
            eyeBtn.setText("👁");
            passwordField.requestFocus();
            passwordField.positionCaret(passwordField.getText().length());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleForgotPassword() {
        Main.showForgotPasswordPage();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.MedicationReminder;
import javafx.animation.KeyFrame;
import javafx.animation.Timeline;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.util.Duration;
import services.MedicationService;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;

public class MedicationTrackerController {

    @FXML private TextField medNameField;
    @FXML private TextField dosageField;
    @FXML private TextField timeField;
    @FXML private VBox pillContainer;

    private MedicationService medService = new MedicationService();
    private Timeline autoRefresh;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        loadMedications();
        
        // Rafraichir toutes les minutes pour verifier les retards
        autoRefresh = new Timeline(new KeyFrame(Duration.minutes(1), e -> loadMedications()));
        autoRefresh.setCycleCount(Timeline.INDEFINITE);
        autoRefresh.play();
    }

    private void loadMedications() {
        try {
            int userId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            List<MedicationReminder> list = medService.getByUserId(userId);
            pillContainer.getChildren().clear();
            for (MedicationReminder m : list) {
                pillContainer.getChildren().add(createMedCard(m));
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleAddMedication() {
        if (medNameField.getText().isEmpty() || timeField.getText().isEmpty()) return;
        try {
            MedicationReminder m = new MedicationReminder(
                Main.getCurrentUserId(),
                medNameField.getText(),
                dosageField.getText(),
                timeField.getText(),
                false
            );
            medService.add(m);
            medNameField.clear(); dosageField.clear(); timeField.clear();
            loadMedications();
        } catch (SQLException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    private HBox createMedCard(MedicationReminder m) {
        HBox card = new HBox(20);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        card.setPadding(new javafx.geometry.Insets(15));
        
        // --- LOGIQUE TEMPS RÉEL ---
        boolean isDelayed = false;
        try {
            LocalTime now = LocalTime.now();
            LocalTime medTime = LocalTime.parse(m.getTime(), DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm"));
            if (now.isAfter(medTime) && !m.isTaken()) {
                isDelayed = true;
            }
        } catch (Exception e) { /* Format heure invalide */ }

        // --- STYLE DYNAMIQUE ---
        String bg = "white";
        String border = "#E2E8F0";
        String statusText = "Prévu à " + m.getTime();
        String statusColor = "#64748B";

        if (m.isTaken()) {
            bg = "#F0FDF4";
            border = "#10B981";
            statusText = "✓ Pris à l'heure";
            statusColor = "#10B981";
        } else if (isDelayed) {
            bg = "#FEF2F2"; // Rouge très clair
            border = "#EF4444"; // Rouge
            statusText = "⚠️ RETARD / OUBLI (Prévu à " + m.getTime() + ")";
            statusColor = "#EF4444";
        }

        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: " + bg + "; -fx-background-radius: 12; -fx-border-color: " + border + "; -fx-border-width: 2; -fx-border-radius: 12; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.05), 8, 0, 0, 2);");

        VBox info = new VBox(5);
        Label name = new Label(m.getMedName().toUpperCase());
        name.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 15; -fx-text-fill: #1E293B;");
        
        Label detail = new Label(m.getDosage());
        detail.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 13; -fx-text-fill: #475569;");
        
        Label status = new Label(statusText);
        status.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: " + statusColor + ";");
        
        info.getChildren().addAll(name, detail, status);
        HBox.setHgrow(info, Priority.ALWAYS);

        // --- ACTIONS ---
        Button actionBtn = new Button(m.isTaken() ? "Annuler" : "Marquer comme pris");
        if (m.isTaken()) {
            actionBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #10B981; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        } else if (isDelayed) {
            actionBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EF4444; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        } else {
            actionBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: transparent; -fx-border-color: #3B82F6; -fx-border-radius: 20; -fx-text-fill: #3B82F6; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        }
        
        actionBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
            try {
                medService.updateStatus(m.getId(), !m.isTaken());
                loadMedications();
            } catch (SQLException ex) { ex.printStackTrace(); }
        });

        Button delBtn = new Button("✕");
        delBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: transparent; -fx-text-fill: #94A3B8; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        delBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
            try {
                medService.delete(m.getId());
                loadMedications();
            } catch (SQLException ex) { ex.printStackTrace(); }
        });

        card.getChildren().addAll(info, actionBtn, delBtn);
        return card;
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.medicalFile;
import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class MesDossiersPatientController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private VBox dossiersList;

    private medicalFileServ medicalService = new medicalFileServ();
    private userServices userService = new userServices();

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        chargerMesDossiers();
    }

    private void chargerMesDossiers() {
        dossiersList.getChildren().clear();
        int patientId = Main.currentUserId;
        
        List<medicalFile> dossiers = medicalService.getByPatientId(patientId);
        
        if (dossiers.isEmpty()) {
            Label emptyLabel = new Label("Vous n'avez pas encore de dossier médical enregistré.");
            emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 16; -fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-padding: 50;");
            dossiersList.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
            return;
        }

        try {
            List<user> allUsers = userService.getAll();
            
            for (medicalFile mf : dossiers) {
                user doctor = findUserById(allUsers, mf.getDoctorId());
                dossiersList.getChildren().add(createDossierCard(mf, doctor));
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private user findUserById(List<user> users, int id) {
        for (user u : users) {
            if (u.getUserId() == id) return u;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private HBox createDossierCard(medicalFile mf, user doctor) {
        HBox card = new HBox(20);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(20));
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 15; -fx-effect: dropshadow(three-pass-box, rgba(0,0,0,0.05), 10, 0, 0, 5);");
        
        // Icon Area
        VBox iconBox = new VBox();
        iconBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        iconBox.setMinWidth(60);
        iconBox.setMinHeight(60);
        iconBox.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EBF8FF; -fx-background-radius: 12;");
        Label iconLabel = new Label("📋");
        iconLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 24;");
        iconBox.getChildren().add(iconLabel);
        
        // Info Area
        VBox infoBox = new VBox(5);
        String doctorName = (doctor != null) ? "Dr. " + doctor.getFirstName() + " " + doctor.getLastName() : "Médecin Inconnu";
        String specialty = (doctor != null && doctor.getSpecialty() != null) ? doctor.getSpecialty() : "Spécialité non renseignée";
        
        Label lblDoctor = new Label(doctorName);
        lblDoctor.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 18; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        
        Label lblSpecialty = new Label(specialty);
        lblSpecialty.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 14; -fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        
        Label lblDate = new Label("Créé le : " + mf.getCreationdate().toString() + " | Statut : " + mf.getPatientstatus());
        lblDate.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 13; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        
        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(lblDoctor, lblSpecialty, lblDate);
        
        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
        
        // Button
        Button btnVoir = new Button("Consulter");
        btnVoir.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #2D3748; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 10 25; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        btnVoir.setOnAction(e -> {
            Main.showMonDossierMedicalDetail(mf.getIdfile());
        });
        
        card.getChildren().addAll(iconBox, infoBox, spacer, btnVoir);
        
        return card;
    }
}

import entities.Prescription;

public class MesOrdonnancesPatientController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private VBox ordonnancesList;

    private Prescriptionserv prescriptionService = new Prescriptionserv();
    private userServices userService = new userServices();

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        chargerMesOrdonnances();
    }

    private void chargerMesOrdonnances() {
        ordonnancesList.getChildren().clear();
        int patientId = Main.currentUserId;
        
        try {
            List<Prescription> ordonnances = prescriptionService.getByPatientId(patientId);
            
            if (ordonnances.isEmpty()) {
                Label emptyLabel = new Label("Vous n'avez pas encore d'ordonnance enregistrée.");
                emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 16; -fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-padding: 50;");
                ordonnancesList.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
                return;
            }

            List<user> allUsers = userService.getAll();
            
            for (Prescription p : ordonnances) {
                user doctor = findUserById(allUsers, p.getDoctorId());
                ordonnancesList.getChildren().add(createOrdonnanceCard(p, doctor));
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private user findUserById(List<user> users, int id) {
        for (user u : users) {
            if (u.getUserId() == id) return u;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private HBox createOrdonnanceCard(Prescription p, user doctor) {
        HBox card = new HBox(20);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(20));
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 15; -fx-effect: dropshadow(three-pass-box, rgba(0,0,0,0.05), 10, 0, 0, 5);");
        
        // Icon Area
        VBox iconBox = new VBox();
        iconBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        iconBox.setMinWidth(60);
        iconBox.setMinHeight(60);
        iconBox.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FFF5F5; -fx-background-radius: 12;");
        Label iconLabel = new Label("💊");
        iconLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 24;");
        iconBox.getChildren().add(iconLabel);
        
        // Info Area
        VBox infoBox = new VBox(5);
        String doctorName = (doctor != null) ? "Dr. " + doctor.getFirstName() + " " + doctor.getLastName() : "Médecin Inconnu";
        String specialty = (doctor != null && doctor.getSpecialty() != null) ? doctor.getSpecialty() : "Médecin";
        
        Label lblDoctor = new Label(doctorName);
        lblDoctor.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 18; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        
        Label lblSpecialty = new Label(specialty);
        lblSpecialty.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 14; -fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        
        Label lblDate = new Label("Prescrit le : " + p.getPrescriptionDate().toString());
        lblDate.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 13; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        
        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(lblDoctor, lblSpecialty, lblDate);
        
        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
        
        // Button
        Button btnVoir = new Button("Détails");
        btnVoir.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #2D3748; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 10 25; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-cursor: hand;");
        btnVoir.setOnAction(e -> {
            Main.showConsultationPrescriptionPatient(p);
        });
        
        card.getChildren().addAll(iconBox, infoBox, spacer, btnVoir);
        
        return card;
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Prescription;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.event.Event;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.Pagination;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.FlowPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.ImagePattern;
import javafx.scene.shape.Circle;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.Prescriptionserv;
import services.userservDS;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class MesPrescriptionsController {

    @FXML private Pagination pagination;
    
    // Sidebar components
    @FXML private ImageView imgPatient;
    @FXML private Circle circleImage;
    @FXML private Label lblPatientName;

    private final int ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 4;
    private List<Prescription> allPrescriptions = new ArrayList<>();
    private Prescriptionserv prescriptionService = new Prescriptionserv();
    private userservDS userService = new userservDS();
    private int currentPatientId;

    public void chargerPrescriptionsPatient(int patientId) {
        this.currentPatientId = patientId;
        try {
            allPrescriptions = prescriptionService.getByPatientId(patientId);
            updatePagination();
            chargerSidebar(patientId);
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void chargerSidebar(int patientId) {
        userDS user = userService.getById(patientId);
        if (user != null) {
            String fullName = user.getFirstName() + " " + user.getLastName();
            lblPatientName.setText(fullName);
            
            String photoPath = user.getUserphoto();
            if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    Image image = null;
                    File file = new File(photoPath);
                    if (file.exists()) {
                        image = new Image(file.toURI().toString());
                    } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                        image = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath));
                    }
                    
                    if (image != null) {
                        imgPatient.setImage(image);
                        circleImage.setFill(new ImagePattern(image));
                        imgPatient.setVisible(false);
                    }
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    System.err.println("Erreur photo sidebar: " + e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void updatePagination() {
        int pageCount = (int) Math.ceil((double) allPrescriptions.size() / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
        pagination.setPageCount(pageCount > 0 ? pageCount : 1);
        pagination.setPageFactory(this::createPage);
    }

    private Node createPage(int pageIndex) {
        FlowPane flowPane = new FlowPane(20, 20);
        flowPane.setPadding(new Insets(10));
        flowPane.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        
        int fromIndex = pageIndex * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
        int toIndex = Math.min(fromIndex + ITEMS_PER_PAGE, allPrescriptions.size());
        
        if (fromIndex < allPrescriptions.size()) {
            for (int i = fromIndex; i < toIndex; i++) {
                Prescription p = allPrescriptions.get(i);
                flowPane.getChildren().add(createPrescriptionCard(p));
            }
        }
        
        return flowPane;
    }

    private VBox createPrescriptionCard(Prescription p) {
        VBox card = new VBox(12);
        card.getStyleClass().add("patient-card");
        card.setPrefWidth(240);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(15));
        card.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_CENTER);

        Label lblDate = new Label("📅 " + p.getPrescriptionDate().toString());
        lblDate.getStyleClass().add("card-badge");
        
        Label iconLabel = new Label("💊");
        iconLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 40;");

        VBox infoBox = new VBox(5);
        infoBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        
        Label lblTitle = new Label("Ordonnance");
        lblTitle.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        
        Label lblDetail = new Label(p.getPrescription());
        lblDetail.setWrapText(true);
        lblDetail.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-font-size: 13;");
        lblDetail.setMaxHeight(60);

        Label lblDuree = new Label("⏳ Durée : " + p.getTreatmentDuration());
        lblDuree.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 11;");

        Button btnVoir = new Button("👁 Voir");
        btnVoir.getStyleClass().add("btn-card-action");
        btnVoir.setOnAction(e -> consulterPrescription(p));

        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(lblTitle, lblDetail, lblDuree, btnVoir);

        card.getChildren().addAll(lblDate, iconLabel, infoBox);
        return card;
    }

    private void consulterPrescription(Prescription p) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/ConsultationPrescriptionPatient.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            ConsultationPrescriptionPatientController controller = loader.getController();
            controller.setPrescriptionData(p);
            Stage stage = (Stage) pagination.getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setTitle("Consultation Ordonnance #" + p.getPrescriptionId());
            stage.setMaximized(true);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerAuDashboard(Event event) {
        retourDashboard(new ActionEvent(event.getSource(), null));
    }

    @FXML
    public void ouvrirMonDossier(Event event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/MonDossierMedical.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            MonDossierMedicalController controller = loader.getController();
            controller.chargerDonneesPatient(currentPatientId);
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setTitle("Mon Dossier Médical");
            stage.setMaximized(true);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void retourDashboard(ActionEvent event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/DashboardPatient.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.event;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.eventServ;
import utils.RoleGuard;

import java.io.IOException;

public class Modifierevent {

    @FXML private TextField        tfTitre;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cbType;
    @FXML private TextArea         taDescription;
    @FXML private DatePicker       dpDate;
    @FXML private TextField        tfTime;
    @FXML private TextField        tfLieu;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cbStatus;
    @FXML private TextField        tfCapacite;
    @FXML private TextField        tfOrganisateur;
    @FXML private Label            lblMessage;

    private final eventServ service = new eventServ();
    private event eventAModifier;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // [SECURITY] Layer 2 — controller guard: redirect non-admin after scene is ready
        Platform.runLater(() -> {
            if (RoleGuard.denyIfNotAdmin()) {
                tests.Main.showPatientEvents();
            }
        });

        cbType.getItems().addAll("Conférence", "Formation", "Séminaire", "Atelier");
        cbStatus.getItems().addAll("Planifié", "En cours", "Terminé", "Annulé");
    }

    public void setEvent(event e) {
        this.eventAModifier = e;
        tfTitre.setText(e.getTitre());
        cbType.setValue(e.getType());
        taDescription.setText(e.getDescription());
        dpDate.setValue(e.getDate());
        tfTime.setText(e.getTime());
        tfLieu.setText(e.getLieu());
        cbStatus.setValue(e.getStatus());
        if (tfCapacite != null) tfCapacite.setText(String.valueOf(e.getCapaciteMax()));
        if (tfOrganisateur != null) tfOrganisateur.setText("—");
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleModifier() {
        // [SECURITY] Layer 2 — action-level guard
        if (RoleGuard.denyIfNotAdmin()) return;
        if (tfTitre.getText().isEmpty() || cbType.getValue() == null
                || dpDate.getValue() == null || tfLieu.getText().isEmpty()) {
            showMessage("⚠️ Champs obligatoires manquants.", "#DC2626");
            return;
        }

        eventAModifier.setTitre(tfTitre.getText().trim());
        eventAModifier.setType(cbType.getValue());
        eventAModifier.setDescription(taDescription.getText().trim());
        eventAModifier.setDate(dpDate.getValue());
        eventAModifier.setTime(tfTime.getText().trim());
        eventAModifier.setLieu(tfLieu.getText().trim());
        eventAModifier.setStatus(cbStatus.getValue());
        
        try {
            int cap = Integer.parseInt(tfCapacite.getText().trim());
            eventAModifier.setCapaciteMax(cap);
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            // keep old value or set to 0
        }

        service.modifier(eventAModifier);
        showMessage("✅ Événement modifié avec succès !", "#16A34A");
        handleRetour();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleRetour() {
        tests.Main.showAdminEvents();
    }

    private void showMessage(String msg, String color) {
        if (lblMessage != null) {
            lblMessage.setText(msg);
            lblMessage.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: " + color + ";");
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.userDS;
import entities.medicalFile;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import services.userservDS;
