import services.medicalFileServ;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import services.VoskService;
import java.io.IOException;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

public class ModifiermedicalFile {

    // ================= FXML COMPONENTS =================
    @FXML private TextField searchPatient;
    @FXML private ListView<userDS> listPatients;
    @FXML private Label lblPatientName;
    @FXML private TextField idfile;
    @FXML private TextField patientId;
    @FXML private DatePicker creationdate;
    @FXML private DatePicker lastvisit;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> patientstatus;
    @FXML private TextArea generalhistory;
    @FXML private TextArea antecedents;
    @FXML private TextArea allergies;
    @FXML private TextArea followup;
    @FXML private ImageView imgDoctor;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorName;
    @FXML private Label lblIdFile;
    @FXML private Label lblPatientId;

    // ================= SERVICES (non-statiques) =================
    private userservDS userService;        // ✅ Non-static
    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService;  // ✅ Non-static

    // ================= DATA =================
    private ObservableList<userDS> patientsList;
    private userDS selectedPatient;
    private medicalFile currentMedicalFile;
    
    private boolean isRecording = false;
    private Button activeMicButton = null;

    // ✅ Constructeur - initialisation des services
    public ModifiermedicalFile() {
        userService = new userservDS();
        medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();  // ✅ Création de l'instance
        patientsList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Configurer le ComboBox
        ObservableList<String> statuses = FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "stable", "en amélioration", "critique"
        );
        patientstatus.setItems(statuses);

        setupSearchListener();
        setupSelectionListener();
        setupValidationListeners();
        chargerProfilMedecin();
    }

    private void chargerProfilMedecin() {
        if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Dr. Saif Gharbi");
        List<userDS> docs = userService.searchAllByNameOrSurname("Saif");
        for (userDS d : docs) {
            if (d.getFirstName().equalsIgnoreCase("Saif") && d.getLastName().equalsIgnoreCase("Gharbi")) {
                String photoPath = d.getUserphoto();
                if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty() && imgDoctor != null) {
                    try {
                        java.io.File file = new java.io.File(photoPath);
                        if (file.exists()) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                        } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath)));
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {}
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    private void setupSearchListener() {
        searchPatient.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            if (newVal != null && !newVal.trim().isEmpty()) {
                List<userDS> result = userService.searchByNameOrSurname(newVal);
                patientsList.setAll(result);
                listPatients.setItems(patientsList);
                listPatients.setVisible(true);
            } else {
                listPatients.getItems().clear();
                listPatients.setVisible(false);
            }
        });
    }

    private void setupSelectionListener() {
        listPatients.setOnMouseClicked(event -> {
            selectedPatient = listPatients.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
            if (selectedPatient != null) {
                searchPatient.setText(selectedPatient.getFullName());
                listPatients.setVisible(false);
                listPatients.getItems().clear();
                chargerDossierMedical(selectedPatient.getUserId());
            }
        });
    }

    private void chargerDossierMedical(int patientId) {
        try {
            List<medicalFile> dossiers = medicalFileService.getByPatientId(patientId);

            if (dossiers != null && !dossiers.isEmpty()) {
                currentMedicalFile = dossiers.get(0);
                remplirChamps(currentMedicalFile);
            } else {
                showAlert("Information", "Aucun dossier trouvé");
                clearFields();
                this.patientId.setText(String.valueOf(patientId));
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void remplirChamps(medicalFile mf) {
        idfile.setText(String.valueOf(mf.getIdfile()));
        patientId.setText(String.valueOf(mf.getPatientId()));
        if (lblIdFile != null) lblIdFile.setText(String.valueOf(mf.getIdfile()));
        if (lblPatientId != null) lblPatientId.setText(String.valueOf(mf.getPatientId()));
        creationdate.setValue(mf.getCreationdate());
        lastvisit.setValue(mf.getLastvisit());
        generalhistory.setText(mf.getGeneralhistory());
        antecedents.setText(mf.getAntecedents());
        allergies.setText(mf.getAllergies());
        followup.setText(mf.getFollowup());

        if (mf.getPatientstatus() != null) {
            patientstatus.setValue(mf.getPatientstatus());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Méthode publique pour pré-remplir le formulaire depuis la vue consultation.
     */
    public void prechargerDossier(medicalFile mf, userDS patient) {
        currentMedicalFile = mf;
        selectedPatient = patient;
        if (searchPatient != null && patient != null) {
            searchPatient.setText(patient.getFullName());
        }
        if (lblPatientName != null && patient != null) {
            lblPatientName.setText(patient.getFullName());
        }
        remplirChamps(mf);
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleVoiceInput(ActionEvent event) {
        Button micButton = (Button) event.getSource();
        String targetId = (String) micButton.getUserData();
        
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-background-radius: 10;");
            isRecording = false;
            activeMicButton = null;
        } else {
            if (!VoskService.getInstance().isReady()) {
                showAlert("Modèle Vocal Manquant", "Le dossier 'model' contenant le modèle Vosk est introuvable à la racine.");
                return;
            }
            
            isRecording = true;
            activeMicButton = micButton;
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-text-fill: white;");
            
            VoskService.getInstance().startListening(text -> {
                Platform.runLater(() -> {
                    appendToField(targetId, text);
                });
            });
        }
    }

    private void appendToField(String id, String text) {
        if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty()) return;
        String formattedText = text + " ";
        switch (id) {
            case "generalhistory": generalhistory.appendText(formattedText); break;
            case "antecedents": antecedents.appendText(formattedText); break;
            case "allergies": allergies.appendText(formattedText); break;
            case "followup": followup.appendText(formattedText); break;
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void modifierDossier() {
        if (currentMedicalFile == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Aucun dossier chargé");
            return;
        }

        if (!validateInputs()) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            // Mettre à jour l'objet
            currentMedicalFile.setCreationdate(creationdate.getValue());
            currentMedicalFile.setLastvisit(lastvisit.getValue());
            currentMedicalFile.setPatientstatus(patientstatus.getValue());
            currentMedicalFile.setGeneralhistory(generalhistory.getText());
            currentMedicalFile.setAntecedents(antecedents.getText());
            currentMedicalFile.setAllergies(allergies.getText());
            currentMedicalFile.setFollowup(followup.getText());

            // ✅ Appel correct sur l'instance (non-statique)
            medicalFileService.update(currentMedicalFile);

            showAlert("Succès", "Dossier médical modifié avec succès !");
            
            // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
            tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la redirection: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private boolean validateInputs() {
        clearValidationErrors();
        boolean valid = true;
        Node firstInvalidField = null;

        if (selectedPatient == null) {
            markInvalid(searchPatient);
            firstInvalidField = searchPatient;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (creationdate.getValue() == null) {
            markInvalid(creationdate);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = creationdate;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (lastvisit.getValue() == null) {
            markInvalid(lastvisit);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = lastvisit;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (patientstatus.getValue() == null) {
            markInvalid(patientstatus);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = patientstatus;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(generalhistory)) {
            markInvalid(generalhistory);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = generalhistory;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(antecedents)) {
            markInvalid(antecedents);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = antecedents;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(allergies)) {
            markInvalid(allergies);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = allergies;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (isBlank(followup)) {
            markInvalid(followup);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = followup;
            valid = false;
        }

        if (!valid) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez remplir tous les champs obligatoires.");
            firstInvalidField.requestFocus();
            return false;
        }
        if (selectedPatient == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez sélectionner un patient");
            return false;
        }
        if (creationdate.getValue() == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Date de création obligatoire");
            return false;
        }
        if (patientstatus.getValue() == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Sélectionnez un statut");
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void setupValidationListeners() {
        searchPatient.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            if (selectedPatient != null && !selectedPatient.getFullName().equals(newVal)) {
                selectedPatient = null;
            }
            clearInvalid(searchPatient);
        });
        creationdate.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(creationdate));
        lastvisit.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(lastvisit));
        patientstatus.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(patientstatus));
        generalhistory.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(generalhistory));
        antecedents.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(antecedents));
        allergies.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(allergies));
        followup.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(followup));
    }

    private boolean isBlank(TextInputControl field) {
        return field.getText() == null || field.getText().trim().isEmpty();
    }

    private void markInvalid(Node field) {
        if (!field.getStyleClass().contains("error-field")) {
            field.getStyleClass().add("error-field");
        }
    }

    private void clearInvalid(Node field) {
        field.getStyleClass().remove("error-field");
    }

    private void clearValidationErrors() {
        clearInvalid(searchPatient);
        clearInvalid(creationdate);
        clearInvalid(lastvisit);
        clearInvalid(patientstatus);
        clearInvalid(generalhistory);
        clearInvalid(antecedents);
        clearInvalid(allergies);
        clearInvalid(followup);
    }

    @FXML
    public void retour(ActionEvent event) {
        allerGererPatients(event);
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerGererPatients(ActionEvent event) {
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
        }
        // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerAjouterDossier(ActionEvent event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/AjoutermedicalFile.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.setTitle("Ajouter dossier médical");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void clearFields() {
        clearValidationErrors();
        idfile.clear();
        patientId.clear();
        creationdate.setValue(null);
        lastvisit.setValue(null);
        patientstatus.setValue(null);
        generalhistory.clear();
        antecedents.clear();
        allergies.clear();
        followup.clear();
        currentMedicalFile = null;
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
    public void prechargerPatient(userDS patient) {
        this.selectedPatient = patient;
        if (lblPatientName != null) {
            lblPatientName.setText(patient.getFullName());
        }
        chargerDossierMedical(patient.getUserId());
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Participant;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.Participantserv;

import java.io.IOException;

public class Modifierparticipant {

    @FXML private TextField tfNom;
    @FXML private TextField tfPrenom;
    @FXML private TextField tfEmail;
    @FXML private TextField tfTelephone;
    @FXML private Label     lblMessage;

    private final Participantserv service = new Participantserv();
    private Participant participantAModifier;

    /**
     * Appelé depuis GetAllparticipant pour pré-remplir les champs.
     */
    public void setParticipant(Participant p) {
        this.participantAModifier = p;
        tfNom.setText(p.getNom());
        tfPrenom.setText(p.getPrenom());
        tfEmail.setText(p.getEmail());
        tfTelephone.setText(p.getTelephone());
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleModifier() {
        if (tfNom.getText().isEmpty() || tfPrenom.getText().isEmpty() ||
            tfEmail.getText().isEmpty() || tfTelephone.getText().isEmpty()) {
            showMessage("⚠️ Tous les champs sont obligatoires.", "red");
            return;
        }
        if (!tfEmail.getText().contains("@")) {
            showMessage("⚠️ Email invalide.", "red");
            return;
        }

        participantAModifier.setNom(tfNom.getText().trim());
        participantAModifier.setPrenom(tfPrenom.getText().trim());
        participantAModifier.setEmail(tfEmail.getText().trim());
        participantAModifier.setTelephone(tfTelephone.getText().trim());

        service.modifier(participantAModifier);
        showMessage("✅ Participant modifié avec succès !", "green");
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleRetour() {
        tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants();
    }

    private void showMessage(String msg, String color) {
        lblMessage.setText(msg);
        lblMessage.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: " + color + ";");
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Prescription;
import entities.medicalFile;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.Prescriptionserv;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import services.userservDS;
import services.VoskService;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import java.io.File;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

public class ModifierPrescriptionController {

    @FXML private ComboBox<userDS> comboPatient;
    @FXML private DatePicker datePrescription;
    @FXML private TextField txtDuree;
    @FXML private TextArea txtPrescription;

    private Prescriptionserv prescriptionService;
    private userservDS userService;
    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService;
    private Prescription prescriptionEnCours;
    private boolean isRecording = false;

    public ModifierPrescriptionController() {
        prescriptionService = new Prescriptionserv();
        userService = new userservDS();
        medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Charger la liste des patients
        List<userDS> patients = userService.getAll();
        comboPatient.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(patients));
        
        // Custom display pour le ComboBox
        comboPatient.setCellFactory(lv -> new ListCell<userDS>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(userDS item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                setText(empty ? null : item.getFullName());
            }
        });
        comboPatient.setButtonCell(new ListCell<userDS>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(userDS item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                setText(empty ? null : item.getFullName());
            }
        });
        setupValidationListeners();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleVoiceInput(ActionEvent event) {
        Button micButton = (Button) event.getSource();
        String targetId = (String) micButton.getUserData();
        
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-background-radius: 10;");
            isRecording = false;
        } else {
            if (!VoskService.getInstance().isReady()) {
                showAlert("Modèle Vocal Manquant", "Le dossier 'model' contenant le modèle Vosk est introuvable à la racine.");
                return;
            }
            
            isRecording = true;
            micButton.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-text-fill: white;");
            
            VoskService.getInstance().startListening(text -> {
                Platform.runLater(() -> {
                    appendToField(targetId, text);
                });
            });
        }
    }

    private void appendToField(String id, String text) {
        if (text == null || text.trim().isEmpty()) return;
        String formattedText = text + " ";
        if ("txtDuree".equals(id)) {
            txtDuree.appendText(formattedText);
        } else if ("txtPrescription".equals(id)) {
            txtPrescription.appendText(formattedText);
        }
    }

    public void prechargerDonnees(Prescription p) {
        this.prescriptionEnCours = p;
        datePrescription.setValue(p.getPrescriptionDate());
        txtDuree.setText(p.getTreatmentDuration());
        txtPrescription.setText(p.getPrescription());
        
        // Sélectionner le bon patient
        for (userDS patient : comboPatient.getItems()) {
            if (patient.getUserId() == p.getPatientId()) {
                comboPatient.setValue(patient);
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void enregistrerModifications(ActionEvent event) {
        userDS selectedPatient = comboPatient.getValue();
        LocalDate date = datePrescription.getValue();
        String duree = txtDuree.getText();
        String detail = txtPrescription.getText();

        if (!validateInputs(selectedPatient, date, duree, detail)) {
            return;
        }

        prescriptionEnCours.setPrescriptionDate(date);
        prescriptionEnCours.setPrescription(detail);
        prescriptionEnCours.setTreatmentDuration(duree);
        prescriptionEnCours.setPatientId(selectedPatient.getUserId());

        try {
            // Mettre à jour l'idfile si le patient a changé
            List<medicalFile> dossiers = medicalFileService.getByPatientId(selectedPatient.getUserId());
            if (dossiers.isEmpty()) {
                showAlert("Erreur", "Le patient sélectionné n'a pas de dossier médical.");
                return;
            }
            prescriptionEnCours.setIdfile(dossiers.get(0).getIdfile());

            prescriptionService.update(prescriptionEnCours);
            showAlert("Succès", "La prescription a été modifiée avec succès !");
            retourner(event);
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la modification : " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private boolean validateInputs(userDS selectedPatient, LocalDate date, String duree, String detail) {
        clearValidationErrors();
        boolean valid = true;
        Node firstInvalidField = null;

        if (selectedPatient == null) {
            markInvalid(comboPatient);
            firstInvalidField = comboPatient;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (date == null) {
            markInvalid(datePrescription);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = datePrescription;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (duree == null || duree.trim().isEmpty()) {
            markInvalid(txtDuree);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = txtDuree;
            valid = false;
        }
        if (detail == null || detail.trim().isEmpty()) {
            markInvalid(txtPrescription);
            if (firstInvalidField == null) firstInvalidField = txtPrescription;
            valid = false;
        }

        if (!valid) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez remplir tous les champs obligatoires.");
            firstInvalidField.requestFocus();
        }

        return valid;
    }

    private void setupValidationListeners() {
        comboPatient.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(comboPatient));
        datePrescription.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(datePrescription));
        txtDuree.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(txtDuree));
        txtPrescription.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> clearInvalid(txtPrescription));
    }

    private void markInvalid(Node field) {
        if (!field.getStyleClass().contains("error-field")) {
            field.getStyleClass().add("error-field");
        }
    }

    private void clearInvalid(Node field) {
        field.getStyleClass().remove("error-field");
    }

    private void clearValidationErrors() {
        clearInvalid(comboPatient);
        clearInvalid(datePrescription);
        clearInvalid(txtDuree);
        clearInvalid(txtPrescription);
    }

    @FXML
    public void retourner(javafx.event.Event event) {
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
        }
        tests.Main.showDoctorDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerGererPatients(javafx.event.Event event) {
        if (isRecording) {
            VoskService.getInstance().stopListening();
        }
        // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.userDS;
import entities.medicalFile;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.medicalFileServ;

import java.util.List;

public class MonDossierController {

    // ================= FXML COMPONENTS =================
    @FXML private TextField txtNom;
    @FXML private TextField txtPrenom;
    @FXML private TextField txtPatientId;
    @FXML private TextField txtIdFile;
    @FXML private TextField txtCreationDate;
    @FXML private TextField txtLastVisit;
    @FXML private TextField txtStatus;
    @FXML private TextArea txtGeneralHistory;
    @FXML private TextArea txtAntecedents;
    @FXML private TextArea txtAllergies;

    // ================= SERVICES =================
    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService;

    // ================= PATIENT CONNECTÉ =================
    private userDS patientConnecte;

    public MonDossierController() {
        medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
    }

    // Cette méthode est appelée depuis le dashboard du patient
    public void setPatientConnecte(userDS patient) {
        this.patientConnecte = patient;
        chargerDossier();
    }

    private void chargerDossier() {
        if (patientConnecte == null) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Aucun patient connecté");
            return;
        }

        // Afficher les informations du patient
        txtNom.setText(patientConnecte.getLastName());
        txtPrenom.setText(patientConnecte.getFirstName());
        txtPatientId.setText(String.valueOf(patientConnecte.getUserId()));

        try {
            // Récupérer le dossier médical du patient
            List<medicalFile> dossiers = medicalFileService.getByPatientId(patientConnecte.getUserId());

            if (dossiers == null || dossiers.isEmpty()) {
                showAlert("Information", "Aucun dossier médical trouvé");
                return;
            }

            // Prendre le dossier le plus récent
            medicalFile mf = dossiers.get(0);
            afficherDossier(mf);

        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Problème lors du chargement: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void afficherDossier(medicalFile mf) {
        txtIdFile.setText(String.valueOf(mf.getIdfile()));

        txtCreationDate.setText(mf.getCreationdate() != null ? mf.getCreationdate().toString() : "Non renseigné");
        txtLastVisit.setText(mf.getLastvisit() != null ? mf.getLastvisit().toString() : "Non renseigné");

        // Traduire le statut en français
        String status = mf.getPatientstatus();
        if (status != null) {
            switch (status) {
                case "stable":
                    txtStatus.setText("Stable");
                    break;
                case "en amélioration":
                    txtStatus.setText("En amélioration");
                    break;
                case "critique":
                    txtStatus.setText("Critique");
                    break;
                default:
                    txtStatus.setText(status);
            }
        } else {
            txtStatus.setText("Non renseigné");
        }

        txtGeneralHistory.setText(mf.getGeneralhistory() != null ? mf.getGeneralhistory() : "Aucun historique");
        txtAntecedents.setText(mf.getAntecedents() != null ? mf.getAntecedents() : "Aucun antécédent");
        txtAllergies.setText(mf.getAllergies() != null ? mf.getAllergies() : "Aucune allergie");
    }

    @FXML
    public void retourDashboard(ActionEvent event) {
        try {
            // Retourner au dashboard du patient
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/DashboardPatient.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();

            DashboardPatientController controller = loader.getController();
            controller.setPatientConnecte(patientConnecte);

            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            Scene scene = new Scene(root);
            scene.getStylesheets().add(getClass().getResource("/style.css").toExternalForm());
            stage.setScene(scene);
            stage.setTitle("SHIFAA - Dashboard Patient");
            stage.show();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible de retourner: " + e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void deconnexion(ActionEvent event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/PageConnexion.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setTitle("SHIFAA - Connexion");
            stage.show();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", e.getMessage());
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.medicalFile;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.event.Event;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.paint.ImagePattern;
import javafx.scene.shape.Circle;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import services.userservDS;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;

public class MonDossierMedicalController {

    @FXML private Label lblStatus;
    @FXML private Label lblLastVisit;
    @FXML private Label lblCreationDate;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorName;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorSpeciality;
    @FXML private Label lblHistory;
    @FXML private Label lblAntecedents;
    @FXML private Label lblAllergies;
    @FXML private Label lblFollowup;
    
    // Sidebar components
    @FXML private ImageView imgPatient;
    @FXML private Circle circleImage;
    @FXML private Label lblPatientName;

    private medicalFileServ medicalService = new medicalFileServ();
    private userservDS userService = new userservDS();
    private int currentPatientId;

    public void chargerDonneesPatient(int patientId) {
        this.currentPatientId = patientId;
        
        // Charger le premier dossier par défaut
        List<medicalFile> dossiers = medicalService.getByPatientId(patientId);
        if (dossiers != null && !dossiers.isEmpty()) {
            afficherDossier(dossiers.get(0));
        } else {
            afficherDossierVide();
        }
        
        // Charger la sidebar
        chargerSidebar(patientId);
    }

    public void chargerDossierSpecifique(int idfile) {
        medicalFile mf = medicalService.getById(idfile);
        if (mf != null) {
            this.currentPatientId = mf.getPatientId();
            afficherDossier(mf);
            chargerSidebar(currentPatientId);
        }
    }

    private void afficherDossier(medicalFile mf) {
        if (lblStatus != null) {
            String status = mf.getPatientstatus() != null ? mf.getPatientstatus().toLowerCase() : "stable";
            lblStatus.setText(status.toUpperCase());
            
            // Coloration dynamique du badge de statut
            if (status.contains("stable")) {
                lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #C6F6D5; -fx-text-fill: #22543D; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
            } else if (status.contains("amelioration") || status.contains("amélioration")) {
                lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FEFCBF; -fx-text-fill: #744210; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
            } else if (status.contains("critique")) {
                lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FED7D7; -fx-text-fill: #822727; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
            } else {
                lblStatus.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-padding: 8 15; -fx-background-radius: 20;");
            }
        }
        
        if (lblLastVisit != null) lblLastVisit.setText(mf.getLastvisit() != null ? mf.getLastvisit().toString() : "Aucune visite enregistrée");
        if (lblCreationDate != null) lblCreationDate.setText(mf.getCreationdate() != null ? mf.getCreationdate().toString() : "N/A");
        if (lblHistory != null) lblHistory.setText(mf.getGeneralhistory() != null && !mf.getGeneralhistory().isEmpty() ? mf.getGeneralhistory() : "Aucune information");
        if (lblAntecedents != null) lblAntecedents.setText(mf.getAntecedents() != null && !mf.getAntecedents().isEmpty() ? mf.getAntecedents() : "Aucun antécédent déclaré");
        if (lblAllergies != null) lblAllergies.setText(mf.getAllergies() != null && !mf.getAllergies().isEmpty() ? mf.getAllergies() : "Aucune allergie connue");
        if (lblFollowup != null) lblFollowup.setText(mf.getFollowup() != null && !mf.getFollowup().isEmpty() ? mf.getFollowup() : "Pas de suivi particulier");
        
        // Charger le médecin traitant
        chargerInfosMedecin(mf.getDoctorId());
    }

    private void afficherDossierVide() {
        lblStatus.setText("-");
        lblLastVisit.setText("-");
        lblCreationDate.setText("-");
        lblHistory.setText("Aucun dossier trouvé.");
        lblAntecedents.setText("-");
        lblAllergies.setText("-");
        lblFollowup.setText("-");
        chargerInfosMedecin(0);
    }
    private void chargerInfosMedecin(int doctorId) {
        try (java.sql.Connection con = utils.DatabaseConnection.getInstance().getConnection();
             java.sql.Statement stmt = con.createStatement()) {
            
            java.sql.ResultSet rs = null;
            if (doctorId > 0) {
                // Chercher le médecin associé à ce dossier
                rs = stmt.executeQuery("SELECT * FROM user WHERE userId = " + doctorId + " AND (role = 'doctor' OR role = 'médecin')");
            }
            
            if (rs == null || !rs.next()) {
                // S'il n'a pas de médecin associé (ou ancien dossier), on prend le premier médecin du système
                rs = stmt.executeQuery("SELECT * FROM user WHERE role = 'doctor' OR role = 'médecin' LIMIT 1");
                if (!rs.next()) {
                    if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Non assigné");
                    if (lblDoctorSpeciality != null) lblDoctorSpeciality.setText("-");
                    return;
                }
            }
            
            String prenom = rs.getString("firstName");
            String nom = rs.getString("lastName");
            String specialite = null;
            
            try { specialite = rs.getString("specialty"); } catch(Exception e) {}
            if (specialite == null) {
                try { specialite = rs.getString("speciality"); } catch(Exception e) {}
            }
            
            if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Dr. " + prenom + " " + nom);
            if (lblDoctorSpeciality != null) lblDoctorSpeciality.setText(specialite != null && !specialite.trim().isEmpty() ? specialite : "Médecin Généraliste");
            
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Erreur de chargement");
        }
    }

    private void chargerSidebar(int patientId) {
        userDS user = userService.getById(patientId);
        if (user != null) {
            String fullName = user.getFirstName() + " " + user.getLastName();
            if (lblPatientName != null) lblPatientName.setText(fullName);
            
            String photoPath = user.getUserphoto();
            if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    Image image = null;
                    File file = new File(photoPath);
                    if (file.exists()) {
                        image = new Image(file.toURI().toString());
                    } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                        image = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath));
                    }
                    
                    if (image != null) {
                        if (imgPatient != null) imgPatient.setImage(image);
                        if (circleImage != null) circleImage.setFill(new ImagePattern(image));
                        if (imgPatient != null) imgPatient.setVisible(false);
                    }
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    System.err.println("Erreur photo sidebar: " + e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerAuDashboard(Event event) {
        retourDashboard(new ActionEvent(event.getSource(), null));
    }

    @FXML
    public void ouvrirMesPrescriptions(Event event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/MesPrescriptions.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            MesPrescriptionsController controller = loader.getController();
            controller.chargerPrescriptionsPatient(currentPatientId);
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setTitle("Mes Ordonnances");
            stage.setMaximized(true);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void retourDashboard(ActionEvent event) {
        tests.Main.showPatientMedicalFiles();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Participant;
import entities.Reservation;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.LoyaltyService;
import services.ParticipantStatsServ;
import services.ReservationServ;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Vue profil détaillée d'un participant :
 * ■ Score fidélité + badge + priorité
 * ■ Statistiques comportementales
 * ■ Historique réservations
 * ■ Notifications
 */
public class ParticipantProfileController {

    @FXML private Label lblNom;
    @FXML private Label lblEmail;
    @FXML private Label lblTel;
    @FXML private Label lblAvatar;

    // Fidélité
    @FXML private Label       lblBadge;
    @FXML private Label       lblPriority;
    @FXML private Label       lblScore;
    @FXML private ProgressBar pbScore;
    @FXML private Label       lblProchainBadge;

    // Stats
    @FXML private Label lblTotalResa;
    @FXML private Label lblConfirme;
    @FXML private Label lblAnnule;
    @FXML private Label lblPresence;
    @FXML private Label lblTauxPresence;

    // Listes
    @FXML private VBox reservationsPane;
    @FXML private VBox notificationsPane;

    private Participant participant;
    private final LoyaltyService        loyaltyServ = new LoyaltyService();
    private final ParticipantStatsServ  statsServ   = new ParticipantStatsServ();
    private final ReservationServ       resServ     = new ReservationServ();

    private static final DateTimeFormatter FMT = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm");

    // ── Entry point — called from GetAllparticipant ───────────────────────────
    public void setParticipant(Participant p) {
        this.participant = p;
        loadAll();
    }

    private void loadAll() {
        // Enrich with live loyalty data
        loyaltyServ.enrich(participant);
        fillHeader();
        fillLoyalty();

        // Load heavy data in background
        new Thread(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> stats = statsServ.getProfileStats(participant.getId());
            List<Reservation>   resas = resServ.getByParticipant(participant.getId());
            List<Map<String, Object>> notifs = statsServ.getNotifications(participant.getId());
            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                fillStats(stats);
                fillReservations(resas);
                fillNotifications(notifs);
            });
        }, "Profile-Loader").start();
    }

    // ── Header ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void fillHeader() {
        String fullName = participant.getNom() + " " + participant.getPrenom();
        if (lblNom    != null) lblNom.setText(fullName);
        if (lblEmail  != null) lblEmail.setText(participant.getEmail());
        if (lblTel    != null) lblTel.setText(participant.getTelephone() != null ? participant.getTelephone() : "—");
        if (lblAvatar != null) {
            lblAvatar.setText(participant.getNom().substring(0, 1).toUpperCase());
            String avatarStyle = "-fx-background-color:" + getAvatarColor() + "; " +
                "-fx-background-radius:40; -fx-text-fill:white; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                "-fx-font-size:22px; -fx-min-width:60; -fx-min-height:60; -fx-alignment:center;";
            lblAvatar.setStyle(avatarStyle);
        }
    }

    // ── Loyalty section ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void fillLoyalty() {
        int score = participant.getLoyaltyPoints();
        Map<String, Object> progress = loyaltyServ.getBadgeProgress(participant.getId());

        if (lblBadge != null) {
            lblBadge.setText(participant.getBadgeEmoji() + "  " + participant.getBadge().name());
            lblBadge.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:" + participant.getBadgeColor() +
                              "; -fx-font-size:16px; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        }
        if (lblPriority != null) {
            lblPriority.setText(participant.getPriorityEmoji() + "  " + participant.getPriorityLevel().name());
            lblPriority.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:" + participant.getPriorityColor() +
                                 "; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-font-size:13px;");
        }
        if (lblScore != null) lblScore.setText(score + " pts");

        if (pbScore != null) {
            int pct = (int) progress.getOrDefault("progression_pct", 0);
            pbScore.setProgress(Math.min(1.0, pct / 100.0));
            String color = score >= 150 ? "#d97706" : score >= 50 ? "#6b7280" : "#b45309";
            pbScore.setStyle("-fx-accent:" + color + ";");
        }
        if (lblProchainBadge != null) {
            String next = (String) progress.getOrDefault("prochain_badge", "MAX");
            int manque  = (int)    progress.getOrDefault("pts_manquants",   0);
            lblProchainBadge.setText("MAX".equals(next)
                ? "🏆 Badge maximum atteint !"
                : manque + " pts pour " + next);
        }
    }

    // ── Stats section ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void fillStats(Map<String, Object> stats) {
        setLbl(lblTotalResa,   stats, "total_reservations");
        setLbl(lblConfirme,    stats, "nb_confirmees");
        setLbl(lblAnnule,      stats, "nb_annulees");
        setLbl(lblPresence,    stats, "nb_presences_reelles");
        setLbl(lblTauxPresence,stats, "taux_presence_reelle");
    }

    private void setLbl(Label lbl, Map<String, Object> m, String key) {
        if (lbl != null) lbl.setText(String.valueOf(m.getOrDefault(key, "0")));
    }

    // ── Reservation history ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void fillReservations(List<Reservation> resas) {
        if (reservationsPane == null) return;
        reservationsPane.getChildren().clear();

        if (resas.isEmpty()) {
            reservationsPane.getChildren().add(emptyLabel("📭 Aucune réservation"));
            return;
        }

        for (Reservation r : resas) {
            reservationsPane.getChildren().add(buildResaRow(r));
        }
    }

    private HBox buildResaRow(Reservation r) {
        String[] sc = getStatusStyle(r.getStatut());
        HBox row = new HBox(12);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        row.setPadding(new Insets(10, 14, 10, 14));
        row.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#ffffff; -fx-background-radius:8; " +
                     "-fx-border-color:" + sc[2] + "; -fx-border-width:0 0 0 4; -fx-border-radius:8; " +
                     "-fx-effect:dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.05),4,0,0,1);");

        Label badge = new Label(sc[0]);
        badge.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + sc[1] + "; -fx-text-fill:" + sc[2] +
                       "; -fx-background-radius:14; -fx-padding:3 9 3 9; -fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        badge.setMinWidth(115);

        VBox info = new VBox(2);
        HBox.setHgrow(info, Priority.ALWAYS);
        Label lEvent = new Label("Événement #" + r.getEventId() + "   |   🎟 " + r.getNombrePlaces() + " place(s)");
        lEvent.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332;");
        Label lDate = new Label("🕐 " + (r.getDateDemande() != null ? r.getDateDemande().format(FMT) : "—"));
        lDate.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#718096;");
        info.getChildren().addAll(lEvent, lDate);

        if (r.getMotifRefus() != null && !r.getMotifRefus().isEmpty()) {
            Label lMotif = new Label("ℹ " + r.getMotifRefus());
            lMotif.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#c53030; -fx-wrap-text:true;");
            lMotif.setWrapText(true);
            info.getChildren().add(lMotif);
        }

        row.getChildren().addAll(badge, info);
        return row;
    }

    // ── Notifications history ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void fillNotifications(List<Map<String, Object>> notifs) {
        if (notificationsPane == null) return;
        notificationsPane.getChildren().clear();

        if (notifs.isEmpty()) {
            notificationsPane.getChildren().add(emptyLabel("🔔 Aucune notification"));
            return;
        }

        for (Map<String, Object> n : notifs) {
            notificationsPane.getChildren().add(buildNotifRow(n));
        }
    }

    private HBox buildNotifRow(Map<String, Object> n) {
        HBox row = new HBox(10);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        row.setPadding(new Insets(10, 14, 10, 14));
        boolean read = Boolean.TRUE.equals(n.get("is_read"));
        row.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + (read ? "#f8fafc" : "#eff6ff") +
                     "; -fx-background-radius:8; -fx-border-color:#e2e8f0; " +
                     "-fx-border-width:1; -fx-border-radius:8;");

        String type = String.valueOf(n.get("type"));
        Label icon = new Label(notifIcon(type));
        icon.setStyle("-fx-font-size:18px;");

        VBox info = new VBox(2);
        HBox.setHgrow(info, Priority.ALWAYS);
        Label sujet = new Label(String.valueOf(n.get("sujet")));
        sujet.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-font-weight:" + (read ? "normal" : "bold") +
                       "; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332;");
        Label date = new Label("📅 " + n.get("sent_at"));
        date.setStyle("-fx-font-size:10px; -fx-text-fill:#718096;");
        info.getChildren().addAll(sujet, date);

        row.getChildren().addAll(icon, info);
        return row;
    }

    // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private String[] getStatusStyle(Reservation.Statut s) {
        switch (s) {
            case CONFIRME:        return new String[]{"✓ CONFIRMÉ",      "#d4edda", "#16a34a"};
            case LISTE_D_ATTENTE: return new String[]{"⏳ ATTENTE",       "#fef3c7", "#d97706"};
            case CHECKED_IN:      return new String[]{"🎯 PRÉSENT",       "#dbeafe", "#1d4ed8"};
            case REFUSE:          return new String[]{"✕ REFUSÉ",         "#fdeaea", "#c53030"};
            case ANNULE:          return new String[]{"↩ ANNULÉ",         "#f3f4f6", "#6b7280"};
            default:              return new String[]{"◷ EN ATTENTE",     "#fef9c3", "#b45309"};
        }
    }

    private String notifIcon(String type) {
        switch (type) {
            case "CONFIRMATION":   return "✅";
            case "REFUS":          return "❌";
            case "WAITLIST_PROMO": return "🎉";
            case "REMINDER":       return "⏰";
            default:               return "🔔";
        }
    }

    private String getAvatarColor() {
        switch (participant.getPriorityLevel()) {
            case VIP:    return "#7c3aed";
            case HIGH:   return "#dc2626";
            case MEDIUM: return "#d97706";
            default:     return "#1e3a5f";
        }
    }

    private Label emptyLabel(String text) {
        Label l = new Label(text);
        l.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#a0aec0; -fx-font-size:13px; -fx-padding:20;");
        return l;
    }

    // ── Navigation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML
    public void handleRetour() {
        tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.feedback;
import entities.feedback.TargetType;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.FlowPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import services.AvatarService;
import services.feedbackServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;

public class PatientAddFeedbackController {

    @FXML private HBox starsBox;
    @FXML private TextArea commentArea;
    @FXML private CheckBox anonymeCheck;
    @FXML private Label titleLabel;

    // Avatar section (visible uniquement en mode anonyme)
    @FXML private VBox avatarSection;
    @FXML private FlowPane avatarContainer;
    @FXML private Label avatarStatusLabel;

    private feedbackServices feedbackService = new feedbackServices();
    private AvatarService avatarService = new AvatarService();
    private int selectedRating = 5;
    private String currentTargetType;
    private int currentTargetId;
    private String currentTargetName;
    private String selectedAvatarBase64 = null;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        createStarButtons();

        // Afficher/masquer la galerie d'avatars selon la case anonyme
        anonymeCheck.selectedProperty().addListener((obs, wasSelected, isNowSelected) -> {
            avatarSection.setVisible(isNowSelected);
            avatarSection.setManaged(isNowSelected);
            if (isNowSelected && avatarContainer.getChildren().isEmpty()) {
                loadAvatarGallery();
            }
            if (!isNowSelected) {
                // Réinitialiser la sélection si on décoche
                selectedAvatarBase64 = null;
                avatarStatusLabel.setText("Aucun avatar sélectionné");
                // Retirer le style de sélection de tous les avatars
                avatarContainer.getChildren().forEach(node ->
                    node.setStyle("-fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.15), 8, 0, 0, 3); -fx-cursor: hand;")
                );
            }
        });
    }

    public void initData(String targetType, int targetId, String targetName) {
        this.currentTargetType = targetType;
        this.currentTargetId = targetId;
        this.currentTargetName = targetName;
        titleLabel.setText("Ajouter un feedback pour " + targetName);
    }

    private void loadAvatarGallery() {
        avatarContainer.getChildren().clear();
        List<String> avatars = avatarService.generateAvatarOptions("feedback", 6);
        for (String base64 : avatars) {
            if (base64 == null || base64.isEmpty()) continue;

            ImageView iv = new ImageView(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(Base64.getDecoder().decode(base64))));
            iv.setFitWidth(80);
            iv.setFitHeight(80);
            iv.setPreserveRatio(true);
            iv.setStyle("-fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.15), 8, 0, 0, 3); -fx-cursor: hand;");

            iv.setOnMouseClicked(e -> {
                // Retirer la sélection des autres
                avatarContainer.getChildren().forEach(node ->
                    node.setStyle("-fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.15), 8, 0, 0, 3); -fx-cursor: hand;")
                );
                // Mettre en surbrillance l'avatar sélectionné
                iv.setStyle("-fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, #FF5C5C, 15, 0.5, 0, 0); -fx-cursor: hand;");
                selectedAvatarBase64 = base64;
                avatarStatusLabel.setText("✓ Avatar sélectionné");
                avatarStatusLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #48BB78; -fx-font-size: 11; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
            });

            avatarContainer.getChildren().add(iv);
        }
    }

    private void createStarButtons() {
        starsBox.getChildren().clear();
        for (int i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
            final int rating = i;
            Button starBtn = new Button(i <= selectedRating ? "★" : "☆");
            starBtn.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 35; -fx-background-color: transparent; -fx-text-fill: #FBBF24; -fx-cursor: hand;");
            starBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
                selectedRating = rating;
                updateStars();
            });
            starsBox.getChildren().add(starBtn);
        }
    }

    private void updateStars() {
        for (int i = 0; i < starsBox.getChildren().size(); i++) {
            Button starBtn = (Button) starsBox.getChildren().get(i);
            starBtn.setText(i < selectedRating ? "★" : "☆");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void saveFeedback() {
        if (commentArea.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez entrer un commentaire");
            return;
        }

        // Si anonyme coché, un avatar doit être sélectionné
        if (anonymeCheck.isSelected() && selectedAvatarBase64 == null) {
            showAlert("Avatar requis", "Veuillez choisir un avatar pour votre feedback anonyme.");
            return;
        }

        TargetType type = currentTargetType.equals("doctor") ? TargetType.doctor : TargetType.pharmacy;

        try {
            feedback newFeedback = new feedback();
            newFeedback.setUserId(Main.getCurrentUserId());
            newFeedback.setTargetType(type);
            newFeedback.setTargetId(currentTargetId);
            newFeedback.setRating(selectedRating);
            newFeedback.setComment(commentArea.getText());
            newFeedback.setCreationDate(new Timestamp(System.currentTimeMillis()));
            newFeedback.setAnonyme(anonymeCheck.isSelected());
            // Sauvegarder l'avatar choisi (null si non anonyme)
            newFeedback.setAvatarUrl(anonymeCheck.isSelected() ? selectedAvatarBase64 : null);

            feedbackService.add(newFeedback);
            showAlert("Succès", "Feedback ajouté avec succès !");
            goBack();

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de l'ajout du feedback");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void goBack() {
        if (currentTargetType.equals("doctor")) {
            Main.showPatientFeedbackForDoctor(currentTargetId, currentTargetName);
        } else {
            Main.showPatientPharmacies();
        }
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Prescription;
import entities.medicalFile;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import services.Prescriptionserv;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import services.userservDS;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import entities.CalendarEvent;
import entities.Prescription;
import java.util.Random;

public class PatientDashboardController {

    @FXML private Label lblDate;
    @FXML private Label lblWelcome;
    @FXML private Label lblNextAppointment;
    @FXML private javafx.scene.layout.VBox vboxRappels;
    @FXML private Label lblNextDoctor;
    @FXML private Label lblActivePrescriptions;
    @FXML private Label lblLastVisit;
    @FXML private Label lblTotalDocuments;
    @FXML private Label lblHealthTip;

    private Prescriptionserv prescriptionService = new Prescriptionserv();
    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
    private services.userservDS userService = new services.userservDS();
    private services.CalendarEventService calendarService = new services.CalendarEventService();

    private String[] healthTips = {
        "Pensez à boire au moins 1.5L d'eau aujourd'hui pour rester hydraté.",
        "Une marche de 30 minutes par jour améliore considérablement votre santé cardiaque.",
        "Le sommeil est crucial : essayez de dormir entre 7 et 8 heures par nuit.",
        "Réduisez votre consommation de sucre pour maintenir une énergie stable toute la journée.",
        "N'oubliez pas de faire des pauses régulières si vous travaillez devant un écran.",
        "Consommer des fruits et légumes de saison booste votre système immunitaire."
    };

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        setCurrentDate();
        loadPatientData();
        loadHealthTip();
    }

    private void setCurrentDate() {
        LocalDate now = LocalDate.now();
        java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter formatter = java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("EEEE, d MMMM yyyy", java.util.Locale.FRENCH);
        String formattedDate = now.format(formatter);
        lblDate.setText(formattedDate.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + formattedDate.substring(1));
    }

    private void loadPatientData() {
        int patientId = Main.currentUserId;
        userDS patient = userService.getById(patientId);
        
        if (patient != null) {
            lblWelcome.setText("Bonjour, " + patient.getFirstName() + " !");
        }

        try {
            // 1. Charger les ordonnances actives
            List<Prescription> prescriptions = prescriptionService.getByPatientId(patientId);
            lblActivePrescriptions.setText(String.valueOf(prescriptions.size()));
            
            // Affichage dynamique des rappels
            vboxRappels.getChildren().clear();
            if (prescriptions.isEmpty()) {
                vboxRappels.setManaged(false);
                vboxRappels.setVisible(false);
            } else {
                vboxRappels.setManaged(true);
                vboxRappels.setVisible(true);
                // Afficher les 2 dernières prescriptions comme rappels
                int count = 0;
                for (Prescription p : prescriptions) {
                    if (count >= 2) break;
                    vboxRappels.getChildren().add(createRappelNode(p));
                    count++;
                }
            }

            // 2. Charger les infos du dossier médical
            List<medicalFile> dossiers = medicalFileService.getByPatientId(patientId);
            if (!dossiers.isEmpty()) {
                medicalFile lastFile = dossiers.get(0); // La liste est triée par date desc
                lblLastVisit.setText(lastFile.getLastvisit() != null ? lastFile.getLastvisit().toString() : "N/A");
                lblTotalDocuments.setText(String.valueOf(dossiers.size()));
            } else {
                lblLastVisit.setText("Aucune visite");
                lblTotalDocuments.setText("0");
            }

            // 3. Charger le prochain rendez-vous depuis le calendrier
            CalendarEvent nextEvent = calendarService.getNextEventForUser(patientId);
            if (nextEvent != null) {
                lblNextAppointment.setText(nextEvent.getDate().toString() + " à " + nextEvent.getTime());
                lblNextDoctor.setText(nextEvent.getTitle()); // Souvent le titre contient le nom du médecin ou type de rdv
            } else {
                lblNextAppointment.setText("Aucun RDV");
                lblNextDoctor.setText("Prévoyez une consultation");
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private javafx.scene.Node createRappelNode(Prescription p) {
        javafx.scene.layout.HBox hbox = new javafx.scene.layout.HBox(15);
        hbox.setAlignment(javafx.geometry.Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        hbox.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F0FFF4; -fx-padding: 15; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-border-color: #C6F6D5; -fx-border-radius: 10;");
        
        Label icon = new Label("💊");
        icon.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 24;");
        
        javafx.scene.layout.VBox texts = new javafx.scene.layout.VBox(2);
        Label title = new Label("Traitement : " + p.getPrescription());
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #22543D;");
        Label sub = new Label("Durée : " + p.getTreatmentDuration() + " - Ajoutée le " + p.getPrescriptionDate());
        sub.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-text-fill: #2F855A;");
        
        texts.getChildren().addAll(title, sub);
        hbox.getChildren().addAll(icon, texts);
        return hbox;
    }
    private void loadHealthTip() {
        Random random = new Random();
        lblHealthTip.setText(healthTips[random.nextInt(healthTips.length)]);
    }

    @FXML
    public void prendreRDV() {
        System.out.println("Redirection vers la prise de RDV...");
        Main.showPatientRequestAppointment();
    }

    @FXML
    public void consulterDossier() {
        // Redirection vers la consultation du dossier patient
        Main.showPatientMedicalFile();
    }

    @FXML
    public void voirOrdonnances() {
        // Redirection vers la liste des ordonnances du patient
        Main.showPatientPrescriptions();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goToHealthStats() {
        Main.showPatientHealthStats();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import entities.user.Role;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class PatientDoctorListController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private TextField searchNameField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> specialtyFilter;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> locationFilter;
    @FXML private HBox paginationBox;
    @FXML private Label statusLabel;
    @FXML private FlowPane cardsContainer;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> sortComboBox;

    private ObservableList<user> allDoctorList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private ObservableList<user> filteredDoctorList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private ObservableList<user> currentPageList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private userServices userService = new userServices();

    private int itemsPerPage = 6;
    private int currentPage = 1;
    private int totalPages = 1;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        specialtyFilter.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Toutes les spécialités", "Généraliste", "Cardiologue", "Dentiste",
                "Ophtalmologue", "Pédiatre", "Dermatologue", "Neurologue", "Gynécologue"
        ));
        specialtyFilter.setValue("Toutes les spécialités");

        locationFilter.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Toutes les villes", "Ariana", "Béja", "Ben Arous", "Bizerte", "Gabès",
                "Gafsa", "Jendouba", "Kairouan", "Kasserine", "Kébili", "Le Kef",
                "Mahdia", "La Manouba", "Médenine", "Monastir", "Nabeul", "Sfax",
                "Sidi Bouzid", "Siliana", "Sousse", "Tataouine", "Tozeur", "Tunis", "Zaghouan"
        ));
        locationFilter.setValue("Toutes les villes");

        sortComboBox.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Ordre alphabétique", "A - Z", "Z - A"));
        sortComboBox.setValue("Ordre alphabétique");

        searchNameField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldText, newText) -> {
            currentPage = 1;
            filterDoctors();
        });

        specialtyFilter.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            if (newVal != null) {
                currentPage = 1;
                filterDoctors();
            }
        });

        locationFilter.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            if (newVal != null) {
                currentPage = 1;
                filterDoctors();
            }
        });

        sortComboBox.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            filterDoctors();
        });

        loadDoctors();
    }

    public void loadDoctors() {
        try {
            List<user> allUsers = userService.getAll();
            List<user> doctors = allUsers.stream()
                    .filter(u -> u.getRole() == Role.doctor && u.getAccountStatus() == user.AccountStatus.active)
                    .collect(Collectors.toList());
            allDoctorList.setAll(doctors);
            filterDoctors();
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            if (statusLabel != null) statusLabel.setText("Erreur de chargement");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void filterDoctors() {
        String nameStr = searchNameField.getText().toLowerCase();
        String specStr = specialtyFilter.getValue();
        String locStr = locationFilter.getValue();
        String sortOrder = sortComboBox.getValue();

        List<user> filtered = allDoctorList.stream()
                .filter(u -> {
                    boolean matchName = true;
                    if (!nameStr.isEmpty()) {
                        matchName = u.getFirstName().toLowerCase().contains(nameStr) ||
                                u.getLastName().toLowerCase().contains(nameStr);
                    }
                    boolean matchSpec = true;
                    if (specStr != null && !"Toutes les spécialités".equals(specStr)) {
                        matchSpec = (u.getSpecialty() != null && u.getSpecialty().trim().equalsIgnoreCase(specStr.trim()));
                    }
                    boolean matchLoc = true;
                    if (locStr != null && !"Toutes les villes".equals(locStr)) {
                        matchLoc = (u.getAddress() != null && u.getAddress().toLowerCase().contains(locStr.toLowerCase()));
                    }
                    return matchName && matchSpec && matchLoc;
                })
                .collect(Collectors.toList());

        // Alphabetical sorting
        if ("A - Z".equals(sortOrder)) {
            filtered.sort((u1, u2) -> (u1.getFirstName() + u1.getLastName()).compareToIgnoreCase(u2.getFirstName() + u2.getLastName()));
        } else if ("Z - A".equals(sortOrder)) {
            filtered.sort((u1, u2) -> (u2.getFirstName() + u2.getLastName()).compareToIgnoreCase(u1.getFirstName() + u1.getLastName()));
        }

        filteredDoctorList.setAll(filtered);
        updatePagination();
        displayCurrentPage();
        if (statusLabel != null) statusLabel.setText("Total: " + filteredDoctorList.size() + " docteurs");
    }

    @FXML
    public void resetFilters() {
        searchNameField.setText("");
        specialtyFilter.setValue("Toutes les spécialités");
        locationFilter.setValue("Toutes les villes");
        sortComboBox.setValue("Ordre alphabétique");
        currentPage = 1;
        filterDoctors();
    }

    private void updatePagination() {
        totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) filteredDoctorList.size() / itemsPerPage);
        if (totalPages == 0) totalPages = 1;
        if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages;

        createPaginationButtons();
    }

    private void createPaginationButtons() {
        if (paginationBox == null) return;
        paginationBox.getChildren().clear();

        Button prevBtn = new Button("◀");
        prevBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
        prevBtn.setDisable(currentPage == 1);
        prevBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
            if (currentPage > 1) {
                currentPage--;
                displayCurrentPage();
                createPaginationButtons();
            }
        });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(prevBtn);

        int startPage = Math.max(1, currentPage - 2);
        int endPage = Math.min(totalPages, startPage + 4);

        if (startPage > 1) {
            Button firstBtn = createPageButton(1);
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(firstBtn);
            if (startPage > 2) {
                Label dots = new Label("...");
                dots.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-padding: 2; -fx-font-size: 11;");
                paginationBox.getChildren().add(dots);
            }
        }

        for (int i = startPage; i <= endPage; i++) {
            Button pageBtn = createPageButton(i);
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(pageBtn);
        }

        if (endPage < totalPages) {
            if (endPage < totalPages - 1) {
                Label dots = new Label("...");
                dots.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-padding: 2; -fx-font-size: 11;");
                paginationBox.getChildren().add(dots);
            }
            Button lastBtn = createPageButton(totalPages);
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(lastBtn);
        }

        Button nextBtn = new Button("▶");
        nextBtn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
        nextBtn.setDisable(currentPage == totalPages);
        nextBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
            if (currentPage < totalPages) {
                currentPage++;
                displayCurrentPage();
                createPaginationButtons();
            }
        });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(nextBtn);
    }

    private Button createPageButton(int pageNum) {
        Button btn = new Button(String.valueOf(pageNum));
        btn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button");
        if (pageNum == currentPage) {
            btn.getStyleClass().add("pagination-button-active");
        }
        btn.setOnAction(e -> {
            currentPage = pageNum;
            displayCurrentPage();
            createPaginationButtons();
        });
        return btn;
    }

    private void displayCurrentPage() {
        int start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, filteredDoctorList.size());

        currentPageList.clear();
        if (start < filteredDoctorList.size()) {
            currentPageList.setAll(filteredDoctorList.subList(start, end));
        }

        displayCards(currentPageList);
    }

    private void displayCards(ObservableList<user> users) {
        if (cardsContainer == null) return;
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();

        if (users.isEmpty()) {
            Label emptyLabel = new Label("Aucun docteur trouvé");
            emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 14;");
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
            return;
        }

        for (user u : users) {
            VBox card = createDoctorCard(u);
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(card);
        }
    }

    private VBox createDoctorCard(user doctor) {
        VBox card = new VBox(12);
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 12; -fx-background-radius: 12; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.05), 10, 0, 0, 2);");
        card.setPadding(new Insets(15));
        card.setPrefWidth(280);

        HBox header = new HBox(12);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        ImageView photo = new ImageView();
        photo.setFitHeight(55);
        photo.setFitWidth(55);
        javafx.scene.shape.Circle clip = new javafx.scene.shape.Circle(27.5, 27.5, 27.5);
        photo.setClip(clip);
        
        if (doctor.getUserPhoto() != null && !doctor.getUserPhoto().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                String cleanBase64 = doctor.getUserPhoto().trim().replaceAll("\\s+", "");
                byte[] imageBytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(cleanBase64);
                photo.setImage(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(imageBytes)));
            } catch (Exception e) {
                photo.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
            }
        } else {
            photo.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
        }

        VBox nameBox = new VBox(2);
        Label name = new Label("Dr. " + doctor.getFirstName() + " " + doctor.getLastName());
        name.setStyle("-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 15; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
        Label specialty = new Label(doctor.getSpecialty() != null ? doctor.getSpecialty() : "Généraliste");
        specialty.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #48BB78; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-padding: 2 8; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-font-size: 10;");
        nameBox.getChildren().addAll(name, specialty);
        header.getChildren().addAll(photo, nameBox);

        VBox infoBox = new VBox(5);
        Label phone = new Label("📞 " + doctor.getPhone());
        phone.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        Label fee = new Label("💰 Consultation: " + doctor.getConsultationFee() + " DT");
        fee.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #718096;");
        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(phone, fee);

        HBox actions = new HBox(10);
        actions.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        Button feedbackBtn = new Button("⭐ Avis");
        feedbackBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-padding: 6 15; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11;");
        HBox.setHgrow(feedbackBtn, Priority.ALWAYS);
        feedbackBtn.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        feedbackBtn.setOnAction(e -> Main.showPatientFeedbackForDoctor(doctor.getUserId(), "Dr. " + doctor.getFirstName() + " " + doctor.getLastName()));

        Button appointementBtn = new Button("📅 RDV");
        appointementBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-padding: 6 15; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        appointementBtn.setOnAction(e -> Main.showPatientRequestAppointment());
        
        actions.getChildren().addAll(feedbackBtn, appointementBtn);

        card.getChildren().addAll(header, new Separator(), infoBox, actions);
        return card;
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.feedback;
import entities.feedback.TargetType;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import services.feedbackServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Timestamp;

public class PatientEditFeedbackController {

    @FXML private HBox starsBox;
    @FXML private TextArea commentArea;
    @FXML private CheckBox anonymeCheck;
    @FXML private Label titleLabel;

    private feedbackServices feedbackService = new feedbackServices();
    private feedback currentFeedback;
    private int selectedRating;
    private String currentTargetType;
    private int currentTargetId;
    private String currentTargetName;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
    }

    public void initData(feedback fb, String targetType, int targetId, String targetName) {
        this.currentFeedback = fb;
        this.currentTargetType = targetType;
        this.currentTargetId = targetId;
        this.currentTargetName = targetName;
        this.selectedRating = fb.getRating();

        titleLabel.setText("Modifier le feedback pour " + targetName);
        commentArea.setText(fb.getComment());
        anonymeCheck.setSelected(fb.isAnonyme());

        createStarButtons();
    }

    private void createStarButtons() {
        starsBox.getChildren().clear();
        for (int i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
            final int rating = i;
            Button starBtn = new Button(i <= selectedRating ? "★" : "☆");
            starBtn.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 35; -fx-background-color: transparent; -fx-text-fill: #FBBF24; -fx-cursor: hand;");
            starBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
                selectedRating = rating;
                updateStars();
            });
            starsBox.getChildren().add(starBtn);
        }
    }

    private void updateStars() {
        for (int i = 0; i < starsBox.getChildren().size(); i++) {
            Button starBtn = (Button) starsBox.getChildren().get(i);
            starBtn.setText(i < selectedRating ? "★" : "☆");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void updateFeedback() {
        if (commentArea.getText().trim().isEmpty()) {
