            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez entrer un commentaire");
            return;
        }

        try {
            currentFeedback.setRating(selectedRating);
            currentFeedback.setComment(commentArea.getText());
            currentFeedback.setAnonyme(anonymeCheck.isSelected());
            currentFeedback.setCreationDate(new Timestamp(System.currentTimeMillis()));

            feedbackService.update(currentFeedback);
            showAlert("Succès", "Feedback modifié avec succès !");

            goBack();

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la modification du feedback");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void goBack() {
        if (currentTargetType.equals("doctor")) {
            Main.showPatientFeedbackForDoctor(currentTargetId, currentTargetName);
        } else {
            Main.showPatientPharmacies();
        }
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Participant;
import entities.Reservation;
import entities.Reservation.Statut;
import entities.event;
import entities.user;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import services.Participantserv;
import services.ReservationServ;
import services.eventServ;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class PatientEventsController {

    // ── FXML ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private FlowPane         cardsPane;
    @FXML private TextField        tfRecherche;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cbType;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cbStatut;
    @FXML private Label            lblTotal;
    @FXML private Label            lblStatTotal;
    @FXML private Label            lblStatMes;
    @FXML private Label            lblStatDispo;
    @FXML private Button           btnTabTous;
    @FXML private Button           btnTabMes;

    // ── State ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private final eventServ        eventService  = new eventServ();
    private final Participantserv  partService   = new Participantserv();
    private final ReservationServ  resService    = new ReservationServ();
    private final userServices     userService   = new userServices();

    private List<event>       allEvents     = new ArrayList<>();
    private Set<Integer>      myEventIds    = new HashSet<>();
    private Map<Integer,Statut> myStatuts   = new HashMap<>(); // eventId -> statut
    private int               myParticipantId = -1;

    private boolean           showingMyOnly = false;

    // ── Init ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Populate filter combo-boxes
        cbType.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
            "Tous les types",
            "Don de sang", "Vaccination", "Conférence médicale",
            "Consultation", "Sensibilisation", "Atelier santé",
            "Dépistage", "Premiers secours", "Conférence", "Séminaire", "Atelier"
        ));
        cbType.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();

        cbStatut.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
            "Tous", "Planifié", "En cours", "Terminé", "Annulé"
        ));
        cbStatut.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();

        loadMyProfile();
        loadData();
    }

    // ── Load patient profile (find / create Participant record) ──────────────
    private void loadMyProfile() {
        user me = currentUser();
        if (me == null) return;

        List<Participant> all = partService.getAll();
        Participant p = all.stream()
            .filter(x -> x.getEmail() != null
                      && x.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(me.getEmail()))
            .findFirst().orElse(null);

        if (p != null) {
            myParticipantId = p.getId();
            resService.getByParticipant(myParticipantId).forEach(r -> {
                myEventIds.add(r.getEventId());
                myStatuts.put(r.getEventId(), r.getStatut());
            });
        }
        // If p == null, the participant profile will be created on first booking
    }

    // ── Helper: fetch logged-in user from DB via Main.currentUserId ──────────
    private user currentUser() {
        int id = Main.currentUserId;
        if (id <= 0) return null;
        try {
            return userService.getById(id);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    // ── Load all events + update stats ───────────────────────────────────────
    private void loadData() {
        allEvents = eventService.getAll();

        long total = allEvents.size();
        long dispo = allEvents.stream()
            .filter(e -> {
                int cnt = eventService.getParticipantCount(e.getId());
                int max = e.getCapaciteMax();
                boolean open = "Planifié".equalsIgnoreCase(e.getStatus())
                            || "En cours".equalsIgnoreCase(e.getStatus());
                return open && (max == 0 || cnt < max);
            }).count();

        if (lblStatTotal != null) lblStatTotal.setText(String.valueOf(total));
        if (lblStatMes   != null) lblStatMes.setText(String.valueOf(myEventIds.size()));
        if (lblStatDispo != null) lblStatDispo.setText(String.valueOf(dispo));

        renderCards(filteredList());
    }

    // ── Filtering logic ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private List<event> filteredList() {
        String search = tfRecherche != null ? tfRecherche.getText().toLowerCase().trim() : "";
        String type   = cbType   != null && cbType.getValue()   != null ? cbType.getValue()   : "Tous les types";
        String statut = cbStatut != null && cbStatut.getValue() != null ? cbStatut.getValue() : "Tous";

        List<event> base = showingMyOnly
            ? allEvents.stream().filter(e -> myEventIds.contains(e.getId())).collect(Collectors.toList())
            : allEvents;

        return base.stream().filter(e -> {
            boolean matchSearch = search.isEmpty()
                || e.getTitre().toLowerCase().contains(search)
                || (e.getLieu() != null && e.getLieu().toLowerCase().contains(search));
            boolean matchType   = "Tous les types".equals(type)
                || type.equalsIgnoreCase(e.getType());
            boolean matchStatut = "Tous".equals(statut)
                || statut.equalsIgnoreCase(e.getStatus());
            return matchSearch && matchType && matchStatut;
        }).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    // ── Render cards into FlowPane ────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void renderCards(List<event> list) {
        if (cardsPane == null) return;
        cardsPane.getChildren().clear();

        String context = showingMyOnly ? "participation(s)" : "événement(s)";
        if (lblTotal != null)
            lblTotal.setText("Affichage de " + list.size() + " " + context);

        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            Label empty = new Label(showingMyOnly
                ? "📭  Vous n'êtes inscrit(e) à aucun événement pour l'instant."
                : "📭  Aucun événement trouvé.");
            empty.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0; -fx-font-size: 14; -fx-padding: 40;");
            cardsPane.getChildren().add(empty);
            return;
        }

        for (event ev : list) {
            cardsPane.getChildren().add(buildCard(ev));
        }
    }

    // ── Build one event card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private VBox buildCard(event ev) {
        int  count     = eventService.getParticipantCount(ev.getId());
        int  max       = ev.getCapaciteMax();
        boolean isFull = max > 0 && count >= max;
        boolean isOpen = "Planifié".equalsIgnoreCase(ev.getStatus())
                      || "En cours".equalsIgnoreCase(ev.getStatus());
        boolean inscrit = myEventIds.contains(ev.getId());
        Statut  statut  = myStatuts.get(ev.getId());

        // ── Card container ──
        VBox card = new VBox(0);
        card.setPrefWidth(300);
        card.setMaxWidth(300);
        card.setStyle(
            "-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 15; " +
            "-fx-border-color: " + borderColor(ev.getType()) + "; " +
            "-fx-border-width: 0 0 0 5; -fx-border-radius: 15; " +
            "-fx-effect: dropshadow(three-pass-box, rgba(0,0,0,0.07), 12, 0, 0, 4);");

        // ── Banner ──
        StackPane banner = new StackPane();
        banner.setPrefHeight(130);
        String[] colors = bannerColors(ev.getType());
        banner.setStyle(
            "-fx-background-color: linear-gradient(to bottom right," + colors[0] + "," + colors[1] + ");" +
            "-fx-background-radius: 15 15 0 0;");

        // Type badge
        Label typeBadge = new Label(ev.getType() != null ? ev.getType().toUpperCase() : "");
        typeBadge.setStyle("-fx-background-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.22); -fx-text-fill: white; " +
            "-fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-padding: 3 11 3 11; " +
            "-fx-font-size: 10.5; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(typeBadge, Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        StackPane.setMargin(typeBadge, new Insets(12, 0, 0, 12));

        // Status badge
        String[] sc = statusColors(ev.getStatus());
        Label statusBadge = new Label(sc[2] + "  " + ev.getStatus());
        statusBadge.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + sc[0] + "; -fx-text-fill:" + sc[1] + "; " +
            "-fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-padding: 3 11 3 11; " +
            "-fx-font-size: 10.5; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(statusBadge, Pos.TOP_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(statusBadge, new Insets(12, 12, 0, 0));

        // Center emoji
        Label emoji = new Label(typeEmoji(ev.getType()));
        emoji.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 44; -fx-opacity: 0.75;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(emoji, Pos.CENTER);

        banner.getChildren().addAll(emoji, typeBadge, statusBadge);

        // ── Body ──
        VBox body = new VBox(10);
        body.setPadding(new Insets(16, 18, 16, 18));

        // Title
        Label title = new Label(ev.getTitre());
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 15; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748; -fx-wrap-text: true;");
        title.setMaxWidth(264);
        title.setWrapText(true);

        // Meta info
        VBox meta = new VBox(5);
        if (ev.getDate() != null) {
            String time = (ev.getTime() != null && !ev.getTime().isEmpty()) ? "  ·  " + ev.getTime() : "";
            meta.getChildren().add(infoRow("📅", ev.getDate().toString() + time));
        }
        if (ev.getLieu() != null && !ev.getLieu().isEmpty())
            meta.getChildren().add(infoRow("📍", ev.getLieu()));

        // Capacity + progress
        String capLabel = max > 0 ? count + " / " + max + " places" : count + " inscrits";
        String capColor = isFull ? "#E53E3E" : "#48BB78";
        HBox capRow = infoRow("👥", capLabel);
        ((Label) capRow.getChildren().get(1)).setStyle(
            "-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: " + capColor + ";");
        meta.getChildren().add(capRow);

        if (max > 0) {
            ProgressBar pb = new ProgressBar(Math.min((double) count / max, 1.0));
            pb.setPrefWidth(264);
            pb.setMaxHeight(5);
            pb.setStyle(isFull ? "-fx-accent:#E53E3E;"
                : count >= max * 0.8 ? "-fx-accent:#ED8936;" : "-fx-accent:#48BB78;");
            meta.getChildren().add(pb);
        }

        // Description (clamped)
        Label desc = new Label(ev.getDescription() != null ? ev.getDescription() : "");
        desc.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-wrap-text: true;");
        desc.setWrapText(true);
        desc.setMaxWidth(264);
        desc.setMaxHeight(36);

        Separator sep = new Separator();
        sep.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #EDF2F7;");

        // ── Action button ──
        Button btnAction;
        if (inscrit) {
            // Already registered — show statut chip + annuler button
            String chipText, chipColor;
            if (statut == Statut.CONFIRME) {
                chipText = "✅  Inscription confirmée";
                chipColor = "#F0FFF4; -fx-text-fill: #276749;";
            } else if (statut == Statut.LISTE_D_ATTENTE) {
                chipText = "⏳  En liste d'attente";
                chipColor = "#FFFFF0; -fx-text-fill: #B7791F;";
            } else {
                chipText = "✅  Inscrit(e)";
                chipColor = "#F0FFF4; -fx-text-fill: #276749;";
            }
            Label chip = new Label(chipText);
            chip.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            chip.setStyle("-fx-background-color: " + chipColor +
                " -fx-background-radius: 9; -fx-padding: 8 14; " +
                "-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 12.5;");

            Button btnAnnuler = new Button("✕  Annuler ma participation");
            btnAnnuler.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            btnAnnuler.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FFF5F5; -fx-text-fill: #C53030; " +
                "-fx-background-radius: 9; -fx-padding: 8 14; -fx-cursor: hand; " +
                "-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-border-color: #FED7D7; -fx-border-radius: 9;");
            btnAnnuler.setOnAction(e -> doAnnuler(ev));

            VBox btnBox = new VBox(7, chip, btnAnnuler);
            body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, sep, desc, btnBox);
        } else if (!isOpen) {
            btnAction = new Button("🔒  Événement " + ev.getStatus());
            btnAction.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            btnAction.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F7FAFC; -fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0; " +
                "-fx-background-radius: 9; -fx-padding: 10 14; -fx-font-size: 12.5; " +
                "-fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 9;");
            btnAction.setDisable(true);
            body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, sep, desc, btnAction);
        } else if (isFull) {
            btnAction = new Button("🔒  Complet — Liste d'attente");
            btnAction.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            btnAction.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FFF5F5; -fx-text-fill: #C53030; " +
                "-fx-background-radius: 9; -fx-padding: 10 14; -fx-font-size: 12.5; " +
                "-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-cursor: hand;");
            btnAction.setOnAction(e -> doParticiper(ev, true));
            body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, sep, desc, btnAction);
        } else {
            btnAction = new Button("🎟  Participer à cet événement");
            btnAction.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            btnAction.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #4A6CF7; -fx-text-fill: white; " +
                "-fx-background-radius: 9; -fx-padding: 10 14; -fx-font-size: 12.5; " +
                "-fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-cursor: hand;");
            btnAction.setOnAction(e -> doParticiper(ev, false));

            btnAction.setOnMouseEntered(e -> btnAction.setStyle(btnAction.getStyle()
                .replace("#4A6CF7", "#3a5ce6")));
            btnAction.setOnMouseExited(e -> btnAction.setStyle(btnAction.getStyle()
                .replace("#3a5ce6", "#4A6CF7")));
            body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, sep, desc, btnAction);
        }

        // Hover on card
        String baseStyle = card.getStyle();
        card.setOnMouseEntered(e -> card.setStyle(baseStyle.replace(
            "rgba(0,0,0,0.07),12,0,0,4", "rgba(0,0,0,0.14),18,0,0,6")));
        card.setOnMouseExited(e -> card.setStyle(baseStyle));

        card.getChildren().addAll(banner, body);
        return card;
    }

    // ── Participate ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void doParticiper(event ev, boolean isWaitlist) {
        user me = currentUser();
        if (me == null) {
            alert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Session expirée", "Veuillez vous reconnecter.");
            return;
        }

        // Double-check not already registered
        if (myEventIds.contains(ev.getId())) {
            alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "Déjà inscrit(e)",
                "Vous êtes déjà inscrit(e) à « " + ev.getTitre() + " ».");
            return;
        }

        // Confirmation dialog
        String msg = isWaitlist
            ? "Cet événement est complet. Souhaitez-vous rejoindre la liste d'attente ?"
            : "Voulez-vous participer à « " + ev.getTitre() + " » ?";

        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION, msg, ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        confirm.setTitle("Confirmer la participation");
        confirm.setHeaderText(isWaitlist ? "🕐 Rejoindre la liste d'attente" : "🎟 Participer à l'événement");
        Optional<ButtonType> result = confirm.showAndWait();
        if (result.isEmpty() || result.get() != ButtonType.YES) return;

        // Find or create Participant profile
        ensureParticipantProfile(me);
        if (myParticipantId < 0) {
            alert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de créer votre profil participant.");
            return;
        }

        // Submit reservation
        Reservation res = resService.soumettreDemande(ev.getId(), myParticipantId, 1);
        if (res == null) {
            alert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Une erreur est survenue. Veuillez réessayer.");
            return;
        }

        switch (res.getStatut()) {
            case CONFIRME:
                myEventIds.add(ev.getId());
                myStatuts.put(ev.getId(), Statut.CONFIRME);
                updateStats();
                alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "✅ Inscription confirmée !",
                    "Vous êtes maintenant inscrit(e) à « " + ev.getTitre() + " ».\nBonne participation !");
                break;
            case LISTE_D_ATTENTE:
                myEventIds.add(ev.getId());
                myStatuts.put(ev.getId(), Statut.LISTE_D_ATTENTE);
                updateStats();
                alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "⏳ Liste d'attente",
                    "L'événement est complet. Vous avez été ajouté(e) à la liste d'attente.\n" +
                    "Vous serez notifié(e) si une place se libère.");
                break;
            case REFUSE:
                String motif = res.getMotifRefus() != null ? res.getMotifRefus() : "Capacité atteinte.";
                alert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "❌ Inscription refusée", motif);
                break;
            default:
                alert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Statut inattendu", res.getStatut().name());
        }

        refresh();
    }

    // ── Cancel participation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void doAnnuler(event ev) {
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
            "Voulez-vous annuler votre participation à « " + ev.getTitre() + " » ?",
            ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        confirm.setTitle("Annuler la participation");
        confirm.setHeaderText("✕  Annulation");
        Optional<ButtonType> res = confirm.showAndWait();
        if (res.isEmpty() || res.get() != ButtonType.YES) return;

        // Find the reservation and cancel it
        if (myParticipantId >= 0) {
            resService.getByParticipant(myParticipantId).stream()
                .filter(r -> r.getEventId() == ev.getId())
                .findFirst()
                .ifPresent(r -> resService.annulerReservation(r.getId()));
        }

        myEventIds.remove(ev.getId());
        myStatuts.remove(ev.getId());
        updateStats();
        alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION, "✅ Participation annulée",
            "Votre inscription à « " + ev.getTitre() + " » a été annulée.");
        refresh();
    }

    // ── Find / create Participant record for current user ────────────────────
    private void ensureParticipantProfile(user me) {
        if (myParticipantId >= 0) return;
        if (me == null) return;
        List<Participant> all = partService.getAll();
        Participant p = all.stream()
            .filter(x -> x.getEmail() != null
                      && x.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(me.getEmail()))
            .findFirst().orElse(null);

        if (p == null) {
            String name  = extractName(me.getEmail());
            String phone = me.getPhone() != null ? me.getPhone() : "";
            p = new Participant(name, "", me.getEmail(), phone);
            partService.ajouter(p);
            // Re-fetch to get assigned id
            p = partService.getAll().stream()
                .filter(x -> x.getEmail() != null
                          && x.getEmail().equalsIgnoreCase(me.getEmail()))
                .findFirst().orElse(null);
        }
        if (p != null) myParticipantId = p.getId();
    }

    // ── Update stat labels without full reload ────────────────────────────────
    private void updateStats() {
        if (lblStatMes != null) lblStatMes.setText(String.valueOf(myEventIds.size()));
    }

    // ── Full refresh (re-query DB + re-render) ────────────────────────────────
    private void refresh() {
        // Reload reservations
        myEventIds.clear();
        myStatuts.clear();
        if (myParticipantId >= 0) {
            resService.getByParticipant(myParticipantId).forEach(r -> {
                myEventIds.add(r.getEventId());
                myStatuts.put(r.getEventId(), r.getStatut());
            });
        }
        loadData();
    }

    // ── FXML Handlers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML
    public void handleRecherche() {
        renderCards(filteredList());
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleFiltre() {
        renderCards(filteredList());
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleReset() {
        if (tfRecherche != null) tfRecherche.clear();
        if (cbType   != null) cbType.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        if (cbStatut != null) cbStatut.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
        showingMyOnly = false;
        activateTab(true);
        renderCards(filteredList());
    }

    @FXML
    public void showTousLesEvenements() {
        showingMyOnly = false;
        activateTab(true);
        renderCards(filteredList());
    }

    @FXML
    public void showMesParticipations() {
        showingMyOnly = true;
        activateTab(false);
        renderCards(filteredList());
    }

    // ── Tab style toggle ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void activateTab(boolean tousActive) {
        String active   = "-fx-background-color:#4A6CF7; -fx-text-fill:white; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                          "-fx-font-size:13; -fx-cursor:hand;";
        String inactive = "-fx-background-color:#EBF8FF; -fx-text-fill:#2B6CB0; " +
                          "-fx-font-size:13; -fx-cursor:hand;";
        if (btnTabTous != null) {
            String s = tousActive ? active : inactive;
            btnTabTous.setStyle(s + " -fx-background-radius:9 0 0 9; -fx-padding:9 20;");
        }
        if (btnTabMes != null) {
            String s = tousActive ? inactive : active;
            btnTabMes.setStyle(s + " -fx-background-radius:0 9 9 0; -fx-padding:9 20;");
        }
    }

    // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private HBox infoRow(String icon, String text) {
        HBox row = new HBox(7);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        Label ico = new Label(icon);
        ico.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12;");
        Label lbl = new Label(text);
        lbl.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568;");
        lbl.setMaxWidth(230);
        row.getChildren().addAll(ico, lbl);
        return row;
    }

    private void alert(Alert.AlertType type, String header, String body) {
        Alert a = new Alert(type, body, ButtonType.OK);
        a.setHeaderText(header);
        a.showAndWait();
    }

    private String extractName(String email) {
        if (email == null) return "Patient";
        int at = email.indexOf('@');
        if (at > 0) {
            String n = email.substring(0, at);
            return n.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + n.substring(1).toLowerCase();
        }
        return email;
    }

    // ── Color / styling helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    private String borderColor(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "#E2E8F0";
        switch (norm(type)) {
            case "DON DE SANG":         return "#DC2626";
            case "VACCINATION":         return "#16A34A";
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE": return "#2563EB";
            case "CONSULTATION":        return "#7C3AED";
            case "SENSIBILISATION":     return "#D97706";
            case "ATELIER SANTE":       return "#0D9488";
            case "DEPISTAGE":           return "#0284C7";
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":    return "#EA580C";
            case "CONFERENCE":          return "#2563EB";
            case "SEMINAIRE":           return "#16A34A";
            case "ATELIER":             return "#D97706";
            default:                    return "#4A6CF7";
        }
    }

    private String[] bannerColors(String type) {
        if (type == null) return new String[]{"#2D3748", "#4A5568"};
        switch (norm(type)) {
            case "DON DE SANG":         return new String[]{"#7F1D1D", "#DC2626"};
            case "VACCINATION":         return new String[]{"#14532D", "#16A34A"};
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE": return new String[]{"#1E3A5F", "#2563EB"};
            case "CONSULTATION":        return new String[]{"#312E81", "#7C3AED"};
            case "SENSIBILISATION":     return new String[]{"#92400E", "#D97706"};
            case "ATELIER SANTE":       return new String[]{"#134E4A", "#0D9488"};
            case "DEPISTAGE":           return new String[]{"#1E3A5F", "#0284C7"};
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":    return new String[]{"#7C2D12", "#EA580C"};
            case "CONFERENCE":          return new String[]{"#1E3A5F", "#4A6CF7"};
            case "SEMINAIRE":           return new String[]{"#14532D", "#16A34A"};
            case "ATELIER":             return new String[]{"#78350F", "#D97706"};
            default:                    return new String[]{"#1A202C", "#4A6CF7"};
        }
    }

    private String typeEmoji(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "📋";
        switch (norm(type)) {
            case "DON DE SANG":         return "🩸";
            case "VACCINATION":         return "💉";
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE": return "🏥";
            case "CONSULTATION":        return "🩺";
            case "SENSIBILISATION":     return "❤";
            case "ATELIER SANTE":       return "🥗";
            case "DEPISTAGE":           return "🔬";
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":    return "🚑";
            case "CONFERENCE":          return "🎓";
            case "SEMINAIRE":           return "📚";
            case "ATELIER":             return "🛠";
            default:                    return "🏥";
        }
    }

    private String[] statusColors(String status) {
        if (status == null) return new String[]{"#E2E8F0", "#4A5568", "◉"};
        switch (status.toLowerCase()) {
            case "planifié":  return new String[]{"#EBF8FF", "#2B6CB0", "◷"};
            case "en cours":  return new String[]{"#F0FFF4", "#276749", "✓"};
            case "terminé":   return new String[]{"#F7FAFC", "#718096", "◉"};
            case "annulé":    return new String[]{"#FFF5F5", "#C53030", "✕"};
            default:          return new String[]{"#E2E8F0", "#4A5568", "◉"};
        }
    }

    private String norm(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        return s.toUpperCase()
            .replace("É","E").replace("È","E").replace("Ê","E")
            .replace("À","A").replace("Â","A")
            .replace("Î","I").replace("Ï","I")
            .replace("Ô","O").replace("Ù","U")
            .replace("Û","U").replace("Ü","U")
            .replace("Ç","C");
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.feedback;
import entities.feedback.TargetType;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.shape.SVGPath;
import services.feedbackServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class PatientFeedbackController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private Label titleLabel;
    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> sortCombo;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> ratingCombo;
    @FXML private Label statusLabel;
    @FXML private FlowPane cardsContainer;
    @FXML private Button addButton;

    private feedbackServices feedbackService = new feedbackServices();
    private ObservableList<feedback> feedbackList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private String currentTargetType;
    private int currentTargetId;
    private String currentTargetName;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        sortCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
            "Plus récents", "Plus anciens"
        ));
        sortCombo.setValue("Plus récents");

        if (ratingCombo != null) {
            ratingCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Toutes les notes", "⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ (5)", "⭐⭐⭐⭐ (4)", "⭐⭐⭐ (3)", "⭐⭐ (2)", "⭐ (1)"
            ));
            ratingCombo.setValue("Toutes les notes");
            ratingCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> applyFilters());
        }

        searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldText, newText) -> {
            applyFilters();
        });

        sortCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            applyFilters();
        });
    }

    public void loadFeedbacks(String targetType, int targetId, String targetName) {
        this.currentTargetType = targetType;
        this.currentTargetId = targetId;
        this.currentTargetName = targetName;

        titleLabel.setText("⭐ Feedbacks pour " + targetName);
        
        // Cacher le bouton ajouter si c'est pour soi-même
        if (addButton != null) {
            addButton.setVisible(Main.getCurrentUserId() != currentTargetId);
            addButton.setManaged(Main.getCurrentUserId() != currentTargetId);
        }
        
        refreshFeedbacks();
    }

    private void refreshFeedbacks() {
        try {
            List<feedback> allFeedbacks = feedbackService.getAll();
            feedbackList.clear();

            TargetType type = currentTargetType.equals("doctor") ? TargetType.doctor : TargetType.pharmacy;

            for (feedback fb : allFeedbacks) {
                if (fb.getTargetType() == type && fb.getTargetId() == currentTargetId) {
                    feedbackList.add(fb);
                }
            }

            displayCards(feedbackList);
            statusLabel.setText("Total: " + feedbackList.size() + " feedbacks");

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            statusLabel.setText("Erreur de chargement: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void applyFilters() {
        String searchText = searchField.getText().toLowerCase();
        String sortOrder = sortCombo.getValue();
        String ratingFilter = (ratingCombo != null) ? ratingCombo.getValue() : "Toutes les notes";

        // Extraire la note numérique sélectionnée (ex: "⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ (5)" -> 5)
        int selectedRating = -1; // -1 = toutes
        if (ratingFilter != null && !ratingFilter.equals("Toutes les notes")) {
            try {
                // Le chiffre est entre parenthèses à la fin
                String numPart = ratingFilter.replaceAll(".*\\((\\d)\\)", "$1");
                selectedRating = Integer.parseInt(numPart);
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        final int filterRating = selectedRating;

        ObservableList<feedback> filtered = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
        for (feedback fb : feedbackList) {
            boolean matchesSearch = fb.getComment().toLowerCase().contains(searchText);
            boolean matchesRating = (filterRating == -1) || (fb.getRating() == filterRating);
            if (matchesSearch && matchesRating) {
                filtered.add(fb);
            }
        }

        if ("Plus récents".equals(sortOrder)) {
            filtered.sort((f1, f2) -> f2.getCreationDate().compareTo(f1.getCreationDate()));
        } else {
            filtered.sort((f1, f2) -> f1.getCreationDate().compareTo(f2.getCreationDate()));
        }

        displayCards(filtered);
        statusLabel.setText("Affichage: " + filtered.size() + " / " + feedbackList.size());
    }

    private void displayCards(ObservableList<feedback> feedbacks) {
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();

        if (feedbacks.isEmpty()) {
            Label emptyLabel = new Label("Aucun feedback pour cet établissement");
            emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 16;");
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
            return;
        }

        for (feedback fb : feedbacks) {
            StackPane card = createFeedbackCard(fb);
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(card);
        }
    }

    private StackPane createFeedbackCard(feedback fb) {
        StackPane cardRoot = new StackPane();
        cardRoot.setPrefWidth(280);
        cardRoot.setMaxWidth(280);

        // Fond principal blanc arrondi
        VBox cardBody = new VBox(10);
        cardBody.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.1), 10, 0, 0, 4); -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 20;");
        cardBody.setPadding(new Insets(60, 20, 20, 20));
        StackPane.setMargin(cardBody, new Insets(20, 0, 0, 0));

        // Ruban bleu (Header)
        VBox ribbon = new VBox(2);
        ribbon.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #3B5998; -fx-background-radius: 15 0 20 0; -fx-padding: 8 15;");
        ribbon.setMaxWidth(200);
        ribbon.setMaxHeight(45);
        
        String authorName = "Utilisateur Anonyme";
        if (!fb.isAnonyme()) {
            authorName = getAuthorName(fb.getUserId());
        }
        Label nameLabel = new Label(authorName);
        nameLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: white; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 12;");
        Label dateLabel = new Label(formatDate(fb.getCreationDate()));
        dateLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #CBD5E0; -fx-font-size: 10;");
        ribbon.getChildren().addAll(nameLabel, dateLabel);

        // Avatar
        ImageView avatar = new ImageView();
        avatar.setFitHeight(80);
        avatar.setFitWidth(80);
        javafx.scene.shape.Circle clip = new javafx.scene.shape.Circle(40, 40, 40);
        avatar.setClip(clip);
        loadAuthorPhoto(avatar, fb);
        
        StackPane avatarContainer = new StackPane(avatar);
        avatarContainer.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-background-radius: 45; -fx-padding: 3; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.1), 5, 0, 0, 2);");
        avatarContainer.setMaxSize(86, 86);

        // Etoiles
        Label starsLabel = new Label(getStars(fb.getRating()));
        starsLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 18; -fx-text-fill: #F6AD55;");

        // Commentaire
        HBox commentBox = new HBox(15);
        Region accentLine = new Region();
        accentLine.setPrefWidth(3);
        accentLine.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #3B5998; -fx-background-radius: 2;");
        
        Label commentLabel = new Label(fb.getComment());
        commentLabel.setWrapText(true);
        commentLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 13; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-font-style: italic;");
        commentLabel.setPrefHeight(80);
        commentLabel.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        commentBox.getChildren().addAll(accentLine, commentLabel);

        // Actions
        HBox actions = new HBox(10);
        actions.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
        if (fb.getUserId() == Main.getCurrentUserId()) {
            Button editBtn = new Button();
            SVGPath editIcon = new SVGPath();
            editIcon.setContent("M 3 17.25 V 21 h 3.75 L 17.81 9.94 l -3.75 -3.75 L 3 17.25 z M 20.71 7.04 c 0.39 -0.39 0.39 -1.02 0 -1.41 l -2.34 -2.34 c -0.39 -0.39 -1.02 -0.39 -1.41 0 l -1.83 1.83 l 3.75 3.75 l 1.83 -1.83 z");
            editIcon.setFill(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#2B6CB0"));
            editBtn.setGraphic(editIcon);
            editBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #BEE3F8; -fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-padding: 8; -fx-cursor: hand;");
            editBtn.setOnAction(e -> Main.showEditFeedback(fb, currentTargetType, currentTargetId, currentTargetName));

            Button deleteBtn = new Button();
            SVGPath trashIcon = new SVGPath();
            trashIcon.setContent("M 6 19 c 0 1.1 0.9 2 2 2 h 8 c 1.1 0 2 -0.9 2 -2 V 7 H 6 v 12 z M 19 4 h -3.5 l -1 -1 h -5 l -1 1 H 5 v 2 h 14 V 4 z");
            trashIcon.setFill(javafx.scene.paint.Color.web("#C53030"));
            deleteBtn.setGraphic(trashIcon);
            deleteBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FED7D7; -fx-background-radius: 20; -fx-padding: 8; -fx-cursor: hand;");
            deleteBtn.setOnAction(e -> deleteFeedback(fb));
            actions.getChildren().addAll(editBtn, deleteBtn);
        }

        cardBody.getChildren().addAll(starsLabel, commentBox, actions);

        cardRoot.getChildren().addAll(cardBody, ribbon, avatarContainer);
        StackPane.setAlignment(ribbon, Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        StackPane.setAlignment(avatarContainer, Pos.TOP_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(avatarContainer, new Insets(0, 20, 0, 0));

        return cardRoot;
    }

    private void loadAuthorPhoto(ImageView imageView, feedback fb) {
        // Si le feedback est anonyme et qu'un avatar a été choisi, l'afficher
        if (fb.isAnonyme() && fb.getAvatarUrl() != null && !fb.getAvatarUrl().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                byte[] bytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(fb.getAvatarUrl().trim().replaceAll("\\s+", ""));
                imageView.setImage(new Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                return;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                // fallback ci-dessous
            }
        }

        // Feedback non anonyme : afficher la photo de profil de l'auteur
        if (!fb.isAnonyme()) {
            try {
                services.userServices userService = new services.userServices();
                for (entities.user u : userService.getAll()) {
                    if (u.getUserId() == fb.getUserId()) {
                        if (u.getUserPhoto() != null && !u.getUserPhoto().isEmpty()) {
                            byte[] bytes = java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(u.getUserPhoto().trim().replaceAll("\\s+", ""));
                            imageView.setImage(new Image(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                            return;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                // fallback ci-dessous
            }
        }

        // Fallback : avatar par défaut
        imageView.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
    }

    private String getAuthorName(int userId) {
        try {
            services.userServices userService = new services.userServices();
            for (entities.user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == userId) {
                    return u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName();
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {}
        return "Patient";
    }

    private void deleteFeedback(feedback fb) {
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirm.setTitle("Confirmation");
        confirm.setHeaderText("Supprimer le feedback");
        confirm.setContentText("Êtes-vous sûr de vouloir supprimer ce feedback ?");

        if (confirm.showAndWait().orElse(ButtonType.CANCEL) == ButtonType.OK) {
            try {
                feedbackService.delete(fb);
                showAlert("Succès", "Feedback supprimé avec succès !");
                refreshFeedbacks();
            } catch (SQLException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la suppression: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void resetFilters() {
        searchField.clear();
        if (ratingCombo != null) ratingCombo.setValue("Toutes les notes");
        sortCombo.setValue("Plus récents");
        applyFilters();
    }

    @FXML
    private void showAddFeedbackDialog() {
        Main.showAddFeedback(currentTargetType, currentTargetId, currentTargetName);
    }

    @FXML
    private void goBack() {
        String role = Main.getCurrentUserRole();
        if (role.equals("doctor")) {
            Main.showDoctorPatients();
        } else {
            if (currentTargetType.equals("doctor")) {
                Main.showPatientDoctors();
            } else {
                Main.showPatientPharmacies();
            }
        }
    }

    private String getStars(int rating) {
        StringBuilder stars = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < rating; i++) stars.append("★");
        for (int i = rating; i < 5; i++) stars.append("☆");
        return stars.toString();
    }

    private String formatDate(Timestamp timestamp) {
        if (timestamp == null) return "Date inconnue";
        return timestamp.toLocalDateTime().format(java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm"));
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.AnchorPane;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class PatientMainController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private AnchorPane contentContainer;
    @FXML private AnchorPane mainAnchor; // Le nouveau conteneur parent
    @FXML private ImageView logoView;
    @FXML private ImageView profileImageView;
    @FXML private Label userNameLabel;

    private static PatientMainController instance;
    
    public static PatientMainController getInstance() {
        return instance;
    }

    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private Parent activeChatbot = null;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        instance = this;
        try {
            Image logo = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/logo.png"));
            if (logo != null) logoView.setImage(logo);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Logo non trouvé");
        }
        loadUserInfo();
    }

    public void loadUserInfo() {
        try {
            int userId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == userId) {
                    userNameLabel.setText(u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName());
                    String photoBase64 = (u.getAvatarUrl() != null && !u.getAvatarUrl().isEmpty()) ? u.getAvatarUrl() : u.getUserPhoto();
                    
                    if (photoBase64 != null && photoBase64.startsWith("generated_")) {
                        photoBase64 = u.getUserPhoto();
                    }

                    if (photoBase64 != null && !photoBase64.isEmpty()) {
                        try {
                            String cleanBase64 = photoBase64.trim().replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]", "");
                            byte[] imageBytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(cleanBase64);
                            Image image = new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(imageBytes));
                            profileImageView.setImage(image);
                        } catch (Exception e) { setDefaultProfileImage(); }
                    } else { setDefaultProfileImage(); }
                    break;
                }
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    private void setDefaultProfileImage() {
        try {
            Image defaultImage = new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png", 80, 80, true, true);
            if (defaultImage != null) profileImageView.setImage(defaultImage);
        } catch (Exception e) {}
    }

    public void updateContent(Parent content) {
        if (contentContainer != null) {
            contentContainer.getChildren().clear();
            contentContainer.getChildren().add(content);
            AnchorPane.setTopAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setBottomAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setLeftAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setRightAnchor(content, 0.0);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void toggleChatbot() {
        if (activeChatbot != null) {
            mainAnchor.getChildren().remove(activeChatbot);
            activeChatbot = null;
        } else {
            try {
                FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/ChatbotView.fxml"));
                activeChatbot = loader.load();
                
                // Positionnement flottant en bas à droite
                mainAnchor.getChildren().add(activeChatbot);
                AnchorPane.setBottomAnchor(activeChatbot, 100.0); // Au-dessus du bouton
                AnchorPane.setRightAnchor(activeChatbot, 30.0);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML private void goToDashboard() { Main.showPatientDashboard(); }
    @FXML private void goToProfile() { Main.showUserProfile(); }
    @FXML private void goToDoctors() { Main.showPatientDoctors(); }
    @FXML private void goToAppointments() { Main.showPatientAppointments(); }
    @FXML private void goToRequestAppointment() { Main.showPatientRequestAppointment(); }
    @FXML private void goToPharmacies() { Main.showPatientPharmacies(); }
    @FXML private void goToMedicalFiles() { Main.showPatientMedicalFiles(); }
    @FXML private void goToPrescriptions() { Main.showPatientPrescriptions(); }
    @FXML private void goToProducts() { Main.showPatientProducts(); }
    @FXML
    private void goToEvents() {
        Main.showPatientEvents();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToHealthStats() {
        Main.showPatientHealthStats();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToPillTracker() {
        Main.showPatientPillTracker();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToReclamation() {
        Main.showPatientReclamation();
    }
    @FXML private void logout() { Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import entities.user.Role;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class PatientPharmacyListController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> locationFilter;
    @FXML private Label statusLabel;
    @FXML private FlowPane cardsContainer;

    @FXML private HBox paginationBox;

    private ObservableList<user> allPharmacyList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private ObservableList<user> filteredPharmacyList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private ObservableList<user> currentPageList = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
    private userServices userService = new userServices();

    private int itemsPerPage = 8;
    private int currentPage = 1;
    private int totalPages = 1;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        locationFilter.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Toutes les villes", "Ariana", "Béja", "Ben Arous", "Bizerte", "Gabès",
                "Gafsa", "Jendouba", "Kairouan", "Kasserine", "Kébili", "Le Kef",
                "Mahdia", "La Manouba", "Médenine", "Monastir", "Nabeul", "Sfax",
                "Sidi Bouzid", "Siliana", "Sousse", "Tataouine", "Tozeur", "Tunis", "Zaghouan"
        ));
        locationFilter.setValue("Toutes les villes");

        searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldText, newText) -> {
            currentPage = 1;
            filterPharmacies();
        });

        locationFilter.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            currentPage = 1;
            filterPharmacies();
        });
    }

    public void loadPharmacies() {
        try {
            List<user> allUsers = userService.getAll();
            List<user> pharmacies = allUsers.stream()
                    .filter(u -> u.getRole() == Role.pharmacist && u.getAccountStatus() == user.AccountStatus.active)
                    .collect(Collectors.toList());
            allPharmacyList.setAll(pharmacies);
            filterPharmacies();
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            statusLabel.setText("Erreur de chargement");
        }
    }

    private void filterPharmacies() {
        String searchText = searchField.getText().toLowerCase();
        String locText = locationFilter.getValue();

        List<user> filtered = allPharmacyList.stream()
            .filter(u -> {
                boolean matchSearch = true;
                if (!searchText.isEmpty()) {
                    matchSearch = (u.getPharmacyName() != null && u.getPharmacyName().toLowerCase().contains(searchText)) ||
                                 u.getFirstName().toLowerCase().contains(searchText) ||
                                 u.getLastName().toLowerCase().contains(searchText);
                }
                
                boolean matchLoc = true;
                if (!"Toutes les villes".equals(locText)) {
                    matchLoc = (u.getAddress() != null && u.getAddress().toLowerCase().contains(locText.toLowerCase()));
                }
                
                return matchSearch && matchLoc;
            })
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        filteredPharmacyList.setAll(filtered);
        updatePagination();
        displayCurrentPage();
        statusLabel.setText("Total: " + filteredPharmacyList.size() + " pharmacies");
    }

    @FXML
    public void filterPharmaciesBtn() {
        currentPage = 1;
        filterPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void resetFilters() {
        searchField.setText("");
        locationFilter.setValue("Toutes les villes");
        currentPage = 1;
        filterPharmacies();
    }

    private void updatePagination() {
        totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) filteredPharmacyList.size() / itemsPerPage);
        if (totalPages == 0) totalPages = 1;
        if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages;

        createPaginationButtons();
    }

    private void createPaginationButtons() {
        if (paginationBox == null) return;
        paginationBox.getChildren().clear();

        Button prevBtn = new Button("◀");
        prevBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #4A5568; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-padding: 3 8; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11;");
        prevBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
            if (currentPage > 1) {
                currentPage--;
                displayCurrentPage();
                createPaginationButtons();
            }
        });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(prevBtn);

        int startPage = Math.max(1, currentPage - 2);
        int endPage = Math.min(totalPages, startPage + 4);

        if (startPage > 1) {
            Button firstBtn = createPageButton(1);
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(firstBtn);
            if (startPage > 2) {
                Label dots = new Label("...");
                dots.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-padding: 2; -fx-font-size: 11;");
                paginationBox.getChildren().add(dots);
            }
        }

        for (int i = startPage; i <= endPage; i++) {
            Button pageBtn = createPageButton(i);
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(pageBtn);
        }

        if (endPage < totalPages) {
            if (endPage < totalPages - 1) {
                Label dots = new Label("...");
                dots.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-padding: 2; -fx-font-size: 11;");
                paginationBox.getChildren().add(dots);
            }
            Button lastBtn = createPageButton(totalPages);
            paginationBox.getChildren().add(lastBtn);
        }

        Button nextBtn = new Button("▶");
        nextBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #4A5568; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-padding: 3 8; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11;");
        nextBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
            if (currentPage < totalPages) {
                currentPage++;
                displayCurrentPage();
                createPaginationButtons();
            }
        });
        paginationBox.getChildren().add(nextBtn);
    }

    private Button createPageButton(int pageNum) {
        Button btn = new Button(String.valueOf(pageNum));
        String style = (pageNum == currentPage)
                ? "-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-padding: 3 8; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11;"
                : "-fx-background-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-background-radius: 4; -fx-padding: 3 8; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 11;";
        btn.setStyle(style);
        btn.setOnAction(e -> {
            currentPage = pageNum;
            displayCurrentPage();
            createPaginationButtons();
        });
        return btn;
    }

    private void displayCurrentPage() {
        int start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, filteredPharmacyList.size());

        currentPageList.clear();
        if (start < filteredPharmacyList.size()) {
            currentPageList.setAll(filteredPharmacyList.subList(start, end));
        }

        displayCards(currentPageList);
    }

    private void displayCards(ObservableList<user> users) {
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();

        if (users.isEmpty()) {
            Label emptyLabel = new Label("Aucune pharmacie trouvée");
            emptyLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 16;");
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(emptyLabel);
            return;
        }

        for (user u : users) {
            VBox card = createPharmacyCard(u);
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(card);
        }
    }

    private VBox createPharmacyCard(user pharmacy) {
        VBox card = new VBox(10);
        card.setStyle("-fx-background-color: white; -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-border-radius: 15; -fx-background-radius: 15; -fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.08), 10, 0, 0, 2);");
        card.setPadding(new Insets(15));
        card.setPrefWidth(280);

        HBox headerBox = new HBox(10);
        headerBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        ImageView profileView = new ImageView();
        profileView.setFitHeight(55);
        profileView.setFitWidth(55);
        
        // Clip circulaire
        javafx.scene.shape.Circle clip = new javafx.scene.shape.Circle(27.5, 27.5, 27.5);
        profileView.setClip(clip);

        if (pharmacy.getUserPhoto() != null && !pharmacy.getUserPhoto().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                String cleanBase64 = pharmacy.getUserPhoto().trim().replaceAll("\\s+", "");
                byte[] imageBytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(cleanBase64);
                profileView.setImage(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(imageBytes)));
            } catch (Exception e) {
                profileView.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
            }
        } else {
            profileView.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
        }

        VBox nameBox = new VBox(3);
        Label nameLabel = new Label(pharmacy.getPharmacyName() != null ? pharmacy.getPharmacyName() : pharmacy.getFirstName());
        nameLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 16; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");

        Label typeLabel = new Label("Pharmacie");
        typeLabel.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #ED8936; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-padding: 2 8; -fx-background-radius: 10; -fx-font-size: 11;");

        nameBox.getChildren().addAll(nameLabel, typeLabel);
        headerBox.getChildren().addAll(profileView, nameBox);

        VBox infoBox = new VBox(5);
        Label emailLabel = new Label("📧 " + pharmacy.getEmail());
        emailLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 12;");
        Label phoneLabel = new Label("📞 " + pharmacy.getPhone());
        phoneLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 12;");
        
        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(emailLabel, phoneLabel);

        // Boutons
        HBox buttonsBox = new HBox(10);
        buttonsBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);

        Button appointementBtn = new Button("📅 Contacter");
        appointementBtn.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #FF5C5C; -fx-text-fill: white; -fx-background-radius: 8; -fx-padding: 6 12; -fx-cursor: hand; -fx-font-size: 12;");
        appointementBtn.setOnAction(e -> showAlert("Contact", "Fonctionnalité de contact à venir"));

        buttonsBox.getChildren().addAll(appointementBtn);

        card.getChildren().addAll(headerBox, new Separator(), infoBox, buttonsBox);
        return card;
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.AnchorPane;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class PharmacistMainController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private AnchorPane contentContainer;
    @FXML private ImageView  logoView;
    @FXML private ImageView  profileImageView;
    @FXML private Label      userNameLabel;

    private static PharmacistMainController instance;
    private final userServices userService = new userServices();

    public static PharmacistMainController getInstance() { return instance; }

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL location, ResourceBundle resources) {
        instance = this;
        try {
            Image logo = new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/logo.png"));
            if (logo != null) logoView.setImage(logo);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        loadUserInfo();
    }

    public void loadUserInfo() {
        try {
            int userId = Main.getCurrentUserId();
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == userId) {
                    userNameLabel.setText(u.getFirstName() + " " + u.getLastName());
                    String photoBase64 = (u.getAvatarUrl() != null && !u.getAvatarUrl().isEmpty())
                        ? u.getAvatarUrl() : u.getUserPhoto();
                    if (photoBase64 != null && photoBase64.startsWith("generated_"))
                        photoBase64 = u.getUserPhoto();
                    if (photoBase64 != null && !photoBase64.isEmpty()) {
                        try {
                            byte[] bytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(
                                photoBase64.trim().replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]", ""));
                            profileImageView.setImage(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                        } catch (Exception e) { setDefaultProfileImage(); }
                    } else { setDefaultProfileImage(); }
                    break;
                }
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    private void setDefaultProfileImage() {
        try {
            profileImageView.setImage(
                new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png", 80, 80, true, true));
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    public void updateContent(javafx.scene.Parent content) {
        if (contentContainer != null) {
            contentContainer.getChildren().clear();
            contentContainer.getChildren().add(content);
            AnchorPane.setTopAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setBottomAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setLeftAnchor(content, 0.0);
            AnchorPane.setRightAnchor(content, 0.0);
        }
    }

    @FXML private void goToStock()  { Main.showPharmacyStock(); }
    @FXML private void goToEvents() { Main.showPharmacistEvents(); }
    @FXML private void logout()     { Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Pharmacy;
import entities.Product;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ButtonType;
import javafx.scene.control.ComboBox;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ListCell;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import services.PharmacyServices;
import services.PharmacyStockService;
import tests.Main;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public class PharmacyAddStockController {

    @FXML private Label pageTitleLabel;
    @FXML private Label pageSubtitleLabel;
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;

    @FXML private ComboBox<Pharmacy> pharmacyCombo;
    @FXML private ComboBox<Product> productCombo;
    @FXML private TextField quantityField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> statusCombo;

    @FXML private Button saveButton;

    private PharmacyServices pharmacyServices;
    private PharmacyStockService pharmacyStockService;

    private static Product selectedProduct;

    public static void setSelectedProduct(Product product) {
        selectedProduct = product;
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        pharmacyServices = new PharmacyServices();
        pharmacyStockService = new PharmacyStockService();

        setupStatusCombo();
        loadPharmacies();

        if (selectedProduct == null) {
            loadAddMode();
        } else {
            loadUpdateMode();
        }
    }

    private void setupStatusCombo() {
        statusCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Disponible",
                "Stock faible",
                "Rupture"
        ));

        statusCombo.setValue("Disponible");
    }

    private void loadPharmacies() {
        try {
            List<Pharmacy> pharmacies = pharmacyServices.getAll();
            pharmacyCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(pharmacies));

            pharmacyCombo.setCellFactory(param -> new ListCell<>() {
                @Override
                protected void updateItem(Pharmacy pharmacy, boolean empty) {
                    super.updateItem(pharmacy, empty);
                    setText(empty || pharmacy == null ? null : pharmacy.getPharmacyName());
                }
            });

            pharmacyCombo.setButtonCell(new ListCell<>() {
                @Override
                protected void updateItem(Pharmacy pharmacy, boolean empty) {
                    super.updateItem(pharmacy, empty);
                    setText(empty || pharmacy == null ? "Choisir une pharmacie" : pharmacy.getPharmacyName());
                }
            });

            pharmacyCombo.setOnAction(event -> {
                Pharmacy selected = pharmacyCombo.getValue();

                if (selected != null && selectedProduct == null) {
                    loadProductsForStock();
                }
            });

            if (!pharmacies.isEmpty()) {
                pharmacyCombo.setValue(pharmacies.get(0));

                if (selectedProduct == null) {
                    loadProductsForStock();
                }
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de charger les pharmacies.");
        }
    }

    private void loadProductsForStock() {
        try {
            List<Product> products = pharmacyStockService.getAllProductsForStock();
            productCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(products));

            productCombo.setCellFactory(param -> new ListCell<>() {
                @Override
                protected void updateItem(Product product, boolean empty) {
                    super.updateItem(product, empty);
                    setText(empty || product == null ? null : formatProductName(product));
                }
            });

            productCombo.setButtonCell(new ListCell<>() {
                @Override
                protected void updateItem(Product product, boolean empty) {
                    super.updateItem(product, empty);
                    setText(empty || product == null ? "Choisir un produit" : formatProductName(product));
                }
            });

            if (!products.isEmpty()) {
                productCombo.setValue(products.get(0));
                fillProductStockFields(products.get(0));
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de charger les produits.");
        }
    }

    private void loadAddMode() {
        pageTitleLabel.setText("Ajouter stock");
        pageSubtitleLabel.setText("Choisissez la pharmacie, le produit, la quantite et le statut.");
        saveButton.setText("Enregistrer");

        pharmacyCombo.setDisable(false);
        productCombo.setDisable(false);

        productCombo.setOnAction(event -> {
            Product product = productCombo.getValue();

            if (product != null) {
                fillProductStockFields(product);
            }
        });

        messageLabel.setText("");
    }

    private void loadUpdateMode() {
        pageTitleLabel.setText("Modifier le stock");
        pageSubtitleLabel.setText("Modifiez la quantite et le statut du medicament.");
        saveButton.setText("Enregistrer");

        selectPharmacyById(selectedProduct.getPharmacyId());

        pharmacyCombo.setDisable(true);
        productCombo.setDisable(true);

        productCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(selectedProduct));
        productCombo.setValue(selectedProduct);

        productCombo.setCellFactory(param -> new ListCell<>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(Product product, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(product, empty);
                setText(empty || product == null ? null : formatProductName(product));
            }
        });

        productCombo.setButtonCell(new ListCell<>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(Product product, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(product, empty);
                setText(empty || product == null ? "Produit" : formatProductName(product));
            }
        });

        fillProductStockFields(selectedProduct);
        messageLabel.setText("");
    }

    @FXML
    public void saveStock() {
        Pharmacy pharmacy = pharmacyCombo.getValue();
        Product product = selectedProduct == null ? productCombo.getValue() : selectedProduct;

        if (pharmacy == null) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez choisir une pharmacie.");
            return;
        }

        if (product == null) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez choisir un produit.");
            return;
        }

        String quantityText = quantityField.getText() == null ? "" : quantityField.getText().trim();
        String status = statusCombo.getValue() == null ? "Disponible" : statusCombo.getValue();

        if (quantityText.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Veuillez saisir la quantite.");
            return;
        }

        int quantity;

        try {
            quantity = Integer.parseInt(quantityText);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Quantite invalide", "Veuillez saisir un nombre valide.");
            return;
        }

        if (quantity < 0) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Quantite invalide", "La quantite ne peut pas etre negative.");
            return;
        }

        pharmacyStockService.updateProductStock(
                product.getProductId(),
                pharmacy.getPharmacyId(),
                quantity,
                status
        );

        showSuccessAndGoBack("Stock enregistre avec succes.");
    }

    @FXML
    public void clearForm() {
        if (selectedProduct == null) {
            Product product = productCombo.getValue();

            if (product != null) {
                fillProductStockFields(product);
            } else {
                quantityField.clear();
                statusCombo.setValue("Disponible");
            }
        } else {
            loadUpdateMode();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goBackStock() {
        selectedProduct = null;
        Main.showPharmacyStock();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goStock() {
        selectedProduct = null;
        Main.showPharmacyStock();
    }

    private void fillProductStockFields(Product product) {
        quantityField.setText(String.valueOf(product.getAvailableQuantity()));

        String status = product.getAvailabilityStatus();
        statusCombo.setValue(status == null || status.trim().isEmpty() ? "Disponible" : status);
    }

    private void selectPharmacyById(int pharmacyId) {
        if (pharmacyCombo == null || pharmacyCombo.getItems() == null) {
            return;
        }

        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : pharmacyCombo.getItems()) {
            if (pharmacy.getPharmacyId() == pharmacyId) {
                pharmacyCombo.setValue(pharmacy);
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private String formatProductName(Product product) {
        String dosage = safe(product.getDosage());

        if (dosage.isEmpty()) {
            return safe(product.getProductName());
        }

        return safe(product.getProductName()) + " - " + dosage;
    }

    private void showSuccessAndGoBack(String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle("Succes");
        alert.setHeaderText("Succes");
        alert.setContentText(message);

        Optional<ButtonType> result = alert.showAndWait();

        if (result.isPresent()) {
            selectedProduct = null;
            Main.showPharmacyStock();
        }
    }

    private void showAlert(Alert.AlertType type, String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(type);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(title);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Pharmacy;
import entities.Product;
import javafx.animation.FadeTransition;
import javafx.animation.ParallelTransition;
import javafx.animation.ScaleTransition;
import javafx.animation.TranslateTransition;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.concurrent.Task;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ComboBox;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ListCell;
import javafx.scene.control.TextArea;
import javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow;

import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;

public class PharmacyStockController {

    @FXML private ComboBox<Pharmacy> pharmacyCombo;
    @FXML private javafx.scene.layout.FlowPane stockContainer;
    @FXML private Label sectionTitleLabel;

    private PharmacyServices pharmacyServices;
    private PharmacyStockService pharmacyStockService;
    private OpenFdaRecallService openFdaRecallService;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        pharmacyServices = new PharmacyServices();
        pharmacyStockService = new PharmacyStockService();
        openFdaRecallService = new OpenFdaRecallService();

        loadPharmacies();
    }

    private void loadPharmacies() {
        try {
            List<Pharmacy> pharmacies = pharmacyServices.getAll();
            pharmacyCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(pharmacies));

            pharmacyCombo.setCellFactory(param -> new ListCell<>() {
                @Override
                protected void updateItem(Pharmacy pharmacy, boolean empty) {
                    super.updateItem(pharmacy, empty);
                    setText(empty || pharmacy == null ? null : pharmacy.getPharmacyName());
                }
            });

            pharmacyCombo.setButtonCell(new ListCell<>() {
                @Override
                protected void updateItem(Pharmacy pharmacy, boolean empty) {
                    super.updateItem(pharmacy, empty);
                    setText(empty || pharmacy == null ? "Choisir une pharmacie" : pharmacy.getPharmacyName());
                }
            });

            pharmacyCombo.setOnAction(event -> {
                Pharmacy selected = pharmacyCombo.getValue();

                if (selected != null) {
                    loadProductsByPharmacy(selected);
                }
            });

            if (!pharmacies.isEmpty()) {
                pharmacyCombo.setValue(pharmacies.get(0));
                loadProductsByPharmacy(pharmacies.get(0));
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible de charger les pharmacies.");
        }
    }

    private void loadProductsByPharmacy(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        stockContainer.getChildren().clear();

        if (sectionTitleLabel != null) {
            sectionTitleLabel.setText("Produits de : " + safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyName()));
        }

        try {
            List<Product> products = pharmacyStockService.getProductsByPharmacy(pharmacy.getPharmacyId());

            if (products.isEmpty()) {
                stockContainer.getChildren().add(createEmptyState(pharmacy));
                return;
            }

            int index = 0;

            for (Product product : products) {
                VBox card = createProductCard(product);
                stockContainer.getChildren().add(card);
                playCardEntrance(card, index);
                index++;
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR, "Erreur", "Impossible d'afficher les produits.");
        }
    }

    private VBox createEmptyState(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        VBox emptyBox = new VBox(10);
        emptyBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        emptyBox.setPrefWidth(940);
        emptyBox.setPrefHeight(260);
        emptyBox.setPadding(new Insets(28));
        emptyBox.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FFFFFF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 18;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #E2E8F0;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 18;"
        );

        DropShadow shadow = new DropShadow();
        shadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.06));
        shadow.setRadius(18);
        shadow.setOffsetY(5);
        emptyBox.setEffect(shadow);

        Label title = new Label("Aucun produit lie a cette pharmacie");
        title.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 18px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
        );

        Label text = new Label("Utilisez le bouton + Ajouter stock pour associer un medicament a "
                + safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyName()) + ".");
        text.setWrapText(true);
        text.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #64748B;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;"
        );

        emptyBox.getChildren().addAll(title, text);
        return emptyBox;
    }

    private VBox createProductCard(Product product) {
        VBox card = new VBox(11);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(16));
        card.setPrefWidth(295);
        card.setMinWidth(295);
        card.setMaxWidth(295);
        card.setMinHeight(445);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FFFFFF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 18;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #E2E8F0;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 18;"
        );

        DropShadow shadow = new DropShadow();
        shadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.07));
        shadow.setRadius(18);
        shadow.setOffsetY(5);
        card.setEffect(shadow);

        StackPane imageFrame = createImageFrame(product);
        Region separatorTop = createSeparator();

        Label name = new Label(safe(product.getProductName()));
        name.setWrapText(true);
        name.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        name.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 18px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;"
        );

        HBox priceAndStatus = new HBox(10);
        priceAndStatus.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        Label price = new Label(formatPrice(product.getPrice()) + " DT");
        price.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 19px;" +
