                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #10B981;"
        );

        Region priceSpacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(priceSpacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label status = createStatusBadge(product.getAvailabilityStatus());
        priceAndStatus.getChildren().addAll(price, priceSpacer, status);

        HBox quantityRow = createInfoRow("Quantite disponible", String.valueOf(product.getAvailableQuantity()));

        Region spacer = new Region();
        VBox.setVgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Region separatorBottom = createSeparator();

        Button editButton = new Button("Modifier stock");
        editButton.setPrefHeight(44);
        editButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        editButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );

        editButton.setOnAction(event -> {
            PharmacyAddStockController.setSelectedProduct(product);
            Main.showPharmacyAddStock();
        });

        Button safetyButton = new Button("Verifier securite");
        safetyButton.setPrefHeight(42);
        safetyButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        safetyButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FEE2E2;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #EF4444;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );

        safetyButton.setOnAction(event -> verifyProductSafety(product, safetyButton));

        setupButtonHover(editButton);
        setupSafetyButtonHover(safetyButton);
        setupCardHover(card);

        card.getChildren().addAll(
                imageFrame,
                separatorTop,
                name,
                priceAndStatus,
                quantityRow,
                spacer,
                separatorBottom,
                editButton,
                safetyButton
        );

        return card;
    }

    private StackPane createImageFrame(Product product) {
        StackPane frame = new StackPane();
        frame.setPrefWidth(263);
        frame.setMinHeight(140);
        frame.setPrefHeight(140);
        frame.setMaxHeight(140);
        frame.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        frame.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 16;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #DBEAFE;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 16;"
        );

        ImageView imageView = new ImageView();
        imageView.setFitWidth(230);
        imageView.setFitHeight(118);
        imageView.setPreserveRatio(true);
        imageView.setSmooth(true);

        if (loadProductImage(product.getImage(), imageView)) {
            frame.getChildren().add(imageView);
            setupImageHover(imageView);
        } else {
            Label icon = new Label("RX");
            icon.setStyle(
                    "-fx-font-size: 30px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #94A3B8;"
            );
            frame.getChildren().add(icon);
        }

        return frame;
    }

    private HBox createInfoRow(String key, String value) {
        HBox row = new HBox(8);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        row.setPadding(new Insets(10, 12, 10, 12));
        row.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #F8FAFC;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #E2E8F0;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 12;"
        );

        Label keyLabel = new Label(key);
        keyLabel.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #64748B;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 12px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
        );

        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label valueLabel = new Label(value);
        valueLabel.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
        );

        row.getChildren().addAll(keyLabel, spacer, valueLabel);
        return row;
    }

    private Region createSeparator() {
        Region line = new Region();
        line.setPrefHeight(1);
        line.setMaxHeight(1);
        line.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F1F5F9;");
        VBox.setMargin(line, new Insets(2, 0, 2, 0));
        return line;
    }

    private boolean loadProductImage(String imagePath, ImageView imageView) {
        try {
            if (imagePath != null && !imagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                File file = new File(imagePath);

                if (file.exists()) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                    return true;
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur image produit : " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return false;
    }

    private Label createStatusBadge(String statusText) {
        String status = safe(statusText);

        if (status.isEmpty()) {
            status = "Rupture";
        }

        String bg = "#FEE2E2";
        String text = "#EF4444";

        if (status.equalsIgnoreCase("Disponible")) {
            bg = "#D1FAE5";
            text = "#10B981";
        } else if (status.equalsIgnoreCase("Stock faible")) {
            bg = "#FEF3C7";
            text = "#F59E0B";
        }

        Label badge = new Label(status);
        badge.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        badge.setMinHeight(30);
        badge.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: " + bg + ";" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: " + text + ";" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 12px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 999;" +
                        "-fx-padding: 7 12 7 12;"
        );

        return badge;
    }

    @FXML
    public void goAddStock() {
        PharmacyAddStockController.setSelectedProduct(null);
        Main.showPharmacyAddStock();
    }

    @FXML
    public void refreshStock() {
        Pharmacy selected = pharmacyCombo.getValue();

        if (selected != null) {
            loadProductsByPharmacy(selected);
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goStock() {
        Main.showPharmacyStock();
    }

    private void verifyProductSafety(Product product, Button sourceButton) {
        if (product == null || safe(product.getProductName()).isEmpty()) {
            showAlert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, "Champ obligatoire", "Nom du medicament introuvable.");
            return;
        }

        String productName = safe(product.getProductName());

        if (sourceButton != null) {
            sourceButton.setDisable(true);
            sourceButton.setText("Verification...");
        }

        Task<List<String>> recallTask = new Task<>() {
            @Override
            protected List<String> call() {
                return openFdaRecallService.searchDrugRecalls(productName);
            }
        };

        recallTask.setOnSucceeded(event -> {
            if (sourceButton != null) {
                sourceButton.setDisable(false);
                sourceButton.setText("Verifier securite");
            }

            List<String> recalls = recallTask.getValue();

            if (recalls == null || recalls.isEmpty()) {
                showAlert(
                        Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION,
                        "Securite medicament",
                        "Aucun rappel trouve pour ce medicament"
                );
                return;
            }

            showRecallDialog(productName, recalls);
        });

        recallTask.setOnFailed(event -> {
            if (sourceButton != null) {
                sourceButton.setDisable(false);
                sourceButton.setText("Verifier securite");
            }

            showAlert(
                    Alert.AlertType.ERROR,
                    "Erreur openFDA",
                    "Impossible de verifier les rappels de ce medicament."
            );
        });

        Thread thread = new Thread(recallTask);
        thread.setDaemon(true);
        thread.start();
    }

    private void showRecallDialog(String productName, List<String> recalls) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle("Securite medicament");
        alert.setHeaderText("Rappels openFDA : " + productName);

        TextArea textArea = new TextArea(formatRecallResults(recalls));
        textArea.setEditable(false);
        textArea.setWrapText(true);
        textArea.setPrefWidth(680);
        textArea.setPrefHeight(360);
        textArea.setStyle(
                "-fx-control-inner-background: #F8FAFC;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;"
        );

        alert.getDialogPane().setContent(textArea);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private String formatRecallResults(List<String> recalls) {
        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();

        for (int i = 0; i < recalls.size(); i++) {
            builder.append("Rappel ").append(i + 1).append("\n");
            builder.append(recalls.get(i));

            if (i < recalls.size() - 1) {
                builder.append("\n\n------------------------------\n\n");
            }
        }

        return builder.toString();
    }

    private void setupCardHover(VBox card) {
        card.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(250), card);
            translate.setToY(-4);

            DropShadow hoverShadow = new DropShadow();
            hoverShadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.11));
            hoverShadow.setRadius(24);
            hoverShadow.setOffsetY(8);
            card.setEffect(hoverShadow);

            translate.play();
        });

        card.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(250), card);
            translate.setToY(0);

            DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
            normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.07));
            normalShadow.setRadius(18);
            normalShadow.setOffsetY(5);
            card.setEffect(normalShadow);

            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupImageHover(ImageView imageView) {
        imageView.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            ScaleTransition scale = new ScaleTransition(Duration.millis(250), imageView);
            scale.setToX(1.03);
            scale.setToY(1.03);
            scale.play();
        });

        imageView.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            ScaleTransition scale = new ScaleTransition(Duration.millis(250), imageView);
            scale.setToX(1.0);
            scale.setToY(1.0);
            scale.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupButtonHover(Button button) {
        button.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #1E293B;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(-2);
            translate.play();
        });

        button.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(0);
            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupSafetyButtonHover(Button button) {
        button.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #FECACA;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #B91C1C;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(-2);
            translate.play();
        });

        button.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #FEE2E2;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #EF4444;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(0);
            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void playCardEntrance(Node node, int index) {
        node.setOpacity(0);
        node.setTranslateY(18);

        FadeTransition fade = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(330), node);
        fade.setFromValue(0);
        fade.setToValue(1);
        fade.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 65L));

        TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(330), node);
        translate.setFromY(18);
        translate.setToY(0);
        translate.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 65L));

        new ParallelTransition(fade, translate).play();
    }

    private String formatPrice(double price) {
        return String.format("%.3f", price).replace(".", ",");
    }

    private void showAlert(Alert.AlertType type, String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(type);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(title);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Prescription;
import entities.userDS;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.event.ActionEvent;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.Prescriptionserv;
import services.userservDS;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import com.itextpdf.text.Document;
import com.itextpdf.text.DocumentException;
import com.itextpdf.text.Element;
import com.itextpdf.text.FontFactory;
import com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph;
import com.itextpdf.text.Phrase;
import com.itextpdf.text.BaseColor;
import com.itextpdf.text.Font;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPCell;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPTable;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfWriter;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;

public class PrescriptionController {

    @FXML private Pagination pagination;
    @FXML private DatePicker filterDate;
    @FXML private ImageView imgDoctor;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorName;
    
    private final int ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 4;
    private List<Prescription> allPrescriptions = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<Prescription> prescriptionsAffichees = new ArrayList<>();
    private int currentPatientId = -1;
    
    private Prescriptionserv prescriptionService;
    private userservDS userService;
    private java.util.Map<Integer, userDS> patientCache = new java.util.HashMap<>();

    public PrescriptionController() {
        prescriptionService = new Prescriptionserv();
        userService = new userservDS();
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        // Listener pour filtrage par date
        filterDate.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> appliquerFiltres());
        
        pagination.setPageFactory(this::createPage);
        chargerPatients();
        chargerPrescriptions();
        chargerProfilMedecin();
    }

    private void chargerPatients() {
        List<userDS> patients = userService.getAll();
        for (userDS p : patients) {
            patientCache.put(p.getUserId(), p);
        }
    }

    private void chargerProfilMedecin() {
        if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Dr. Saif Gharbi");
        List<userDS> docs = userService.searchAllByNameOrSurname("Saif");
        for (userDS d : docs) {
            if (d.getFirstName().equalsIgnoreCase("Saif") && d.getLastName().equalsIgnoreCase("Gharbi")) {
                String photoPath = d.getUserphoto();
                if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty() && imgDoctor != null) {
                    try {
                        File file = new File(photoPath);
                        if (file.exists()) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                        } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath)));
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {}
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void appliquerFiltres() {
        List<Prescription> filtered = new ArrayList<>(allPrescriptions);

        // Filtrer par date uniquement
        if (filterDate.getValue() != null) {
            java.time.LocalDate selectedDate = filterDate.getValue();
            filtered = filtered.stream()
                .filter(p -> p.getPrescriptionDate().equals(selectedDate))
                .toList();
        }

        this.prescriptionsAffichees = filtered;
        updatePagination();
    }

    @FXML
    public void reinitialiserFiltres() {
        filterDate.setValue(null);
        this.prescriptionsAffichees = new ArrayList<>(allPrescriptions);
        updatePagination();
    }

    private Node createPage(int pageIndex) {
        // Cas vide : aucune ordonnance pour ce patient
        if (prescriptionsAffichees.isEmpty()) {
            VBox emptyState = new VBox(20);
            emptyState.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
            emptyState.setPadding(new Insets(80));

            Label icon = new Label("📋");
            icon.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 60;");

            Label msg = new Label("Pas d'ordonnances ici");
            msg.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 22; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568;");

            Label sub = new Label("Ce patient n'a aucune prescription enregistrée.");
            sub.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 14; -fx-text-fill: #A0AEC0;");

            emptyState.getChildren().addAll(icon, msg, sub);
            return emptyState;
        }

        FlowPane flowPane = new FlowPane(20, 20);
        flowPane.setPadding(new Insets(10));
        flowPane.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);

        int fromIndex = pageIndex * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
        int toIndex = Math.min(fromIndex + ITEMS_PER_PAGE, prescriptionsAffichees.size());

        if (fromIndex < prescriptionsAffichees.size()) {
            for (int i = fromIndex; i < toIndex; i++) {
                Prescription p = prescriptionsAffichees.get(i);
                userDS patient = patientCache.get(p.getPatientId());
                if (patient == null) patient = userService.getById(p.getPatientId());
                VBox card = createPrescriptionCard(p, patient);
                flowPane.getChildren().add(card);
            }
        }

        return flowPane;
    }

    private VBox createPrescriptionCard(Prescription p, userDS patient) {
        VBox card = new VBox(10);
        card.getStyleClass().add("patient-card");
        card.setPrefWidth(240);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(12));
        card.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_CENTER);

        // Header (Date)
        HBox header = new HBox();
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        Label lblDate = new Label("📅 " + p.getPrescriptionDate().toString());
        lblDate.getStyleClass().add("card-badge");
        header.getChildren().add(lblDate);

        // Icon (Prescription)
        VBox iconWrapper = new VBox();
        iconWrapper.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        iconWrapper.setMinHeight(60);
        Label iconLabel = new Label("📄");
        iconLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 35;");
        iconWrapper.getChildren().add(iconLabel);

        // Content
        VBox infoBox = new VBox(8);
        infoBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        
        Label lblPatient = new Label(patient != null ? patient.getFullName() : "Patient Inconnu");
        lblPatient.getStyleClass().add("card-title");
        
        Label lblTreatment = new Label(p.getPrescription());
        lblTreatment.setWrapText(true);
        lblTreatment.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #4A5568; -fx-font-size: 13px; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
        
        Label lblDuration = new Label("⏳ " + p.getTreatmentDuration());
        lblDuration.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #718096; -fx-font-size: 12px;");

        infoBox.getChildren().addAll(lblPatient, lblTreatment, lblDuration);

        // Actions
        HBox actionBox = new HBox(10);
        actionBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        
        Button btnVoir = new Button("👁 Voir");
        btnVoir.getStyleClass().add("btn-card-action");
        btnVoir.setOnAction(e -> consulterPrescription(p, patient));
        
        Button btnModif = new Button("✎ Modif");
        btnModif.getStyleClass().add("btn-card-action");
        btnModif.setOnAction(e -> modifierPrescription(p));
        
        Button btnDel = new Button("🗑");
        btnDel.getStyleClass().add("btn-card-delete");
        btnDel.setOnAction(e -> supprimerPrescription(p));

        actionBox.getChildren().addAll(btnVoir, btnModif, btnDel);

        card.getChildren().addAll(header, iconWrapper, infoBox, actionBox);
        return card;
    }

    private void modifierPrescription(Prescription p) {
        tests.Main.showModifierPrescription(p);
    }

    private void consulterPrescription(Prescription p, userDS patient) {
        tests.Main.showConsultationPrescription(p, patient);
    }

    private void chargerPrescriptions() {
        try {
            // Sécurité stricte : Ne charger QUE les ordonnances de CE médecin
            allPrescriptions = prescriptionService.getByDoctorId(tests.Main.currentUserId);
            this.prescriptionsAffichees = allPrescriptions.stream()
                .filter(p -> p.getDoctorId() == tests.Main.currentUserId)
                .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toList());
            updatePagination();
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    public void filterByPatient(int patientId) {
        this.currentPatientId = patientId;
        try {
            // Sécurité stricte : Ne récupérer que les prescriptions de CE médecin pour CE patient
            List<Prescription> doctorPrescriptions = prescriptionService.getByDoctorId(tests.Main.currentUserId);
            this.allPrescriptions = doctorPrescriptions.stream()
                .filter(p -> p.getDoctorId() == tests.Main.currentUserId)
                .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toList());
            
            this.prescriptionsAffichees = allPrescriptions.stream()
                .filter(p -> p.getPatientId() == patientId)
                .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toList());
            updatePagination();
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void updatePagination() {
        int pageCount = (int) Math.ceil((double) prescriptionsAffichees.size() / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
        pagination.setPageCount(pageCount > 0 ? pageCount : 1);
        pagination.setCurrentPageIndex(0);
        pagination.setPageFactory(this::createPage);
    }

    private void supprimerPrescription(Prescription p) {
        Alert confirmation = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirmation.setTitle("Confirmation");
        confirmation.setHeaderText("Supprimer la prescription");
        confirmation.setContentText("Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer cette prescription ?");
        
        if (confirmation.showAndWait().get() == ButtonType.OK) {
            try {
                prescriptionService.delete(p);
                chargerPrescriptions();
            } catch (SQLException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerAjouterPrescription(javafx.event.Event event) {
        if (currentPatientId != -1) {
            tests.Main.showAjouterPrescriptionForPatientId(currentPatientId);
        } else {
            tests.Main.showAjouterPrescription();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerStatistiques(javafx.event.Event event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/Statistiques.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.setTitle("Mes Statistiques");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerGererPatients(javafx.event.Event event) {
        // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }
    
    @FXML
    public void allerAjouterDossier(javafx.event.Event event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/AjoutermedicalFile.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
            stage.setTitle("Ajouter dossier médical");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void exporterPDF() {
        if (prescriptionsAffichees.isEmpty()) {
            showAlert("Export PDF", "Aucune prescription à exporter.");
            return;
        }

        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Enregistrer le rapport des Prescriptions");
        fileChooser.setInitialFileName("Rapport_Prescriptions.pdf");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("PDF Files", "*.pdf"));
        
        File file = fileChooser.showSaveDialog(pagination.getScene().getWindow());
        
        if (file != null) {
            Document document = new Document();
            try {
                PdfWriter.getInstance(document, new FileOutputStream(file));
                document.open();
                
                // Couleurs du design (Thème Vert)
                BaseColor colorPrimary = new BaseColor(0, 153, 41); // Vert #009929
                BaseColor colorSecondary = new BaseColor(74, 85, 104); // Bleu Ardoise #4A5568
                BaseColor colorHeaderBg = new BaseColor(45, 55, 72); // Gris foncé #2D3748
                BaseColor colorBorder = new BaseColor(226, 232, 240); // Gris clair #E2E8F0

                // Polices
                Font fontTitle = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 22, Font.BOLD, colorPrimary);
                Font fontHeader = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 12, Font.BOLD, BaseColor.WHITE);
                Font fontData = FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 11, Font.NORMAL, colorSecondary);
                
                // Titre principal
                Paragraph title = new Paragraph("RAPPORT DES PRESCRIPTIONS - SHIFAA", fontTitle);
                title.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                title.setSpacingAfter(10);
                document.add(title);
                
                // Sous-titre
                Paragraph subtitle = new Paragraph("Généré le : " + java.time.LocalDate.now().toString(), 
                                                FontFactory.getFont(FontFactory.HELVETICA, 10, Font.NORMAL, BaseColor.GRAY));
                subtitle.setAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                subtitle.setSpacingAfter(30);
                document.add(subtitle);
                
                // Table
                PdfPTable table = new PdfPTable(4); // ID, Patient, Date, Traitement
                table.setWidthPercentage(100);
                table.setSpacingBefore(10f);
                float[] columnWidths = {1f, 3f, 2f, 5f};
                table.setWidths(columnWidths);
                
                // Headers
                String[] headers = {"ID", "PATIENT", "DATE", "PRESCRIPTION / TRAITEMENT"};
                for (String h : headers) {
                    PdfPCell cell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(h, fontHeader));
                    cell.setBackgroundColor(colorHeaderBg);
                    cell.setHorizontalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
                    cell.setVerticalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_MIDDLE);
                    cell.setPadding(10);
                    cell.setBorderColor(colorBorder);
                    table.addCell(cell);
                }
                
                // Data
                boolean alternate = false;
                for (Prescription p : prescriptionsAffichees) {
                    userDS patient = userService.getById(p.getPatientId());
                    String patientName = (patient != null) ? patient.getFullName() : "N/A";
                    
                    BaseColor rowColor = alternate ? new BaseColor(247, 250, 252) : BaseColor.WHITE;
                    
                    addStyledCell(table, String.valueOf(p.getPrescriptionId()), fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, patientName, fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, p.getPrescriptionDate().toString(), fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    addStyledCell(table, p.getPrescription(), fontData, rowColor, colorBorder);
                    
                    alternate = !alternate;
                }
                
                document.add(table);
                document.close();
                
                showAlert("Succès", "Le rapport PDF a été généré avec succès !");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                showAlert("Erreur", "Erreur lors de la génération : " + e.getMessage());
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }

    private void addStyledCell(PdfPTable table, String text, Font font, BaseColor bgColor, BaseColor borderColor) {
        PdfPCell cell = new PdfPCell(new Phrase(text, font));
        cell.setBackgroundColor(bgColor);
        cell.setBorderColor(borderColor);
        cell.setPadding(8);
        cell.setHorizontalAlignment(Element.ALIGN_CENTER);
        table.addCell(cell);
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Participant;
import entities.event;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.EmailService;
import services.Participantserv;
import services.RecommendedEvent;
import services.RecommendationService;
import utils.SessionManager;
import entities.user;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class RecommendationsController {

    // ── FXML ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private ComboBox<Participant> cbParticipant;
    @FXML private FlowPane             cardsPane;
    @FXML private Label                lblStatTotal;
    @FXML private Label                lblStatCtr;
    @FXML private Label                lblStatConv;
    @FXML private Label                lblStatTopCat;
    @FXML private Label                lblInfo;
    @FXML private Button               btnEnvoyerEmail;
    @FXML private Button               btnModePersonal;
    @FXML private Button               btnModeTrend;
    @FXML private Button               btnModePopular;
    @FXML private ImageView            imgAvatar;
    @FXML private Label                lblInitials;
    @FXML private Label                lblUserName;
    @FXML private Label                lblUserRole;

    private final RecommendationService recoServ  = new RecommendationService();
    private final Participantserv        partServ  = new Participantserv();
    private final EmailService           emailServ = new EmailService();

    private String  currentMode = "trending";
    private List<RecommendedEvent> lastPersonalRecs = null;

    // ── Init ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        loadUserProfile();
        chargerParticipants();
        chargerStats();
        handleModeTrend(); // default view: trending
    }

    private void loadUserProfile() {
        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser == null) return;
        String name = extractName(currentUser.getEmail());
        if (lblUserName != null) lblUserName.setText(name);
        if (lblUserRole != null) lblUserRole.setText(currentUser.getRole() != null ? currentUser.getRole().name() : "");
        if (imgAvatar != null) imgAvatar.setVisible(false);
        if (lblInitials != null) { lblInitials.setVisible(true); lblInitials.setText(initials(name)); }
    }

    private void chargerParticipants() {
        List<Participant> list = partServ.getAll();
        cbParticipant.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(list));
        cbParticipant.setConverter(new javafx.util.StringConverter<Participant>() {
            @Override public String toString(Participant p) {
                if (p == null) return "";
                return p.getPrenom() + " " + p.getNom() + "  [" + p.getEmail() + "]";
            }
            @Override public Participant fromString(String s) { return null; }
        });
    }

    private void chargerStats() {
        new Thread(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> stats = recoServ.getStatistics();
            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                lblStatTotal.setText(String.valueOf(stats.getOrDefault("totalRecommendations", 0)));
                lblStatCtr.setText(String.valueOf(stats.getOrDefault("clickRate", "0%")));
                lblStatConv.setText(String.valueOf(stats.getOrDefault("conversionRate", "0%")));
                Object cat = stats.get("topRecommendedCategory");
                lblStatTopCat.setText(cat != null && !"N/A".equals(cat.toString())
                    ? shorten(cat.toString(), 14) : "—");
            });
        }).start();
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // MODE HANDLERS
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    @FXML
    public void handleParticipantChange() {
        if ("personal".equals(currentMode)) handleModePersonal();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleModePersonal() {
        setActiveMode("personal");
        Participant p = cbParticipant.getValue();
        if (p == null) {
            showPlaceholder("👤 Sélectionnez un participant dans la liste pour voir ses recommandations personnalisées.");
            return;
        }
        lblInfo.setText("Recommandations personnalisées pour " + p.getPrenom() + " " + p.getNom());
        cardsPane.getChildren().clear();
        Label loading = new Label("⏳ Calcul en cours...");
        loading.setStyle("-fx-font-size:14px; -fx-text-fill:#9333ea; -fx-padding:40;");
        cardsPane.getChildren().add(loading);

        new Thread(() -> {
            List<RecommendedEvent> recs = recoServ.getPersonalized(p.getId(), 12);
            lastPersonalRecs = recs;
            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                cardsPane.getChildren().clear();
                if (recs.isEmpty()) {
                    showPlaceholder("📭 Aucune recommandation disponible. L'historique de réservations est vide.");
                    return;
                }
                int rank = 1;
                for (RecommendedEvent r : recs) cardsPane.getChildren().add(buildRecoCard(r, rank++));
                chargerStats();
            });
        }).start();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleModeTrend() {
        setActiveMode("trending");
        lblInfo.setText("Événements avec le plus de réservations récentes.");
        cardsPane.getChildren().clear();
        new Thread(() -> {
            List<Map<String, Object>> list = recoServ.getTrending(12);
            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                cardsPane.getChildren().clear();
                if (list.isEmpty()) { showPlaceholder("📭 Aucun événement tendance pour l'instant."); return; }
                int rank = 1;
                for (Map<String, Object> m : list) cardsPane.getChildren().add(buildTrendCard(m, rank++));
            });
        }).start();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleModePopular() {
        setActiveMode("popular");
        lblInfo.setText("Événements avec le plus de participants confirmés.");
        cardsPane.getChildren().clear();
        new Thread(() -> {
            List<Map<String, Object>> list = recoServ.getPopular(12);
            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                cardsPane.getChildren().clear();
                if (list.isEmpty()) { showPlaceholder("📭 Aucun événement populaire pour l'instant."); return; }
                int rank = 1;
                for (Map<String, Object> m : list) cardsPane.getChildren().add(buildPopularCard(m, rank++));
            });
        }).start();
    }

    @FXML
    public void handleActualiser() {
        chargerStats();
        switch (currentMode) {
            case "personal": handleModePersonal();  break;
            case "trending": handleModeTrend();     break;
            case "popular":  handleModePopular();   break;
        }
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // EMAIL NOTIFICATION
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    @FXML
    public void handleEnvoyerEmail() {
        Participant p = cbParticipant.getValue();
        if (p == null) {
            alert("Sélectionnez d'abord un participant.");
            return;
        }
        if (p.getEmail() == null || p.getEmail().isBlank()) {
            alert("Ce participant n'a pas d'adresse email.");
            return;
        }

        List<RecommendedEvent> recs = lastPersonalRecs;
        if (recs == null || recs.isEmpty()) {
            // Generate on the spot
            recs = recoServ.getPersonalized(p.getId(), 8);
            lastPersonalRecs = recs;
        }
        if (recs.isEmpty()) {
            alert("Aucune recommandation à envoyer pour ce participant.");
            return;
        }

        final List<RecommendedEvent> finalRecs = recs;
        new Thread(() -> {
            boolean ok = emailServ.sendRecommendations(p.getEmail(),
                p.getPrenom() + " " + p.getNom(), finalRecs);
            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                if (ok) {
                    Alert a = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION,
                        "✅ Recommandations envoyées à " + p.getEmail(), ButtonType.OK);
                    a.setHeaderText("Email envoyé avec succès");
                    a.showAndWait();
                } else {
                    alert("❌ Échec de l'envoi. Vérifiez la configuration SMTP.");
                }
            });
        }).start();
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // CARD BUILDERS
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    private VBox buildRecoCard(RecommendedEvent r, int rank) {
        event ev    = r.getEvent();
        String type = ev.getType() != null ? ev.getType() : "Événement";
        String[] tc = typeColors(type);

        // Banner
        StackPane banner = new StackPane();
        banner.setPrefHeight(130);
        banner.setStyle(String.format(
            "-fx-background-color:linear-gradient(to bottom right,%s,%s);" +
            "-fx-background-radius:12 12 0 0;", tc[0], tc[1]));

        // Rank badge
        Label rankBadge = new Label("#" + rank);
        rankBadge.setStyle("-fx-background-color:rgba(0,0,0,0.3); -fx-text-fill:white; " +
            "-fx-background-radius:12; -fx-padding:3 8; -fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(rankBadge, Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        StackPane.setMargin(rankBadge, new Insets(10, 0, 0, 10));

        // Score badge
        String scoreColor = r.getScore() >= 70 ? "#16a34a" : r.getScore() >= 40 ? "#d97706" : "#2563eb";
        Label scoreBadge = new Label(r.getScore() + " pts");
        scoreBadge.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + scoreColor + "; -fx-text-fill:white; " +
            "-fx-background-radius:12; -fx-padding:3 8; -fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(scoreBadge, Pos.TOP_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(scoreBadge, new Insets(10, 10, 0, 0));

        // Center emoji
        Label emoji = new Label(typeEmoji(type));
        emoji.setStyle("-fx-font-size:40px; -fx-opacity:0.75;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(emoji, Pos.CENTER);
        StackPane.setMargin(emoji, new Insets(0, 0, 18, 0));

        // Bottom type label
        Label typeLabel = new Label(type.toUpperCase());
        typeLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size:10px; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
            "-fx-text-fill:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);");
        StackPane.setAlignment(typeLabel, Pos.BOTTOM_LEFT);
        StackPane.setMargin(typeLabel, new Insets(0, 0, 10, 10));

        banner.getChildren().addAll(rankBadge, scoreBadge, emoji, typeLabel);

        // Body
        VBox body = new VBox(8);
        body.setPadding(new Insets(12, 14, 12, 14));
        body.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#ffffff;");

        Label title = new Label(ev.getTitre());
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-size:14px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332; -fx-wrap-text:true;");
        title.setMaxWidth(250);
        title.setWrapText(true);

        VBox meta = new VBox(3);
        if (ev.getDate() != null) meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("📅", ev.getDate().toString()));
        if (ev.getLieu() != null) meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("📍", ev.getLieu()));

        // Score bar
        HBox scoreRow = new HBox(8);
        scoreRow.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        Label scoreIcon = new Label("🎯");
        scoreIcon.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px;");
        ProgressBar pb = new ProgressBar(r.getScore() / 100.0);
        pb.setPrefWidth(130);
        pb.setMaxHeight(5);
        pb.setStyle("-fx-accent:" + scoreColor + ";");
        Label scoreVal = new Label(r.getScore() + "/100");
        scoreVal.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        scoreRow.getChildren().addAll(scoreIcon, pb, scoreVal);

        // Reason
        Label reason = new Label("💡 " + r.getReason());
        reason.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#6b46c1; -fx-wrap-text:true; -fx-font-style:italic;");
        reason.setWrapText(true);
        reason.setMaxWidth(250);

        Separator sep = new Separator();
        sep.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#f0f4f0;");

        // Buttons
        HBox btns = new HBox(8);
        Button btnRes = styledBtn("🎟 Réserver", "#9333ea", "white");
        Button btnIgn = styledBtn("✕ Ignorer", "#e2e8f0", "#4a5568");
        btnIgn.setStyle(btnIgn.getStyle() + "-fx-font-size:11.5px;");
        btnIgn.setOnAction(e -> cardsPane.getChildren().remove(
            (javafx.scene.Node) btnIgn.getScene().lookup("#card_" + rank)));
        btns.getChildren().addAll(btnRes, btnIgn);

        body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, scoreRow, sep, reason, btns);

        VBox card = wrapCard(banner, body, tc[2]);
        return card;
    }

    private VBox buildTrendCard(Map<String, Object> m, int rank) {
        String type  = str(m, "type");
        String[] tc  = typeColors(type);
        int    trend = num(m, "recentReservations");
        int    avail = num(m, "availableSeats");

        StackPane banner = colorBanner(tc, rank, "🔥", type, trend + " rés.");

        VBox body = new VBox(8);
        body.setPadding(new Insets(12, 14, 12, 14));
        body.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#ffffff;");

        Label title = new Label(str(m, "title"));
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-size:14px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332; -fx-wrap-text:true;");
        title.setMaxWidth(250); title.setWrapText(true);

        VBox meta = new VBox(3);
        meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("📅", str(m, "date")));
        meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("📍", str(m, "lieu")));
        meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("🔥", trend + " réservations récentes"));

        Label avLabel = new Label(avail > 0 ? "✅ " + avail + " places dispo" : "⚠ Presque complet");
        avLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11.5px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:" +
            (avail > 5 ? "#16a34a" : "#d97706") + ";");

        body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, new Separator(), avLabel);
        return wrapCard(banner, body, tc[2]);
    }

    private VBox buildPopularCard(Map<String, Object> m, int rank) {
        String type     = str(m, "type");
        String[] tc     = typeColors(type);
        int    conf     = num(m, "confirmedParticipants");
        String fillRate = str(m, "fillRate");
        int    popScore = num(m, "popularityScore");

        StackPane banner = colorBanner(tc, rank, "⭐", type, "Score " + popScore);

        VBox body = new VBox(8);
        body.setPadding(new Insets(12, 14, 12, 14));
        body.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#ffffff;");

        Label title = new Label(str(m, "title"));
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-size:14px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332; -fx-wrap-text:true;");
        title.setMaxWidth(250); title.setWrapText(true);

        VBox meta = new VBox(3);
        meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("📅", str(m, "date")));
        meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("👥", conf + " participants confirmés"));
        meta.getChildren().add(metaRow("📊", "Taux remplissage : " + fillRate));

        // Popularity bar
        double fill = 0;
        try { fill = Double.parseDouble(fillRate.replace("%", "")) / 100.0; } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        ProgressBar pb = new ProgressBar(Math.min(fill, 1.0));
        pb.setPrefWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        pb.setMaxHeight(6);
        pb.setStyle("-fx-accent:#9333ea;");

        body.getChildren().addAll(title, meta, pb);
        return wrapCard(banner, body, tc[2]);
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // HELPERS
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    private StackPane colorBanner(String[] tc, int rank, String icon, String type, String badge) {
        StackPane banner = new StackPane();
        banner.setPrefHeight(130);
        banner.setStyle(String.format(
            "-fx-background-color:linear-gradient(to bottom right,%s,%s);" +
            "-fx-background-radius:12 12 0 0;", tc[0], tc[1]));

        Label rankL = new Label("#" + rank);
        rankL.setStyle("-fx-background-color:rgba(0,0,0,0.3); -fx-text-fill:white;" +
            "-fx-background-radius:12; -fx-padding:3 8; -fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(rankL, Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        StackPane.setMargin(rankL, new Insets(10, 0, 0, 10));

        Label badgeL = new Label(icon + " " + badge);
        badgeL.setStyle("-fx-background-color:rgba(0,0,0,0.25); -fx-text-fill:white;" +
            "-fx-background-radius:12; -fx-padding:3 8; -fx-font-size:11px;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(badgeL, Pos.TOP_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(badgeL, new Insets(10, 10, 0, 0));

        Label emojiL = new Label(typeEmoji(type));
        emojiL.setStyle("-fx-font-size:40px; -fx-opacity:0.75;");
        StackPane.setAlignment(emojiL, Pos.CENTER);
        StackPane.setMargin(emojiL, new Insets(0, 0, 18, 0));

        Label typeL = new Label(type.toUpperCase());
        typeL.setStyle("-fx-font-size:10px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);");
        StackPane.setAlignment(typeL, Pos.BOTTOM_LEFT);
        StackPane.setMargin(typeL, new Insets(0, 0, 10, 10));

        banner.getChildren().addAll(rankL, badgeL, emojiL, typeL);
        return banner;
    }

    private VBox wrapCard(StackPane banner, VBox body, String borderColor) {
        VBox card = new VBox();
        card.setPrefWidth(275);
        card.setMaxWidth(275);
        card.setStyle(
            "-fx-background-color:#ffffff;" +
            "-fx-background-radius:12;" +
            "-fx-border-color:" + borderColor + ";" +
            "-fx-border-width:0 0 0 4;" +
            "-fx-border-radius:12;" +
            "-fx-effect:dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.09),8,0,0,2);");
        card.setOnMouseEntered(e -> card.setStyle(card.getStyle()
            .replace("rgba(0,0,0,0.09),8,0,0,2","rgba(0,0,0,0.18),16,0,0,4")));
        card.setOnMouseExited(e -> card.setStyle(card.getStyle()
            .replace("rgba(0,0,0,0.18),16,0,0,4","rgba(0,0,0,0.09),8,0,0,2")));
        card.getChildren().addAll(banner, body);
        return card;
    }

    private HBox metaRow(String icon, String text) {
        HBox row = new HBox(6);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        Label i = new Label(icon); i.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px;");
        Label t = new Label(text); t.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11.5px; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568;");
        t.setMaxWidth(210); t.setWrapText(false);
        row.getChildren().addAll(i, t);
        return row;
    }

    private Button styledBtn(String text, String bg, String fg) {
        Button b = new Button(text);
        b.setStyle(String.format(
            "-fx-background-color:%s; -fx-text-fill:%s;" +
            "-fx-background-radius:7; -fx-font-size:12px;" +
            "-fx-padding:6 12; -fx-cursor:hand; -fx-font-weight:bold;", bg, fg));
        HBox.setHgrow(b, Priority.ALWAYS);
        b.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        return b;
    }

    private void setActiveMode(String mode) {
        currentMode = mode;
        String active   = "-fx-background-color:#9333ea; -fx-text-fill:white; " +
                          "-fx-background-radius:20; -fx-padding:7 16; -fx-cursor:hand; -fx-font-size:12.5px;";
        String inactive = "-fx-background-color:#e2e8f0; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568; " +
                          "-fx-background-radius:20; -fx-padding:7 16; -fx-cursor:hand; -fx-font-size:12.5px;";
        btnModePersonal.setStyle("personal".equals(mode) ? active : inactive);
        btnModeTrend   .setStyle("trending".equals(mode) ? active : inactive);
        btnModePopular .setStyle("popular" .equals(mode) ? active : inactive);
        btnEnvoyerEmail.setDisable(false);
    }

    private void showPlaceholder(String msg) {
        cardsPane.getChildren().clear();
        Label lbl = new Label(msg);
        lbl.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#a0aab8; -fx-font-size:14px; -fx-padding:40;");
        cardsPane.getChildren().add(lbl);
    }

    private String[] typeColors(String type) {
        if (type == null) return new String[]{"#1a2332","#2d3748","#e2e8f0"};
        switch (type.toUpperCase()
            .replace("É","E").replace("È","E").replace("Ê","E")
            .replace("À","A").replace("Ç","C")) {
            case "DON DE SANG":         return new String[]{"#7f1d1d","#dc2626","#dc2626"};
            case "VACCINATION":         return new String[]{"#14532d","#16a34a","#16a34a"};
            case "CONFERENCE MEDICALE": return new String[]{"#1e3a5f","#2563eb","#2563eb"};
            case "CONSULTATION":        return new String[]{"#312e81","#7c3aed","#7c3aed"};
            case "SENSIBILISATION":     return new String[]{"#92400e","#d97706","#d97706"};
            case "ATELIER SANTE":       return new String[]{"#134e4a","#0d9488","#0d9488"};
            case "DEPISTAGE":           return new String[]{"#1e3a5f","#0284c7","#0284c7"};
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":    return new String[]{"#7c2d12","#ea580c","#ea580c"};
            default:                    return new String[]{"#1a2332","#9333ea","#9333ea"};
        }
    }

    private String typeEmoji(String type) {
        if (type == null) return "🏥";
        switch (type.toUpperCase()
            .replace("É","E").replace("È","E").replace("Ê","E")
            .replace("À","A").replace("Ç","C")) {
            case "DON DE SANG":     return "🩸";
            case "VACCINATION":     return "💉";
            case "CONSULTATION":    return "🩺";
            case "SENSIBILISATION": return "❤";
            case "ATELIER SANTE":   return "🥗";
            case "DEPISTAGE":       return "🔬";
            case "PREMIERS SECOURS":return "🚑";
            default:                return "🏥";
        }
    }

    private String str(Map<String, Object> m, String k) {
        Object v = m.get(k); return v != null ? v.toString() : "";
    }
    private int num(Map<String, Object> m, String k) {
        Object v = m.get(k);
        if (v instanceof Number) return ((Number) v).intValue();
        try { return Integer.parseInt(v != null ? v.toString() : "0"); } catch (Exception e) { return 0; }
    }
    private String shorten(String s, int max) {
        return s.length() > max ? s.substring(0, max) + "…" : s;
    }
    private String extractName(String email) {
        if (email == null) return "Utilisateur";
        int at = email.indexOf('@');
        String n = at > 0 ? email.substring(0, at) : email;
        return n.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + n.substring(1).toLowerCase();
    }
    private String initials(String name) {
        if (name == null || name.isEmpty()) return "U";
        String[] p = name.split(" ");
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (String s : p) if (!s.isEmpty()) sb.append(s.charAt(0));
        return sb.toString().toUpperCase();
    }
    private void alert(String msg) {
        new Alert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING, msg, ButtonType.OK).showAndWait();
    }

    // ── Navigation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerEvenements()   { tests.Main.showAdminEvents(); }
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerParticipants() { tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants(); }
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerReservations() { tests.Main.showAdminEventReservations(); }
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerAnalytiques()  { tests.Main.showAdminEventAnalytics(); }
    @FXML public void handleLogout()               { tests.Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import entities.user.Role;
import entities.user.Gender;
import entities.user.AccountStatus;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import services.EmailService;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;
import utils.CaptchaGenerator;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;

public class SignUpController {

    @FXML private VBox signUpForm;
    @FXML private VBox verificationSection;
    @FXML private TextField verificationCodeField;
    @FXML private Label verificationText;

    @FXML private TextField firstNameField;
    @FXML private TextField lastNameField;
    @FXML private TextField emailField;
    @FXML private PasswordField passwordField;
    @FXML private PasswordField confirmPasswordField;
    @FXML private TextField passwordVisible;
    @FXML private TextField confirmPasswordVisible;
    private boolean passwordShown = false;
    private boolean confirmPasswordShown = false;
    @FXML private TextField phoneField;
    @FXML private TextField ageField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<Gender> genderCombo;
    @FXML private TextArea addressArea;
    @FXML private ComboBox<Role> roleCombo;
    @FXML private Label errorLabel;
    @FXML private Label errorLabelSignUp;

    @FXML private ImageView profileImageView;
    @FXML private Label photoStatus;
    private String base64Image = "";

    @FXML private ImageView logoView;
    @FXML private ImageView captchaImageView;
    @FXML private TextField captchaInputField;
    private CaptchaGenerator captchaGenerator;

    @FXML private VBox doctorFields;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> specialtyField;
    @FXML private TextField orderNumberField;
    @FXML private TextField consultationFeeField;

    @FXML private VBox pharmacyFields;
    @FXML private TextField pharmacyNameField;
    @FXML private TextField licenceNumberField;
    @FXML private Label documentStatus;
    @FXML private Label documentStatusPharma;
    @FXML private Label signatureStatus;
    private String base64Document = "";
    private String base64Signature = "";

    @FXML private VBox assistantFields;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> doctorSelectionCombo;
    private java.util.List<user> allDoctors = new java.util.ArrayList<>();

    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private String generatedVerificationCode;
    private user pendingUser;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        genderCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(Gender.values()));
        genderCombo.setValue(Gender.M);

        if (specialtyField != null) {
            specialtyField.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Cardiologue", "Dermatologue", "Généraliste", "Gynécologue",
                "Pédiatre", "Neurologue", "Ophtalmologue", "Psychiatre", "Dentiste", "Orthopédiste"
            ));
        }

        roleCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(Role.patient, Role.doctor, Role.pharmacist, Role.assistant));
        roleCombo.setValue(Role.patient);
        roleCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> updateFieldsForRole(newVal));

        updateFieldsForRole(Role.patient);
        setDefaultProfileImage();
        captchaGenerator = new CaptchaGenerator();
        captchaImageView.setImage(captchaGenerator.getCaptchaImage());
    }

    @FXML private void refreshCaptcha() {
        captchaGenerator.generateCaptcha();
        captchaImageView.setImage(captchaGenerator.getCaptchaImage());
        captchaInputField.clear();
    }

    private void updateFieldsForRole(Role role) {
        if (doctorFields != null) { doctorFields.setVisible(false); doctorFields.setManaged(false); }
        if (pharmacyFields != null) { pharmacyFields.setVisible(false); pharmacyFields.setManaged(false); }
        if (assistantFields != null) { assistantFields.setVisible(false); assistantFields.setManaged(false); }

        if (role == Role.doctor && doctorFields != null) { doctorFields.setVisible(true); doctorFields.setManaged(true); }
        else if (role == Role.pharmacist && pharmacyFields != null) { pharmacyFields.setVisible(true); pharmacyFields.setManaged(true); }
        else if (role == Role.assistant && assistantFields != null) { assistantFields.setVisible(true); assistantFields.setManaged(true); loadDoctorsForAssistant(); }
    }

    private void loadDoctorsForAssistant() {
        try {
            List<user> allUsers = userService.getAll();
            allDoctors = allUsers.stream().filter(u -> u.getRole() == Role.doctor).collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toList());
            javafx.collections.ObservableList<String> doctorNames = FXCollections.observableArrayList();
            for (user d : allDoctors) doctorNames.add("Dr. " + d.getFirstName() + " " + d.getLastName());
            if (doctorSelectionCombo != null) doctorSelectionCombo.setItems(doctorNames);
        } catch (SQLException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    private void setDefaultProfileImage() {
        profileImageView.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png", 80, 80, true, true));
    }

    @FXML private void choosePhoto() {
        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Images", "*.png", "*.jpg", "*.jpeg"));
        File selectedFile = fileChooser.showOpenDialog(null);
        if (selectedFile != null) {
            try {
                profileImageView.setImage(new Image(selectedFile.toURI().toString(), 150, 150, true, true));
                BufferedImage bufferedImage = ImageIO.read(selectedFile);
                ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                ImageIO.write(bufferedImage, "jpg", baos);
                base64Image = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(baos.toByteArray());
                photoStatus.setText("Photo sélectionnée !");
            } catch (IOException e) { e.printStackTrace(); }
        }
    }

    @FXML private void chooseDocument() {
        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Choisir un document PDF");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("PDF Documents", "*.pdf"));
        File selectedFile = fileChooser.showOpenDialog(null);
        if (selectedFile != null) {
            try {
                byte[] fileContent = java.nio.file.Files.readAllBytes(selectedFile.toPath());
                base64Document = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(fileContent);
                String fileName = selectedFile.getName();
                if (documentStatus != null) documentStatus.setText(fileName);
                if (documentStatusPharma != null) documentStatusPharma.setText(fileName);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                errorLabel.setText("Erreur lors de la lecture du fichier");
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML private void chooseSignature() {
        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Choisir une image de signature");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().add(new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Images", "*.png", "*.jpg", "*.jpeg"));
        File selectedFile = fileChooser.showOpenDialog(null);
        if (selectedFile != null) {
            try {
                BufferedImage img = ImageIO.read(selectedFile);
                ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                ImageIO.write(img, "png", baos);
                base64Signature = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(baos.toByteArray());
                if (signatureStatus != null) signatureStatus.setText("✓ " + selectedFile.getName());
                signatureStatus.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11; -fx-text-fill: #48BB78;");
            } catch (IOException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                errorLabelSignUp.setText("Erreur lors de la lecture de la signature");
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void togglePasswordVisibility() {
        passwordShown = !passwordShown;
        if (passwordShown) {
            passwordVisible.setText(passwordField.getText());
            passwordField.setVisible(false);  passwordField.setManaged(false);
            passwordVisible.setVisible(true); passwordVisible.setManaged(true);
            passwordVisible.requestFocus();
            passwordVisible.positionCaret(passwordVisible.getText().length());
        } else {
            passwordField.setText(passwordVisible.getText());
            passwordVisible.setVisible(false); passwordVisible.setManaged(false);
            passwordField.setVisible(true);    passwordField.setManaged(true);
            passwordField.requestFocus();
            passwordField.positionCaret(passwordField.getText().length());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void toggleConfirmPasswordVisibility() {
        confirmPasswordShown = !confirmPasswordShown;
        if (confirmPasswordShown) {
            confirmPasswordVisible.setText(confirmPasswordField.getText());
            confirmPasswordField.setVisible(false);  confirmPasswordField.setManaged(false);
            confirmPasswordVisible.setVisible(true);  confirmPasswordVisible.setManaged(true);
            confirmPasswordVisible.requestFocus();
            confirmPasswordVisible.positionCaret(confirmPasswordVisible.getText().length());
        } else {
            confirmPasswordField.setText(confirmPasswordVisible.getText());
            confirmPasswordVisible.setVisible(false); confirmPasswordVisible.setManaged(false);
            confirmPasswordField.setVisible(true);    confirmPasswordField.setManaged(true);
            confirmPasswordField.requestFocus();
            confirmPasswordField.positionCaret(confirmPasswordField.getText().length());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleSignUp() {
        errorLabel.setText("");
        errorLabelSignUp.setText("");

        // Synchroniser les champs visibles → masqués avant validation
        if (passwordShown) passwordField.setText(passwordVisible.getText());
        if (confirmPasswordShown) confirmPasswordField.setText(confirmPasswordVisible.getText());

        if (!captchaGenerator.validate(captchaInputField.getText())) {
            errorLabelSignUp.setText("Captcha incorrect");
            refreshCaptcha();
            return;
        }

        // --- VALIDATION DES CHAMPS OBLIGATOIRES GÉNÉRAUX ---
        if (firstNameField.getText().isEmpty() || emailField.getText().isEmpty() || passwordField.getText().isEmpty() || roleCombo.getValue() == null) {
            errorLabelSignUp.setText("Veuillez remplir les champs obligatoires (*)");
            return;
        }

        Role selectedRole = roleCombo.getValue();

        // --- VALIDATION SPÉCIFIQUE (OBLIGATOIRE) ---
        if (selectedRole == Role.doctor) {
            if (specialtyField.getValue() == null || orderNumberField.getText().isEmpty() || consultationFeeField.getText().isEmpty()) {
                errorLabelSignUp.setText("Tous les champs Docteur sont obligatoires (*)");
                return;
            }
            if (base64Document.isEmpty()) {
                errorLabelSignUp.setText("Veuillez importer votre diplôme (PDF)");
                return;
            }
            if (base64Signature.isEmpty()) {
                errorLabelSignUp.setText("Veuillez importer votre image de signature (*)");
                return;
            }
        } else if (selectedRole == Role.pharmacist) {
            if (pharmacyNameField.getText().isEmpty() || licenceNumberField.getText().isEmpty()) {
                errorLabelSignUp.setText("Tous les champs Pharmacie sont obligatoires (*)");
                return;
            }
            if (base64Document.isEmpty()) {
                errorLabelSignUp.setText("Veuillez importer votre licence (PDF)");
                return;
            }
        } else if (selectedRole == Role.assistant) {
            if (doctorSelectionCombo.getValue() == null) {
                errorLabelSignUp.setText("Veuillez sélectionner un médecin référent (*)");
                return;
            }
        }

        try {
            pendingUser = new user();
            pendingUser.setFirstName(firstNameField.getText());
            pendingUser.setLastName(lastNameField.getText());
            pendingUser.setEmail(emailField.getText());
            pendingUser.setPassword(passwordField.getText());
            pendingUser.setRole(selectedRole);
            
            int age = 0;
            if (!ageField.getText().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    age = Integer.parseInt(ageField.getText());
                } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                    errorLabelSignUp.setText("L'âge doit être un nombre");
                    return;
                }
            }
            pendingUser.setAge(age);
            pendingUser.setGender(genderCombo.getValue());
            pendingUser.setUserPhoto(base64Image);
            pendingUser.setAddress(addressArea.getText());
            pendingUser.setPhone(phoneField.getText());
            
            // Gestion des champs spécifiques
            if (selectedRole == Role.doctor) {
                pendingUser.setSpecialty(specialtyField.getValue());
                pendingUser.setOrderNumber(orderNumberField.getText());
                pendingUser.setConsultationFee(Float.parseFloat(consultationFeeField.getText()));
                pendingUser.setImageSignature(base64Signature);
            } else if (selectedRole == Role.pharmacist) {
                pendingUser.setPharmacyName(pharmacyNameField.getText());
                pendingUser.setLicenceNumber(licenceNumberField.getText());
            } else if (selectedRole == Role.assistant) {
                // Associer au docteur sélectionné
                String docName = doctorSelectionCombo.getValue();
                user doc = allDoctors.stream()
                        .filter(d -> ("Dr. " + d.getFirstName() + " " + d.getLastName()).equals(docName))
                        .findFirst().orElse(null);
                if (doc != null) pendingUser.setDoctorId(doc.getUserId());
            }

            if (selectedRole == Role.doctor || selectedRole == Role.pharmacist) {
                pendingUser.setProfessionalDocument(base64Document);
            }

            // Les comptes professionnels sont désactivés par défaut (attente admin)
            // Sauf les patients (auto-approuvés)
            if (selectedRole == Role.patient) {
                pendingUser.setAccountStatus(AccountStatus.active);
            } else {
                pendingUser.setAccountStatus(AccountStatus.pending); // En attente de validation Admin
            }

            generatedVerificationCode = EmailService.generateVerificationCode();

            // Désactiver le bouton et afficher un message d'attente
            errorLabelSignUp.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #3182CE;");
            errorLabelSignUp.setText("⏳ Envoi du code en cours...");

            final String codeToSend = generatedVerificationCode;
            final String emailToSend = pendingUser.getEmail();

            // Envoi dans un thread séparé pour ne pas bloquer l'UI
            Thread emailThread = new Thread(() -> {
                boolean sent = EmailService.sendVerificationEmail(emailToSend, codeToSend);
                javafx.application.Platform.runLater(() -> {
                    errorLabelSignUp.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
                    if (sent) {
                        signUpForm.setVisible(false);
                        signUpForm.setManaged(false);
                        verificationSection.setVisible(true);
                        verificationSection.setManaged(true);
                        verificationText.setText("Un code a été envoyé à : " + emailToSend);
                    } else {
                        errorLabelSignUp.setText("❌ Échec envoi email. Vérifiez votre connexion internet.");
                    }
                });
            });
            emailThread.setDaemon(true);
            emailThread.start();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            errorLabelSignUp.setText("Erreur : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleVerifyRegistration() {
        String input = verificationCodeField.getText().trim();
        if (input.equals(generatedVerificationCode)) {
            try {
                userService.add(pendingUser);
                
                Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
                alert.setTitle("Bienvenue");
                alert.setHeaderText(null);
                alert.setContentText("Compte créé avec succès ! En attente de validation admin.");
                alert.showAndWait();
                
                Main.showLoginPage();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                errorLabel.setText("Erreur : " + e.getMessage());
            }
        } else {
            errorLabel.setText("Code incorrect");
        }
    }

    @FXML private void backToForm() {
        verificationSection.setVisible(false);
        verificationSection.setManaged(false);
        signUpForm.setVisible(true);
        signUpForm.setManaged(true);
    }

    @FXML private void goToLogin() { Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Reservation;
import entities.event;
import entities.Participant;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.stage.Modality;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.QrCodeService;
import services.ReservationServ;
import services.eventServ;
import services.Participantserv;
import utils.SessionManager;
import entities.user;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;

public class SmartBookingController {

    // ── Sidebar profile ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private Label lblUserName;
    @FXML private Label lblUserRole;
    @FXML private Label lblInitials;

    // ── Booking form ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private ComboBox<String>  cbEvent;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String>  cbParticipant;
    @FXML private Spinner<Integer>  spPlaces;
    @FXML private Label             lblPlacesRestantes;
    @FXML private Label             lblResultat;

    // ── Reservations list ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private VBox              reservationsPane;
    @FXML private Label             lblTotalRes;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String>  cbFiltreStatut;

    private final ReservationServ resServ    = new ReservationServ();
    private final eventServ        eServ     = new eventServ();
    private final Participantserv  pServ     = new Participantserv();
    private final QrCodeService    qrService = new QrCodeService();

    private List<event>       events;
    private List<Participant> participants;

    private static final DateTimeFormatter FMT = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm");

    // ── Init ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        loadUserProfile();
        loadComboData();
        if (cbFiltreStatut != null) {
            cbFiltreStatut.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                "Tous", "CONFIRME", "LISTE_D_ATTENTE", "CHECKED_IN", "REFUSE", "ANNULE", "EN_ATTENTE"));
            cbFiltreStatut.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
            cbFiltreStatut.setOnAction(e -> chargerReservations());
        }
        chargerReservations();
    }

    private void loadComboData() {
        events = eServ.getAll();
        participants = pServ.getAll();

        if (cbEvent != null) {
            cbEvent.getItems().clear();
            events.forEach(ev -> cbEvent.getItems().add("[" + ev.getId() + "] " + ev.getTitre()));
            cbEvent.setOnAction(e -> majPlacesRestantes());
        }
        if (cbParticipant != null) {
            cbParticipant.getItems().clear();
            participants.forEach(p -> cbParticipant.getItems().add("[" + p.getId() + "] " + p.getNom() + " " + p.getPrenom()));
        }
        if (spPlaces != null) {
            SpinnerValueFactory<Integer> vf = new SpinnerValueFactory.IntegerSpinnerValueFactory(1, 20, 1);
            spPlaces.setValueFactory(vf);
        }
    }

    private void majPlacesRestantes() {
        int idx = cbEvent.getSelectionModel().getSelectedIndex();
        if (idx < 0 || idx >= events.size()) {
            if (lblPlacesRestantes != null) lblPlacesRestantes.setText("—");
            return;
        }
        event ev = events.get(idx);
        int restantes = resServ.getPlacesRestantes(ev.getId());
        if (lblPlacesRestantes != null) {
            String txt = restantes == Integer.MAX_VALUE ? "∞ (pas de limite)" : restantes + " place(s) restante(s)";
            lblPlacesRestantes.setText(txt);
            lblPlacesRestantes.setStyle(restantes <= 0
                ? "-fx-text-fill:#e05a5a; -fx-font-weight:bold;"
                : "-fx-text-fill:#16a34a; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        }
    }

    // ── Handle booking submission ────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML
    public void handleReserver() {
        int evIdx = cbEvent.getSelectionModel().getSelectedIndex();
        int pIdx  = cbParticipant.getSelectionModel().getSelectedIndex();

        if (evIdx < 0) { showResult("⚠️ Sélectionnez un événement.", "#d97706"); return; }
        if (pIdx < 0)  { showResult("⚠️ Sélectionnez un participant.", "#d97706"); return; }

        int eventId       = events.get(evIdx).getId();
        int participantId = participants.get(pIdx).getId();
        int places        = spPlaces.getValue();

        // ── Automatic decision by the Smart Booking Engine ──────────────────
        Reservation res = resServ.soumettreDemande(eventId, participantId, places);

        if (res.getStatut() == Reservation.Statut.CONFIRME) {
            showResult("✅ Réservation CONFIRMÉE ! (" + places + " place(s) accordée(s))", "#16a34a");
        } else {
            showResult("❌ Réservation REFUSÉE – " + res.getMotifRefus(), "#e05a5a");
        }

        majPlacesRestantes();
        chargerReservations();
    }

    // ── Load reservation list ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void chargerReservations() {
        if (reservationsPane == null) return;
        reservationsPane.getChildren().clear();

        String filtre = (cbFiltreStatut != null && cbFiltreStatut.getValue() != null)
