            ? cbFiltreStatut.getValue() : "Tous";

        List<Reservation> all = resServ.getAll();
        List<Reservation> affichees = all.stream()
            .filter(r -> "Tous".equals(filtre) || r.getStatut().name().equals(filtre))
            .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toList());

        if (lblTotalRes != null)
            lblTotalRes.setText("Total : " + affichees.size() + " réservation(s)");

        if (affichees.isEmpty()) {
            Label empty = new Label("📭  Aucune réservation trouvée.");
            empty.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#a0aab8; -fx-font-size:14px; -fx-padding:30;");
            reservationsPane.getChildren().add(empty);
            return;
        }

        for (Reservation r : affichees) {
            reservationsPane.getChildren().add(buildReservationRow(r));
        }
    }

    private HBox buildReservationRow(Reservation r) {
        // Status color
        String[] sc = getStatusStyle(r.getStatut());

        HBox row = new HBox(14);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        row.setPadding(new Insets(12, 16, 12, 16));
        row.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#ffffff; -fx-background-radius:10; " +
                     "-fx-effect:dropshadow(gaussian,rgba(0,0,0,0.06),6,0,0,2); " +
                     "-fx-border-color:" + sc[2] + "; -fx-border-width:0 0 0 4; -fx-border-radius:10;");

        // Status badge
        Label badge = new Label(sc[0]);
        badge.setStyle("-fx-background-color:" + sc[1] + "; -fx-text-fill:" + sc[2] +
                       "; -fx-background-radius:20; -fx-padding:3 10 3 10; " +
                       "-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        badge.setMinWidth(110);

        // Details
        VBox details = new VBox(3);
        HBox.setHgrow(details, Priority.ALWAYS);
        String eventName = getEventName(r.getEventId());
        String partName  = getPartName(r.getParticipantId());
        Label lEvent = new Label("📅 " + eventName);
        lEvent.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1a2332;");
        Label lPart = new Label("👤 " + partName + "   |   🎟 " + r.getNombrePlaces() + " place(s)");
        lPart.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568;");
        Label lDate = new Label("🕐 " + (r.getDateDemande() != null ? r.getDateDemande().format(FMT) : "—"));
        lDate.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#718096;");
        details.getChildren().addAll(lEvent, lPart, lDate);

        if (r.getMotifRefus() != null && !r.getMotifRefus().isEmpty()) {
            Label lMotif = new Label("ℹ " + r.getMotifRefus());
            lMotif.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#c53030; -fx-wrap-text:true;");
            lMotif.setMaxWidth(400);
            lMotif.setWrapText(true);
            details.getChildren().add(lMotif);
        }

        // Buttons area
        VBox buttons = new VBox(6);
        buttons.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);

        // QR Code button — visible for CONFIRME and CHECKED_IN
        if (r.getStatut() == Reservation.Statut.CONFIRME
                || r.getStatut() == Reservation.Statut.CHECKED_IN) {
            Button btnQr = new Button("🔳 QR");
            btnQr.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#eff6ff; -fx-text-fill:#1d4ed8; " +
                           "-fx-background-radius:7; -fx-font-size:12px; -fx-padding:5 12 5 12; " +
                           "-fx-cursor:hand; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
            btnQr.setOnAction(e -> showQrCodePopup(r.getId(), eventName));
            buttons.getChildren().add(btnQr);
        }

        // Cancel button — only for CONFIRME
        if (r.getStatut() == Reservation.Statut.CONFIRME) {
            Button btnAnnuler = new Button("✕ Annuler");
            btnAnnuler.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#fff5f5; -fx-text-fill:#c53030; " +
                                "-fx-background-radius:7; -fx-font-size:12px; -fx-padding:5 12 5 12; -fx-cursor:hand;");
            btnAnnuler.setOnAction(e -> {
                resServ.annulerReservation(r.getId());
                chargerReservations();
                majPlacesRestantes();
            });
            buttons.getChildren().add(btnAnnuler);
        }

        row.getChildren().addAll(badge, details, buttons);

        return row;
    }

    // ── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private String[] getStatusStyle(Reservation.Statut s) {
        switch (s) {
            case CONFIRME:        return new String[]{"✓ CONFIRMÉ",      "#d4edda", "#16a34a"};
            case LISTE_D_ATTENTE: return new String[]{"⏳ LISTE ATTENTE", "#fef3c7", "#d97706"};
            case CHECKED_IN:      return new String[]{"🎯 PRÉSENT",       "#dbeafe", "#1d4ed8"};
            case REFUSE:          return new String[]{"✕ REFUSÉ",         "#fdeaea", "#c53030"};
            case ANNULE:          return new String[]{"↩ ANNULÉ",         "#f3f4f6", "#6b7280"};
            case EN_ATTENTE:      return new String[]{"◷ EN ATTENTE",     "#fef9c3", "#b45309"};
            default:              return new String[]{"? INCONNU",        "#e2e8f0", "#4a5568"};
        }
    }

    // ── QR Code popup viewer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void showQrCodePopup(int reservationId, String eventName) {
        String path = qrService.getQrCodePath(reservationId);
        if (path == null) {
            showResult("❌ QR Code introuvable pour la réservation #" + reservationId + ". Annulez et re-réservez.", "#e05a5a");
            return;
        }

        Stage popup = new Stage();
        popup.initModality(Modality.APPLICATION_MODAL);
        popup.setTitle("QR Code — Réservation #" + reservationId);
        popup.setResizable(false);

        VBox content = new VBox(16);
        content.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        content.setPadding(new Insets(28));
        content.setStyle("-fx-background-color:white;");

        // Header
        Label titre = new Label("🔳  QR Code d'accès");
        titre.setStyle("-fx-font-size:18px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#1e3a5f;");

        Label sous = new Label(eventName);
        sous.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-text-fill:#4a5568;");

        // QR image
        File qrFile = new File(path);
        Image qrImg = new Image(qrFile.toURI().toString(), 300, 300, true, true);
        ImageView qrView = new ImageView(qrImg);
        qrView.setFitWidth(300);
        qrView.setFitHeight(300);
        qrView.setPreserveRatio(true);
        qrView.setStyle("-fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.15), 12, 0, 0, 4);");

        // Info
        Label info = new Label("Réservation #" + reservationId + "  ·  Présentez ce QR à l'entrée");
        info.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#718096; -fx-font-style:italic;");

        // Close button
        Button btnClose = new Button("  Fermer  ");
        btnClose.setStyle("-fx-background-color:#1e3a5f; -fx-text-fill:white; -fx-font-weight:bold; " +
                          "-fx-background-radius:8; -fx-padding:9 24 9 24; -fx-cursor:hand; -fx-font-size:13px;");
        btnClose.setOnAction(e -> popup.close());

        content.getChildren().addAll(titre, sous, qrView, info, btnClose);

        popup.setScene(new Scene(content, 400, 460));
        popup.show();
    }

    private String getEventName(int eventId) {
        return events.stream().filter(e -> e.getId() == eventId)
            .map(event::getTitre).findFirst().orElse("Event #" + eventId);
    }

    private String getPartName(int pId) {
        return participants.stream().filter(p -> p.getId() == pId)
            .map(p -> p.getNom() + " " + p.getPrenom()).findFirst().orElse("Participant #" + pId);
    }

    private void showResult(String msg, String color) {
        if (lblResultat != null) {
            lblResultat.setText(msg);
            lblResultat.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:" + color + "; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-font-size:13px;");
        }
    }

    private void loadUserProfile() {
        user currentUser = SessionManager.getInstance().getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser != null && lblUserName != null) {
            String name = extractName(currentUser.getEmail());
            lblUserName.setText(name);
            if (lblUserRole != null) lblUserRole.setText(currentUser.getRole() != null ? currentUser.getRole().name() : "");
            if (lblInitials != null) lblInitials.setText(initials(name));
        }
    }

    private String extractName(String email) {
        if (email == null || email.isEmpty()) return "Utilisateur";
        int at = email.indexOf('@');
        if (at > 0) { String n = email.substring(0, at); return n.substring(0,1).toUpperCase() + n.substring(1).toLowerCase(); }
        return email;
    }

    private String initials(String name) {
        if (name == null || name.isEmpty()) return "U";
        String[] p = name.split(" ");
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (String s : p) if (!s.isEmpty()) sb.append(s.charAt(0));
        return sb.toString().toUpperCase();
    }

    // ── Navigation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerEvenements()   { tests.Main.showAdminEvents(); }
    @FXML public void handleNaviguerParticipants() { tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants(); }
    @FXML public void handleLogout()               { tests.Main.showLoginPage(); }
}
package Controler;

import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.chart.CategoryAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.LineChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.NumberAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.PieChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.XYChart;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.medicalFileServ;
import services.userservDS;
import entities.userDS;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class StatistiquesController {

    @FXML private LineChart<String, Number> lineChartActivite;
    @FXML private PieChart pieChartStatus;
    @FXML private CategoryAxis xAxis;
    @FXML private NumberAxis yAxis;
    @FXML private Label lblTotalDossiers;
    @FXML private Label lblTotalPatients;
    @FXML private Label lblNouveauxCeMois;
    @FXML private ImageView imgDoctor;
    @FXML private Label lblDoctorName;

    private medicalFileServ medicalFileService = new medicalFileServ();
    private userservDS userService = new userservDS();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        chargerStats();
        chargerProfilMedecin();
    }

    private void chargerStats() {
        Map<String, Integer> stats = medicalFileService.getStatsByMonth();
        
        XYChart.Series<String, Number> series = new XYChart.Series<>();
        series.setName("Nouveaux Dossiers");

        int total = 0;
        int ceMois = 0;
        String currentMonth = java.time.LocalDate.now().getMonth().getDisplayName(
            java.time.format.TextStyle.FULL, java.util.Locale.FRENCH
        );
        // Capitalize first letter
        currentMonth = currentMonth.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + currentMonth.substring(1);

        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> entry : stats.entrySet()) {
            series.getData().add(new XYChart.Data<>(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue()));
            total += entry.getValue();
            if (entry.getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(currentMonth)) {
                ceMois = entry.getValue();
            }
        }

        lineChartActivite.getData().clear();
        lineChartActivite.getData().add(series);
        chargerStatsStatus();
        
        lblTotalDossiers.setText(String.valueOf(total));
        lblTotalPatients.setText(String.valueOf(userService.getAll().size()));
        lblNouveauxCeMois.setText(String.valueOf(ceMois));
    }

    private void chargerStatsStatus() {
        Map<String, Integer> statsStatus = medicalFileService.getStatsByStatus();
        int totalStatus = statsStatus.values().stream().mapToInt(Integer::intValue).sum();
        ObservableList<PieChart.Data> pieData = FXCollections.observableArrayList();

        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> entry : statsStatus.entrySet()) {
            double percent = totalStatus == 0 ? 0 : (entry.getValue() * 100.0 / totalStatus);
            String label = String.format("%s %.0f%%", entry.getKey(), percent);
            pieData.add(new PieChart.Data(label, entry.getValue()));
        }

        pieChartStatus.setData(pieData);
    }

    private void chargerProfilMedecin() {
        if (lblDoctorName != null) lblDoctorName.setText("Dr. Saif Gharbi");
        List<userDS> docs = userService.searchAllByNameOrSurname("Saif");
        for (userDS d : docs) {
            if (d.getFirstName().equalsIgnoreCase("Saif") && d.getLastName().equalsIgnoreCase("Gharbi")) {
                String photoPath = d.getUserphoto();
                if (photoPath != null && !photoPath.trim().isEmpty() && imgDoctor != null) {
                    try {
                        File file = new File(photoPath);
                        if (file.exists()) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                        } else if (getClass().getResource("/" + photoPath) != null) {
                            imgDoctor.setImage(new Image(getClass().getResourceAsStream("/" + photoPath)));
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {}
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerGererPatients(javafx.event.Event event) {
        // Redirection vers la liste du portail docteur
        tests.Main.showDoctorPatients();
    }

    @FXML
    public void allerPrescriptions(javafx.event.Event event) {
        try {
            FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/Prescription.fxml"));
            Parent root = loader.load();
            Stage stage = (Stage) ((Node) event.getSource()).getScene().getWindow();
            stage.setScene(new Scene(root));
            stage.setMaximized(true);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.event;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.control.cell.PropertyValueFactory;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.eventServ;

import java.io.IOException;

public class Suppevent {

    @FXML private TableView<event>            tableView;
    @FXML private TableColumn<event, Integer> colId;
    @FXML private TableColumn<event, String>  colTitre;
    @FXML private TableColumn<event, String>  colType;
    @FXML private TableColumn<event, String>  colDate;
    @FXML private TableColumn<event, String>  colLieu;
    @FXML private TableColumn<event, String>  colStatus;
    @FXML private Label                       lblMessage;

    private final eventServ service = new eventServ();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        colId.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("id"));
        colTitre.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("titre"));
        colType.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("type"));
        colDate.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("date"));
        colLieu.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("lieu"));
        colStatus.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("status"));
        chargerDonnees();
    }

    private void chargerDonnees() {
        tableView.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(service.getAll()));
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleSupprimer() {
        event selected = tableView.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
        if (selected == null) {
            showMessage("⚠️ Veuillez sélectionner un événement.", "orange"); return;
        }
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
            "Confirmer la suppression de « " + selected.getTitre() + " » ?",
            ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        confirm.showAndWait().ifPresent(btn -> {
            if (btn == ButtonType.YES) {
                service.supprimer(selected.getId());
                chargerDonnees();
                showMessage("✅ Événement supprimé.", "green");
            }
        });
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleRetour() {
        tests.Main.showAdminEvents();
    }

    private void showMessage(String msg, String color) {
        if (lblMessage != null) {
            lblMessage.setText(msg);
            lblMessage.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: " + color + ";");
        }
    }

    public void setEvent(event selected) {
        if (selected != null) tableView.getSelectionModel().select(selected);
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Participant;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.control.cell.PropertyValueFactory;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.Participantserv;

import java.io.IOException;

public class Suppparticipant {

    @FXML private TableView<Participant>            tableView;
    @FXML private TableColumn<Participant, Integer> colId;
    @FXML private TableColumn<Participant, String>  colNom;
    @FXML private TableColumn<Participant, String>  colPrenom;
    @FXML private TableColumn<Participant, String>  colEmail;
    @FXML private TableColumn<Participant, String>  colTelephone;
    @FXML private Label                             lblMessage;

    private final Participantserv service = new Participantserv();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        colId.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("id"));
        colNom.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("nom"));
        colPrenom.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("prenom"));
        colEmail.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("email"));
        colTelephone.setCellValueFactory(new PropertyValueFactory<>("telephone"));
        chargerDonnees();
    }

    private void chargerDonnees() {
        tableView.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(service.getAll()));
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleSupprimer() {
        Participant selected = tableView.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
        if (selected == null) {
            showMessage("⚠️ Veuillez sélectionner un participant.", "orange");
            return;
        }
        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
            "Supprimer " + selected.getNom() + " " + selected.getPrenom() + " ?",
            ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        confirm.showAndWait().ifPresent(btn -> {
            if (btn == ButtonType.YES) {
                service.supprimer(selected.getId());
                chargerDonnees();
                showMessage("✅ Participant supprimé.", "green");
            }
        });
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleRetour() {
        tests.Main.showAdminEventParticipants();
    }

    private void showMessage(String msg, String color) {
        lblMessage.setText(msg);
        lblMessage.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: " + color + ";");
    }
}

package Controler;

import entities.appointment;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import services.appointmentServices;
import java.sql.Date;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Time;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public class UpdateAppointment {

    @FXML private TextField searchId, patientId, doctorId, appointmentTime;
    @FXML private DatePicker appointmentDate;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> type, paymentMethod;
    @FXML private TextArea reason;
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;

    private final appointmentServices service = new appointmentServices();
    private appointment current;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        type.getItems().addAll("Consultation", "Contrôle", "Urgence");
        paymentMethod.getItems().addAll("espèce", "carte bancaire");
    }

    @FXML
    void rechercherRdv() {
        try {
            int id = Integer.parseInt(searchId.getText().trim());
            current = service.getAppointmentById(id);
            if (current == null) {
                messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
                messageLabel.setText("Aucun rendez-vous trouvé avec l'ID " + id);
            } else {
                patientId.setText(String.valueOf(current.getPatientId()));
                doctorId.setText(String.valueOf(current.getDoctorId()));
                appointmentDate.setValue(current.getAppointmentDate().toLocalDate());
                appointmentTime.setText(String.valueOf(current.getAppointmentTime()));
                type.setValue(current.getType());
                paymentMethod.setValue(current.getPaymentMethodAsString());
                reason.setText(current.getReason());
                messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
                messageLabel.setText("Rendez-vous chargé. Modifiez puis cliquez sur Modifier.");
            }
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("ID invalide.");
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    void modifierRdv() {
        if (current == null) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Veuillez d'abord rechercher un rendez-vous.");
            return;
        }
        try {
            current.setPatientId(Integer.parseInt(patientId.getText().trim()));
            current.setDoctorId(Integer.parseInt(doctorId.getText().trim()));
            current.setAppointmentDate(Date.valueOf(appointmentDate.getValue()));
            current.setAppointmentTime(Time.valueOf(appointmentTime.getText().trim()));
            current.setType(type.getValue());
            current.setPaymentMethodFromString(paymentMethod.getValue());
            current.setReason(reason.getText().trim());

            service.update(current);
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #38A169;");
            messageLabel.setText("Rendez-vous mis à jour avec succès !");
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("ID Patient/Médecin invalides.");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Format heure invalide. Utilisez HH:mm:ss");
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.availability;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import services.availabilityServices;

import java.net.URL;
import java.sql.Date;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Time;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class UpdateAvailability implements Initializable {

    @FXML private TextField searchId;
    @FXML private TextField doctorId;
    @FXML private DatePicker date;
    @FXML private TextField time;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> statusRDV;
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;

    private final availabilityServices service = new availabilityServices();
    private availability current;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL url, ResourceBundle rb) {
        // ✅ Remplir le ComboBox avec les valeurs de l'enum StatusRDV
        statusRDV.getItems().addAll("libre", "occupe");
    }

    @FXML
    void rechercherDisponibilite() {
        try {
            int id = Integer.parseInt(searchId.getText().trim());

            // ✅ CORRECTION : getAvailabilityById() au lieu de getById()
            current = service.getAvailabilityById(id);

            if (current == null) {
                messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
                messageLabel.setText("Aucune disponibilité trouvée avec l'ID " + id);
                clearFields();
            } else {
                // ✅ Remplir les champs avec les données existantes
                doctorId.setText(String.valueOf(current.getDoctorId()));

                // ✅ CORRECTION : Date SQL → LocalDate pour DatePicker
                date.setValue(current.getDate().toLocalDate());

                // ✅ Time SQL → String HH:mm:ss
                time.setText(current.getTime().toString());

                // ✅ CORRECTION : getStatusRDVAsString() car statusRDV est un enum
                statusRDV.setValue(current.getStatusRDVAsString());

                messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #2D3748;");
                messageLabel.setText("Disponibilité trouvée. Modifiez puis cliquez sur Modifier.");
            }
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("ID invalide.");
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    void modifierDisponibilite() {
        if (current == null) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Veuillez d'abord rechercher une disponibilité.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            // ✅ Validation doctorId
            int docId = Integer.parseInt(doctorId.getText().trim());

            // ✅ CORRECTION : LocalDate → java.sql.Date
            LocalDate localDate = date.getValue();
            if (localDate == null) {
                messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
                messageLabel.setText("Veuillez sélectionner une date.");
                return;
            }
            Date sqlDate = Date.valueOf(localDate);

            // ✅ Validation et parsing de l'heure HH:mm:ss
            String timeText = time.getText().trim();
            if (!timeText.matches("\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2}")) {
                messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
                messageLabel.setText("Format heure invalide. Utilisez HH:mm:ss");
                return;
            }
            Time sqlTime = Time.valueOf(timeText);

            // ✅ Validation statut
            String selectedStatus = statusRDV.getValue();
            if (selectedStatus == null) {
                messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
                messageLabel.setText("Veuillez sélectionner un statut.");
                return;
            }

            // ✅ Mettre à jour l'objet current
            current.setDoctorId(docId);
            current.setDate(sqlDate);
            current.setTime(sqlTime);
            current.setStatusRDVFromString(selectedStatus);

            service.update(current);

            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #38A169;");
            messageLabel.setText("Disponibilité #" + current.getAvailabilityId() + " modifiée avec succès !");

        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("ID Médecin invalide.");
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            messageLabel.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #FF5C5C;");
            messageLabel.setText("Erreur : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void clearFields() {
        doctorId.clear();
        date.setValue(null);
        time.clear();
        statusRDV.setValue(null);
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Reclamation;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.layout.FlowPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.GeminiModerationService;
import services.ReclamationServices;
import services.ModerationResult;
import java.io.File;
import java.sql.Date;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class UserAddReclamationController {

    @FXML private TextField subjectField;
    @FXML private TextArea descriptionField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> priorityCombo;
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;

    // ==================== ATTACHMENTS ====================
    @FXML private Button btnAddAttachment;
    @FXML private FlowPane attachmentsPreview;

    private final ReclamationServices reclamationServices = new ReclamationServices();
    private final List<File> selectedFiles = new ArrayList<>();

    private final int currentUserId = 1;
    private boolean submitted = false;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        priorityCombo.getItems().addAll("faible", "moyenne", "urgente");
        priorityCombo.setValue("moyenne");

        if (btnAddAttachment != null) {
            btnAddAttachment.setOnAction(e -> chooseAttachments());
        }
    }

    private void chooseAttachments() {
        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Choisir des fichiers (images ou documents)");

        FileChooser.ExtensionFilter imageFilter =
                new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Images", "*.png", "*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.gif", "*.webp");

        FileChooser.ExtensionFilter docFilter =
                new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Documents", "*.pdf", "*.doc", "*.docx", "*.txt");

        FileChooser.ExtensionFilter allFilter =
                new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Tous les fichiers", "*.*");

        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().addAll(imageFilter, docFilter, allFilter);

        // ✅ Correct method name
        fileChooser.setInitialFileName(String.valueOf(true));

        List<File> files = fileChooser.showOpenMultipleDialog(
                (Stage) subjectField.getScene().getWindow());

        if (files != null) {
            for (File file : files) {
                if (file.length() > 15 * 1024 * 1024) { // 15MB limit
                    showWarning("Fichier trop volumineux",
                            file.getName() + " dépasse la limite de 15MB.");
                    continue;
                }
                if (!selectedFiles.contains(file)) {
                    selectedFiles.add(file);
                    addFileToPreview(file);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void addFileToPreview(File file) {
        HBox item = new HBox(8);
        item.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        item.setPadding(new Insets(6, 10, 6, 10));
        item.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #f0f4fa; -fx-background-radius: 8;");

        Label nameLabel = new Label("📎 " + file.getName());
        nameLabel.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 12px;");

        Button removeBtn = new Button("✕");
        removeBtn.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #e74c3c; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-cursor: hand;");

        removeBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
            selectedFiles.remove(file);
            attachmentsPreview.getChildren().remove(item);
        });

        item.getChildren().addAll(nameLabel, removeBtn);
        attachmentsPreview.getChildren().add(item);
    }

    @FXML
    private void ajouterReclamation() {
        String subject = subjectField.getText().trim();
        String description = descriptionField.getText().trim();
        String priority = priorityCombo.getValue();

        if (subject.isEmpty() || subject.length() < 3) {
            showWarning("Sujet invalide", "Le sujet doit contenir au moins 3 caractères.");
            subjectField.requestFocus();
            return;
        }
        if (description.isEmpty() || description.length() < 10) {
            showWarning("Description invalide", "La description doit contenir au moins 10 caractères.");
            descriptionField.requestFocus();
            return;
        }
        if (priority == null || priority.isEmpty()) {
            showWarning("Priorité requise", "Veuillez choisir une priorité.");
            return;
        }

        // Moderation
        try {
            GeminiModerationService moderation = new GeminiModerationService();
            ModerationResult result = moderation.checkReclamation(subject, description);
            if (result.isHarmful()) {
                showWarning("Contenu inapproprié détecté", result.explanation());
                return;
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("⚠️ Moderation error (continuing): " + e.getMessage());
        }

        // Create & Save Reclamation
        Reclamation reclamation = new Reclamation(
                subject, description, Date.valueOf(LocalDate.now()),
                "En attente", priority, currentUserId);

        reclamationServices.add(reclamation);

        // Save Attachments
        if (!selectedFiles.isEmpty()) {
            reclamationServices.addAttachments(reclamation.getReclamationId(), selectedFiles);
        }

        submitted = true;
        showInfo("Succès", "Réclamation ajoutée avec succès !");

        Stage stage = (Stage) subjectField.getScene().getWindow();
        stage.close();
    }

    @FXML
    private void annuler() {
        Stage stage = (Stage) subjectField.getScene().getWindow();
        stage.close();
    }

    private void showWarning(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private void showInfo(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    public boolean isSubmitted() {
        return submitted;
    }
}
package Controler;

import GUI.MapViewWindow;
import entities.Pharmacy;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.layout.FlowPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import services.AssistantProximiteService;
import services.PharmacyRecommendationService.PharmacyRecommendation;
import tests.Main;

import java.util.List;

public class UserAssistantController {

    @FXML private TextField locationField;
    @FXML private FlowPane resultsBox;
    @FXML private Label infoLabel;

    private AssistantProximiteService assistantProximiteService;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        assistantProximiteService = new AssistantProximiteService();
        infoLabel.setText("Saisissez votre emplacement pour afficher les pharmacies les plus proches.");
    }

    @FXML
    public void searchNearestPharmacies() {
        String location = locationField.getText();

        if (location == null || location.trim().isEmpty()) {
            infoLabel.setText("Veuillez saisir votre emplacement. Exemple : Ariana Soghra.");
            return;
        }

        resultsBox.getChildren().clear();
        infoLabel.setText("Recherche des pharmacies les plus proches...");

        Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
            assistantProximiteService.rechercherPharmaciesProches(location);
            String userLocation = assistantProximiteService.getLastUserLocation();
            List<PharmacyRecommendation> recommendations = assistantProximiteService.getLastRecommendations();

            Platform.runLater(() -> {
                resultsBox.getChildren().clear();

                if (recommendations == null || recommendations.isEmpty()) {
                    infoLabel.setText("Aucune pharmacie proche trouvée pour : " + location);
                    return;
                }

                infoLabel.setText("Les pharmacies les plus proches de : " + userLocation);

                int rank = 1;

                for (PharmacyRecommendation recommendation : recommendations) {
                    resultsBox.getChildren().add(createRecommendationCard(rank, userLocation, recommendation));
                    rank++;
                }
            });
        });

        thread.setDaemon(true);
        thread.start();
    }

    private VBox createRecommendationCard(int rank, String userLocation, PharmacyRecommendation recommendation) {
        Pharmacy pharmacy = recommendation.getPharmacy();

        VBox card = new VBox();
        card.setSpacing(10);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(18));
        card.setPrefWidth(330);
        card.setMinHeight(230);
        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: white;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 18;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 18;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #dbe5f0;" +
                        "-fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.10), 12, 0, 0, 4);"
        );

        Label title = new Label(rank + ". " + safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyName()));
        title.setWrapText(true);
        title.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 18px; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #0b1f44;");

        Label address = new Label("Adresse : " + safe(pharmacy.getAddress()));
        address.setWrapText(true);
        address.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #53627c; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Label phone = new Label("Téléphone : " + safe(pharmacy.getPhone()));
        phone.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #53627c; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Label status = new Label("Statut : " + safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyStatus()));
        status.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #ecfdf5;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #166534;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-padding: 7 12 7 12;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;"
        );

        Button routeButton = new Button("Afficher l’itinéraire");
        routeButton.setPrefHeight(42);
        routeButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        routeButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #2563eb;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 14;" +
                        "-fx-padding: 10 16 10 16;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );

        routeButton.setOnAction(event -> MapViewWindow.showRouteInApp(
                userLocation,
                pharmacy.getPharmacyName(),
                pharmacy.getAddress()
        ));

        card.getChildren().addAll(
                title,
                address,
                phone,
                status,
                routeButton
        );

        return card;
    }

    @FXML
    public void clearAssistant() {
        locationField.clear();
        resultsBox.getChildren().clear();
        infoLabel.setText("Saisissez votre emplacement pour afficher les pharmacies les plus proches.");
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showPatientDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showPatientProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showPatientPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goAssistant() {
        Main.showPatientAssistant();
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null || value.trim().isEmpty() ? "Non renseigné" : value.trim();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Notification;
import entities.Reclamation;
import entities.Reponse;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;

import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.chart.BarChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.CategoryAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.NumberAxis;
import javafx.scene.chart.PieChart;
import javafx.scene.chart.XYChart;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ButtonType;

import services.NotificationService;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * UserDashboardController
 *
 * FIXES applied:
 *
 * 1. countByStatus() used .contains("resolu") which NEVER matches "Résolu"
 *    (accented é). Fixed with accent-normalised comparison using
 *    java.text.Normalizer — strips diacritics before comparing.
 *
 * 2. loadLastResponses() now shows BOTH admin responses AND status-change
 *    notifications (type STATUT_CHANGE / RESOLUTION) as a unified activity
 *    timeline, sorted newest first.
 *
 * 3. The recent reclamations TableView (ROW 4) has been removed entirely
 *    from both this controller and the FXML.
 */
public class UserDashboardController implements Initializable {

    private static final int CURRENT_USER_ID = 1;

    // ── Services ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private final ReclamationServices reclamationSvc = new ReclamationServices();
    private final ReponseServices     reponseSvc     = new ReponseServices();
    private final NotificationService notifSvc       = new NotificationService();

    // ── KPI Labels ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private Label lblTotalReclamations;
    @FXML private Label lblTotalTrend;
    @FXML private Label lblEnAttente;
    @FXML private Label lblEnAttentePct;
    @FXML private Label lblResolues;
    @FXML private Label lblResoluesTrend;
    @FXML private Label lblNouvellesReponses;
    @FXML private Label lblReponsesInfo;
    @FXML private Label lblNewResponsesBadge;

    // ── Priority Labels ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private Label lblUrgente;
    @FXML private Label lblMoyenne;
    @FXML private Label lblFaible;

    // ── Charts ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private PieChart                 statusPieChart;
    @FXML private BarChart<String, Number> categoryBarChart;
    @FXML private CategoryAxis             categoryXAxis;
    @FXML private NumberAxis               categoryYAxis;

    // ── Dynamic containers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private VBox pendingBox;
    @FXML private VBox responsesBox;

    @FXML private Button btnRefresh;

    // ── Data cache ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private List<Reclamation> myReclamations = new ArrayList<>();

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @Override
    public void initialize(URL url, ResourceBundle rb) {
        loadAll();
    }

    // ── LOAD ALL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void loadAll() {
        myReclamations = reclamationSvc.getByUser(CURRENT_USER_ID);

        runSafe(this::loadKpis,           "loadKpis");
        runSafe(this::loadPieChart,        "loadPieChart");
        runSafe(this::loadCategoryChart,   "loadCategoryChart");
        runSafe(this::loadPendingAlerts,   "loadPendingAlerts");
        runSafe(this::loadActivityFeed,    "loadActivityFeed");
    }

    private void runSafe(Runnable r, String name) {
        try { r.run(); }
        catch (Exception e) { System.out.println(name + " error: " + e.getMessage()); e.printStackTrace(); }
    }

    // ── KPI CARDS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void loadKpis() {
        long total     = myReclamations.size();
        long enAttente = countByStatus("en attente");
        long enCours   = countByStatus("en cours");
        long resolu    = countByStatus("resolu");       // FIX: accent-normalised
        long urgente   = countByPriority("urgente");
        long moyenne   = countByPriority("moyenne");
        long faible    = countByPriority("faible");

        // Count all responses for this user's reclamations
        int totalResponses = 0;
        for (Reclamation r : myReclamations) {
            totalResponses += reponseSvc.getByReclamation(r.getReclamationId()).size();
        }

        set(lblTotalReclamations, String.valueOf(total));
        set(lblTotalTrend, "Vos réclamations actives");
        set(lblEnAttente, String.valueOf(enAttente));
        set(lblEnAttentePct, total > 0
                ? ((int) Math.round(enAttente * 100.0 / total)) + "% du total" : "");
        set(lblResolues, String.valueOf(resolu));
        set(lblResoluesTrend, total > 0
                ? ((int) Math.round(resolu * 100.0 / total)) + "% résolu" : "");
        set(lblNouvellesReponses, String.valueOf(totalResponses));
        set(lblReponsesInfo, totalResponses + " réponse(s) au total");

        if (lblNewResponsesBadge != null) {
            lblNewResponsesBadge.setVisible(totalResponses > 0);
            lblNewResponsesBadge.setText("● " + totalResponses + " réponse(s)");
        }

        set(lblUrgente, String.valueOf(urgente));
        set(lblMoyenne, String.valueOf(moyenne));
        set(lblFaible,  String.valueOf(faible));
    }

    /**
     * FIX — accent-safe status comparison.
     *
     * Problem: "Résolu".toLowerCase() = "résolu" which does NOT contain "resolu".
     * Solution: strip diacritics with Normalizer before comparing.
     *   "résolu" → normalise → "resolu" ✓
     */
    private long countByStatus(String needle) {
        String normNeedle = normalise(needle);
        return myReclamations.stream()
                .filter(r -> r.getStatusreclam() != null
                        && normalise(r.getStatusreclam()).contains(normNeedle))
                .count();
    }

    private long countByPriority(String needle) {
        return myReclamations.stream()
                .filter(r -> r.getPriority() != null
                        && normalise(r.getPriority()).equalsIgnoreCase(normalise(needle)))
                .count();
    }

    /**
     * Strips accents/diacritics and lowercases for reliable string comparison.
     * "Résolu" → "resolu", "En Attente" → "en attente"
     */
    private String normalise(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        String decomposed = java.text.Normalizer.normalize(s, java.text.Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        return decomposed.replaceAll("\\p{InCombiningDiacriticalMarks}+", "").toLowerCase();
    }

    private void set(Label lbl, String text) {
        if (lbl != null) lbl.setText(text);
    }

    // ── PIE CHART ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void loadPieChart() {
        long enAttente = countByStatus("en attente");
        long enCours   = countByStatus("en cours");
        long resolu    = countByStatus("resolu");

        statusPieChart.getData().clear();
        statusPieChart.layout();

        ObservableList<PieChart.Data> data = FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                new PieChart.Data("En Attente", Math.max(enAttente, 0.01)),
                new PieChart.Data("En Cours",   Math.max(enCours,   0.01)),
                new PieChart.Data("Résolu",     Math.max(resolu,    0.01))
        );
        statusPieChart.setData(data);
        statusPieChart.setLegendVisible(true);
        statusPieChart.setLabelsVisible(true);
        statusPieChart.setAnimated(false);
        statusPieChart.setClockwise(true);
        statusPieChart.setStartAngle(90);

        Platform.runLater(() -> {
            String[] colors = {"#378ADD", "#EF9F27", "#639922"};
            int i = 0;
            for (PieChart.Data d : statusPieChart.getData()) {
                if (d.getNode() != null) {
                    d.getNode().setStyle("-fx-pie-color:" + colors[i % colors.length] + ";");
                }
                if (i == 0) d.setName("En Attente (" + enAttente + ")");
                else if (i == 1) d.setName("En Cours (" + enCours + ")");
                else if (i == 2) d.setName("Résolu (" + resolu + ")");
                i++;
            }
            statusPieChart.layout();
        });
    }

    // ── BAR CHART ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void loadCategoryChart() {
        Map<String, Integer> counts = new HashMap<>();
        for (Reclamation r : myReclamations) {
            String cat = r.getCategory() == null ? "Autre" : r.getCategory();
            if (cat.equalsIgnoreCase("Général")) cat = "Autre";
            counts.merge(cat, 1, Integer::sum);
        }

        categoryBarChart.getData().clear();
        categoryBarChart.setAnimated(false);

        XYChart.Series<String, Number> series = new XYChart.Series<>();
        series.setName("Réclamations");

        String[] barColors = {"#378ADD", "#EF9F27", "#639922", "#BA7517", "#D4537E", "#534AB7"};
        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> entry : counts.entrySet()) {
            series.getData().add(new XYChart.Data<>(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue()));
        }
        categoryBarChart.getData().add(series);

        Platform.runLater(() -> {
            int i = 0;
            for (XYChart.Data<String, Number> d : series.getData()) {
                if (d.getNode() != null) {
                    d.getNode().setStyle("-fx-bar-fill:" + barColors[i % barColors.length] + ";");
                }
                i++;
            }
        });
    }

    // ── PENDING ALERTS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private void loadPendingAlerts() {
        pendingBox.getChildren().clear();

        List<Reclamation> pending = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Reclamation r : myReclamations) {
            if (r.getStatusreclam() != null
                    && normalise(r.getStatusreclam()).contains("attente")) {
                pending.add(r);
            }
        }

        if (pending.isEmpty()) {
            Label ok = new Label("✅ Aucune réclamation en attente");
            ok.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#3B6D11; -fx-font-size:13px; -fx-padding:8 0 8 0;");
            pendingBox.getChildren().add(ok);
            return;
        }
        for (Reclamation r : pending) {
            pendingBox.getChildren().add(buildPendingCard(r));
        }
    }

    private VBox buildPendingCard(Reclamation r) {
        VBox card = new VBox(4);
        long daysOld = 0;
        try {
            daysOld = ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(r.getReclamDate().toLocalDate(), LocalDate.now());
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        boolean isUrgent = normalise(r.getPriority()).equals("urgente");
        boolean isOld    = daysOld > 3;

        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:" + (isUrgent && isOld ? "#fff5f5" : "#fffbf0") + ";"
                        + "-fx-border-color:"   + (isUrgent && isOld ? "#E24B4A" : "#EF9F27") + ";"
                        + "-fx-border-radius:8; -fx-background-radius:8; -fx-padding:10 12 10 12;"
        );

        HBox topRow = new HBox(6);
        topRow.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        Label idLbl = new Label("REC-" + r.getReclamationId());
        idLbl.setStyle("-fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-font-size:12px; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;");

        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        boolean urg = normalise(r.getPriority()).equals("urgente");
        Label badge = new Label(r.getPriority());
        badge.setStyle(urg
                ? "-fx-background-color:#FCEBEB; -fx-text-fill:#A32D2D;"
                + "-fx-font-size:10px; -fx-font-weight:bold;"
                + "-fx-background-radius:10; -fx-padding:2 7 2 7;"
                : "-fx-background-color:#FAEEDA; -fx-text-fill:#854F0B;"
                + "-fx-font-size:10px; -fx-font-weight:bold;"
                + "-fx-background-radius:10; -fx-padding:2 7 2 7;"
        );
        topRow.getChildren().addAll(idLbl, spacer, badge);

        Label subj = new Label(r.getSubject());
        subj.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;");
        subj.setWrapText(true);

        Label meta;
        if (isUrgent && isOld) {
            meta = new Label("⚠ Urgent · " + daysOld + " jours sans réponse · SLA dépassé");
            meta.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#A32D2D;");
        } else {
            meta = new Label("Depuis " + daysOld + " jours · " + r.getCategory());
            meta.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5;");
        }
        card.getChildren().addAll(topRow, subj, meta);
        return card;
    }

    // ── ACTIVITY FEED (responses + status changes) ───────────────────────────
    /**
     * FIX: replaced loadLastResponses() with a unified activity feed that shows:
     *   - Admin responses (from reponse table)
     *   - Status-change notifications (type STATUT_CHANGE / RESOLUTION)
     * Both sorted newest first.
     */
    private void loadActivityFeed() {
        responsesBox.getChildren().clear();

        List<ActivityItem> items = new ArrayList<>();

        // 1. Collect admin responses
        for (Reclamation r : myReclamations) {
            for (Reponse rep : reponseSvc.getByReclamation(r.getReclamationId())) {
                items.add(new ActivityItem(
                        "REPONSE",
                        "✉",
                        "#E6F1FB",
                        "REC-" + r.getReclamationId() + " : " + truncate(rep.getContent(), 80),
                        "Répondu le " + formatTs(rep.getResponseDate()) + " · Admin",
                        rep.getResponseDate() != null
                                ? rep.getResponseDate().toInstant() : java.time.Instant.EPOCH
                ));
            }
        }

        // 2. Collect status-change / resolution notifications for this user
        try {
            List<Notification> notifs = notifSvc.getByUser(CURRENT_USER_ID);
            for (Notification n : notifs) {
                String type = n.getType() == null ? "" : n.getType();
                if (type.equals("STATUT_CHANGE") || type.equals("RESOLUTION")) {
                    String icon  = type.equals("RESOLUTION") ? "✅" : "🔄";
                    String color = type.equals("RESOLUTION") ? "#E8F5E9" : "#E6F1FB";
                    items.add(new ActivityItem(
                            type, icon, color,
                            n.getMessage(),
                            formatTs(n.getCreatedAt()),
                            n.getCreatedAt() != null
                                    ? n.getCreatedAt().toInstant() : java.time.Instant.EPOCH
                    ));
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("⚠️ Notification feed error: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        // Sort newest first
        items.sort((a, b) -> b.instant.compareTo(a.instant));

        if (items.isEmpty()) {
            Label empty = new Label("📭 Aucune activité récente");
            empty.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#5d6f91; -fx-font-size:13px; -fx-padding:8 0 8 0;");
            responsesBox.getChildren().add(empty);
            return;
        }

        int limit = Math.min(items.size(), 15);
        for (int i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
            responsesBox.getChildren().add(buildActivityRow(items.get(i)));
        }
    }

    private HBox buildActivityRow(ActivityItem item) {
        HBox row = new HBox(10);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);
        row.setPadding(new Insets(8, 0, 8, 0));
        row.setStyle(
                "-fx-border-color:transparent transparent #e1e8f0 transparent;"
                        + "-fx-border-width:0 0 1 0;"
        );

        Label icon = new Label(item.icon);
        icon.setPrefWidth(32);
        icon.setPrefHeight(32);
        icon.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        icon.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:" + item.bgColor + ";"
                        + "-fx-background-radius:16; -fx-font-size:14px;"
        );

        VBox body = new VBox(2);
        HBox.setHgrow(body, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label msg = new Label(item.title);
        msg.setStyle("-fx-font-size:12px; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;");
        msg.setWrapText(true);

        Label sub = new Label(item.subtitle);
        sub.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5;");

        body.getChildren().addAll(msg, sub);
        row.getChildren().addAll(icon, body);
        return row;
    }

    // ── HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private String truncate(String s, int max) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        return s.length() > max ? s.substring(0, max) + "…" : s;
    }

    private String formatTs(java.sql.Timestamp ts) {
        if (ts == null) return "—";
        try {
            return ts.toLocalDateTime()
                    .format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm"));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return ts.toString();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void refreshDashboard() {
        loadAll();
        System.out.println("[UserDashboard] refreshed");
    }

    @FXML private void ouvrirUserDashboard()    { MainFx.changeScene("/userdashboard.fxml"); }
    @FXML private void ouvrirUserReclamations() { MainFx.changeScene("/userreclamations.fxml"); }

    // ── Inner classes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private static class ActivityItem {
        final String            type;
        final String            icon;
        final String            bgColor;
        final String            title;
        final String            subtitle;
        final java.time.Instant instant;

        ActivityItem(String type, String icon, String bgColor,
                     String title, String subtitle, java.time.Instant instant) {
            this.type    = type;
            this.icon    = icon;
            this.bgColor = bgColor;
            this.title   = title;
            this.subtitle = subtitle;
            this.instant = instant;
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Reclamation;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import services.ReclamationServices;
import tests.MainFx;

import java.util.Optional;

public class UserDeleteReclamationController {

    @FXML
    private Label idLabel;

    @FXML
    private Label subjectLabel;

    @FXML
    private Label messageLabel;

    private final ReclamationServices reclamationServices = new ReclamationServices();

    private Reclamation reclamation;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        reclamation = MainFx.selectedReclamation;

        if (reclamation != null) {
            idLabel.setText(String.valueOf(reclamation.getReclamationId()));
            subjectLabel.setText(reclamation.getSubject());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void supprimerReclamation() {
        if (reclamation == null) {
            showWarning("Erreur", "Aucune réclamation sélectionnée.");
            return;
        }

        Alert confirm = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION);
        confirm.setTitle("Confirmation");
        confirm.setHeaderText(null);
        confirm.setContentText("Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer cette réclamation ?");

        Optional<ButtonType> result = confirm.showAndWait();

        if (result.isPresent() && result.get() == ButtonType.OK) {
            reclamationServices.delete(reclamation.getReclamationId());

            showInfo("Succès", "Réclamation supprimée avec succès.");

            MainFx.clearSelection();
            MainFx.changeScene("/userreclamations.fxml");
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void retourUserReclamations() {
        MainFx.clearSelection();
        MainFx.changeScene("/userreclamations.fxml");
    }

    @FXML
    private void ouvrirUserDashboard() {
        MainFx.changeScene("/userdashboard.fxml");
    }

    @FXML
    private void ouvrirUserReclamations() {
        MainFx.changeScene("/userreclamations.fxml");
    }

    @FXML
    private void ouvrirUserReponses() {
        MainFx.clearSelection();
        MainFx.changeScene("/userreponses.fxml");
    }

    private void showWarning(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.WARNING);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private void showInfo(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Reclamation;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.Slider;
import javafx.scene.control.TextArea;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.EscalationService;
import services.NotificationService;

/**
 * UserEscaladeController
 * Bound to: userescalade.fxml (modal dialog)
 *
 * FIXED:
 * - Uses existing EscalationService.add() method (3 params)
 * - Uses existing NotificationService.triggerEscaladeManuelle() method
 * - Stores urgency in reason text for backward compatibility
 * - Properly separates admin alert from user confirmation
 */
public class UserEscaladeController {

    @FXML private Label    lblSubject;
    @FXML private Label    lblMeta;
    @FXML private TextArea txtReason;
    @FXML private Slider   sliderUrgence;   // 0–10 integer slider
    @FXML private Label    lblUrgenceValue; // shows the current integer value
    @FXML private Label    lblError;
    @FXML private Button   btnSubmit;
    @FXML private Button   btnCancel;

    private Reclamation     reclamation;
    private int             ownerUserId;
    private boolean         submitted = false;

    private final EscalationService escalationSvc = new EscalationService();

    // ── CALLED BY PARENT BEFORE showAndWait() ────────────────────────────────

    public void setData(Reclamation r, int userId) {
        this.reclamation = r;
        this.ownerUserId = userId;

        if (lblSubject != null) {
            lblSubject.setText(r.getSubject());
        }
        if (lblMeta != null) {
            lblMeta.setText(
                    "REC #" + r.getReclamationId()
                            + "  ·  Priorité : " + r.getPriority()
                            + "  ·  Date : " + r.getReclamDate()
            );
        }
        if (lblError != null) {
            lblError.setVisible(false);
            lblError.setManaged(false);
        }

        // Configure slider: integers 0–10, default = 5
        if (sliderUrgence != null) {
            sliderUrgence.setMin(0);
            sliderUrgence.setMax(10);
            sliderUrgence.setValue(5);
            sliderUrgence.setMajorTickUnit(1);
            sliderUrgence.setMinorTickCount(0);
            sliderUrgence.setSnapToTicks(true);
            sliderUrgence.setShowTickMarks(true);
            sliderUrgence.setShowTickLabels(true);
            sliderUrgence.setBlockIncrement(1);

            updateUrgenceLabel((int) sliderUrgence.getValue());
            sliderUrgence.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
                int intVal = (int) Math.round(newVal.doubleValue());
                sliderUrgence.setValue(intVal);
                updateUrgenceLabel(intVal);
            });
        }
    }

    private void updateUrgenceLabel(int value) {
        if (lblUrgenceValue == null) return;
        String label = value + "/10";
        if (value >= 8) {
            label += "  🔴 Critique";
            lblUrgenceValue.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#A32D2D; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        } else if (value >= 5) {
            label += "  🟠 Élevé";
            lblUrgenceValue.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#854F0B; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        } else {
            label += "  🟡 Modéré";
            lblUrgenceValue.setStyle("-fx-text-fill:#5d6f91; -fx-font-weight:bold;");
        }
        lblUrgenceValue.setText(label);
    }

    public boolean isSubmitted() { return submitted; }

    // ── ACTIONS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @FXML
    private void handleSubmit() {
        String reason = txtReason.getText();

        if (reason == null || reason.trim().isEmpty()) {
            showError("Veuillez saisir une raison.");
            return;
        }
        if (reason.trim().length() < 10) {
            showError("La raison doit contenir au moins 10 caractères.");
            return;
        }
        if (escalationSvc.hasOpenEscalation(reclamation.getReclamationId())) {
            showError("Une demande d'escalade est déjà en cours pour cette réclamation.");
            return;
        }

        // Get urgency level from slider (default 5 if slider absent)
        int urgenceLevel = 5;
        if (sliderUrgence != null) {
            urgenceLevel = (int) Math.round(sliderUrgence.getValue());
        }

        // Build the reason string WITH urgency embedded (for backward compatibility)
        String fullReason = reason.trim() + " [Urgence : " + urgenceLevel + "/10]";

        // ── Persist escalation using EXISTING method signature ──────────
        // escalationSvc.add(reclamationId, userId, reason)
        escalationSvc.add(reclamation.getReclamationId(), ownerUserId, fullReason);

        // ── Notify admin using EXISTING method ───────────────────────────
        // NotificationService.triggerEscaladeManuelle(recId, userId, subject, fullReason)
        NotificationService.triggerEscaladeManuelle(
                reclamation.getReclamationId(),
                ownerUserId,
                reclamation.getSubject(),
                fullReason
        );

        submitted = true;
        close();
    }

    @FXML
    private void handleCancel() {
        close();
    }

    // ── HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void showError(String msg) {
        if (lblError != null) {
            lblError.setText(msg);
            lblError.setVisible(true);
            lblError.setManaged(true);
        }
    }

    private void close() {
        Stage stage = null;
        if (btnCancel != null && btnCancel.getScene() != null) {
            stage = (Stage) btnCancel.getScene().getWindow();
        } else if (btnSubmit != null && btnSubmit.getScene() != null) {
            stage = (Stage) btnSubmit.getScene().getWindow();
        }
        if (stage != null) stage.close();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Notification;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import services.NotificationService;
import tests.MainFx;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * UserNotificationsController
 * Bound to: usernotifications.fxml
 *
 * Shows all notifications for the currently logged-in user.
 * Hardcoded userId = 2 until session management is implemented.
 */
public class UserNotificationsController {

    // ── Sidebar navigation ──────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private Button btnDashboard;
    @FXML private Button btnMesReclamations;
    @FXML private Button btnNotifications;

    // ── Header ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private Label lblUnreadBadge;
    @FXML private Button btnMarkAllRead;

    // ── Content ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @FXML private ScrollPane scrollPane;
    @FXML private VBox notifList;
    @FXML private Label lblEmpty;

    // !! Replace with real session userId when session management is added
    private static final int USER_ID = 2;

    private NotificationService notifSvc = new NotificationService();

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        if (btnNotifications != null) {
            btnNotifications.getStyleClass().add("sidebar-button-active");
        }
        loadNotifications();
    }

    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    //  LOAD
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void loadNotifications() {
        if (notifList == null) { return; }
        notifList.getChildren().clear();

        List<Notification> list = notifSvc.getByUser(USER_ID);

        long unread = 0;
        for (Notification n : list) {
            if (!n.isRead()) { unread++; }
        }

        if (lblUnreadBadge != null) {
            if (unread > 0) {
                lblUnreadBadge.setText(String.valueOf(unread));
                lblUnreadBadge.setVisible(true);
                lblUnreadBadge.setManaged(true);
            } else {
                lblUnreadBadge.setVisible(false);
                lblUnreadBadge.setManaged(false);
            }
        }

        if (list.isEmpty()) {
            if (lblEmpty != null) { lblEmpty.setVisible(true); lblEmpty.setManaged(true); }
            return;
        }
        if (lblEmpty != null) { lblEmpty.setVisible(false); lblEmpty.setManaged(false); }

        for (Notification n : list) {
            notifList.getChildren().add(buildCard(n));
        }
    }

    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    //  CARD BUILDER
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private HBox buildCard(Notification n) {
        HBox card = new HBox(14);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(16, 18, 16, 18));
        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: " + (n.isRead() ? "white" : "#EEF5FF") + ";"
                        + "-fx-border-color: " + (n.isRead() ? "#e1e8f0" : "#378ADD") + ";"
                        + "-fx-border-width: 0 0 0 4;"
                        + "-fx-background-radius: 12;"
                        + "-fx-border-radius: 12;"
                        + "-fx-effect: dropshadow(gaussian, rgba(0,0,0,0.06), 6, 0, 0, 2);"
        );
        card.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);

        // Icon circle
        Label icon = new Label(n.getIcon());
        icon.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 22px;"
                        + "-fx-min-width: 44px; -fx-max-width: 44px;"
                        + "-fx-min-height: 44px; -fx-max-height: 44px;"
                        + "-fx-alignment: center;"
                        + "-fx-background-color: " + iconBg(n.getType()) + ";"
                        + "-fx-background-radius: 22;"
        );

        // Text
        VBox textBox = new VBox(4);
        HBox.setHgrow(textBox, Priority.ALWAYS);

        HBox topRow = new HBox();
        Label typeLabel = new Label(n.getTypeLabel());
        typeLabel.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 11px; -fx-font-weight: bold;"
                        + "-fx-text-fill: " + typeColor(n.getType()) + ";"
                        + "-fx-background-color: " + iconBg(n.getType()) + ";"
                        + "-fx-padding: 2 8 2 8; -fx-background-radius: 10;"
        );
        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
        Label time = new Label(formatTime(n.getCreatedAt()));
        time.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11px; -fx-text-fill: #9aabb5;");
        topRow.getChildren().addAll(typeLabel, spacer, time);

        Label msg = new Label(n.getMessage());
        msg.setWrapText(true);
        msg.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 13px;"
                        + "-fx-text-fill: " + (n.isRead() ? "#5d6f91" : "#0b1f44") + ";"
        );

        Label recRef = new Label("Réclamation #" + n.getReclamationId());
        recRef.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 11px; -fx-text-fill: #9aabb5;");

        textBox.getChildren().addAll(topRow, msg, recRef);

        // Mark read action
        VBox actions = new VBox();
        actions.setStyle("-fx-alignment: center;");
        if (!n.isRead()) {
            Button markBtn = new Button("✓");
            markBtn.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: transparent;"
                            + "-fx-text-fill: #378ADD; -fx-font-size: 16px; -fx-cursor: hand;"
            );
            markBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
                notifSvc.markAsRead(n.getNotificationId());
                loadNotifications();
            });
            markBtn.setTooltip(new javafx.scene.control.Tooltip("Marquer comme lu"));
            actions.getChildren().add(markBtn);
        }

        card.getChildren().addAll(icon, textBox, actions);
        return card;
    }

    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    //  ACTIONS
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @FXML
    private void handleMarkAllRead() {
        notifSvc.markAllAsRead(USER_ID);
        loadNotifications();
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToDashboard() {
        MainFx.changeScene("/userdashboard.fxml");
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToMesReclamations() {
        MainFx.changeScene("/userreclamations.fxml");
    }

    @FXML
    private void goToNotifications() {
        // Already here
    }

    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    //  HELPERS
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private String formatTime(java.sql.Timestamp ts) {
        if (ts == null) { return ""; }
        LocalDateTime ldt = ts.toLocalDateTime();
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        long minutes = java.time.Duration.between(ldt, now).toMinutes();
        if (minutes < 1)  { return "À l'instant"; }
        if (minutes < 60) { return minutes + " min"; }
        long hours = minutes / 60;
        if (hours < 24)   { return hours + "h"; }
        DateTimeFormatter fmt = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm");
        return ldt.format(fmt);
    }

    private String iconBg(String type) {
        if ("REPONSE_AJOUTEE".equals(type))    { return "#E6F1FB"; }
        if ("STATUT_CHANGE".equals(type))       { return "#EAF3DE"; }
        if ("ESCALADE_AUTO".equals(type))       { return "#FCEBEB"; }
        if ("ESCALADE_MANUELLE".equals(type))   { return "#FAEEDA"; }
        if ("RESOLUTION".equals(type))          { return "#EAF3DE"; }
        return "#f4f8fc";
    }

    private String typeColor(String type) {
        if ("REPONSE_AJOUTEE".equals(type))    { return "#185FA5"; }
        if ("STATUT_CHANGE".equals(type))       { return "#3B6D11"; }
        if ("ESCALADE_AUTO".equals(type))       { return "#A32D2D"; }
        if ("ESCALADE_MANUELLE".equals(type))   { return "#854F0B"; }
        if ("RESOLUTION".equals(type))          { return "#3B6D11"; }
        return "#5d6f91";
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Pharmacy;
import javafx.animation.*;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.util.Duration;
import services.PharmacyServices;
import tests.Main;
import utils.PharmacyOpeningUtils;

import java.awt.Desktop;
import java.io.File;
import java.net.URI;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.*;

public class UserPharmacyController {

    @FXML private FlowPane pharmacyContainer;
    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> serviceCombo;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> cityCombo;
    @FXML private Label totalLabel;
    @FXML private Label pageLabel;

    private PharmacyServices pharmacyServices;

    private List<Pharmacy> pharmacies = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<Pharmacy> displayedPharmacies = new ArrayList<>();

    private int currentPage = 0;
    private final int itemsPerPage = 6;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        pharmacyServices = new PharmacyServices();

        setupCombos();
        loadPharmacies();

        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterPharmacies());
        }

        if (serviceCombo != null) {
            serviceCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterPharmacies());
        }

        if (cityCombo != null) {
            cityCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterPharmacies());
        }
    }

    private void setupCombos() {
        if (serviceCombo != null) {
            serviceCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(
                    "Tous les services",
                    "Ouverte maintenant",
                    "Fermée maintenant",
                    "Service : Jour",
                    "Service : Nuit"
            ));
            serviceCombo.setValue("Tous les services");
        }

        if (cityCombo != null) {
            cityCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(getAllTunisianCities()));
            cityCombo.setValue("Toutes les villes");
            cityCombo.setVisibleRowCount(8);
        }
    }

    private void loadPharmacies() {
        try {
            pharmacies = pharmacyServices.getAll();
            displayedPharmacies = new ArrayList<>(pharmacies);

            loadCities();

            currentPage = 0;
            showCurrentPage();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Erreur : impossible de charger les pharmacies.");
            }
        }
    }

    private void loadCities() {
        if (cityCombo == null) {
            return;
        }

        Set<String> finalCities = new TreeSet<>();
        finalCities.addAll(getAllTunisianCitiesWithoutDefault());

        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : pharmacies) {
            String city = extractCity(pharmacy.getAddress());

            if (!city.isEmpty() && !isCountry(city)) {
                finalCities.add(city);
            }
        }

        List<String> cities = new ArrayList<>();
        cities.add("Toutes les villes");
        cities.addAll(finalCities);

        cityCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(cities));
        cityCombo.setValue("Toutes les villes");
        cityCombo.setVisibleRowCount(8);
    }

    private List<String> getAllTunisianCities() {
        List<String> cities = new ArrayList<>();
        cities.add("Toutes les villes");
        cities.addAll(getAllTunisianCitiesWithoutDefault());
        return cities;
    }

    private List<String> getAllTunisianCitiesWithoutDefault() {
        return Arrays.asList(
                "Ariana", "Béja", "Ben Arous", "Bizerte", "Gabès", "Gafsa",
                "Jendouba", "Kairouan", "Kasserine", "Kébili", "Le Kef",
                "Mahdia", "La Manouba", "Médenine", "Monastir", "Nabeul",
                "Sfax", "Sidi Bouzid", "Siliana", "Sousse", "Tataouine",
                "Tozeur", "Tunis", "Zaghouan"
        );
    }

    private void filterPharmacies() {
        currentPage = 0;

        String keyword = searchField == null ? "" : normalizeText(searchField.getText());
        String selectedService = serviceCombo == null ? "Tous les services" : serviceCombo.getValue();
        String selectedCity = cityCombo == null ? "Toutes les villes" : cityCombo.getValue();
