        List<Pharmacy> filtered = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : pharmacies) {
            PharmacyOpeningUtils.PharmacyStatus status =
                    PharmacyOpeningUtils.calculateStatus(pharmacy.getOpeningHours());

            String name = normalizeText(pharmacy.getPharmacyName());
            String address = normalizeText(pharmacy.getAddress());
            String phone = normalizeText(pharmacy.getPhone());
            String openingHours = normalizeText(pharmacy.getOpeningHours());
            String pharmacyStatus = normalizeText(pharmacy.getPharmacyStatus());
            String city = normalizeText(extractCity(pharmacy.getAddress()));

            boolean matchKeyword = keyword.isEmpty()
                    || name.contains(keyword)
                    || address.contains(keyword)
                    || phone.contains(keyword)
                    || openingHours.contains(keyword)
                    || pharmacyStatus.contains(keyword)
                    || city.contains(keyword);

            boolean matchCity = selectedCity == null
                    || selectedCity.equals("Toutes les villes")
                    || city.equals(normalizeText(selectedCity))
                    || address.contains(normalizeText(selectedCity));

            boolean matchService = selectedService == null
                    || selectedService.equals("Tous les services")
                    || normalizeText(status.getMainStatus()).equals(normalizeText(selectedService))
                    || normalizeText(status.getServiceType()).equals(normalizeText(selectedService));

            if (matchKeyword && matchCity && matchService) {
                filtered.add(pharmacy);
            }
        }

        displayedPharmacies = filtered;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    private void showCurrentPage() {
        if (pharmacyContainer == null) {
            return;
        }

        pharmacyContainer.getChildren().clear();

        if (displayedPharmacies == null || displayedPharmacies.isEmpty()) {
            VBox emptyBox = new VBox();
            emptyBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
            emptyBox.setSpacing(8);
            emptyBox.setPrefWidth(900);
            emptyBox.setPrefHeight(250);

            Label title = new Label("Aucune pharmacie trouvée.");
            title.getStyleClass().add("medicine-title");

            Label text = new Label("Essayez un autre filtre.");
            text.getStyleClass().add("admin-subtitle");

            emptyBox.getChildren().addAll(title, text);
            pharmacyContainer.getChildren().add(emptyBox);

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Total : 0 pharmacie");
            }

            if (pageLabel != null) {
                pageLabel.setText("Page 0 / 0");
            }

            return;
        }

        int totalItems = displayedPharmacies.size();
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) totalItems / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < 0) {
            currentPage = 0;
        }

        if (currentPage >= totalPages) {
            currentPage = totalPages - 1;
        }

        int start = currentPage * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, totalItems);

        int index = 0;

        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : displayedPharmacies.subList(start, end)) {
            VBox card = createPharmacyCard(pharmacy);
            pharmacyContainer.getChildren().add(card);
            playCardEntrance(card, index);
            index++;
        }

        if (totalLabel != null) {
            totalLabel.setText("Total : " + totalItems + " pharmacies");
        }

        if (pageLabel != null) {
            pageLabel.setText("Page " + (currentPage + 1) + " / " + totalPages);
        }
    }

    private VBox createPharmacyCard(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        PharmacyOpeningUtils.PharmacyStatus status =
                PharmacyOpeningUtils.calculateStatus(pharmacy.getOpeningHours());

        VBox card = new VBox();
        card.setSpacing(11);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(20));
        card.setPrefWidth(300);
        card.setMinWidth(300);
        card.setMaxWidth(300);
        card.setMinHeight(455);

        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FFFFFF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 20;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: transparent;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 20;"
        );

        DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
        normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
        normalShadow.setRadius(24);
        normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
        card.setEffect(normalShadow);

        StackPane imageFrame = createImageFrame(pharmacy);

        Label name = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyName()));
        name.setWrapText(true);
        name.setStyle(
                "-fx-font-size: 20px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;"
        );

        Label address = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getAddress()));
        address.setWrapText(true);
        address.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #64748B;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                        "-fx-line-spacing: 5;"
        );

        Label phoneTitle = new Label("📞 Téléphone");
        phoneTitle.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #334155; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Label phone = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getPhone()));
        phone.setWrapText(true);
        phone.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #0F172A; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Label hoursTitle = new Label("🕐 Horaires");
        hoursTitle.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #334155; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        Label hours = new Label(safe(pharmacy.getOpeningHours()));
        hours.setStyle("-fx-text-fill: #0F172A; -fx-font-size: 13px;");

        VBox timeBlock = createModernTimeBlock(status);

        Button mapButton = new Button("Voir sur carte");
        mapButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        mapButton.setPrefHeight(42);
        mapButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );
        mapButton.setOnAction(event -> openMap(pharmacy));

        setupMapButtonHover(mapButton);

        card.getChildren().addAll(
                imageFrame,
                name,
                address,
                phoneTitle,
                phone,
                hoursTitle,
                hours,
                createSeparator(),
                timeBlock,
                createSeparator(),
                mapButton
        );

        setupCardHover(card);

        return card;
    }

    private StackPane createImageFrame(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        StackPane imageFrame = new StackPane();
        imageFrame.setPrefWidth(260);
        imageFrame.setPrefHeight(120);
        imageFrame.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 16;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #DBEAFE;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 16;"
        );

        ImageView imageView = new ImageView();
        imageView.setFitWidth(230);
        imageView.setFitHeight(105);
        imageView.setPreserveRatio(true);
        imageView.setSmooth(true);

        if (loadPharmacyImage(pharmacy, imageView)) {
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(imageView);
        } else {
            Label icon = new Label("⚕");
            icon.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 34px; -fx-text-fill: #94A3B8;");
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(icon);
        }

        return imageFrame;
    }

    private boolean loadPharmacyImage(Pharmacy pharmacy, ImageView imageView) {
        try {
            String imagePath = pharmacy.getImage();

            if (imagePath != null && !imagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                File file = new File(imagePath);

                if (file.exists()) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                    return true;
                }
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur chargement image pharmacie : " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return false;
    }

    private VBox createModernTimeBlock(PharmacyOpeningUtils.PharmacyStatus status) {
        VBox box = new VBox();
        box.setSpacing(8);
        box.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);

        boolean isOpen = status.isOpen();

        String serviceIcon = isOpen ? "🌤️ " : "🌙 ";
        String statusIcon = isOpen ? "✅ " : "❌ ";

        String bgColor = isOpen ? "#D1FAE5" : "#FEE2E2";
        String textColor = isOpen ? "#10B981" : "#EF4444";

        Label serviceBadge = createModernBadge(
                serviceIcon + status.getServiceType(),
                bgColor,
                textColor
        );

        Label statusBadge = createModernBadge(
                statusIcon + status.getMainStatus(),
                bgColor,
                textColor
        );

        if (isOpen) {
            startOpenPulse(statusBadge);
        } else {
            addClosedShadow(statusBadge);
        }

        Label detailLabel = new Label("🕐 " + status.getDetailStatus());
        detailLabel.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        detailLabel.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        detailLabel.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #64748B;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                        "-fx-font-style: italic;" +
                        "-fx-padding: 4 0 0 0;"
        );

        box.getChildren().addAll(serviceBadge, statusBadge, detailLabel);

        return box;
    }

    private Label createModernBadge(String text, String backgroundColor, String textColor) {
        Label label = new Label(text);
        label.setMinWidth(200);
        label.setMaxWidth(220);
        label.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        label.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: " + backgroundColor + ";" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: " + textColor + ";" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 999;" +
                        "-fx-padding: 10 24 10 24;"
        );

        DropShadow shadow = new DropShadow();
        shadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.04));
        shadow.setRadius(8);
        shadow.setOffsetY(2);
        label.setEffect(shadow);

        return label;
    }

    private Region createSeparator() {
        Region line = new Region();
        line.setPrefHeight(1);
        line.setMaxHeight(1);
        line.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F1F5F9;");
        VBox.setMargin(line, new Insets(3, 0, 3, 0));
        return line;
    }

    private void startOpenPulse(Label label) {
        DropShadow glow = new DropShadow();
        glow.setColor(Color.web("#10B981", 0.15));
        glow.setRadius(5);
        glow.setSpread(0.12);

        label.setEffect(glow);

        Timeline pulse = new Timeline(
                new KeyFrame(Duration.ZERO,
                        new KeyValue(glow.radiusProperty(), 4),
                        new KeyValue(glow.spreadProperty(), 0.08)
                ),
                new KeyFrame(Duration.seconds(1),
                        new KeyValue(glow.radiusProperty(), 14),
                        new KeyValue(glow.spreadProperty(), 0.28)
                ),
                new KeyFrame(Duration.seconds(2),
                        new KeyValue(glow.radiusProperty(), 4),
                        new KeyValue(glow.spreadProperty(), 0.08)
                )
        );

        pulse.setCycleCount(Animation.INDEFINITE);
        pulse.play();
    }

    private void addClosedShadow(Label label) {
        DropShadow shadow = new DropShadow();
        shadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.04));
        shadow.setRadius(8);
        shadow.setOffsetY(2);
        label.setEffect(shadow);
    }

    private void setupCardHover(VBox card) {
        card.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(-4);

            DropShadow hoverShadow = new DropShadow();
            hoverShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.10));
            hoverShadow.setRadius(32);
            hoverShadow.setOffsetY(12);
            card.setEffect(hoverShadow);

            translate.play();
        });

        card.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(0);

            DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
            normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
            normalShadow.setRadius(24);
            normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
            card.setEffect(normalShadow);

            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupMapButtonHover(Button button) {
        button.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #DBEAFE;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(-2);
            translate.play();
        });

        button.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(0);
            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void playCardEntrance(Node node, int index) {
        node.setOpacity(0);
        node.setTranslateY(20);

        FadeTransition fade = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        fade.setFromValue(0);
        fade.setToValue(1);
        fade.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        translate.setFromY(20);
        translate.setToY(0);
        translate.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        new ParallelTransition(fade, translate).play();
    }

    private void openMap(Pharmacy pharmacy) {
        try {
            String query = safe(pharmacy.getPharmacyName()) + " " + safe(pharmacy.getAddress());
            String encoded = query.trim().replace(" ", "+");
            String url = "https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=" + encoded;

            if (Desktop.isDesktopSupported()) {
                Desktop.getDesktop().browse(new URI(url));
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible d'ouvrir la carte.");
        }
    }

    private String extractCity(String address) {
        if (address == null || address.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return "";
        }

        String cleanAddress = address.trim();
        String[] parts = cleanAddress.split(",");

        if (parts.length >= 2) {
            String last = parts[parts.length - 1].trim();
            String beforeLast = parts[parts.length - 2].trim();

            if (isCountry(last)) {
                return beforeLast;
            }

            return last;
        }

        return cleanAddress;
    }

    private boolean isCountry(String value) {
        String normalized = normalizeText(value);
        return normalized.equals("tunisie")
                || normalized.equals("tunisia")
                || normalized.equals("tunisien");
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return "";
        }

        String result = value.trim().toLowerCase();
        result = Normalizer.normalize(result, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        result = result.replaceAll("\\p{M}", "");
        result = result.replaceAll("\\s+", " ");

        return result;
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    @FXML
    public void resetFilters() {
        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.clear();
        }

        if (serviceCombo != null) {
            serviceCombo.setValue("Tous les services");
        }

        if (cityCombo != null) {
            cityCombo.setValue("Toutes les villes");
        }

        displayedPharmacies = new ArrayList<>(pharmacies);
        currentPage = 0;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    @FXML
    public void nextPage() {
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) displayedPharmacies.size() / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < totalPages - 1) {
            currentPage++;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void previousPage() {
        if (currentPage > 0) {
            currentPage--;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showPatientDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showPatientProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showPatientPharmacies();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goAssistant() {
        Main.showPatientAssistant();
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.ERROR);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(title);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Product;
import javafx.animation.*;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.*;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.util.Duration;
import services.ProductServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.File;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.*;

public class UserProductController {

    @FXML private FlowPane productContainer;
    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> categoryCombo;
    @FXML private Label totalLabel;
    @FXML private Label pageLabel;

    private ProductServices productServices;

    private List<Product> products = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<ProductGroup> groupedProducts = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<ProductGroup> displayedGroups = new ArrayList<>();

    private int currentPage = 0;
    private final int itemsPerPage = 6;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        productServices = new ProductServices();

        setupCombos();
        loadProducts();

        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterProducts());
        }

        if (categoryCombo != null) {
            categoryCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, oldValue, newValue) -> filterProducts());
        }
    }

    private void setupCombos() {
        if (categoryCombo != null) {
            categoryCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList("Toutes les catégories"));
            categoryCombo.setValue("Toutes les catégories");
            categoryCombo.setVisibleRowCount(6);
        }
    }

    private void loadProducts() {
        try {
            products = productServices.getAll();

            groupedProducts = groupProducts(products);
            displayedGroups = new ArrayList<>(groupedProducts);

            loadCategories();

            currentPage = 0;
            showCurrentPage();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Erreur : impossible de charger les produits.");
            }
        }
    }

    private void loadCategories() {
        if (categoryCombo == null) {
            return;
        }

        Set<String> categories = new TreeSet<>();
        categories.add("Toutes les catégories");

        for (ProductGroup group : groupedProducts) {
            String category = safe(group.mainProduct.getCategory());

            if (!category.isEmpty()) {
                categories.add(category);
            }
        }

        categoryCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(categories));
        categoryCombo.setValue("Toutes les catégories");
    }

    private List<ProductGroup> groupProducts(List<Product> productList) {
        Map<String, ProductGroup> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        for (Product product : productList) {
            String key = normalizeText(product.getProductName()) + "|" + normalizeText(product.getDosage());

            if (!map.containsKey(key)) {
                map.put(key, new ProductGroup(product));
            }

            map.get(key).items.add(product);
        }

        return new ArrayList<>(map.values());
    }

    private void filterProducts() {
        currentPage = 0;

        String keyword = searchField == null ? "" : normalizeText(searchField.getText());
        String selectedCategory = categoryCombo == null ? "Toutes les catégories" : categoryCombo.getValue();

        List<ProductGroup> filtered = new ArrayList<>();

        for (ProductGroup group : groupedProducts) {
            Product product = group.mainProduct;

            String name = normalizeText(product.getProductName());
            String description = normalizeText(product.getDescription());
            String dosage = normalizeText(product.getDosage());
            String category = normalizeText(product.getCategory());
            String laboratory = normalizeText(product.getLaboratory());
            String therapeuticClass = normalizeText(product.getTherapeuticClass());

            boolean matchKeyword = keyword.isEmpty()
                    || name.contains(keyword)
                    || description.contains(keyword)
                    || dosage.contains(keyword)
                    || category.contains(keyword)
                    || laboratory.contains(keyword)
                    || therapeuticClass.contains(keyword);

            boolean matchCategory = selectedCategory == null
                    || selectedCategory.equals("Toutes les catégories")
                    || category.equals(normalizeText(selectedCategory));

            if (matchKeyword && matchCategory) {
                filtered.add(group);
            }
        }

        displayedGroups = filtered;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    private void showCurrentPage() {
        if (productContainer == null) {
            return;
        }

        productContainer.getChildren().clear();

        productContainer.setHgap(20);
        productContainer.setVgap(20);
        productContainer.setPadding(new Insets(20, 0, 95, 0));

        if (displayedGroups == null || displayedGroups.isEmpty()) {
            VBox emptyBox = new VBox();
            emptyBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
            emptyBox.setSpacing(8);
            emptyBox.setPrefWidth(900);
            emptyBox.setPrefHeight(250);

            Label title = new Label("Aucun produit trouvé.");
            title.getStyleClass().add("medicine-title");

            Label text = new Label("Essayez un autre filtre.");
            text.getStyleClass().add("admin-subtitle");

            emptyBox.getChildren().addAll(title, text);
            productContainer.getChildren().add(emptyBox);

            if (totalLabel != null) {
                totalLabel.setText("Total : 0 produit");
            }

            if (pageLabel != null) {
                pageLabel.setText("Page 0 / 0");
            }

            return;
        }

        int totalItems = displayedGroups.size();
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) totalItems / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < 0) {
            currentPage = 0;
        }

        if (currentPage >= totalPages) {
            currentPage = totalPages - 1;
        }

        int start = currentPage * itemsPerPage;
        int end = Math.min(start + itemsPerPage, totalItems);

        int index = 0;

        for (ProductGroup group : displayedGroups.subList(start, end)) {
            VBox card = createProductCard(group);
            productContainer.getChildren().add(card);
            playCardEntrance(card, index);
            index++;
        }

        if (totalLabel != null) {
            totalLabel.setText("Total : " + totalItems + " produits");
        }

        if (pageLabel != null) {
            pageLabel.setText("Page " + (currentPage + 1) + " / " + totalPages);
        }
    }

    private VBox createProductCard(ProductGroup group) {
        Product product = group.mainProduct;

        VBox card = new VBox();
        card.setSpacing(10);
        card.setPadding(new Insets(16));
        card.setPrefWidth(285);
        card.setMinWidth(285);
        card.setMaxWidth(285);
        card.setMinHeight(330);
        card.setAlignment(Pos.TOP_LEFT);

        card.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #FFFFFF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 20;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: transparent;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 20;"
        );

        DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
        normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
        normalShadow.setRadius(24);
        normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
        card.setEffect(normalShadow);

        StackPane imageFrame = createImageFrame(product);

        Region separatorTop = createSeparator();

        Label title = new Label(safe(product.getProductName()));
        title.setWrapText(true);
        title.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        title.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 18px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
        );

        Label description = new Label(safe(product.getDescription()));
        description.setWrapText(true);
        description.setMaxHeight(42);
        description.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #64748B;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 13px;" +
                        "-fx-line-spacing: 4;"
        );

        Label price = new Label(formatPrice(product.getPrice()) + " DT");
        price.setStyle(
                "-fx-text-fill: #10B981;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 20px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;"
        );

        Region spacer = new Region();
        VBox.setVgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Region separatorBottom = createSeparator();

        Button detailsButton = new Button("👁 Voir détails");
        detailsButton.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        detailsButton.setPrefHeight(44);
        detailsButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                        "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );
        detailsButton.setOnAction(event -> openProductDetails(product));

        setupDetailsButtonHover(detailsButton);
        setupCardHover(card);

        Button voiceButton = new Button("🔊");
        voiceButton.setPrefHeight(44);
        voiceButton.setPrefWidth(44);
        voiceButton.setMinWidth(44);
        voiceButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #F1F5F9;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 16px;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        );
        voiceButton.setOnAction(event -> services.VoiceService.speak(product.getProductName()));

        voiceButton.setOnMouseEntered(e -> voiceButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #E2E8F0;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 16px;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        ));
        voiceButton.setOnMouseExited(e -> voiceButton.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #F1F5F9;" +
                        "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                        "-fx-font-size: 16px;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                        "-fx-cursor: hand;"
        ));

        HBox bottomBox = new HBox(8);
        bottomBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        HBox.setHgrow(detailsButton, Priority.ALWAYS);
        bottomBox.getChildren().addAll(detailsButton, voiceButton);

        card.getChildren().addAll(
                imageFrame,
                separatorTop,
                title,
                description,
                price,
                spacer,
                separatorBottom,
                bottomBox
        );

        return card;
    }

    private StackPane createImageFrame(Product product) {
        StackPane imageFrame = new StackPane();
        imageFrame.setPrefWidth(253);
        imageFrame.setMinHeight(145);
        imageFrame.setPrefHeight(145);
        imageFrame.setMaxHeight(145);
        imageFrame.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        imageFrame.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                        "-fx-background-radius: 16;" +
                        "-fx-border-color: #DBEAFE;" +
                        "-fx-border-radius: 16;"
        );

        ImageView imageView = new ImageView();
        imageView.setFitWidth(220);
        imageView.setFitHeight(120);
        imageView.setPreserveRatio(true);
        imageView.setSmooth(true);

        if (loadProductImage(product, imageView)) {
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(imageView);
            setupImageHover(imageView);
        } else {
            Label icon = new Label("💊");
            icon.setStyle(
                    "-fx-text-fill: #94A3B8;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 40px;"
            );
            imageFrame.getChildren().add(icon);
        }

        return imageFrame;
    }

    private Region createSeparator() {
        Region line = new Region();
        line.setPrefHeight(1);
        line.setMaxHeight(1);
        line.setStyle("-fx-background-color: #F1F5F9;");
        VBox.setMargin(line, new Insets(2, 0, 2, 0));
        return line;
    }

    private void setupCardHover(VBox card) {
        card.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(-4);

            DropShadow hoverShadow = new DropShadow();
            hoverShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.10));
            hoverShadow.setRadius(32);
            hoverShadow.setOffsetY(12);
            card.setEffect(hoverShadow);

            translate.play();
        });

        card.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(300), card);
            translate.setToY(0);

            DropShadow normalShadow = new DropShadow();
            normalShadow.setColor(Color.web("#000000", 0.06));
            normalShadow.setRadius(24);
            normalShadow.setOffsetY(8);
            card.setEffect(normalShadow);

            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupImageHover(ImageView imageView) {
        imageView.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            ScaleTransition scale = new ScaleTransition(Duration.millis(300), imageView);
            scale.setToX(1.03);
            scale.setToY(1.03);
            scale.play();
        });

        imageView.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            ScaleTransition scale = new ScaleTransition(Duration.millis(300), imageView);
            scale.setToX(1.0);
            scale.setToY(1.0);
            scale.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupDetailsButtonHover(Button button) {
        button.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #DBEAFE;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(-2);

            DropShadow shadow = new DropShadow();
            shadow.setColor(Color.web("#6366F1", 0.15));
            shadow.setRadius(12);
            shadow.setOffsetY(4);
            button.setEffect(shadow);

            translate.play();
        });

        button.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            button.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #EFF6FF;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-font-size: 14px;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );

            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            translate.setToY(0);

            button.setEffect(null);

            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void playCardEntrance(Node node, int index) {
        node.setOpacity(0);
        node.setTranslateY(20);

        FadeTransition fade = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        fade.setFromValue(0);
        fade.setToValue(1);
        fade.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(350), node);
        translate.setFromY(20);
        translate.setToY(0);
        translate.setDelay(Duration.millis(index * 80));

        new ParallelTransition(fade, translate).play();
    }

    private void openProductDetails(Product product) {
        if (product == null) {
            return;
        }

        UserProductDetailsController.setSelectedProduct(product);
        Main.showPatientProductDetails();
    }

    private boolean loadProductImage(Product product, ImageView imageView) {
        try {
            String imagePath = product.getImage();

            if (imagePath != null && !imagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                File file = new File(imagePath);

                if (file.exists()) {
                    imageView.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
                    return true;
                }
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur chargement image produit : " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return false;
    }

    private String formatPrice(double price) {
        return String.format("%.3f", price).replace(".", ",");
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return "";
        }

        String result = value.trim().toLowerCase();
        result = Normalizer.normalize(result, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        result = result.replaceAll("\\p{M}", "");
        result = result.replaceAll("\\s+", " ");

        return result;
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    @FXML
    public void resetFilters() {
        if (searchField != null) {
            searchField.clear();
        }

        if (categoryCombo != null) {
            categoryCombo.setValue("Toutes les catégories");
        }

        displayedGroups = new ArrayList<>(groupedProducts);
        currentPage = 0;
        showCurrentPage();
    }

    @FXML
    public void nextPage() {
        int totalPages = (int) Math.ceil((double) displayedGroups.size() / itemsPerPage);

        if (currentPage < totalPages - 1) {
            currentPage++;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void previousPage() {
        if (currentPage > 0) {
            currentPage--;
            showCurrentPage();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showPatientDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showPatientProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showPatientPharmacies();
    }

    private static class ProductGroup {
        Product mainProduct;
        List<Product> items = new ArrayList<>();

        ProductGroup(Product mainProduct) {
            this.mainProduct = mainProduct;
        }
    }
}
package Controler;

import com.google.zxing.BarcodeFormat;
import com.google.zxing.EncodeHintType;
import com.google.zxing.client.j2se.MatrixToImageWriter;
import com.google.zxing.common.BitMatrix;
import com.google.zxing.qrcode.QRCodeWriter;
import entities.Pharmacy;
import entities.Product;
import javafx.animation.*;
import javafx.embed.swing.SwingFXUtils;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.PerspectiveCamera;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.scene.transform.Rotate;
import javafx.util.Duration;
import services.PharmacyServices;
import services.ProductServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.File;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class UserProductDetailsController {

    @FXML private VBox pageContainer;
    @FXML private VBox mainCard;
    @FXML private VBox imageFrameBox;
    @FXML private VBox qrCard;

    @FXML private ImageView productImage;
    @FXML private ImageView qrImageView;

    @FXML private Label productNameLabel;
    @FXML private Label priceLabel;
    @FXML private Label descriptionLabel;

    @FXML private Label dosageLabel;
    @FXML private Label categoryLabel;
    @FXML private Label formLabel;
    @FXML private Label laboratoryLabel;
    @FXML private Label therapeuticClassLabel;

    @FXML private Label adviceTitle;
    @FXML private Label qrInfoLabel;

    @FXML private Button topReturnButton;
    @FXML private Button securityButton;
    @FXML private VBox securityContent;

    private ProductServices productServices;
    private PharmacyServices pharmacyServices;

    private static Product selectedProduct;

    public static void setSelectedProduct(Product product) {
        selectedProduct = product;
    }

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        productServices = new ProductServices();
        pharmacyServices = new PharmacyServices();

        if (selectedProduct == null) {
            showEmptyData();
            setupVisualEffects();
            return;
        }

        Product productToShow = getProductWithTotalStock(selectedProduct);

        showProductDetails(productToShow);
        generateAvailabilityQrCode(productToShow);
        setupVisualEffects();
        startPriceCounter(productToShow.getPrice());
    }

    private Product getProductWithTotalStock(Product product) {
        try {
            List<Product> allProducts = productServices.getAll();
            int totalStock = 0;

            for (Product p : allProducts) {
                boolean sameName = normalizeText(p.getProductName())
                        .equals(normalizeText(product.getProductName()));

                boolean sameDosage = normalizeText(p.getDosage())
                        .equals(normalizeText(product.getDosage()));

                if (sameName && sameDosage) {
                    totalStock += p.getAvailableQuantity();
                }
            }

            product.setAvailableQuantity(totalStock);

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        return product;
    }

    private void showEmptyData() {
        productNameLabel.setText("Produit non sélectionné");
        priceLabel.setText("0,000 DT");
        descriptionLabel.setText("Aucune description");

        dosageLabel.setText("-");
        categoryLabel.setText("-");
        formLabel.setText("-");
        laboratoryLabel.setText("-");
        therapeuticClassLabel.setText("-");

        if (qrInfoLabel != null) {
            qrInfoLabel.setText("Aucun QR code disponible.");
        }
    }

    private void showProductDetails(Product product) {
        productNameLabel.setText(safe(product.getProductName()));
        priceLabel.setText("0,000 DT");

        descriptionLabel.setText(
                safe(product.getDescription()).isEmpty()
                        ? "Aucune description"
                        : safe(product.getDescription())
        );

        dosageLabel.setText(emptyToDash(product.getDosage()));
        categoryLabel.setText(emptyToDash(product.getCategory()));
        formLabel.setText(emptyToDash(product.getForm()));
        laboratoryLabel.setText(emptyToDash(product.getLaboratory()));
        therapeuticClassLabel.setText(emptyToDash(product.getTherapeuticClass()));

        loadProductImage(product);
    }

    private void loadProductImage(Product product) {
        try {
            String imagePath = product.getImage();

            if (imagePath == null || imagePath.trim().isEmpty()) {
                return;
            }

            File file = new File(imagePath);

            if (file.exists()) {
                productImage.setImage(new Image(file.toURI().toString()));
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Erreur chargement image produit : " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void setupVisualEffects() {
        startPageFade();
        startImageFloating();
        startAdviceGlow();
        setupImage3DEffect();
        setupButtonEffects();
        setupSecurityButtonEffects();
        setupQrCardEffects();
    }

    private void startPageFade() {
        if (mainCard == null) {
            return;
        }

        mainCard.setOpacity(0);
        mainCard.setTranslateY(16);

        FadeTransition fade = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(350), mainCard);
        fade.setFromValue(0);
        fade.setToValue(1);

        TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(350), mainCard);
        translate.setFromY(16);
        translate.setToY(0);

        new ParallelTransition(fade, translate).play();
    }

    private void startImageFloating() {
        if (imageFrameBox == null) {
            return;
        }

        TranslateTransition floating = new TranslateTransition(Duration.seconds(3), imageFrameBox);
        floating.setFromY(-6);
        floating.setToY(6);
        floating.setAutoReverse(true);
        floating.setCycleCount(Animation.INDEFINITE);
        floating.play();
    }

    private void startAdviceGlow() {
        if (adviceTitle == null) {
            return;
        }

        DropShadow glow = new DropShadow();
        glow.setColor(Color.web("#F59E0B", 0.28));
        glow.setRadius(4);
        glow.setSpread(0.08);

        adviceTitle.setEffect(glow);

        Timeline pulse = new Timeline(
                new KeyFrame(Duration.ZERO, new KeyValue(glow.radiusProperty(), 3)),
                new KeyFrame(Duration.seconds(1), new KeyValue(glow.radiusProperty(), 10)),
                new KeyFrame(Duration.seconds(2), new KeyValue(glow.radiusProperty(), 3))
        );

        pulse.setCycleCount(Animation.INDEFINITE);
        pulse.play();
    }

    private void setupImage3DEffect() {
        if (imageFrameBox == null) {
            return;
        }

        imageFrameBox.sceneProperty().addListener((obs, oldScene, newScene) -> {
            if (newScene != null && newScene.getCamera() == null) {
                newScene.setCamera(new PerspectiveCamera());
            }
        });

        Rotate rotateX = new Rotate(0, Rotate.X_AXIS);
        Rotate rotateY = new Rotate(0, Rotate.Y_AXIS);

        imageFrameBox.getTransforms().addAll(rotateX, rotateY);

        DropShadow shadow = new DropShadow();
        shadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.16));
        shadow.setRadius(16);
        shadow.setOffsetX(0);
        shadow.setOffsetY(5);
        imageFrameBox.setEffect(shadow);

        imageFrameBox.setOnMouseMoved(event -> {
            double width = imageFrameBox.getWidth();
            double height = imageFrameBox.getHeight();

            if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
                return;
            }

            double rotateYValue = ((event.getX() / width) - 0.5) * 8;
            double rotateXValue = -((event.getY() / height) - 0.5) * 8;

            rotateX.setAngle(rotateXValue);
            rotateY.setAngle(rotateYValue);

            shadow.setOffsetX(-rotateYValue);
            shadow.setOffsetY(Math.abs(rotateXValue) + 6);
        });

        imageFrameBox.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            Timeline reset = new Timeline(
                    new KeyFrame(Duration.millis(200),
                            new KeyValue(rotateX.angleProperty(), 0),
                            new KeyValue(rotateY.angleProperty(), 0),
                            new KeyValue(shadow.offsetXProperty(), 0),
                            new KeyValue(shadow.offsetYProperty(), 5)
                    )
            );

            reset.play();
        });
    }

    private void setupButtonEffects() {
        setupScaleHover(topReturnButton, 1.03);
    }

    private void setupScaleHover(Button button, double scaleValue) {
        if (button == null) {
            return;
        }

        button.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            ScaleTransition scale = new ScaleTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            scale.setToX(scaleValue);
            scale.setToY(scaleValue);
            scale.play();

            DropShadow glow = new DropShadow();
            glow.setColor(Color.web("#6366F1", 0.20));
            glow.setRadius(14);
            glow.setOffsetY(4);
            button.setEffect(glow);
        });

        button.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            ScaleTransition scale = new ScaleTransition(Duration.millis(200), button);
            scale.setToX(1);
            scale.setToY(1);
            scale.play();

            button.setEffect(null);
        });
    }

    private void setupSecurityButtonEffects() {
        if (securityButton == null) {
            return;
        }

        DropShadow warningGlow = new DropShadow();
        warningGlow.setColor(Color.web("#F59E0B", 0.25));
        warningGlow.setRadius(5);
        warningGlow.setSpread(0.08);

        securityButton.setEffect(warningGlow);

        Timeline pulse = new Timeline(
                new KeyFrame(Duration.ZERO, new KeyValue(warningGlow.radiusProperty(), 4)),
                new KeyFrame(Duration.seconds(1), new KeyValue(warningGlow.radiusProperty(), 12)),
                new KeyFrame(Duration.seconds(2), new KeyValue(warningGlow.radiusProperty(), 4))
        );

        pulse.setCycleCount(Animation.INDEFINITE);
        pulse.play();

        securityButton.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            securityButton.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #1E293B;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );
        });

        securityButton.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            securityButton.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color: #0F172A;" +
                            "-fx-text-fill: white;" +
                            "-fx-font-weight: bold;" +
                            "-fx-background-radius: 12;" +
                            "-fx-cursor: hand;"
            );
        });
    }

    private void setupQrCardEffects() {
        if (qrCard == null) {
            return;
        }

        qrCard.setOnMouseEntered(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), qrCard);
            translate.setToY(-2);

            DropShadow glow = new DropShadow();
            glow.setColor(Color.web("#6366F1", 0.16));
            glow.setRadius(22);
            glow.setOffsetY(8);
            qrCard.setEffect(glow);

            translate.play();
        });

        qrCard.setOnMouseExited(event -> {
            TranslateTransition translate = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(200), qrCard);
            translate.setToY(0);

            DropShadow shadow = new DropShadow();
            shadow.setColor(Color.web("#0F172A", 0.05));
            shadow.setRadius(14);
            shadow.setOffsetY(4);
            qrCard.setEffect(shadow);

            translate.play();
        });
    }

    private void startPriceCounter(double targetPrice) {
        Timeline timeline = new Timeline();
        int frames = 50;

        for (int i = 0; i <= frames; i++) {
            double progress = (double) i / frames;
            double eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
            double value = targetPrice * eased;

            KeyFrame keyFrame = new KeyFrame(
                    Duration.millis(1500 * progress),
                    event -> priceLabel.setText(formatPrice(value) + " DT")
            );

            timeline.getKeyFrames().add(keyFrame);
        }

        timeline.setOnFinished(event -> priceLabel.setText(formatPrice(targetPrice) + " DT"));
        timeline.play();
    }

    private void generateAvailabilityQrCode(Product productToShow) {
        try {
            if (qrImageView == null) {
                return;
            }

            List<Product> sameProducts = getSameProducts(productToShow);
            String qrText = buildQrText(productToShow, sameProducts);

            QRCodeWriter writer = new QRCodeWriter();

            Map<EncodeHintType, Object> hints = new EnumMap<>(EncodeHintType.class);
            hints.put(EncodeHintType.CHARACTER_SET, "UTF-8");
            hints.put(EncodeHintType.MARGIN, 1);

            BitMatrix matrix = writer.encode(
                    qrText,
                    BarcodeFormat.QR_CODE,
                    420,
                    420,
                    hints
            );

            BufferedImage bufferedImage = MatrixToImageWriter.toBufferedImage(matrix);
            qrImageView.setImage(SwingFXUtils.toFXImage(bufferedImage, null));

            if (qrInfoLabel != null) {
                qrInfoLabel.setText("QR Code généré pour : " + safe(productToShow.getProductName()));
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();

            if (qrInfoLabel != null) {
                qrInfoLabel.setText("Erreur lors de la génération du QR Code.");
            }
        }
    }

    private List<Product> getSameProducts(Product productToShow) {
        List<Product> result = new ArrayList<>();

        try {
            List<Product> allProducts = productServices.getAll();

            for (Product product : allProducts) {
                boolean sameName = normalizeText(product.getProductName())
                        .equals(normalizeText(productToShow.getProductName()));

                boolean sameDosage = normalizeText(product.getDosage())
                        .equals(normalizeText(productToShow.getDosage()));

                if (sameName && sameDosage) {
                    result.add(product);
                }
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        return result;
    }

    private String buildQrText(Product productToShow, List<Product> products) {
        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();

        builder.append("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n");
        builder.append("💊 SHIFAA - FICHE MÉDICAMENT\n");
        builder.append("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n");

        builder.append("📌 Produit : ").append(cleanForQr(productToShow.getProductName())).append("\n");
        builder.append("💰 Prix : ").append(formatPrice(productToShow.getPrice())).append(" DT\n");
        builder.append("📦 Quantité totale : ").append(productToShow.getAvailableQuantity()).append(" unités\n\n");

        builder.append("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n");
        builder.append("🏥 PHARMACIES DISPONIBLES\n");
        builder.append("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n");

        if (products == null || products.isEmpty()) {
            builder.append("❌ Aucune pharmacie disponible pour ce médicament.\n\n");
            builder.append("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n");
            builder.append("Merci d'utiliser SHIFAA 💙");
            return builder.toString();
        }

        List<Pharmacy> pharmacies = new ArrayList<>();

        try {
            pharmacies = pharmacyServices.getAll();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        int index = 1;

        for (Product product : products) {
            Pharmacy pharmacy = getPharmacyById(pharmacies, product.getPharmacyId());
            int quantity = product.getAvailableQuantity();

            String icon;
            String state;

            if (quantity <= 0) {
                icon = "❌";
                state = "🔴 Indisponible";
            } else if (quantity <= 9) {
                icon = "⚠️";
                state = "🟠 Stock faible";
            } else {
                icon = "✅";
                state = "🟢 Disponible";
            }

            builder.append(icon).append(" ").append(index).append(". ");

            if (pharmacy == null) {
                builder.append("Pharmacie non définie\n");
                builder.append("📍 Adresse : Non renseignée\n");
            } else {
                builder.append(cleanForQr(pharmacy.getPharmacyName())).append("\n");
                builder.append("📍 Adresse : ").append(cleanForQr(pharmacy.getAddress())).append("\n");

                if (pharmacy.getPhone() != null && !pharmacy.getPhone().trim().isEmpty()) {
                    builder.append("📞 Téléphone : ").append(cleanForQr(pharmacy.getPhone())).append("\n");
                }
            }

            builder.append("📦 Stock : ").append(quantity);
            builder.append(quantity <= 1 ? " unité\n" : " unités\n");

            builder.append("📊 État : ").append(state).append("\n");

            if (pharmacy != null && pharmacy.getAddress() != null && !pharmacy.getAddress().trim().isEmpty()) {
                builder.append("🧭 Pour le guidage : ouvrez Google Maps et cherchez :\n");
                builder.append("   ").append(cleanForQr(pharmacy.getPharmacyName())).append(" - ");
                builder.append(cleanForQr(pharmacy.getAddress())).append("\n");
            }

            builder.append("\n");
            index++;
        }

        builder.append("━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n");
        builder.append("Merci d'utiliser SHIFAA 💙");

        return builder.toString();
    }

    private Pharmacy getPharmacyById(List<Pharmacy> pharmacies, int pharmacyId) {
        for (Pharmacy pharmacy : pharmacies) {
            if (pharmacy.getPharmacyId() == pharmacyId) {
                return pharmacy;
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    @FXML
    public void toggleSecurity() {
        if (securityContent == null || securityButton == null) {
            return;
        }

        boolean show = !securityContent.isVisible();

        securityContent.setVisible(show);
        securityContent.setManaged(show);

        if (show) {
            securityButton.setText("⚠ Sécurité médicament — cliquez pour réduire");
        } else {
            securityButton.setText("⚠ Sécurité médicament — cliquez pour afficher");
        }
    }

    private String formatPrice(double price) {
        return String.format("%.3f", price).replace(".", ",");
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return "";
        }

        String result = value.trim().toLowerCase();
        result = Normalizer.normalize(result, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        result = result.replaceAll("\\p{M}", "");
        result = result.replaceAll("\\s+", " ");

        return result;
    }

    private String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    private String emptyToDash(String value) {
        return safe(value).isEmpty() ? "-" : safe(value);
    }

    private String cleanForQr(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return "Non renseigné";
        }

        return value.trim()
                .replace("é", "e")
                .replace("è", "e")
                .replace("ê", "e")
                .replace("à", "a")
                .replace("ç", "c")
                .replace("É", "E")
                .replace("È", "E")
                .replace("À", "A")
                .replace("Ç", "C");
    }

    @FXML
    public void goBackProducts() {
        Main.showPatientProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goDashboard() {
        Main.showPatientDashboard();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goProducts() {
        Main.showPatientProducts();
    }

    @FXML
    public void goPharmacies() {
        Main.showPatientPharmacies();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.user;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.control.*;
import javafx.scene.image.Image;
import javafx.scene.image.ImageView;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.stage.FileChooser;
import services.userServices;
import tests.Main;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Base64;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;

public class UserProfileController {

    @FXML private TextField firstNameField;
    @FXML private TextField lastNameField;
    @FXML private TextField emailField;
    @FXML private TextField phoneField;
    @FXML private TextField ageField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> genderCombo;
    @FXML private TextArea addressArea;
    @FXML private ImageView profileImageView;
    @FXML private Label imageStatusLabel;

    @FXML private VBox proSection;
    @FXML private VBox specialtyBox;
    @FXML private VBox feeBox;
    @FXML private VBox pharmacyBox;
    @FXML private VBox licenceBox;
    
    @FXML private TextField specialtyField;
    @FXML private TextField feeField;
    @FXML private TextField pharmacyNameField;
    @FXML private TextField licenceField;

    @FXML private Button editButton;
    @FXML private VBox imageButtonsBox;
    @FXML private VBox passwordSection;
    @FXML private PasswordField newPasswordField;
    @FXML private PasswordField confirmNewPasswordField;
    @FXML private HBox actionButtonsBox;

    private user currentUser;
    private userServices userService = new userServices();
    private String base64Image = null;
    private String selectedAvatarUrl = null;

    @FXML
    public void initialize() {
        genderCombo.setItems(javafx.collections.FXCollections.observableArrayList("Homme", "Femme"));
        loadUserData(Main.getCurrentUserId());
    }

    public void loadUserData(int userId) {
        try {
            for (user u : userService.getAll()) {
                if (u.getUserId() == userId) {
                    currentUser = u;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (currentUser != null) {
                firstNameField.setText(currentUser.getFirstName());
                lastNameField.setText(currentUser.getLastName());
                emailField.setText(currentUser.getEmail());
                phoneField.setText(currentUser.getPhone());
                ageField.setText(String.valueOf(currentUser.getAge()));
                genderCombo.setValue(currentUser.getGender() == user.Gender.M ? "Homme" : "Femme");
                addressArea.setText(currentUser.getAddress());
                
                base64Image = currentUser.getUserPhoto();
                selectedAvatarUrl = currentUser.getAvatarUrl();
                loadProfileImage();
                setupRoleSpecificFields();
                toggleEditMode(false); // Mode lecture seule par défaut
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible de charger le profil");
        }
    }

    private void toggleEditMode(boolean enable) {
        // Toggle field editability
        firstNameField.setEditable(enable);
        lastNameField.setEditable(enable);
        emailField.setEditable(enable);
        phoneField.setEditable(enable);
        ageField.setEditable(enable);
        addressArea.setEditable(enable);
        specialtyField.setEditable(enable);
        feeField.setEditable(enable);
        pharmacyNameField.setEditable(enable);
        licenceField.setEditable(enable);

        // ComboBox interaction
        genderCombo.setMouseTransparent(!enable);

        String editStyle = "-fx-background-color: white; -fx-border-color: #E2E8F0; -fx-text-fill: #4A5568;";
        String readStyle = "-fx-background-color: transparent; -fx-border-color: transparent; -fx-text-fill: #2D3748; -fx-opacity: 1;";

        // Apply styles
        firstNameField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        lastNameField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        emailField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        phoneField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        ageField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        addressArea.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        specialtyField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        feeField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        pharmacyNameField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        licenceField.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);
        genderCombo.setStyle(enable ? editStyle : readStyle);

        // Toggle visibility
        editButton.setVisible(!enable);
        editButton.setManaged(!enable);
        
        imageButtonsBox.setVisible(enable);
        imageButtonsBox.setManaged(enable);

        passwordSection.setVisible(enable);
        passwordSection.setManaged(enable);
        
        actionButtonsBox.setVisible(enable);
        actionButtonsBox.setManaged(enable);
        
        if (!enable) {
            newPasswordField.clear();
            confirmNewPasswordField.clear();
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void startEditing() {
        toggleEditMode(true);
    }

    private void setupRoleSpecificFields() {
        user.Role role = currentUser.getRole();
        
        // Par défaut, cacher tout
        proSection.setVisible(false);
        proSection.setManaged(false);
        specialtyBox.setVisible(false);
        specialtyBox.setManaged(false);
        feeBox.setVisible(false);
        feeBox.setManaged(false);
        pharmacyBox.setVisible(false);
        pharmacyBox.setManaged(false);
        licenceBox.setVisible(false);
        licenceBox.setManaged(false);

        if (role == user.Role.doctor) {
            proSection.setVisible(true);
            proSection.setManaged(true);
            specialtyBox.setVisible(true);
            specialtyBox.setManaged(true);
            feeBox.setVisible(true);
            feeBox.setManaged(true);
            specialtyField.setText(currentUser.getSpecialty());
            feeField.setText(String.valueOf(currentUser.getConsultationFee()));
        } else if (role == user.Role.pharmacist) {
            proSection.setVisible(true);
            proSection.setManaged(true);
            pharmacyBox.setVisible(true);
            pharmacyBox.setManaged(true);
            licenceBox.setVisible(true);
            licenceBox.setManaged(true);
            pharmacyNameField.setText(currentUser.getPharmacyName());
            licenceField.setText(currentUser.getLicenceNumber());
        }
    }

    private void loadProfileImage() {
        String photoToLoad = (selectedAvatarUrl != null && !selectedAvatarUrl.isEmpty()) ? selectedAvatarUrl : base64Image;

        // Protection contre les anciennes données de test (contenant 'generated_')
        if (photoToLoad != null && photoToLoad.startsWith("generated_")) {
            photoToLoad = (base64Image != null && !base64Image.startsWith("generated_")) ? base64Image : null;
        }

        if (photoToLoad != null && !photoToLoad.trim().isEmpty()) {
            try {
                // Nettoyage rigoureux de la chaîne Base64
                String clean = photoToLoad.trim().replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]", "");
                byte[] bytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(clean);
                profileImageView.setImage(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                imageStatusLabel.setText(selectedAvatarUrl != null && !selectedAvatarUrl.isEmpty() ? "Avatar 3D chargé" : "Photo réelle chargée");
                return;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.err.println("Erreur décodage image: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        }
        profileImageView.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
        imageStatusLabel.setText("Aucune image");
    }

    @FXML
    private void chooseImage() {
        FileChooser fileChooser = new FileChooser();
        fileChooser.setTitle("Choisir une photo de profil");
        fileChooser.getExtensionFilters().addAll(
            new FileChooser.ExtensionFilter("Images", "*.png", "*.jpg", "*.jpeg")
        );
        File file = fileChooser.showOpenDialog(profileImageView.getScene().getWindow());
        if (file != null) {
            try {
                // Compression et redimensionnement pour éviter "Packet for query is too large"
                BufferedImage originalImage = ImageIO.read(file);
                if (originalImage == null) {
                    showAlert("Erreur", "Format d'image non supporté");
                    return;
                }

                // Redimensionner si trop grand (max 400px)
                int targetWidth = 400;
                int targetHeight = (int) (originalImage.getHeight() * (400.0 / originalImage.getWidth()));
                
                if (originalImage.getWidth() <= targetWidth) {
                    targetWidth = originalImage.getWidth();
                    targetHeight = originalImage.getHeight();
                }

                // Utiliser TYPE_INT_RGB pour le format JPG (pas de transparence)
                BufferedImage resizedImage = new BufferedImage(targetWidth, targetHeight, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
                Graphics2D g = resizedImage.createGraphics();
                // Remplir le fond en blanc (au cas où l'image originale avait de la transparence)
                g.setColor(java.awt.Color.WHITE);
                g.fillRect(0, 0, targetWidth, targetHeight);
                g.drawImage(originalImage, 0, 0, targetWidth, targetHeight, null);
                g.dispose();

                // Convertir en Base64 avec compression JPEG
                ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                ImageIO.write(resizedImage, "jpg", baos);
                byte[] bytes = baos.toByteArray();
                
                base64Image = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(bytes);
                
                // Prévisualisation immédiate
                profileImageView.setImage(new Image(new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes)));
                imageStatusLabel.setText("Image optimisée: " + (bytes.length/1024) + " KB");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
                showAlert("Erreur", "Impossible de traiter l'image: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void removeImage() {
        base64Image = "";
        selectedAvatarUrl = "";
        profileImageView.setImage(new Image("https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/149/149071.png"));
        imageStatusLabel.setText("Image supprimée");
    }

    @FXML
    private void saveProfile() {
        try {
            // Check password change
            String newPass = newPasswordField.getText();
            String confirmPass = confirmNewPasswordField.getText();
            if (newPass != null && !newPass.isEmpty()) {
                if (!newPass.equals(confirmPass)) {
                    showAlert("Erreur", "Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas.");
                    return;
                }
                currentUser.setPassword(newPass);
            }

            currentUser.setFirstName(firstNameField.getText());
            currentUser.setLastName(lastNameField.getText());
            currentUser.setEmail(emailField.getText());
            currentUser.setPhone(phoneField.getText());
            currentUser.setAge(Integer.parseInt(ageField.getText()));
            currentUser.setGender(genderCombo.getValue().equals("Homme") ? user.Gender.M : user.Gender.F);
            currentUser.setAddress(addressArea.getText());
            currentUser.setUserPhoto(base64Image);
            currentUser.setAvatarUrl(selectedAvatarUrl);

            if (currentUser.getRole() == user.Role.doctor) {
                currentUser.setSpecialty(specialtyField.getText());
                currentUser.setConsultationFee(Float.parseFloat(feeField.getText()));
            } else if (currentUser.getRole() == user.Role.pharmacist) {
                currentUser.setPharmacyName(pharmacyNameField.getText());
                currentUser.setLicenceNumber(licenceField.getText());
            }

            userService.update(currentUser);
            Main.refreshMainMenu();
            showAlert("Succès", "Profil mis à jour avec succès !");
            toggleEditMode(false);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Veuillez vérifier les formats numériques (âge, tarifs)");
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            showAlert("Erreur", "Base de données: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @FXML
    private void cancelChanges() {
        loadUserData(currentUser.getUserId());
    }

    private void showAlert(String title, String message) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(title);
        alert.setHeaderText(null);
        alert.setContentText(message);
        alert.showAndWait();
    }
}
package Controler;

import entities.Reclamation;
import entities.Reponse;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.fxml.Initializable;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ButtonBar;
import javafx.scene.control.ButtonType;
import javafx.scene.control.ComboBox;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.Separator;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.layout.FlowPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.stage.Modality;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import services.EscalationService;
import services.ReclamationServices;
import services.ReponseServices;
import tests.MainFx;

import java.net.URL;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ResourceBundle;

public class UserReclamationController implements Initializable {

    @FXML private FlowPane cardsContainer;
    @FXML private TextField searchField;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> filterStatut;
    @FXML private ComboBox<String> filterPriorite;
    @FXML private Label totalLabel;
    @FXML private Label messageLabel;
    @FXML private Label notificationBanner;

    private final ReclamationServices reclamationSvc = new ReclamationServices();
    private final ReponseServices     reponseSvc     = new ReponseServices();
    private final EscalationService   escalationSvc  = new EscalationService();

    private final int currentUserId = 1;

    private List<Reclamation> allReclamations;

    @Override
    public void initialize(URL url, ResourceBundle rb) {
        filterStatut.getItems().addAll("Tous les statuts", "En attente", "En cours", "Résolu");
        filterStatut.setValue("Tous les statuts");

        filterPriorite.getItems().addAll("Toutes les priorités", "faible", "moyenne", "urgente");
        filterPriorite.setValue("Toutes les priorités");

        if (MainFx.notificationUserId == currentUserId && !MainFx.notificationMessage.isEmpty()) {
            if (notificationBanner != null) {
                notificationBanner.setText("🔔 " + MainFx.notificationMessage);
                notificationBanner.setVisible(true);
                notificationBanner.setManaged(true);
            }
            MainFx.clearNotification();
        } else {
            if (notificationBanner != null) {
                notificationBanner.setVisible(false);
                notificationBanner.setManaged(false);
            }
        }

        loadAll();
    }

    private void loadAll() {
        allReclamations = reclamationSvc.getByUser(currentUserId);
        applyFilters();
    }

    @FXML
    private void applyFilters() {
        if (allReclamations == null) { return; }

        String keyword  = searchField.getText() == null ? "" : searchField.getText().toLowerCase().trim();
        String statut   = filterStatut.getValue();
        String priorite = filterPriorite.getValue();

        List<Reclamation> filtered = new ArrayList<Reclamation>();
        for (Reclamation r : allReclamations) {
            boolean matchSearch = keyword.isEmpty()
                    || r.getSubject().toLowerCase().contains(keyword)
                    || r.getDescription().toLowerCase().contains(keyword)
                    || r.getStatusreclam().toLowerCase().contains(keyword)
                    || r.getPriority().toLowerCase().contains(keyword);

            boolean matchStatut = statut == null || statut.equals("Tous les statuts")
                    || r.getStatusreclam().equalsIgnoreCase(statut);

            boolean matchPriorite = priorite == null || priorite.equals("Toutes les priorités")
                    || r.getPriority().equalsIgnoreCase(priorite);

            if (matchSearch && matchStatut && matchPriorite) {
                filtered.add(r);
            }
        }

        buildCards(filtered);
    }

    @FXML
    private void reinitialiserFiltres() {
        searchField.clear();
        filterStatut.setValue("Tous les statuts");
        filterPriorite.setValue("Toutes les priorités");
        buildCards(allReclamations);
    }

    private void buildCards(List<Reclamation> reclamations) {
        cardsContainer.getChildren().clear();
        if (totalLabel != null) {
            totalLabel.setText("Total : " + reclamations.size() + " réclamations");
        }
        if (messageLabel != null) {
            messageLabel.setText(reclamations.isEmpty() ? "Aucune réclamation trouvée." : "");
        }
        for (Reclamation r : reclamations) {
            cardsContainer.getChildren().add(buildCard(r));
        }
    }

    private VBox buildCard(Reclamation r) {
        List<Reponse> reponses      = reponseSvc.getByReclamation(r.getReclamationId());
        Reponse existingReponse     = reponses.isEmpty() ? null : reponses.get(0);
        boolean alreadyEscalated   = escalationSvc.hasOpenEscalation(r.getReclamationId());
        boolean isResolved         = "Résolu".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getStatusreclam())
                || "resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getStatusreclam());

        VBox card = new VBox(0);
        card.setPrefWidth(340);
        card.setStyle(buildCardStyle(r, existingReponse, alreadyEscalated));

        HBox header = new HBox(10);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        header.setPadding(new Insets(14, 16, 10, 16));

        Label date = new Label("📅 " + r.getReclamDate());
        date.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5;");

        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label statusBadge = buildStatusBadge(r.getStatusreclam());
        header.getChildren().addAll(date, spacer, statusBadge);

        HBox metaRow = new HBox(8);
        metaRow.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        metaRow.setPadding(new Insets(0, 16, 6, 16));

        Label type = new Label("📁 Réclamation");
        type.setStyle("-fx-font-size:11px; -fx-text-fill:#9aabb5;");

        Label priorityBadge = buildPriorityBadge(r.getPriority());

        if (alreadyEscalated) {
            Label escBadge = new Label("🚨 Escalade");
            escBadge.setStyle(
                    "-fx-background-color:#FCEBEB; -fx-text-fill:#A32D2D;"
                            + "-fx-font-size:10px; -fx-font-weight:bold;"
                            + "-fx-background-radius:10; -fx-padding:2 7 2 7;"
            );
            metaRow.getChildren().addAll(type, priorityBadge, escBadge);
        } else {
            metaRow.getChildren().addAll(type, priorityBadge);
        }

        Label subject = new Label(r.getSubject());
        subject.setStyle("-fx-font-size:15px; -fx-font-weight:bold; -fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;");
        subject.setPadding(new Insets(0, 16, 4, 16));
        subject.setWrapText(true);

        Label description = new Label(r.getDescription());
        description.setStyle("-fx-font-size:13px; -fx-text-fill:#5d6f91;");
        description.setPadding(new Insets(0, 16, 12, 16));
        description.setWrapText(true);
        description.setMaxWidth(300);

        Separator divider = new Separator();
        divider.setPadding(new Insets(0, 16, 0, 16));

        VBox responseZone = buildResponseZone(r, existingReponse, alreadyEscalated);

        HBox actions = new HBox(10);
        actions.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        actions.setPadding(new Insets(12, 16, 14, 16));

        Button btnModifier = new Button("✏ Modifier");
        btnModifier.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:white;"
                        + "-fx-border-color:#dce5f0;"
                        + "-fx-border-radius:8;"
                        + "-fx-background-radius:8;"
                        + "-fx-font-size:12px;"
                        + "-fx-padding:6 14 6 14;"
                        + "-fx-cursor:hand;"
                        + "-fx-text-fill:#0b1f44;"
        );
        btnModifier.setOnAction(e -> ouvrirModifier(r));

        Button btnSupprimer = new Button("🗑 Supprimer");
        btnSupprimer.setStyle(
                "-fx-background-color:#FCEBEB;"
                        + "-fx-text-fill:#A32D2D;"
                        + "-fx-font-size:12px;"
